### [1] [An ensemble of graph generative models for assisted history matching of the Brugge field case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12927)
*Gleb Shishaev,Anton Shirokov,Shadfar Davoodi,Mohammed Al-Shargabi,Vasily Demyanov,Daniel Arnold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于图自编码器的分区生成模型，在160维联合潜空间用CMA-ES做Brugge油田辅助历史拟合，多生成器分区方案保持地质合理性并达到与EnKF、PSO-PCA、MKL-PSO相当的井级拟合效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助历史拟合存在非唯一性：多个地质实现可产生等效生产响应但单井拟合差异显著；当油田包含多个沉积环境与建模策略时，这种歧义进一步加剧。需要一种能直接作用于非规则角点网格、并保持各沉积区分异地质特征的历史拟合参数化方法。

Method: 1) 对Brugge基准油田按地层/沉积区分别建立专用生成模型（图自编码器），各生成器直接作用于角点网格活动单元，无需转换为规则格架；2) 各生成器共享/联合构成160维联合潜空间，供优化循环使用；3) 采用协方差矩阵自适应进化策略（CMA-ES）在联合潜空间搜索；4) 目标函数为多分量：静态井数据失配、动态生产失配，以及基于解码器诱导潜度量的地质合理性惩罚项；5) 历史拟合前用拓扑数据分析（TDA）做潜空间诊断，判断单一生成器是否足够或需多生成器表述。

Result: 分区（多生成器）方案得到连贯的潜空间，保留了随区域变化的孔隙度—渗透率主要关系，并在拟合过程中将解约束在学习到的地质流形内；拟合模型复现的油田产油与产水落在先验不确定性范围内，井级拟合效果与已发表的Brugge研究（EnKF、PSO+参数化PCA、MKL+PSO）相当。

Conclusion: 基于图自编码器的分区生成模型可有效处理非规则网格与多沉积区地质非均质性，在联合潜空间中实现受地质约束的辅助历史拟合，性能与主流方法相当，同时提供更明确的地质可解释性与潜空间可控性。

Abstract: Assisted history matching remains non-unique because many geological realisations can reproduce equivalent production responses with substantial differences between individual well matches. This ambiguity increases when a field comprises several depositional settings and modelling strategies. The paper applied a graph autoencoder approach to history matching of a benchmark Brugge field that comprises different depositional zones. Each stratigraphic zone is modelled with its dedicated generative model that comprises a joint latent space for optimisation history matching loops. Graph-based generators operate directly on the active cells of the corner-point grid, so its irregular geometry is handled without conversion to a regular lattice. The optimisation search in the 160-dimensional joint latent space is done with the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy. The multi-component objective function includes static well data mismatch, dynamic production mismatch, and a geological realism penalty based on the Decoder induced latent metric. Latent space diagnostics with topological data analysis are applied prior to history matching to test whether one generator is sufficient or whether a multiple generator formulation is required. The results show that the zone-based formulation gives coherent latent spaces, preserves the main porosity and permeability relations that vary from one zone to another, and supports history matching while keeping the solutions within the learned geological manifold. The matched models reproduce field oil and water production within the prior uncertainty range and provide a comparable well-level match to previously published Brugge studies based on Ensemble Kalman Filter, Particle Swarm Optimisation with principal component analysis of parameterisation, and Multiple Kernel Learning with Particle Swarm Optimisation.

</details>


### [2] [Predictive Surprise as Self-Grounding Concept Bottleneck for Interpretable Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12955)
*Sachith Abeywickrama,Emadeldeen Eldele,Min Wu,Xiaoli Li,Chau Yuen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ConceptTime：用冻结概率预测器的"预测意外度"作为单一自监督信号，同时完成时间序列分段与概念表征，实现准确、可解释、可弃权的三合一分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险敏感领域的时间序列分类器需同时满足三项要求：准确、可解释、证据弱时能弃权。现有方法难以三者兼顾，且障碍是结构性的：定义有意义的时间模式需先知道分段边界，而合理边界又需先知道预期模式，形成循环依赖。既有方法用固定窗口或专家标注词表打破循环，但至少牺牲三项性质之一。

Method: ConceptTime用单一自监督信号闭合循环。冻结的概率预测器在每个时间步输出下一观测的分布，预测与真实信号的失配即"预测意外度"（predictive surprise）。该信号既定位分段边界，又通过预测器刻画各分段——分段由"相对预期的行为"而非"外观"来锚定。这些摘要聚类成动态模式词表即"概念"；轻量头基于概念序列分类，到最近概念的距离天然给出可靠性度量。

Result: 在17个公开基准上，ConceptTime与SOTA黑盒精度相当，胜过所有可解释基线；在Epilepsy上仅用1%标签仍保持近98%精度；在OOD检测与删除忠实性上可与softmax、原型距离及SHAP/TimeX/TimeX++竞争，且无需任何标注、语言模型调用或领域专家。

Conclusion: 通过预测意外度这一统一自监督信号，ConceptTime将分段、概念发现与可靠性估计整合为单一框架，在不依赖标注与外部模型的前提下同时实现准确性、可解释性与弃权能力。

Abstract: Time-series classifiers deployed in risk-sensitive domains must be (1) accurate, (2) interpretable, and (3) able to abstain when evidence is weak. Existing methods do not deliver all 3 at once, and the obstacle is structural, rather than logistical. Meaningful temporal patterns cannot be defined without knowing segment boundaries, yet meaningful boundaries cannot be placed without knowing which patterns to expect. Existing approaches break this circularity by using fixed windows or expert-annotated vocabularies, thereby sacrificing at least one of the 3 properties. We introduce ConceptTime, which closes the loop through a single self-supervised signal. A frozen probabilistic forecaster predicts a distribution over the next observation at every timestep of the sequence. We call the mismatch between its prediction and the realized signal the predictive surprise. This signal both locates segment boundaries and characterizes each resulting segment through the forecaster. Segments are thus grounded by how they behaved relative to expectation, not by what they look like. These summaries cluster into a vocabulary of dynamical regimes that we call concepts. A lightweight head classifies from the concept sequence, and the distance to the nearest concept yields reliability by design. Across 17 public benchmarks, ConceptTime is competitive with state-of-the-art black-box accuracy, beats every interpretable baseline, and retains near-98% accuracy on Epilepsy with 1% of labels. It further remains competitive with softmax, prototype-distance, and SHAP/TimeX/TimeX++ on OOD detection and deletion faithfulness, without a single annotation, language model call, or domain expert. Code is available at: https://github.com/Sachithx/ConceptTime.git.

</details>


### [3] [ADIndex: Layer-Adaptive Sparse Attention Indexing via Group-Level Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12956)
*Jin-Fei Qi,Yiduo Li,Jiang-Xin Shi,Kan Liu,Tao Lan,Hanlin Tang,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ADIndex，一种面向 token 级稀疏注意力的自适应、蒸馏驱动索引方法，针对 DSA 索引器仍需全前缀打分导致的延迟瓶颈，通过分层校准、组级蒸馏与低延迟推理策略，在保持近无损性能的同时获得最高 6.22x/3.40x 的 prefill/decoding kernel 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSeek Sparse Attention 虽通过 token 级选择降低注意力计算量，但其 indexer 仍需为每个 query 对整个因果前缀打分，使索引成为长上下文推理的主要延迟来源（长上下文下 indexer 可占 prefill GPU 时间 50% 以上）。作者还观察到层间存在显著异质性，即不同层的检索重叠程度不同，这为分层自适应设计提供了依据。

Method: ADIndex 包含三部分：(i) 分层校准策略，基于跨 query 检索重叠情况自适应调整，以适配层间异质性；(ii) 组级蒸馏方案，利用上述异质性高效训练自适应分层分组 indexer，从单个 query 中捕获显著检索信号；(iii) 低延迟推理策略，通过 query 组内 token bank 共享减少重复的全前缀扫描，同时保留 query 级细化。

Result: 在 DeepSeek-V3.2 与 GLM-5.1 上，于 LongBench、LongBench v2、RULER 基准保持近无损任务性能；相对标准 DSA，在 256K 上下文下分层复用配置的 prefill 与 decoding kernel 分别加速最高 6.22x 和 3.40x，128K 上下文下端到端服务加速 1.54x。

Conclusion: 索引器是长上下文稀疏注意力的关键延迟瓶颈，层间异质性可被利用来共享检索结果；ADIndex 通过分层自适应与组级蒸馏在精度与效率间取得良好折中，为稀疏注意力推理加速提供了可行路径。

Abstract: DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) reduces the computational cost of attention through token-level selection. However, its indexer still scores the entire causal prefix for each query, making indexing a dominant source of latency in long-context inference. Notably, the indexer alone can account for more than 50% of total prefill GPU time at long context lengths. To address this bottleneck, we propose ADIndex, an Adaptive and Distillation-driven Indexing method for token-level sparse attention. ADIndex integrates three key components: (i) a layer-wise calibration strategy that adaptively accommodates cross-query retrieval overlap, motivated by our observation of pronounced inter-layer heterogeneity; (ii) a group-level distillation scheme that leverages this heterogeneity to efficiently train adaptive layer-wise group indexers, capturing salient retrieval signals from individual queries; and (iii) a low-latency inference strategy that reduces repeated full-prefix scans through token bank sharing within query groups while retaining query-specific refinement. Experiments on DeepSeek-V3.2 and GLM-5.1 demonstrate that ADIndex preserves near-lossless task performance on LongBench, LongBench v2, and RULER while delivering substantial efficiency gains over standard DSA, including prefill and decoding kernel speedups of up to 6.22x and 3.40x, respectively, at a context length of 256K across the layer-wise reuse configurations, as well as an end-to-end serving speedup of 1.54x at a context length of 128K.

</details>


### [4] [Exploration via Exploitation: The Blessing of Reward Diversity in Personalized Federated RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12964)
*Jiin Woo,Gauri Joshi,Yuejie Chi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 

Method: 

Result: 

Conclusion: 

Abstract: While federated reinforcement learning (RL) has traditionally guaranteed high efficiency in homogeneous environments, collaboration is often hindered when agents possess distinct goals. In this paper, we flip this paradigm, framing reward heterogeneity not as a hurdle to consensus, but as a structural asset that catalyzes exploration. We first propose Personalized Federated Upper Confidence Bound Value Iteration (PF-UCBVI), which achieves optimal linear speedup by decoupling shared dynamics from personalized objectives. To eliminate the risks of explicit exploration, we then introduce Personalized Federated Exploration-Free Value Iteration (PF-EFVI), a purely greedy algorithm that leverages reward diversity to ensure state-action coverage. We prove that PF-EFVI attains logarithmic regret without explicit exploration bonuses under sufficient reward diversity. Our results reveal that agent disagreement shifts the exploration burden from temporal complexity to spatial diversity, enabling efficient collective learning without explicit exploration.

</details>


### [5] [StatPrompt-LLM: A Lightweight Statistical Refinement for Foundation-Style Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12974)
*Aniket Sinha,Veronica W. Tsou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 StatPrompt-LLM，在 Time-LLM 的提示机制中注入数据集级统计信息，并引入零初始化自回归旁路作为线性预测通路，在电力与气象数据集的低前缀（10%）设定下显著降低预测误差，但全前缀表现依赖数据集且带来额外计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型式时间序列预测（将大语言模型适配到序列数据）近年兴起，但如何向这类模型有效暴露趋势、尺度、时间依赖等基本时序结构仍是未解问题，尤其在观测数据有限（低前缀/少样本）时更为突出。

Method: 开展跨代预测架构的探索性实证研究；提出 StatPrompt-LLM——对 Time-LLM 的轻量统计式改进：在原有提示机制中增强数据集级统计信息，并加入零初始化自回归旁路作为互补的线性预测路径。在 ETTh1、ETTh2、Weather 上，于全前缀与缩减前缀两种配置下评估，并进行消融与计算开销分析。

Result: 在 10% 缩减前缀配置下，样本内预测误差低于 Time-LLM，各数据集不加权平均相对下降为 MSE 40.2%、MAE 21.4%；全前缀结果更依赖数据集。消融显示，在组合架构中屏蔽部分额外统计描述子组时，性能变化较温和且依赖数据集。计算分析表明相对 Time-LLM 增加了参数量与运行时间开销。

Conclusion: 统计提示与线性旁路的结合在观测受限场景下收益明显，但在数据充足时增益不稳定，且存在效率代价；统计描述子组的贡献有限，提示该方法更适合作为低数据条件下的轻量增强方案，而非通用替代。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting has recently seen a paradigm shift with the introduction of foundation-style models that adapt large pretrained language architectures to sequential data. However, effectively exposing fundamental temporal structures, such as trend, scale, and temporal dependence, to these models remains an open challenge, particularly when the available observations are limited. In this paper, we conduct an exploratory empirical study across different generations of forecasting architectures and propose StatPrompt-LLM, a lightweight statistical refinement of Time-LLM. StatPrompt-LLM augments the existing Time-LLM prompting mechanism with dataset-level statistical information and introduces a zero-initialised autoregressive bypass as a complementary linear forecasting pathway. We evaluate our framework on electricity and meteorological time-series benchmarks, including ETTh1, ETTh2, and Weather, under full-prefix and reduced-prefix configurations. Under the 10% reduced-prefix configuration, StatPrompt-LLM achieved lower in-sample prediction errors than Time-LLM, with unweighted average relative reductions across datasets of 40.2% in MSE and 21.4% in MAE. Full-prefix results were more dataset-dependent. Ablation analyses revealed relatively modest and dataset-dependent changes when selected additional statistical descriptor groups were masked within the combined architecture. Computational profiling indicated additional model parameters and runtime overhead relative to Time-LLM.

</details>


### [6] [MADSNet: A Multi-Scale Axial-Attentive Dense Spiking Network for Spike Camera Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12980)
*Sirui Li,Rui Zhao,Siqi Cai,Haizhou Li,Tiejun Huang,Yajing Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MADSNet，一种多尺度轴向注意力稠密脉冲网络，用于脉冲相机图像重建，在提升重建保真度的同时保持低延迟与低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲相机以高时间分辨率的二值脉冲流编码动态场景，SNN 能以稀疏、仅累加方式处理此类信号，但现有基于 SNN 的重建方法通常较浅，重建保真度仍落后于 ANN 方法。

Method: 采用分层脉冲编码器—解码器结构：多尺度脉冲 stem 提取早期特征，轴向脉冲注意力捕获长程依赖，稠密脉冲瓶颈增强特征复用，脉冲—连续解码器从脉冲域表示重建连续强度图像。

Result: 在灰度与彩色脉冲重建实验中，MADSNet 在 SREDS 上分别比 SSIR 和 Spk2ImgNet 高出 4.3 dB 和 1.94 dB PSNR；相较 Spk2ImgNet 延迟约降低 15 倍，估计能耗仅约 1/40。

Conclusion: 精心设计的 SNN 架构能够在保持计算效率的同时提升重建质量，说明 SNN 在脉冲相机视觉任务中具备与 ANN 竞争甚至更优的潜力。

Abstract: Spike cameras encode dynamic scenes into high-temporal-resolution binary spike streams for efficient high-speed vision. Although spiking neural networks (SNNs) can process such signals with sparse and accumulate-only computation, existing SNN-based reconstruction methods are typically shallow and still lag behind ANN-based models in reconstruction fidelity. This paper proposes MADSNet, a Multi-scale Axial-attentive Dense Spiking Network for spike camera image reconstruction. MADSNet adopts a hierarchical spiking encoder--decoder, where a multi-scale spike stem extracts early features, axial spike attention captures long-range dependencies, a dense spiking bottleneck enhances feature reuse, and a spike-to-continuous decoder reconstructs continuous intensity images from spike-domain representations. Experiments on gray-scale and color spike reconstruction show that MADSNet outperforms SSIR by 4.3 dB PSNR and Spk2ImgNet by 1.94 dB on SREDS. Compared with Spk2ImgNet, it achieves about 15$\times$ lower latency and uses only about 1/40 of the estimated energy, demonstrating that carefully designed SNN architectures can improve reconstruction quality while preserving computational efficiency.

</details>


### [7] [Third Eye: Beyond Immediate Gains in Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12988)
*Sujato Dutta,Padala Indira Sneha,Kypu Mahitha,Shashank Vanga,Neeraj Choudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"Third Eye"评测框架，将自更新（self-update）的即时收益与"再次原生更新后"的持续性收益分离评估；实验发现候选选择使即时效用看似中性实则部分为负，而后续更新会进一步降低效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多以更新后的即时性能衡量自更新（如模型自我生成数据再训练）的价值，但一个有用的自更新在模型再次更新后是否仍然有用尚不清楚；即时表现可能因候选筛选而被高估，无法代表自更新的完整价值。

Method: 构建Third Eye评测：5个3–8B骨干模型（Qwen3-4B、Llama-3.2-3B、SmolLM3-3B、Qwen3-8B、Llama-3.1-8B），覆盖数学与代码任务；共64次全新运行，每次运行3个候选、每候选3次延续（continuation）更新；选择集与最终评分项互斥；核心对比为平衡的Qwen3-4B/Llama-3.2-3B；辅以目标域与OOD损失分析、匹配配方与recipient-donor对照实验以检验缓解手段与机制。

Result: 平衡对比中即时（被选中）效用接近零，但延续后效用下降1.78个百分点（同时95%区间[0.68, 2.88]）；候选平均的即时效用已为负，说明"中性"的第一阶段部分源于选择效应。延续同样降低SmolLM3-3B与Llama-3.1-8B的效用，Qwen3-8B结论不确定。保留性变化主要由目标域与OOD损失驱动；匹配配方与recipient-donor对照既未找到可靠缓解手段，也未识别出单一机制。

Conclusion: 应将持续性自更新作为"适应转移（adaptation transition）"来评估，采用独立评分与无更新基线，而非把即时性能当作其全部价值。

Abstract: Does a useful self-update remain useful after the model updates again? Third Eye separates immediate performance from the outcome of a further native update. We evaluate five 3-8B backbones on math and code: 64 fresh runs, three candidates per run, and three continuations per candidate. Selection and final scoring use disjoint items. In the balanced Qwen3-4B/Llama-3.2-3B comparison, immediate-selected utility is near zero, but continuation reduces it by 1.78 percentage points (simultaneous 95% interval [0.68, 2.88]). Candidate-averaged immediate utility is already negative: selection partly accounts for the apparently neutral first step. Continuation also lowers utility for SmolLM3-3B and Llama-3.1-8B; Qwen3-8B remains inconclusive. Target and out-of-distribution losses dominate retention changes. Matched recipe and recipient-donor controls identify neither a reliable mitigation nor a single mechanism. The results support evaluating persistent self-updates as adaptation transitions, with independent scoring and a no-update baseline, rather than interpreting immediate performance as their full value.

</details>


### [8] [Multimodal Graph Learning: A Survey of Models, Benchmarks, and Frontiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12990)
*Rui Xue,Tianfu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述以"算法为中心、以评测为依据"的视角系统梳理多模态属性图（MAG）学习，提出统一消息传递形式化与五级融合分类体系，并整合数据集、基准与评测协议，最后展望与AI Agent相关的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAG（节点携带文本、图像等模态、边编码实体关系）广泛应用于电商、社交媒体、内容平台与创作应用，相关研究沿"图中心"与"基础模型中心"两条互补路线发展，但已有基准在数据集与评测协议上互不一致，缺乏统一的形式化框架与可信的评测对照，因而需要对方法、评测与前沿方向进行系统整合。

Method: 采用算法中心与评测导向的分析框架，提出三方面贡献：(1) 建模：给出统一的消息传递形式化，将方法按五个融合层级（特征、传播、拓扑、学习、基础模型）组织，覆盖判别式与图条件生成式模型，并把代表性方法作为该形式化的特例推导，比较其假设、表达能力与计算成本，指出跨模态敏感性是区分相似架构的关键属性；(2) 数据集与评测：回顾近期基准，总结数据集与指标，定义与模型无关的描述符（标签同质性、平均度、聚类系数、边来源），并将每种评测协议映射到其使用的基线；(3) 前沿：连接MAG与AI Agent，涵盖Agent感知与记忆所依赖的多模态图及"图工程"（将Agent工作流组织为显式图），并讨论开放问题与研究方向。

Result: 本文为综述，不报告新实验，其成果体现为：一个覆盖判别与生成任务的统一消息传递形式化框架及五级融合分类法；代表性方法的特例化推导与假设、表达力、成本对比，并识别出跨模态敏感性这一区分性属性；一套数据集/指标汇总、四个模型无关图描述符以及评测协议与基线的对应关系；以及与AI Agent相关的多模态图、图工程和研究议题梳理。

Conclusion: MAG学习可被统一置于五级融合的消息传递框架下，并通过模型无关描述符与协议—基线映射使评测更具可比性；未来需回答在强大多模态基础模型存在时图结构何时真正有用等开放问题，并推动面向Agent的多模态图与图工程研究。

Abstract: Multimodal attributed graphs (MAGs), in which nodes carry text, images, and other modalities while edges encode relations among entities, underlie e-commerce, social media, content platforms, and creative applications. Learning on MAGs has developed along two complementary lines: graph-centric methods redesign how modalities are fused, propagated, and connected within message passing, while foundation-model-centric methods pretrain transferable multimodal graph encoders or bring graph context into multimodal large language models for prediction and generation. In parallel, several benchmarks have appeared with different datasets and evaluation protocols. This survey takes an algorithm-centric and evaluation-grounded view of the field and makes three contributions. Modeling: we introduce a unified message-passing formulation and organize methods into five integration levels (feature, propagation, topology, learning, and foundation model), covering discriminative and graph-conditioned generative models; within this formulation we derive representative methods as special cases, compare their assumptions, expressiveness, and cost, and identify cross-modal sensitivity as a property that separates otherwise similar architectures. Datasets and evaluation: we review recent benchmarks, summarize their datasets and metrics, define model-independent descriptors (label homophily, average degree, clustering, and edge provenance), and map each evaluation protocol to the baselines it uses. Frontiers: we connect multimodal graph learning to AI agents, covering the multimodal graphs that agents perceive and remember and graph engineering, the practice of organizing agent workflows as explicit graphs; we then discuss open problems, concrete efforts for the community, and research directions, including when graph structure helps in the presence of strong multimodal foundation models.

</details>


### [9] [Linear Probes to Bayesian Manifolds: Geometry of Arithmetic in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12998)
*Anshul Kumar,Neha Prasad,Manoj Kumar,Barnabás Póczos,Deeksha Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个四阶段贝叶斯流程，将"探针精度"这一单一指标拆分为可解码性、形状、归属和因果使用四项独立主张，并在GPT-J 6B、Llama 3.1 8B、Pythia 6.9B的双位数加法与乘法任务上验证：几乎所有概念均可解码，但强烈支持的形状比较均指向ribbon或环面，且81%的形状是从相关概念"继承"而非"自有"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探针常用于从LLM激活中提取helix、circle等几何结构，学界往往据此推断模型"用"该结构进行计算。但探针精度混淆了四个不同主张（可解码性、形状、归属、因果使用），单一精度无法支撑"模型据此计算"的结论，因此需要分离检验。

Method: 提出四阶段贝叶斯流程：阶段1检验可解码性；阶段2比较候选形状，仅当证据超过预设阈值才选最优形状；阶段3剔除预先指定的相关概念集合，判断形状是自有还是继承；阶段4对形状进行干预，检验模型输出是否依赖该形状。实验对象为三个模型的二位数加法与乘法激活。

Result: 几乎所有概念均可解码，但解码方向在概念间大量重叠；所有强支持比较选出的形状均为ribbon（半径随数值变化的helix）或torus。在1,330个有归属判定的单元中，19%为自有形状、81%为继承形状；自有与继承形状对输出产生影响的比例相近。对形状干预可保留对完整探针干预约61%的效应，且仅用至多5个维度（探针中位数为9维）。

Conclusion: 仅凭探针判定会高估模型实际计算所依赖的结构；建议报告探针对四个主张的分别判定结果，而非单一准确率。

Abstract: Linear probes are a standard method to study geometric structures, such as helices and circles, from the activations of large language models (LLMs), and such structure is often taken as evidence that the model computes with it. Probe accuracy alone cannot establish this, because it conflates four separate claims. These are (1) that the concept is decodable from the activations, (2) that they form a specific shape, (3) that the shape belongs to the probed concept rather than to a related concept the model also represents, and (4) that the output of the model depends on the shape. We call these claims decodability, shape, ownership, and causal use, and we test them separately with a four-stage Bayesian pipeline. Stage 2 compares candidate shapes and selects the best one only when its evidence passes a predefined threshold. Stage 3 removes a set of related concepts, specified in advance, and tests whether the shape is owned or inherited. Stage 4 intervenes on the shape to test whether the output of the model depends on it. We apply the pipeline to two-digit addition and multiplication in GPT-J 6B, Llama 3.1 8B, and Pythia 6.9B. Nearly every concept is decodable, but the decoded directions overlap substantially across concepts. Every strongly supported comparison selects a ribbon, which is a helix whose radius changes with the value, or a torus. Of 1,330 cells with an ownership verdict, 19% of shapes are owned and 81% are inherited. Owned and inherited shapes move the output about equally often. Intervening on the shape retains approximately 61% of the effect of intervening on the full probe while using at most five dimensions, against a median of nine for the probe. Our results indicate that a probe verdict alone overstates what a model computes with, and we propose that probe results be reported as verdicts on all four claims rather than as a single accuracy.

</details>


### [10] [One Table, Many Queries: Benchmarking Generalist Tabular Models Across Generation, Imputation, and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13001)
*Yuanrui Wang,Kaifang Li,Xuanyue Li,Ziyun Li,Yan Lu,Xingxuan Zhang,Peng Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 One Table Benchmark（OTB），在 56 个数据集、46 种注册方法、20 个能力维度上对齐数据划分与任务契约，系统比较表格数据的生成、插补与预测三类任务，并通过跨目标训练干预揭示任务间共享与冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成、插补与预测刻画同一表格分布的不同侧面，但通常被分开评估，导致三者共享哪些能力、训练目标如何相互作用、单一模型能否同时胜任三任务均不清晰。

Method: 构建统一基准：56 数据集、46 方法、20 能力维度，统一数据划分并显式定义任务契约；在对齐支持集上比较任务排名；在 5 个数据集、3 个随机种子上，以 7 种非空目标组合训练 5 个骨干网络；对比固定模型画像与专家组合（specialist portfolios）；并对冻结基础模型进行评估。

Result: 排名分析通过数值恢复与依赖保持连接插补与预测，通过预测效用连接生成与二者；分布保真度遵循不同甚至相反的排序。目标干预显示：纳入任务自身目标带来的合并收益最大；生成具有最广泛的正向辅助效应覆盖；预测有望提升插补质量与合成数据效用，且效果随骨干而异；交叉叠加效应表明目标收益不可简单相加，依赖已有目标。固定模型比较揭示效用—保真权衡：部分通用模型在效用上超过专家组合，而保真度仍倾向专业化。冻结基础模型在预测与数值点插补上表现强，合成数据效用亦具潜力。

Conclusion: 结果支持发展统一的表格模型，使其共享有用的条件信息，同时改进类别重建与忠实的联合采样能力。

Abstract: Generation, imputation, and prediction query different aspects of the same tabular distribution, but are usually evaluated separately. This leaves unclear which capabilities they share, how their training objectives interact, and whether one model can perform all three well. We introduce the One Table Benchmark (\ours{}), spanning 56 datasets, 46 registered methods, and 20 capability axes under aligned data splits and explicit task contracts. We compare task ranks on aligned support, train five backbones under all seven non-empty objective combinations on five datasets with three seeds, and contrast fixed-model profiles with specialist portfolios. Rank comparisons connect imputation and prediction through numerical recovery and dependence preservation, and link generation to both through predictive utility; distributional fidelity follows different, sometimes opposing, rankings. Objective interventions show the largest pooled gains from including the recipient's own objective. Generation has the broadest coverage of positive pooled auxiliary effects, while prediction shows potential to improve imputation and synthetic-data utility, with responses that vary across backbones. Cross-addition effects reveal that objective benefits need not add and depend on the objectives already present. Fixed-model comparisons expose corresponding utility--fidelity trade-offs: some generalists surpass specialist portfolios on utility, while fidelity continues to favor specialization. Frozen foundation models show strong prediction and numerical point imputation, together with promising synthetic-data utility, highlighting their potential across all three tasks. These findings motivate unified tabular models that share useful conditional information while improving categorical reconstruction and faithful joint sampling.

</details>


### [11] [Branch-and-Commit for Frozen RL Agents: When and Where to Spend Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13003)
*Sahar Admoni,Assaf Hallak,Yftah Ziser,Ofra Amir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"branch-and-commit"（分支-提交）方法：在可复制/恢复状态的环境中，从当前状态采样多条后续轨迹，评估后从选中分支终点继续；指出分支收益取决于候选回报的"离散度"（最佳与均值的差），且增益主要来自在同一动作的不同随机实现间挑选，而非选择更好的动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中策略常被冻结、无法重训练或修改，但数字环境的状态可被复制与恢复。此时操作者有一个额外选项：对当前状态采样多条延续并择优继续。作者希望弄清这种做法的适用条件、增益来源以及计算预算应如何分配。

Method: 在4个环境、2个冻结策略、5种延续验证器与一种无模型选择规则下进行实证研究；以"离散度"（最佳候选回报与平均候选回报之差）为分析变量；分解增益来源（动作选择 vs. 同一动作的随机实现）；并按预测离散度对分支进行门控（gating），在留出种子上评估预算效率。

Result: 低离散度设置下分支无收益；主设置 Craftax-Medium 中回报提升12.8%。增益大部分来自在相同动作的不同随机实现中选择，而非动作层面的择优。多种高水平验证器达到相近回合回报，表明在给定预算下验证器质量并非主要瓶颈。基于预测离散度的门控在留出种子上保留了均匀分支69%的改进，同时仅使用48%的分支调用次数。

Conclusion: branch-and-commit 的有效性由离散度决定，其收益更多代表对随机性的利用而非策略改进；计算应优先分配给可能出现有用离散度的状态，据此门控可在显著节省预算的同时保留大部分增益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents are often deployed with policies that cannot be retrained or modified. In digital environments whose states can be copied and resumed, an operator has another option: sample several continuations from the current state, evaluate them, and continue from the endpoint of the selected branch. We call this procedure branch-and-commit. We study when it improves return, what produces the improvement, and where its computational budget should be spent. Across four environments, two frozen policies, five continuation verifiers, and a model-free selection rule, we find that the benefit of branching depends on dispersion: the difference between the best candidate return and the mean candidate return. Branching provides no benefit in low-dispersion settings, while increasing return by 12.8% in our main Craftax-Medium setting. Most of this gain comes not from selecting better actions, but from selecting among different stochastic realizations of the same action. Several competent verifiers achieve similar episode returns, suggesting that verifier quality is not the main constraint at our operating budget. Instead, computation is better allocated to states where useful dispersion is likely. On held-out seeds, gating branching using predicted dispersion retains 69% of the improvement from uniform branching while using 48% as many branching calls. These results characterize when branch-and-commit is useful and clarify what its gains represent.

</details>


### [12] [The Shape of a Program: Path Signatures for Trace-to-Program Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13005)
*Mohamed Ghanem,Bernd Finkbeiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从粗糙路径理论视角出发，用路径签名变换（log-signature）编码程序执行轨迹，在Karel、List、RASP三个程序归纳任务上优于LSTM、Transformer与Fourier基线，并利用"收缩性"实现无预言机的监督冗余发现，同时获得更可解释的潜空间几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从执行轨迹合成程序是算法可解释性与复杂系统逆向工程的核心难题。现有序列模型（LSTM、Transformer等）虽能处理轨迹，但缺乏对轨迹分布的理论刻画，且在训练数据稀缺时表现受限；同时这类模型的隐表示几何往往不具可解释性。

Method: 将执行轨迹视为高维粗糙路径，采用随机分析中的路径签名变换（path signature transform）及其对数签名（log-signature）作为轨迹编码器；其签名期望可证明地刻画程序所诱导轨迹的分布。基于"轨迹→程序"流水线天然具有收缩性（常输出比真值更短但仍合法的程序），设计自举（bootstrapping）流程以在无外部预言机条件下发现真值监督中的冗余。

Result: 在Karel、List、RASP三个程序归纳域上，log-signature 轨迹编码器在几乎全部设置下优于LSTM、Transformer和Fourier基线，数据稀缺时优势最大；在人为注入冗余的数据集上成功识别出监督冗余；其诱导的潜空间几何显著更可解释——每个状态最近邻均位于其自身的执行轨迹上。

Conclusion: 路径签名/对数签名提供了理论有据且数据高效的轨迹表示，可作为程序归纳的强编码器；轨迹到程序的收缩性可被转化为无需预言机的冗余检测与监督自举机制；该编码器还带来更强的潜空间可解释性，为程序归纳与逆向工程提供了新的分析视角。

Abstract: Synthesizing programs from execution traces is a fundamental challenge in algorithmic interpretability and the reverse engineering of complex systems. We bring a rough-path-theoretic perspective to program induction, treating execution traces as high-dimensional rough paths and encoding them with the path signature transform, a tool from stochastic analysis whose expectation provably characterizes the distribution of traces a program induces. On three program-induction domains, Karel, List, and RASP, our log-signature trace encoder outperforms LSTM, Transformer, and Fourier baselines in almost every setting, with its largest margins when training data is scarce. We further observe that the trace-to-program pipeline is naturally contractive: it often produces valid programs that are shorter than the ground truth. We turn this into a bootstrapping procedure that exposes redundancy in the ground-truth supervision without any external oracle, which we demonstrate on redundancy-injected versions of our datasets. Finally, the log-signature encoder induces a markedly more interpretable latent geometry than the baselines, in which the nearest neighbors of every state lie on its own execution trace.

</details>


### [13] [FaST: Factored Synthesis of Transitions for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13013)
*Daniel Campa,Spandan Das,Srinjoy Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FaST 将转移元组按链式法则分解为状态、动作、动力学、奖励、终止五个阶段，分别用匹配其统计特性的估计器建模。在十二个 D4RL 基准上，用其合成数据训练的 TD3+BC/IQL 匹配 SynthER 扩散基线且生成速度快 90 倍以上；配合"每个状态采样 N 个候选动作并保留最高优势者"的定向动作合成，Best-of-10 在 11/12 个 medium 与 medium-replay 设置上超越真实数据训练（平均 +8.9，最高 +28.4）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的合成数据生成器把转移元组 (s, a, s', r, d) 当作整体联合建模，无法原生地单独重采样某一因子，从而限制了条件查询、定向生成与效率；作者希望既能规模化离线 RL 的合成数据，又能对单个因子进行可控操作。

Method: 提出 FaST（Factored Synthesis of Transitions）：依据链式法则将转移分解为状态、动作、动力学、奖励、终止五个阶段，每阶段采用与其统计性质（如离散/连续、条件结构）相匹配的估计器分别拟合。由此获得显式的"给定状态的动作条件分布"，可对固定状态重复查询，采样 N 个候选动作，并用在同一数据上拟合的价值函数保留最高优势动作，实现生成阶段的行为改进而非事后过滤。

Result: 在 12 个 D4RL locomotion 与 maze 基准上，TD3+BC 与 IQL 在 FaST 转移上训练的效果与 SynthER 扩散基线相当，生成速度提升逾 90 倍。Best-of-10 合成在 11/12 个 medium 与 medium-replay 设置上超过真实数据训练，平均 +8.9 D4RL 分，最高 +28.4；与优势过滤的 SynthER 具有竞争力且生成成本仅为其一小部分。在 expert 混合数据上，两种机制出现相同程度的退化。

Conclusion: 因子化生成是高效且可操控的合成数据方案：生成质量可与整体式扩散模型持平，而速度大幅领先，并天然支持定向动作合成以获得超越真实数据的策略。expert 混合数据上的同步失效表明瓶颈在于优势估计本身，而非任一定向机制，提示后续工作应改进价值/优势估计而非采样策略。

Abstract: Synthetic data augmentation promises to scale offline reinforcement learning beyond fixed logged datasets, but leading diffusion-based generators model the transition tuple (state, action, next state, reward, terminal) as a monolith: no single factor can be natively redrawn in isolation. We present FaST (Factored Synthesis of Transitions), which factors transitions via the chain rule into state, action, dynamics, reward, and terminal stages, fitting each with an estimator matched to its statistical properties. Across twelve D4RL locomotion and maze benchmarks, TD3+BC and IQL agents trained on FaST transitions match the SynthER diffusion baseline while generating transitions over 90x faster. The explicit action-given-state conditional can also be re-queried for a fixed state, enabling targeted action synthesis: drawing N candidate actions per generated state and keeping the highest-advantage one under a value function fit to the same data, thereby synthesizing improved behavior during generation rather than filtering for it post-hoc. Best-of-10 synthesis beats training on real data on 11 of 12 medium and medium-replay settings (+8.9 D4RL points on average, up to +28.4), and is competitive with advantage-filtered SynthER at a fraction of its generation cost. On expert mixtures both mechanisms degrade identically, tracing the failure to the advantage estimate itself rather than to either targeting mechanism.

</details>


### [14] [Permutation-Based Pre-trained Model Merging via Reciprocal Orthogonality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13016)
*Akira Ito,Daiki Chijiwa,Atsutoshi Kumagai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在层间寻找使两模型权重与对方表示"互正交"(RO)的置换，可将线性模式连通性(LMC)所需的网络宽度大幅降低，且能合并不同数据分布训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明极宽网络(MLP每隐层8192单元、VGG-11约16倍宽)在温度校准下无需置换即可实现LMC，机制为LEWC；但如此宽度代价高昂，本文质疑其必要性并探索在较小宽度下促成LEWC。

Method: 提出互正交(RO)置换搜索：抑制每个模型权重对另一模型表示的干扰，使两模型计算可在合并网络中并存。将搜索建模为逐层独立的二次分配问题(QAP)，用Frank–Wolfe算法近似求解其连续松弛。

Result: 实验显示该方法在更小的宽度扩展下即可实现LMC；还能成功合并在不同数据分布上训练的模型——这是最大化权重相似度的权重匹配(weight matching)难以处理的场景；合并后预测近似原模型的logit集成。

Conclusion: 宽度并非LMC的必要条件，置换可替代宽度；以互正交为目标的置换搜索是一种更经济、适用面更广的模型合并途径。

Abstract: Ito et al. (2026) showed that sufficiently wide, independently trained neural networks can exhibit linear mode connectivity (LMC) with softmax temperature calibration, even without permutation. LMC means that a low-loss linear path connects the models' parameters. This behavior is explained by layerwise exponentially weighted connectivity (LEWC), whereby each merged layer outputs an exponentially weighted sum of the original models' corresponding representations, yielding ensemble-like predictions. However, prior work observed LEWC with substantial widening, such as 8192 units per hidden layer for MLPs and $16\times$ width for VGG-11, raising the question of whether such widths are necessary. We therefore search for permutations that encourage reciprocal orthogonality (RO), which suppresses interference between each model's weights and the other model's representations. This allows both models' computations to coexist in the merged network, promoting LEWC at smaller widths. We formulate the search as independent layerwise quadratic assignment problems and approximately solve their continuous relaxations using Frank--Wolfe. Experiments show that our method facilitates LMC with less width expansion. It also merges models trained on different data distributions, where weight matching, which maximizes weight similarity through permutations, struggles. The merged predictions approximate those of a logit ensemble of the original models.

</details>


### [15] [Explanation-Aware Fairness Beyond Output Parity: Joint Output-Attribution Regularization for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13052)
*Gökhan Özbulak,Oscar Jimenez-del-Toro,Lilian Berton,André Anjos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将输出层公平性与表示空间的特征归因差异联系起来，证明对"非线性编码器+线性头"结构，结果条件下的输出分数差异等于各子组特征归因差异的有符号和（允许通过抵消效应产生小幅预测差），并据此提出归因差异惩罚项，验证其与输出层均等机会公平代理的互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输出层公平（如均等机会）并不约束不同人口群体在表示空间中是否依赖相似的预测贡献，因此仅约束输出无法保证解释层面的公平；作者希望探明输出分数差异与特征归因差异之间的定量关系，并检验归因公平能否作为输出公平的补充约束。

Method: 在"非线性编码器+线性分类头"设定下进行理论推导，建立输出层分数差异与子组间特征归因差异的关系式，指出小幅预测差距可经由抵消效应（cancellation effect）产生；实验上在HAM10000与Harvard-GF数据集上，针对性别与种族公平，训练960个DINOv2-LoRA模型，比较在输出层均等机会代理基础上加入归因差异惩罚项的效果，并使用配对95%置信区间进行评估。

Result: 归因增强在六种设置下均提升效用—公平权衡的超体积（hypervolume），相对增益为1.49%–10.57%（配对95%置信区间）；在预设效用约束下，增强模型族实现9.0%–38.7%更低的均等机会差，归因差异与抵消比亦下降，同时保持预测效用。

Conclusion: 输出公平无法涵盖归因层面的公平，二者存在可量化的联系；特征归因公平可作为解释感知的补充约束，与输出层公平代理结合后能在不牺牲效用的前提下改善公平表现。该结论支持将归因公平视为输出公平的互补性约束。

Abstract: Output-level fairness does not constrain whether demographic groups rely on similar predictive contributions in representation space. For a nonlinear encoder with a linear head, we show that outcome-conditioned output score disparity equals the signed sum of feature attribution differences across subgroups by allowing small prediction gaps to arise through cancellation effect. We test whether an attribution disparity penalty complements output-level equalized odds based fairness surrogates. Across 960 DINOv2-LoRA models on HAM10000 and Harvard-GF datasets for gender and race fairness, attribution augmentation increases utility-fairness trade-off performance for hypervolume in six different settings with paired 95% confidence intervals and relative gains of 1.49-10.57%. Under a predefined utility constraint, augmented model families also achieve 9.0-38.7% lower equalized odds difference, lower attribution disparity, and reduced cancellation ratio while preserving predictive utility. These results support attribution fairness as a complementary explanation-aware constraint on output fairness.

</details>


### [16] [Continuous-Time Set-Transformers for Asynchronous WiFi-IMU Indoor Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13057)
*Mohamed Bachar,Ilyass Abouelaziz,Yuehua Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种连续时间Transformer，将WiFi RSSI与惯性测量表示为带时间戳的无序token集合，用单一注意力块同时融合跨模态与跨时间信息，无需重采样与模态专用分支，并可优雅应对观测缺失或过期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内定位常融合WiFi RSSI指纹与惯性测量，但各传感器以不同速率异步采样，观测在时间上很少对齐，且可能缺失或陈旧。现有方法多将数据重采样到统一时钟并用独立分支分别处理各模态，既引入误差又结构冗余。

Method: 构建连续时间Transformer：每个token编码测量值、模态标识及自采集以来的经过时间；将观测视为无序时间戳集合，用单个注意力块联合融合跨模态、跨时间信息，保持token顺序不变性；训练时随机掩蔽整模态与单个观测，以支持缺失或过期输入下的推理。

Result: 在各自基准上验证模态专用编码器，并在仿真与真实数据集（含跨天采集的跨会话数据集）上评估融合模型：跨会话漂移下优于开放式WiFi指纹基线，与学习式融合及航位推算基线相当，且在观测缺失或陈旧时性能平滑退化。

Conclusion: 结果表明单一连续时间Transformer可作为一种实用的异步传感器室内定位框架，规避重采样与多分支设计，并对不完整、过时观测具备鲁棒性。

Abstract: Indoor localization commonly combines WiFi received signal strength indicator (RSSI) fingerprints with inertial measurements. However, these sensor streams are sampled asynchronously at different rates; therefore, observations rarely align in time and may be missing or stale. Most existing methods resample the data onto a shared clock and process each modality using a separate branch. We instead present a continuous-time transformer that represents observations as an unordered set of timestamped tokens. Each token encodes its measurement, modality, and elapsed time since acquisition. A single attention block jointly fuses information across modalities and time, avoiding both resampling and modality-specific branches while remaining invariant to token order. During training, we randomly mask entire modalities and individual observations, enabling the model to operate under missing or outdated inputs. We validate the modality-specific encoders on their respective benchmarks and evaluate the fused model on both simulated and real datasets, including a crosssession dataset collected on unseen days. The proposed approach improves upon an open WiFi fingerprinting baseline under cross-session drift, remains competitive with learned fusion and dead-reckoning baselines, and degrades gracefully as observations become missing or stale. These results suggest that a single continuoustime transformer provides a practical framework for indoor localization from asynchronous sensors.

</details>


### [17] [INTENT: Out-of-Sample, Out-of-Time, and Cold-Start Evaluation on User Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13069)
*Mattias Kvist,Erik Lidman Hillbom,Edoardo de Cal,Davis Siemens,Adam Fredriksson,Rickard Brüel-Gabrielsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出公开基准INTENT，用"用户/时段/物品是否见过"构成的七格评估体系衡量银行等序列基础模型的下一动作预测，审计59个数据集族仅留5个，指出格子决定最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行在交易、App会话、授权等有序客户事件序列上训练基础模型，部署时需向前预测，常涉及训练中未见过的客户与商户；但各机构在私有数据上评估，结果不可比。现有公开基准很少测试模型未见过的时间段，推荐研究多以留出每个用户最后若干事件的方式评估，无法刻画"新用户、新时段、新物品"的泛化能力。

Method: 构建INTENT基准（INTEraction、Novelty、Transfer，面向下一动作预测）：按用户、时间段、目标物品是否在训练中出现，划出七个评估格子。审计59个公开数据集族，保留5个；最常见缺陷是缺少可排序事件的自然日历时间戳。因无任一日期落在全部5个数据集的时间跨度内，各数据集按其自身cutoff切分，切分规则在评估任何模型前固定。各数据集求和后每格至少含111,769名用户。

Result: 参考结果中格子决定最优模型：Transformer在cutoff后已见物品上领先，但在新物品上得分为零；嵌入物品元数据的变体主要在MicroLens上有效；"重复用户上一物品"的无参数基线在5个数据集中的4个上于新物品格子领先。该无参数对照还表明，三个数据集上cutoff后的大部分性能下降源于用户行为变化，而非模型本身。

Conclusion: 单一排行榜掩盖了模型能力的结构性差异，评估须按格子拆分并引入无参数对照以分离行为漂移。流水线、cutoff规则及各格用户数均已公开，便于复现与跨模型可比。

Abstract: Banks now train foundation models on ordered sequences of customer events such as transactions, app sessions, and authorizations. Deployed, such a model predicts forward in time, often for customers and merchants absent from training, yet each is evaluated on private data, so no two can be compared. Public benchmarks for such sequences rarely test a period the model has not seen; recommendation studies usually hold out each user's last events instead. We present INTENT (INTEraction, Novelty, and Transfer evaluation for next-action prediction), a public benchmark that scores a model in seven cells defined by whether the user, the period, and the target item appeared in training. An audit of 59 public dataset families retains five datasets; the most common defect is the lack of a calendar timestamp that orders events. No date lies within the time span of all five datasets, so each is split at its own cutoff, chosen by one rule fixed before any model is evaluated. Summed over the datasets, every cell holds at least 111,769 users. In reference results the cell determines the best model: a transformer leads on seen items after the cutoff but scores zero on new items, a variant that embeds item metadata helps mainly on MicroLens, and repeating the user's last item leads on new items in four of five datasets. This parameter-free control also shows that on three datasets most of the score lost after the cutoff reflects changed behavior rather than the model. The pipeline, the cutoff rule, and the per-cell user counts are publicly available.

</details>


### [18] [IC-GNN: Modeling Individualized Brain Community Organization for Disease Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13070)
*Yang Zhang,Xiao Zhou,Jonathan Warrell,Avram Holmes,Xuan Zhang,Mark Gerstein,for the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IC-GNN（atlas-anchored图神经网络），在atlas定义的脑区上直接学习个体化的多尺度离散功能社区，从而兼顾个体化建模与组间可比性，在ABIDE、ADNI和UK Biobank三个大规模数据集上优于15种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑网络具有模块化组织，社区结构在个体和疾病间差异显著，这对基础神经科学与精准医学都很重要。但常用的组水平预定义脑图谱忽视这种异质性；现有被试特异性功能分区虽能刻画社区边界变异，却需较长扫描时间才能获得细粒度分辨率，并给跨被试比较带来困难。

Method: 提出IC-GNN：一种atlas-anchored模型，直接在atlas定义的脑区上构建个体化功能社区。模型从静息态fMRI中导出离散的、个体化的多尺度社区，细化社区引导的连接，并将共享的区域权重自适应到每个被试的社区上下文，从而同时支持个体化建模与组水平比较。

Result: 在来自ABIDE（自闭症谱系障碍）、ADNI（阿尔茨海默病）与UK Biobank（抑郁）的3万余名参与者上，IC-GNN在15种基线方法中性能最优；个体化社区在扫描内及跨session均具可重复性，且自身具有预测能力；模型识别出的疾病相关社区组织变化与已有文献一致。

Conclusion: IC-GNN通过atlas锚定的个体化社区建模，解决了组水平图谱与个体化分区之间的权衡，为疾病预测与生物标志物发现提供了有前景的方法。

Abstract: Brain networks exhibit modular organization, where community structures vary substantially across individuals and diseases. Understanding these individual differences is crucial for advancing both basic neuroscience research and precision medicine. However, widely used predefined group-level brain atlases often overlook this heterogeneity. While individualized functional parcellations capture subject-specific variability in community boundaries, they often require lengthy scan times to achieve fine-grained resolution and complicate cross-subject comparisons. To address this trade-off and investigate how individualized community organization captures disease-associated changes across individuals, we propose IC-GNN, an atlas-anchored model that forms distinct, individualized functional communities directly over atlas-defined brain regions. IC-GNN derives discrete, individualized multiscale communities from resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data, refines community-guided connections, and adapts shared regional weights to each subject's community context to enable both individualized modeling and group-level comparisons. Across more than 30,000 participants from ABIDE (autism spectrum disorder), ADNI (Alzheimer's disease), and the UK Biobank (depression), IC-GNN achieves the highest performance compared with 15 baseline methods. We further demonstrate that these individualized communities are reproducible within scans and across sessions, and carry predictive power on their own. Furthermore, IC-GNN identifies disease-related shifts in community organization that align with established literature, highlighting a promising approach for modeling individualized community structure for disease prediction and biomarker discovery.

</details>


### [19] [One Temperature to Rule Them All?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13077)
*Aviv Orly,Ori Shem-Ur,Yaron Oz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在pass@K设定下系统分析采样温度多样性（多温度分配）何时优于单一调优温度：难度越高的问题越难共享高效温度（但仅为对数关系），理论最优多温度方案相对按K调优的单温度基线增益通常有限，其原因是"dulling effects"带来的温度选择鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作表明跨样本变化温度可提升多样本推理，但实践中仍普遍使用固定温度，这一矛盾缺乏系统解释。论文希望在标准的pass@K框架下厘清：温度多样性何时有用、何时单一调优温度已足够，并为温度选择提供理论依据。

Method: 1）在多模型、多数据集上估计每题的成功概率随温度变化的"温度-成功率"曲线，归纳其一致性性质（包括问题难度与高效采样温度共享程度的关系）；2）推导一种高效方法来评估理论最优的多温度分配方案，并与以采样预算K为函数调优的单温度基线对比；3）提出"dulling effects"（钝化效应）数学机制，解释多温度增益有限及温度选择对问题分布不敏感（鲁棒）的原因。

Result: 1）较难的问题更不易共享高效采样温度，但该趋势仅随难度呈对数增长；2）理论最优多温度分配确实可提升性能，但在温度随预算K调优的条件下，相对单温度基线的增益通常不大；3）"钝化效应"解释了该现象，并说明温度选择的鲁棒性来源。

Conclusion: 多温度采样并非普遍必要：当温度可依据采样预算K调优时，单温度方案已接近饱和收益。论文给出了温度多样性适用边界的判据以及理想温度选取原则，为实践中简化采样策略提供了依据。

Abstract: Inference-time sampling is a central mechanism for improving large language models, with temperature controlling the tradeoff between reliability and diversity. While recent work suggests that varying temperature across samples can improve multi-sample inference, simple fixed-temperature schemes remain the standard in practice. We investigate this discrepancy in the canonical pass@K setting, asking when temperature diversity is useful, and when a single tuned temperature suffices. To this end, we analyze per-question success probabilities across multiple models and datasets, tracking how they vary with sampling temperature. We identify several consistent properties of these profiles, including that harder questions are less likely to share efficient sampling temperatures, although this trend is only logarithmic in hardness. We then derive an efficient method for evaluating the theoretically optimal multi-temperature allocation scheme, and show that although such a scheme improves performance, its gains over single-temperature baselines are typically modest when temperature is tuned as a function of the sampling budget K. We explain this phenomenon through mathematical mechanisms that we call dulling effects, and show how they ensure the robustness of temperature choices across different question distributions. Our results clarify when multi-temperature sampling is useful, and how temperatures should ideally be chosen.

</details>


### [20] [Which Successes Make Good References? Action-Sequence Alignment for Multi-Turn On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13135)
*Mufei Li,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Zhuoer Wang,Samson Koelle,Jie Yuan,Manqing Mao,Hong Wang,Yanjun Lin,James Feng,Daniel Edmiston,Pan Li,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将多轮工具使用智能体的同任务成功轨迹选择建模为自由目标最优传输问题，等价于为每条失败轨迹分配最兼容的成功参考；用可观测的动作序列对齐（LCS）作为潜在状态兼容性的代理，实验表明 LCS 是唯一在 ALFWorld 与 τ³-bench airline 上均优于无参考 OPD 的选择器，并迁移到 retail 域仍有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation 虽通过学生自身轨迹监督缓解训练-推理分布错配，但在多轮工具使用场景中，教师若以同任务成功轨迹作为参考可提供 hindsight；然而不同成功轨迹可能到达互不兼容的交互状态，导致监督信号具误导性。因此关键问题在于如何为每条失败轨迹挑选兼容的成功参考。

Method: 把成功参考选择形式化为 free-target optimal transport 问题，退化为为每条失败轨迹指派最兼容的成功参考。由于潜在状态路径不可见，采用动作序列对齐作为兼容性的可观测代理，并以最长公共子序列（LCS）实现：识别共享的有序动作主干骨架，同时容忍可恢复的绕行（detours）。据此把轨迹兼容性确立为多轮 OPD 中参考选择的核心准则。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 与 τ³-bench 的 airline 域上，LCS 是所评估的参考选择器中唯一同时在 ALFWorld 总体得分与 airline 全部三项指标上优于无参考 OPD 的方法。未做额外优化时，airline 训练的模型可迁移至 retail 域，LCS 在该域三项指标上均有提升。

Conclusion: 轨迹兼容性应作为多轮 on-policy distillation 中参考选择的核心标准；以 LCS 为代表的简单可观测对齐代理能有效实现兼容参考匹配，带来跨任务与跨域收益，为参考增强的 OPD 提供了可解释、低成本的选择准则。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) reduces train-inference distribution mismatch by supervising a student on its own trajectories. For multi-turn tool-use agents, conditioning the teacher on a validated same-task successful trajectory as a reference can provide useful hindsight, but different successes may reach incompatible interaction states and offer misleading supervision. We cast successful-reference selection as a free-target optimal transport problem, which reduces to assigning each failed trajectory its most compatible successful reference. Because latent state paths are hidden, we use action-sequence alignment as an observable proxy for compatibility and instantiate it with longest common subsequence (LCS), which identifies a shared ordered backbone of actions while allowing recoverable detours. This perspective makes trajectory compatibility the central criterion for reference selection in multi-turn OPD. Experiments on ALFWorld and the airline domain of $τ^3$-bench show that LCS is the only evaluated reference selector to improve over reference-free OPD on both ALFWorld overall scores and all three airline metrics. Without further optimization, the airline-trained models also transfer to the retail domain, where LCS improves all three metrics.

</details>


### [21] [Byzantine-Robust Muon: Resilient Momentum Orthogonalization for Distributed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13137)
*Grigory Malinovsky,Yury Demidovich,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在统一的一阶信赖域框架下，分析了基于LMO与动量正交化的Muon优化器在拜占庭鲁棒分布式/联邦学习中的性质，摆脱i.i.d.假设，引入非欧几里得异质性度量，给出非凸与星凸目标的收敛保证，并在ResNet-18、ViT-Tiny及字节级语言模型实验中验证Muon优于SGD、归一化SGD与sign更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon等基于LMO与动量正交化的优化方法在大规模与分布式学习中经验表现突出，但其对对抗性行为（拜占庭攻击）和数据异质性的鲁棒性尚缺乏理论理解；现有分析多依赖i.i.d.假设与欧几里得几何，难以匹配正交化更新的信赖域结构。

Method: 将Muon类更新统一纳入一阶信赖域框架，结合鲁棒聚合（如RFA）研究拜占庭鲁棒分布式与联邦学习；分析不依赖i.i.d.假设，提出与信赖域几何一致的非欧几里得异质性度量；在显式聚合假设下推导非凸与星凸目标的收敛界，并在显式加性oracle误差条件下把结论扩展至非精确正交化。

Result: 理论上给出拜占庭鲁棒Muon优化的收敛刻画，误差界含与已知不可能性结果一致的加性异质性误差下限；非精确正交化时，界分子中非凸拜占庭项不变而进度分母变小。实验上，CIFAR-10（ResNet-18、ViT-Tiny及其他图像基准）与TinyStories 38M字节级语言模型中，Muon配置的攻击后精度更高（困惑度更低），优于SGD、归一化SGD及CIFAR-10上的sign更新；固定诚实节点对照显示有界RFA查询攻击下RFA显著优于平均，Muon-NS在同检查点下优于张量级欧氏归一化，且held-out困惑度低于调优的AdamW。

Conclusion: Muon类正交化更新在拜占庭鲁棒分布式与联邦学习中具备可证的理论保证与经验优势，其鲁棒性源于信赖域几何与聚合机制的协同；异质性导致的加性误差下限不可消除但与几何一致的度量相匹配，非精确正交化仅影响进度项，实证上持续优于SGD/归一化SGD/sign与AdamW基线。

Abstract: Optimization methods, including the Muon optimizer based on the Linear Minimization Oracle (LMO) and momentum orthogonalization, have shown strong empirical performance in large-scale and distributed learning, yet their robustness to adversarial behavior and data heterogeneity is not well understood. We study these methods within a unified first-order trust-region framework and analyze Muon-based updates with robust aggregation for Byzantine-robust distributed and federated learning. Our analysis does not rely on the i.i.d. assumption and introduces a non-Euclidean notion of heterogeneity consistent with the trust-region geometry. Under this framework, we establish convergence guarantees for both general non-convex and star-convex objectives. Our results provide a theoretical characterization of Byzantine-robust Muon-based optimization, with an additive heterogeneity error floor consistent with known impossibility results, under explicit aggregation assumptions. Under an explicit additive oracle-error condition, the guarantees also cover inexact orthogonalization, with the non-convex Byzantine terms in the bound's numerator unchanged but a smaller progress denominator. In simulated federated training with ResNet-18 and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10, additional image benchmarks, and a 38M-parameter byte-level language model on TinyStories, the tested Muon configuration attains higher attacked accuracy (lower perplexity) than the tested SGD and normalized-SGD configurations, and than sign updates on CIFAR-10. Fixed-honest controls further show a substantial RFA advantage over averaging under a bounded RFA-query attack, higher attacked accuracy for Muon-NS than tensorwise Euclidean normalization from shared checkpoints, and lower held-out TinyStories perplexity than a tuned AdamW baseline.

</details>


### [22] [High Probability Risk Control for Online Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13163)
*Yihong Guo,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线策略学习中的风险控制问题：智能体自适应采集数据，需在每一轮以高概率保证所选新策略的风险低于阈值。针对反馈协变量偏移（FCS）带来的风险评估与策略选择难题，提出基于置换的加权风险估计器（权重用Sinkhorn高效近似）并结合高斯自助法构造风险UCB进行策略选择，理论上给出方差界与渐近有效的同时置信界，实验验证其风险估计方差更低、选择更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略学习中策略更新与数据采集相互耦合，产生反馈协变量偏移（FCS）：观测数据通过演化中的策略和数据采集过程依赖历史，导致数据分布随策略不断漂移。这带来两个实质困难：其一，对每个候选策略进行高效且可信的风险评估；其二，在高概率风险控制约束下进行有效的策略选择。现有方法难以同时解决这两点，因此需要新的评估与选择框架。

Method: 1) 提出基于置换（permutation）的加权风险估计器，其权重通过Sinkhorn算法高效近似求解，用于在FCS下对每个候选策略进行风险评估；2) 提出高斯自助法（Gaussian bootstrap）获得估计风险的上置信界（UCB），并选择UCB低于目标阈值的策略；3) 理论分析证明该风险估计器的方差上界不大于标准重要性加权（IW）估计器，且所提策略控制方法给出渐近有效的同时风险界，并为所选策略提供渐近高概率风险控制。

Result: 在在线上下文赌博机（online contextual bandit）、安全问答基准（safe question answering）和医疗理赔过滤（Medical Claim Filtering）三类任务上的实验表明：方法能维持在目标风险水平，风险低于基线方法，风险估计方差更低，并实现具有高概率风险控制保障的可靠策略选择；进一步的消融实验支持了文中的理论论断。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合Sinkhorn近似的置换加权评估器与高斯自助UCB，可有效应对在线策略学习中的反馈协变量偏移，实现低方差的风险评估与高概率有效的策略选择，兼顾理论保证与实证效果。

Abstract: We study risk control for online policy learning, where an agent adaptively collects data and aims to learn a new policy whose risk stays below a tolerance threshold at every round with high probability. Such policy updates and data collection induce feedback covariate shift (FCS): observed data depend on history through evolving policies and the data-collection process. FCS brings two substantive problems for risk control: 1) efficient risk evaluation for each candidate policy and 2) valid policy selection with high-probability risk control. To address these, we propose a permutation-based weighted risk evaluator with weights efficiently approximated by the Sinkhorn algorithm and select policies whose estimated risk upper confidence bounds (UCB) fall below the target threshold, with the UCB obtained through a proposed Gaussian bootstrap method. Theoretically, we prove that our risk evaluator has a no-larger variance upper bound than that of the standard importance-weighted evaluator. Our policy control method also yields an asymptotically valid simultaneous risk bound and provides asymptotic high-probability risk control for the selected policy. Empirically, we evaluate our method on the online contextual bandit problem, safe question answering benchmark, and Medical Claim Filtering tasks. Our method maintains the target risk level, achieves lower risk than baseline methods, has lower variance in risk estimation, and achieves reliable policy selection with high-probability risk control. Further ablation studies support our theoretical claims.

</details>


### [23] [RadRef: A Reference-Free Metric for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13168)
*Shelly Schwartz,Alan Ropp,Michael B. Sneider,Nur Yildirim,Cree M. Gaskin,Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RadRef是一种无需参考文本的放射报告评价指标：将报告中的主张与由图像推断出的临床发现信念进行比对，并按与临床指征的相关性加权，可在缺乏放射科医生参考报告时评估AI生成报告质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成放射学报告的评价通常依赖与放射科医生撰写的参考报告比对，当不存在参考报告时无法评分，限制了报告生成系统在实际临床流程中的质量监测。

Method: 提出RadRef：在临床发现词表上，由图像推断发现信念（image-derived beliefs），将报告陈述的主张与这些信念比对打分，并按与临床指征的相关性加权；同一图像grounded表示可给出假阳性读出版本RadRef-FF。评测包括ReXVal上的同片非同分报告对与放射科医生排序的一致性、100例MIMIC-CXR研究的100份模型生成+100份医生撰写报告的双人独立标注读者研究，以及受控扰动实验。

Result: ReXVal上与医生排序在非并列同片报告对上的一致率为73.1%，总体可比于主流参考型指标；在假发现上具竞争力，在遗漏（漏诊发现）上相对优势最明显。读者研究中，遗漏指标Kendall tau-b=0.295，两位读者之间为0.386，且无对比指标在该目标上与RadRef有显著差异；RadRef-FF的tau-b在ReXVal为0.32、读者研究为0.29。受控扰动证实其依赖图像、报告与临床指征三者。

Conclusion: RadRef可在尚无医生参考报告时实现基于图像的评估，支持临床场景下报告生成系统的质量监测，并在存在参考报告时作为参考型指标的补充。

Abstract: AI-generated radiology reports are usually evaluated by comparison with a radiologist-authored reference, leaving no way to score them when no reference exists. We introduce RadRef, a reference-free metric that scores report claims against image-derived beliefs over a clinical finding vocabulary, weighted by relevance to the clinical indication. On ReXVal, RadRef achieves 73.1% agreement on non-tied same-radiograph report pairs with radiologist rankings, comparable to leading reference-based metrics overall. It is also competitive on false findings and has its clearest relative result on omissions (missed findings). We further evaluate 100 model-generated and 100 radiologist-authored reports from 100 MIMIC-CXR studies, all independently annotated by two board-certified radiologists. On omissions, RadRef attains Kendall's tau-b = 0.295, against 0.386 between the two readers themselves, and no compared metric differs significantly from RadRef on this target. The same image-grounded representation provides a false-finding readout, RadRef-FF, with tau-b = 0.32 on ReXVal and 0.29 in the reader study, without requiring a reference. Controlled perturbations confirm that RadRef depends on the radiograph, report, and clinical indication. RadRef enables image-grounded evaluation before a radiologist-authored reference exists, supporting quality monitoring of report-generation systems in clinical settings, and complements reference-based metrics when one is available.

</details>


### [24] [Making Strides in Symbolic Imitation Learning: Offline Demonstrations, Online Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13182)
*Bryce MacInnis,Malcolm I. Heywood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出符号模仿学习框架：将专家策略离线蒸馏为带线性运动基元的决策列表，再用在线增强随机搜索（ARS）微调常数与基元，在20个基准任务中11项超越专家策略且策略精简可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在连续控制中因函数逼近能力强而占主导，但可解释性差；进化/符号方法虽可解释，却因表达能力有限而性能落后。作者希望在保持接近专家性能的同时获得领域专家可理解的简洁策略。

Method: 两阶段：1）符号模仿学习——利用离线数据集将专家（oracle）策略蒸馏为决策列表（decision list），每个分支对应一个线性模型运动基元；2）在线微调——用增强随机搜索（Augmented-Random Search, ARS）优化决策列表中的常数与运动基元参数。

Result: 在20个基准RL任务上性能逼近专家策略，并在其中11个任务上超过专家；同时生成的策略精简（parsimonious）且可被领域专家更好理解。

Conclusion: 将符号化决策列表与线性运动基元的可解释表达，与离线模仿蒸馏和在线ARS调优相结合，可兼顾性能与可解释性，为连续控制提供了一条可解释的替代路径。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) methods have been the dominant paradigm in continuous control tasks due to their efficacy in function approximation, while interpretable evolutionary methods have lagged largely behind for the opposite reason. In this work, we introduce Symbolic Imitation Learning, a framework for distilling an oracle policy through an offline dataset into a decision list with linear model motion primitives. We show that this, in conjunction, with online Augmented-Random Search (ARS) tuning of the constants and motion primitives approximates the performance of the oracle across 20 benchmark RL tasks and exceeds the oracle in 11 of those 20 tasks while simultaneously producing parsimonious, interpretable policies that domain experts can better understand.

</details>


### [25] [Prediction-Powered LLM Ranking with Adaptively Collected Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13186)
*Gabriel Sargent,Will Wei Sun,Zhengwu Zhang,Yufeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PP-Rank 框架，将 LLM 评判器预测与人类反馈结合，在自适应采样下实现有效推断，以更低成本提升 LLM 排名评估精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类反馈成本高，评估平台多采用自适应采样优先采集信息量大的模型比较；为在有限人类反馈下进一步提升精度，可利用大量未评级回复与低成本 LLM 评判器，但评判器偏差与自适应依赖破坏了标准 i.i.d. 推断框架的有效性。

Method: 构建 Prediction-Powered Ranking（PP-Rank）框架，将 LLM 评判预测与人类反馈结合；在给定类中通过数据驱动方式确定预测权重以最小化渐近协方差迹；采用联合鞅分析处理自适应人类反馈与辅助数据间的依赖，建立一般评估目标与同时排名推断的渐近正态性；并推导减少探索（优先低成本比较）且匹配保守方差界的规则，刻画成对均值常规 √T 率估计的分配极限。

Result: 在 UltraFeedback-Extended 半合成实验中，相较仅用人类反馈的基线，在分数估计、置信区间宽度与排名认证方面均有改进；合成实验显示对评判器质量与自适应分配具有稳健性，并可在不损失精度的情况下显著降低评估成本。

Conclusion: PP-Rank 在自适应采样下为结合辅助预测的 LLM 排名评估提供了有效推断与方差最优权重，兼具精度提升与成本节约，是面向下游决策的可靠评估工具。

Abstract: Ranking large language models (LLMs) through human evaluations is essential for downstream decision-making. Because human feedback is expensive, evaluation platforms often collect it adaptively, prioritizing informative model comparisons using previously observed feedback. To further improve precision with limited human feedback, evaluators can draw on abundant unrated responses and relatively inexpensive LLM judges; however, judge bias and adaptive dependence challenge standard i.i.d. inference frameworks. We propose Prediction-Powered Ranking (PP-Rank), a framework that combines LLM-judge predictions with human feedback to improve precision while providing valid inference under adaptive sampling. Data-driven prediction weights minimize the asymptotic covariance trace within the proposed class. In theory, we establish asymptotic normality for general evaluation targets and simultaneous ranking inference. A joint martingale analysis handles dependence between adaptive human feedback and auxiliary data. We further derive a rule for reducing exploration that prioritizes lower-cost comparisons while matching a conservative variance bound, and characterize allocation limits for regular $\sqrt{T}$-rate estimation of pairwise means. Semi-synthetic experiments on UltraFeedback-Extended show improvements over human-only baselines in score estimation, confidence interval width, and ranking certification. Synthetic experiments demonstrate robustness to judge quality and adaptive allocation, while showing that PP-Rank can substantially reduce evaluation costs without sacrificing precision.

</details>


### [26] [Structured Kernel Surrogate Models for Multimodal Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13198)
*Ziniu Zhang,Zhenshuo Zhang,Jianglin Lu,Ruoxuan Xiong,Yun Fu,Hongyang R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"跨样本模态归因"问题，并设计结构化核代理模型SKS，通过组合核刻画样本与模态间交互，显著提升归因准确性与下游数据选择效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多模态系统中，同一模态对不同样本的作用不同：可能冗余，也可能单独或与其他模态组合后才具独特信息量。已有工作仅在单样本层面估计影响，无法刻画"跨样本—跨模态"的交互，这一问题尚未解决。

Method: 提出结构化核代理模型，将样本与模态的选择状态映射为联合训练时的预测结果；算法SKS在样本与模态选择状态上使用结构化组合核做核岭回归，核函数由"单样本内各模态两两之间的核相似度"与"样本对之间的核相似度"相乘构成；并设计基于梯度的估计算法以高效估计该代理模型。

Result: 在两个多模态基准（涵盖卫星图像、路网、文本、音频、表格数据）上，SKS与真实预测结果的相关性比现有方法高57.8%；下游数据选择中平均准确率提升1.93%；所选数据训练出的模型Hessian迹更低，损失面更平坦；用于卫星图像选择时可定位事故常发的路段而非背景图像。

Conclusion: 结构化组合核能有效建模样本与模态间的交互影响，为跨样本模态归因提供可行方案，并在数据选择与可解释性上带来实证收益。

Abstract: We study the problem of cross-sample modality attribution, which involves measuring how one modality in one sample affects the learning outcome in other multimodal samples. This problem is crucial in a multimodal system because a data modality (such as a text description) may be redundant for one sample (a self-explanatory image), but the same data modality would otherwise be uniquely informative for another sample, or informative when combined with other modalities. Prior work focuses on influence estimation at the level of one sample, and it remains an open question how one should capture the interactions across both samples and modalities. To capture scenarios like the above, we propose structured kernel surrogate models, which are surrogate mappings from both samples and modalities to the prediction outcome when they are trained together. We design an algorithm, SKS, which applies kernel ridge regression with a structured, compositional kernel on the selection states of individual samples and modalities. Specifically, the kernel function multiplies kernel-based similarity scores between every pair of data modalities for one sample and a pair of two samples. Additionally, we design a gradient-based estimation algorithm to efficiently estimate the structured kernel surrogate model. We evaluate our approach on two multimodal benchmarks, spanning a range of data modalities including satellite images, road networks, text, audio, and tabular data. We find that SKS achieves $57.8$% higher correlation with the true prediction outcomes than existing methods. In downstream data selection, SKS improves the average accuracy by $1.93$% over baselines. Models with data selected by SKS have lower Hessian traces, indicating a flatter loss surface. Using SKS to select satellite images identifies road segments, where accident scenes commonly occur, as opposed to background images.

</details>


### [27] [XAI-Driven Confidence Estimation for OOD-Resilient Runtime Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13199)
*Mahmoud Sanad,Mohammed Bakr Sikal,Osama Abboud,Heba Khdr,Jörg Henkel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 LEX，首个面向机器学习资源管理的后置可解释性框架，用高斯混合模型对训练输入嵌入聚类并为每簇关联误差界，实现免重训的输入级运行时置信度估计。在 Intel Alder Lake 上对 LSTM 的 IPS 预测模型、34 个基准应用评估，未见负载上约 94% 包含率，置信区间比全局基线紧约 17%，开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机器学习的资源管理（RM）模型在运行时遇到分布外（OOD）输入会性能退化；现有方法仅使用单一全局误差裕度，过于保守且无法区分可靠预测与灾难性失效，缺乏输入特定的可靠性信号。

Method: LEX 为后置（post-hoc）XAI 框架：对训练输入空间的嵌入表示用高斯混合模型（GMM）聚类，为每个簇关联预测误差界，从而在推理时为输入样本分配细粒度、输入特定的置信度，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在 Intel Alder Lake 上评估基于 LSTM 的 IPS 预测模型，覆盖 34 个基准应用：未见负载上达到约 94% 的包含率（containment），且相比全局基线，逐输入置信区间紧约 17%；运行时开销可忽略。

Conclusion: LEX 通过提供可解释且可靠的输入特定置信度信号，提升了 ML 预测的可信度，使基于机器学习的资源管理在运行时对 OOD 输入具备韧性，是对该领域的有益推进。

Abstract: Machine learning-based resource management (RM) models degrade under out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs at runtime, yet existing approaches in the literature rely on a single global error margin that is overly conservative and fails to distinguish reliablepredictions from catastrophic failures. We propose Lightweight Runtime EXplainability (LEX), the first post-hoc XAI framework for input-specific runtime confidence estimation in ML-based RM. LEX clusters the embeddings of the training input space using a Gaussian Mixture Model and associates per-cluster prediction error bounds to input samples, enabling fine-grained runtime confidence signals without retraining. Evaluated on a state-of-the-art LSTM-based IPS forecasting model on Intel Alder Lake, across 34 benchmark applications, LEX achieves ~94% containment on unseen workloads and produces ~17% tighter per-input confidence bounds than global baselines. With its negligible runtime overhead, LEX advances the field by providing explainable and reliable input-specific confidence signals of ML predictions, thereby making ML-based resource management resilient to OOD at runtime.

</details>


### [28] [Audited Gradients, Anchored Policies: Model-Based Reinforcement Learning for Efficient Flow Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13203)
*Ruige Kong,Stefania Fresca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"动作梯度审计"机制：在部署前用高保真求解器的中心差分估计检验可微代理的动作梯度，据结果选择可信梯度来源拟合低维反馈律，再以其为锚点通过二次惩罚约束神经策略优化，在四个算例上以3–156倍更少的高保真交互达到或超过无模型基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流动反馈控制器设计依赖高保真求解器逐候选评估，代价高昂；可微代理虽降低成本，却引入了预测误差无法察觉的失效模式——代理可紧密跟随流场轨迹却严重错估目标对动作的导数（动作梯度），导致策略优化被误导。因此需要一种不依赖预测精度的可信性判据。

Method: 定义动作梯度为目标对动作的导数，可经代理或高保真求解器计算。在留出状态上，以预先固定的接受准则将代理动作梯度与求解器中心差分估计比对，称为审计。审计通过则用代理动作梯度拟合低维反馈律，否则用求解器估计拟合；该反馈律用于初始化神经策略，并在优化中以二次惩罚将策略约束在其附近（锚点惩罚）。

Result: 四个算例中所得控制器性能优于或匹配无模型基线，高保真求解器交互减少3至156倍。案例显示：某代目标预测相关系数0.9996但动作梯度仅0.459，说明预测精度不能识别可用代理；另一代动作梯度与求解器估计相关0.996，但在无锚点惩罚下直接经其优化策略使目标恶化0.52%，说明梯度准确本身不足以保证控制器可用；第三个代理在某个固定动作处通过审计，却在部署控制器实际施加的动作附近失败，说明审计结论是局部的。

Conclusion: 代理可信性应由动作梯度而非预测精度来判定，且审计具有局部性，需在部署动作邻域内评估；通过审计的自适应梯度来源选择加锚点惩罚，可在显著降低高保真交互成本的同时保证控制器性能。

Abstract: Feedback controllers for flows are expensive to design, since each candidate must be evaluated with a high-fidelity solver. Differentiable surrogates reduce that cost, but they also introduce a failure mode that prediction error cannot detect. Policy optimization uses the derivative of the objective with respect to the action, which we call the action gradient and which can be computed either through the surrogate or through the high-fidelity solver. Optimization through the surrogate uses only its action gradient, and a surrogate can follow the flow trajectory closely while misestimating this derivative. The surrogate action gradient is compared against a central finite-difference estimate from the high-fidelity solver on held-out states, under acceptance criteria fixed before the comparison, and we call that comparison an audit. A low-dimensional feedback law is then fitted to the surrogate action gradient when the surrogate passes the audit, and to the solver estimate when it does not. This law initializes a neural policy, and a quadratic penalty holds the policy near it during optimization. The proposed approach is validated on four test cases. The resulting controllers outperform or match a model-free baseline, while using 3 to 156 times fewer high-fidelity solver interactions across the four test cases. One surrogate predicts the objective at correlation 0.9996 but its action gradient at only 0.459, so prediction accuracy does not identify a usable surrogate. Another surrogate, whose action gradient agrees with the solver estimate at correlation 0.996, makes the objective 0.52% worse when the policy is optimized through it without the anchor penalty, so an accurate gradient alone does not provide a working controller. Finally, a surrogate that passes the audit at one fixed action fails it around the actions the deployed controller applies, so a pass is local.

</details>


### [29] [Wrong the Same Way: Cross-Prompt Sharing Both Suppresses and Amplifies Reward Hacking in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13218)
*Michael Li,Rohan Pandey,Ray Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在一个可精确计算全部响应分布的小型受控环境中，区分了"奖励黑客由基座模型继承"与"RL放大黑客"两种观点，结论是：KL正则最优下RLVR只是对接受集重新加权（黑客率精确继承），而参数共享与跨提示的核加权概率决定了漂移方向，最终黑客的放大或抑制取决于模型精度落在哪些提示上，而非验证器错误率或泄漏普遍性本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于RLVR与不完美验证器交互存在两种并存解释（继承 vs. 放大），且早前实证研究结论相互矛盾；作者希望用一个可精确求解的受控环境给出机制性解释，并指出验证器修复应针对模型跨提示重复犯的错误。

Method: 构造足够小、可精确计算完整响应分布的受控环境；操纵"泄漏普遍性"（接受同一错误响应的提示比例）与验证器静态假阳性率。以KL正则最优作为零模型（仅重加权、不改变接受集构成），借助已知分解将漂移写为各提示上"奖励与经验切向核的协方差"之和；对比无参数共享（逐提示独立副本）与有参数共享；在Adam、SGD与采样GRPO及不同学习率下测试收敛行为。

Result: 无参数共享时，其他提示的协方差项消失，黑客率精确继承基座率。有共享参数时，通过baseline项，每个接受错误答案的提示按模型在该处产生该答案的概率将其推高、拒绝的提示将其压低；因此共享策略会把少数提示接受的泄漏压到基率以下、把多数提示接受的泄漏放大。在Adam、SGD、采样GRPO的每个收敛学习率下，高普遍性终点的黑客水平都高于低普遍性，但是否跨越基率取决于步长；逐提示独立副本消除了这种依赖。推动方向不由验证器错误率或普遍性单独决定，而由"模型对错误答案的核加权概率中有多大比例落在接受它的提示上"决定，冻结基座模型的对照实验支持这一点。两个移除同等数量错误的补丁在真实准确率上相差可达38个百分点。

Conclusion: RLVR对奖励黑客的效应不能归结为"继承"或"放大"的单一叙事：KL正则最优下是纯重加权式的精确继承，偏离该零模型的漂移由参数共享经baseline引发的跨提示协方差决定。因此"何时放大"取决于模型核加权错误概率在接受提示与非接受提示间的分配。验证器修复应针对模型跨提示重复的错误；作者将这一对齐关系作为解释早期研究分歧的假说提出。

Abstract: Two accounts of how reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) interacts with an imperfect verifier coexist: in one, reward hacking is inherited from the base model; in the other, RL amplifies it. We study both in a controlled environment small enough to compute the entire response distribution exactly. We vary the universality of a leak, or the fraction of prompts that accept the same wrong response, and the verifier's static false-positive rate. At its KL-regularized optimum, RLVR acts as a filter that reweights the accepted set without altering its composition, so the hack rate is inherited exactly. Taking that as a null model, a known decomposition writes the drift from it as a sum over prompts of covariances between reward and the empirical tangent kernel. The terms from other prompts vanish without parameter sharing. With sharing, every prompt that accepts a wrong answer pushes it up and every prompt that rejects it pushes it down through the baseline, in proportion to the model's probability of producing it there. A shared-parameter policy thus drives a leak that few prompts accept below the base rate and amplifies one that most prompts accept. At every convergent learning rate under Adam, SGD, and sampled GRPO, hacking ends higher at high universality than at low, but whether either end crosses the base rate depends on the step size. Independent per-prompt copies remove the dependence. The direction of this push depends on neither the verifier's error rate nor universality alone, but on how much of the model's kernel-weighted probability of a wrong answer falls on prompts that accept it, which frozen base model controls support. Verifier fixes should target the mistakes the model repeats across prompts: two patches that remove equally many errors differ by up to 38 points of true accuracy. We propose this alignment as a hypothesis for why earlier studies disagree.

</details>


### [30] [GigaEdge Spench: An SDK for Practical Streaming Speech Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13243)
*Artem Sokolov,Artem Khaidarov,Yana Lysova,Alexander Gladkikh,Vitaliy Yulis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GigaEdge Spench 是一个开源的流式语音增强 SDK，支持在 CPU 服务器、移动端与浏览器上构建由神经降噪、归一化与动态处理、采样率转换和回声消除等模块组成的增强通道，并提供 C++ 共享核心与多平台接口；论文在 EARS-WHAM v2 上评测模型质量与计算开销，并与 RNNoise、DPDFNet2 对比，同时分析模型选择、chunk 大小与执行环境对部署的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式语音增强在实际部署中面临跨平台（服务器、移动端、浏览器）、模型与信号处理模块组合、资源受限（CPU 实时性）等问题；现有模型与工具缺少统一的、可配置的部署系统，也缺乏对模型选择、分块大小和执行环境如何影响实际使用成本的系统实验研究。

Method: 提出 GigaEdge Spench 开源 SDK：模块化增强通道（神经降噪、归一化与动态处理、采样率转换、声学回声消除，归一化可无需神经模型独立运行）；发布 8 kHz INT8 降噪模型与 48 kHz FP32 判别式/混合模型；共享 C++ 核心通过原生接口支持 ONNX Runtime 与 TensorFlow Lite，并提供 Android/iOS SDK 与 WebAssembly 集成；支持独立流上下文共享模型资源，以及同一流连续帧合并为单次推理（temporal frame grouping）；在 EARS-WHAM v2 上与 RNNoise、DPDFNet2 进行质量与计算开销对比，并测量 CPU 上共享模型可并发处理的流数量；SDK 计时均基于 ONNX Runtime。

Result: 所有测试的原生与 Web 配置均能快于实时处理文件，但较大的 chunk 需要更长的输入收集时间；报告了共享模型在 CPU 上可处理的并发流数量，并给出各模型在 EARS-WHAM v2 上的质量与处理成本对比（含与 RNNoise、DPDFNet2 的比较）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是一个可配置的跨平台部署系统，以及对模型选择、chunk 大小与执行环境如何影响其使用的实验研究；结果表明所发布配置在真实条件下具备实时可部署性，且连续帧合并与资源共享有助于提升部署效率。

Abstract: We present GigaEdge Spench, an open-source SDK for streaming speech enhancement on CPU servers, mobile devices, and in browsers. Applications can build enhancement channels from neural denoising, normalization and dynamics blocks, sample-rate conversion, and acoustic echo cancellation where supported. These stages can be combined, while normalization can also run without a neural model. The release includes an 8 kHz INT8 denoiser and discriminative and hybrid 48 kHz FP32 models. A shared C++ core supports ONNX Runtime and TensorFlow Lite through native interfaces, Android and iOS SDKs, and WebAssembly integration. Independent stream contexts share model resources, and consecutive frames from one stream can be processed in a single inference call. We evaluate the released models on EARS-WHAM v2 and compare their quality and processing costs with RNNoise and DPDFNet2. All tested native and Web configurations process files faster than real time, although larger chunks require more time to collect input. We also measure how many streams a shared model can process on a CPU. Our contribution is a configurable deployment system and an experimental study of how model choice, chunk size, and execution environment affect its use. All reported SDK timing uses ONNX Runtime. Index Terms: speech enhancement, streaming inference, cross-platform deployment, temporal frame grouping, computational efficiency

</details>


### [31] [Domain-Routed Delta Optimization for Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13249)
*Lin Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出领域路由增量优化（DRD-MOPD），将多教师在线策略蒸馏中"哪些参数组件由哪个领域样本更新"显式化：学生模型为冻结基座与若干可训练组件的加和，所有组件参与每次前向，四种更新模式配合独立优化器状态；并通过角度、投影系数与能量指标度量专家几何保留度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多教师在线策略蒸馏通常只给样本分配领域教师，却对共享学生参数做统一更新，导致领域样本更新同一组参数、互相干扰；如 Open-MOPD 直接从基座模型蒸馏，其发布最终检查点的增量能量有 97.02% 落在原始专家方向张成空间之外，说明专家几何被严重破坏。

Method: 学生 = 冻结基座 + 可训练组件之和，组件以"专家减基座"权重初始化；所有组件参与每次前向；用四种更新模式定义样本领域与组件之间的对应关系，每种模式配备独立优化器状态；以角度、投影系数与能量三类指标分析更新规则并度量对原始专家几何的保留。

Result: Own 检查点经 20 次更新后，沿各组件原始方向的投影系数接近 1，组件两两夹角为 88.04°–92.41°；导出 BF16 学生的增量的span外能量为 4.90%，而保存的 FP32 组件之和为 10.52%；该检查点在六个基准上宏平均得分 32.72%，其初始 task-arithmetic 合并为 32.44%。

Conclusion: 显式的领域—组件更新路由可在蒸馏中较好保持原始专家几何，并在所评估的训练阶段与设置下取得略优于初始 task-arithmetic 合并的能力；相关测量仅描述该阶段与设置下的几何与能力表现。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation assigns each sample a domain teacher but typically updates shared student parameters. We introduce Domain-Routed Delta Optimization (DRD-MOPD), which specifies which parameter components each domain's samples update. The student is the sum of a frozen base and trainable components initialized from expert-minus-base weights. All components participate in every forward pass; four update modes define the correspondence between sample domains and components, each with independent optimizer states. We analyze these update rules and measure retention of the original expert geometry through angles, projection coefficients, and energy. Prior work such as Open-MOPD distills directly from the base model; 97.02% of its released final checkpoint's increment energy lies outside the span of the original expert directions. In comparison, our Own checkpoint after 20 updates retains coefficients near one along each component's original direction and pairwise angles ranging from 88.04 to 92.41 degrees; its exported BF16 student's increment has 4.90% outside energy, versus 10.52% for the sum of saved FP32 components. The same checkpoint achieves a 32.72% macro score across six benchmarks, compared with 32.44% for its initial task-arithmetic merge. These measurements describe geometry and capability at the evaluated training stages and settings.

</details>


### [32] [Off-policy Learning with Excursion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13275)
*Jiamin He,Mark Rowland,Zhaohan Daniel Guo,Hado van Hasselt,Diana Borsa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"excursion策略"——智能体先按目标策略执行多步"远足"再回退到行为策略，并给出其动态规划理论、可靠的离策略TD估值算法；实验表明其效率-稳定性权衡与混合策略相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略RL需在偏差、方差与稳定性之间权衡。现有做法是在行为策略与目标策略间做线性插值（混合策略），但这只覆盖了更广泛的插值机制空间中的一点，机制设计空间受限。

Method: 提出结构化的excursion策略：在目标策略下执行多步"远足"后回退到行为策略；推导该类策略的动态规划（Bellman型）结果，作为RL使用的理论基础；并据此设计可证明正确的离策略时序差分（TD）算法用于excursion价值估计。

Result: 在连续控制基准上，excursion策略的效率-稳定性权衡与混合策略相当；理论分析揭示了二者在行为上的概念性差异。

Conclusion: 多步excursion是混合插值之外一种有理论依据的、可行的偏差-方差权衡替代机制，为离策略RL的数据分布设计提供了新维度。

Abstract: Off-policy reinforcement learning (RL) tries to learn the value of a target policy while data comes from a different behavior policy. This often requires trading off bias for variance or stability. One way to trade these off is to linearly interpolate between the behavior and target policies, but this represents only one point in a broader space of interpolation mechanisms. We introduce excursion policies--a structured alternative where the agent follows the target policy for a multi-step "excursion" before reverting to the behavior policy. We derive dynamic programming results for these policies, establishing the theoretical basis for their use in RL. We further develop provably sound off-policy temporal-difference (TD) algorithms for excursion value estimation. We find that excursion policies provide an efficiency-stability trade-off comparable to mixture policies in continuous control benchmarks, and our analysis illustrates conceptual differences in their behavior.

</details>


### [33] [An Analytical Model of Compute-limited Multistage Training Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13276)
*Nishil Patel,Jin Hwa Lee,Basile Confavreux,Andrew Saxe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建可解析的高维理论模型，刻画无监督预训练（线性自编码器）+监督微调（带噪逐步标签）+强化学习（稀疏回合奖励）三阶段的交互与算力分配规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基础模型普遍经历多阶段训练，但各阶段之间如何相互作用、算力应如何在阶段间分配，仍缺乏理论理解。

Method: 采用随机矩阵理论与算子投影闭合（operator-projection closure），推导出与仿真高度吻合的低维近似学习动力学；并以 OLMo-2 1B 在 GSM8K 上的现象作为经验佐证；另提出一个互补的组合式模型。

Result: (1) 有限预训练数据导致依赖表征的性能上限，贝叶斯分类误差随预训练样本比例呈逆平方根标度，SFT 与 RL 的渐近误差呈同一指数；(2) 更优预训练降低对噪声监督的敏感性；(3) SFT 初始化可缩短稀疏奖励导致的 RL 初始平台期（OLMo-2 1B/GSM8K 亦可见），更长 SFT 热启动还提升 RL 渐近性能；(4) 三阶段最优算力分配依赖监督质量与总预算，RL 算力占比随预算呈非单调变化；(5) RL 选择性强化任务相关预训练技能，基本保留未用技能。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了表征质量、反馈结构与算力分配之间的关联机制，为多阶段训练设计提供理论依据。

Abstract: Modern foundation models are trained through multiple stages, yet how these stages interact and how compute should be allocated across them remain poorly understood. We introduce an analytically tractable high-dimensional model combining unsupervised pretraining with a linear autoencoder, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with noisy stepwise labels, and reinforcement learning (RL) with sparse episodic rewards. Using random matrix theory and an operator-projection closure, we derive approximate low-dimensional learning dynamics that closely match simulations. We show that finite pretraining data imposes a representation-dependent performance limit, with Bayes classification error scaling as the inverse square root of the pretraining sample ratio, and observe the same exponent in asymptotic SFT and RL errors.
