Abrupt changes in lane markings with an incompatible signal phase can jeopardize drivers. To sidestep such a dangerous action change, we train a model with human feedback to filter infeasible action transitions in a rule-based manner. We devised a deep Q-network that can accommodate joint actions for traffic signal and lane marking plans. To ensure the implementation of the proposed operation, a variable sign for lane markings is necessary at each stop line of an intersection. The experimental results confirm that the proposed methodology enhances the intersection performance by increasing throughput.

</details>


### [67] [Multifidelity Operator Inference for Non-intrusive Model Reduction under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13624)
*Vivian Zhang,Tomoki Koike,Atticus Rex,Elizabeth Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多保真算子推断（MF-OpInf），用充足的低保真数据补充稀缺的高保真快照，以多保真线性回归降低降阶算子估计的方差，在同等计算预算下比仅用高保真数据的OpInf更准确地预测PDE解场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真PDE模型的多次查询分析（不确定性量化、优化设计）计算代价高昂；代理模型可替代昂贵仿真。机器学习类代理模型（如算子推断OpInf）非侵入、无需访问仿真源码，但受计算预算限制，高保真训练数据稀缺，导致OpInf模型方差大，在高风险工程设计中是严重隐患。

Method: MF-OpInf：从同一问题的较粗离散（低保真）获取大量数据，与稀缺的高保真快照结合；利用多保真线性回归框架，在不同保真度数据间融合，估计降阶算子。

Result: 在模型PDE问题上的数值实验表明，在相同计算预算下，MF-OpInf相比仅用高保真的OpInf具有更高的PDE解场预测精度。

Conclusion: 将多保真思想引入算子推断可有效缓解数据稀缺导致的高方差问题，提升降阶代理模型的鲁棒性与精度，适用于工程设计中的多次查询分析。

Abstract: Many-query analyses, in which a computational model is repeatedly evaluated, play an important role in uncertainty quantification and optimization for engineering design. However, many-query analyses can be prohibitively expensive when considering high-fidelity numerical models, especially those governed by partial differential equations (PDEs). Surrogate modeling replaces costly simulations with cheap approximations, enabling practical uncertainty quantification and design workflows. Machine learning (ML) approaches to surrogate modeling have gained significant traction in the development of surrogate models due to their non-intrusive nature; they do not require access to the underlying simulation code. The Operator Inference (OpInf) approach, in particular, fits reduced operators to state and corresponding time derivative data. However, high-fidelity training data is scarce due to limited computational budgets, and OpInf models trained on such scarce data often exhibit high variance, a critical liability in high-risk engineering design. We propose Multifidelity Operator Inference (MF-OpInf), which reduces this variance by supplementing scarce high-fidelity snapshots with abundant low-fidelity data drawn from coarser discretizations of the same problem. MF-OpInf handles data across varying fidelity levels by utilizing a multifidelity linear regression framework to estimate the reduced operators. Numerical experiments on model PDE problems demonstrate that MF-OpInf predicts PDE solution fields with higher accuracy compared to the high-fidelity-only OpInf with the same computational budget.

</details>


### [68] [MouldIt: Data Efficiency of Multi-Task Temporal Representation Learning in Injection Moulding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13646)
*Abdullah Ayad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于时间卷积与注意力机制的变分自编码器 MouldIt，用于注塑成型过程的多任务表示学习，并系统评估其数据效率与多模态收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能工业方案需同时完成多任务（如部件合格/失效分类与未知失效检测），这要求模型理解底层工艺过程。作者以注塑成型为例，考察表示学习相对经典机器学习的优势，重点关注数据效率与多模态数据带来的增益。

Method: 基于真实 Erlangen Träger 零件参考周期的合成工艺感知数据集（含模内信号与多种合成失效模式）；提出 MouldIt——时间卷积与注意力结合的变分自编码器，在重构模内信号的同时将零件分类为合格或某类失效；在已知类分类与未知类检测两类任务上，于 9 种数据规模、单模态与多模态设置下评测，并与 accept-all 基线及 kNN、逻辑回归、随机森林、PCA（均改造以支持两任务）及 PCA+RF 组合对比。

Result: MouldIt 在两类任务上均优于经典方法：单模态下从 4000 个循环起、多模态下从 2000 个循环起即领先；分选损失分别达 6.0±0.7%（单模态）与 1.0±0.3%（多模态），在所有基线及 PCA+随机森林组合中排名第一。

Conclusion: 表示学习型模型在注塑过程中具备更高的数据效率与多模态优势，可在较少样本下实现更优的部件分选与未知失效检测，为多任务智能工业方案提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Smart industrial solutions that can address multiple tasks require models that understand the underlying processes. We examine representation learning for modeling the injection moulding process as an example of such solutions. We consider its data efficiency and multi-modal benefit compared to classical machine learning alternatives. To this end, we synthesize a process-informed dataset based on a real reference cycle of an Erlanger Träger part. We then introduce MouldIt, a temporal convolution and attention based variational autoencoder that reconstructs in-mould signals, while classifying parts as acceptable or as one of our synthetic failure modes. We evaluate MouldIt on two tasks, known-class classification and unknown-class detection, across nine dataset sizes in single-modal and multi-modal settings. We compare it with an accept-all baseline and the classical methods k-nearest neighbors, logistic regression, random forests, and principal component analysis, which we adapt to support both tasks. MouldIt outperforms classical methods across both tasks from 4000 cycles in the single-modal setting and from 2000 cycles in the multi-modal setting. It achieves part sorting losses of 6.0 $\pm$ 0.7% and 1.0 $\pm$ 0.3% in the single-modal and multi-modal settings respectively, ranking first across all baselines and a combination of principal component analysis and random forests.

</details>


### [69] [CP-MLPs: A Tensor-Rank Theory of Tied and Untied MLP Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13647)
*Md Rifat Arefin,Farzaneh Heidari,Irina Rish,Guillaume Rabusseau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CP-MLPs 张量框架，将乘性单元分解为"检测/读取/写入"三种角色，从张量秩角度精确刻画了 tied 与 untied 门控 MLP 在表达性上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 前馈块同时采用平方激活、门控以及参数绑定/解绑等多种设计，但已有工作缺乏对各自贡献表达性的统一、可精确量化的理论解释，尤其不清楚 untied 门控为何以及在何种目标下优于 tied 结构。

Method: 引入 CP-MLPs 张量框架：每个乘性单元贡献一个秩-1 交互，包含检测输入特征、读取数值、写入输出三个角色。tied 门控（如 ReLU²）强制检测与读取共享同一方向，untied 门控（如 ReGLU、SwiGLU）允许二者不同。基于此在逐次数（degree-wise）上给出精确的张量秩分离结果，并用 border rank 分析近似能力，辅以训练实验验证。

Result: 对不定二次型交互与角色非对称单项式，untied 单元具有严格的宽度效率优势；而 tied 单元在对称纯幂目标上结构匹配。实验观察到损失平台低于精确表示所需宽度，但该平台本身不能证明存在正近似间隙，任意精度近似由 border rank 决定。在匹配输入投影预算的受训门控 MLP 中，untied 块在部分比较中保持优势。

Conclusion: 解绑的宽度优势依赖于目标函数结构：并非普遍更优。精确表示能力与近似能力需分别用张量秩与 border rank 衡量，损失平台不等于近似间隙；理论分离与受训模型中的优势仅在同一预算下的部分情形成立。

Abstract: Transformer feed-forward blocks combine several design choices: squared activations, gating, and tied versus untied parameter sharing, whose separate contributions to expressivity remain poorly understood. We introduce CP-MLPs, a tensor framework in which each multiplicative unit contributes a rank-1 interaction with three roles: detecting an input feature, reading a value, and writing to the output. Tied gated blocks such as ReLU$^2$ force the detector and value roles to share the same direction; untied gated blocks such as ReGLU and SwiGLU let them differ. We show that this distinction yields exact, degree-wise tensor-rank separations: untied detector-value units are strictly more width-efficient for indefinite quadratic interactions and role-asymmetric monomials, while tied units are structurally matched to symmetric pure-power targets. The width advantage of untying therefore depends on the target structure. Experiments show loss plateaus below the exact-representation width, but these plateaus alone do not establish a positive approximation gap: arbitrarily accurate approximation is governed by border rank. In trained gated MLPs, untied blocks retain an advantage in selected comparisons at matched input-projection budget. Code is available at https://github.com/rarefin/CP-MLPs.

</details>


### [70] [SepsisFlow: an event-stream foundation model for continuous risk warning and trajectory modelling in ICU sepsis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13661)
*Yawen Zhang,Wanzhe Xu,Zhenhua Zhang,Jinyu Wu,Yizhou Zhuang,Yitao Yang,Kenta Nakai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SepsisFlow 构建了覆盖脓毒症"发病前—发病时—发病后"全时段的统一小时级框架：发病前预测 6/12/24 小时风险，发病时生成患者状态以定义发病情境，发病后刻画 72 小时早期病程轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脓毒症预警模型多只预测"是否会发生"，报警后临床医生无法获知发病情境与早期演变轨迹；需要从 ICU 入科起按同一小时时钟，连续回答风险、情境与病程三个问题。

Method: ①发病前：在 257 万份 EHR 上预训练的 Mamba 模型 + 约 1700 个小时级特征输入梯度提升树，预测 6/12/24 小时风险；②发病时：门控网络融合 Mamba 与表格编码器，生成连续患者状态以界定发病情境；③发病后：注意力双向循环网络编码 72 小时器官支持与生理轨迹，刻画早期病程。

Result: MIMIC-IV 中，同等报警次数下检出病例多于 NEWS2、qSOFA 与 SIRS，逐小时更新每 100 例约多检 8 例，并优于 16 个重训练算法；eICU 中保持风险排序能力。发病情境方面，心血管 ICU 情境 28 天死亡率约 2%，其他情境 15%–17%；各情境内，发病后持续器官支持者死亡率约为降阶梯者的 5 倍。

Conclusion: SepsisFlow 建立了重症监护中连续风险预测与轨迹分层的基础模型范式，将预警从单点二分类扩展为情境与病程的分层刻画。

Abstract: Sepsis is a leading cause of ICU death with heterogeneous onset and course, yet most warning models predict only whether sepsis will occur, leaving clinicians uncertain of its onset context and early trajectory once an alarm sounds. To answer these questions on one hourly clock from ICU admission, we built SepsisFlow: before onset, it combines a Mamba model pretrained on 2.57 million EHRs with about 1,700 hourly features in gradient-boosted trees to predict 6/12/24-h risk; at onset, it fuses Mamba and tabular encoders via a gated network into continuous patient states defining onset contexts; after onset, it encodes 72-h organ-support and physiological trajectories via an attention bidirectional recurrent network into early courses. For pre-onset warning in MIMIC-IV, SepsisFlow detected more cases than NEWS2, qSOFA, and SIRS at equal alarms, gaining about 8 cases per 100 from hourly updating, while outperforming 16 retrained algorithms and maintaining risk ranking in eICU. At onset, its contexts separated 2% cardiovascular-ICU from 15%-17% other 28-day mortality, and within each context after onset, persistent support carried fivefold higher mortality than de-escalation. SepsisFlow establishes a foundation-model paradigm for continuous risk and trajectory stratification in critical care.

</details>


### [71] [Bootstrapped On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13680)
*Yue Wu,Xueyu Zhou,Yangrong Hu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现 on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) 在持续训练下会失效甚至性能大幅下降，原因在于动态学生与冻结的特权教师之间的分布失配不断增大；据此提出两阶段框架 BOPD，先通过 OPSD 将参考条件监督中的任务相关信息内化到中间模型，再将该无参考的中间模型蒸馏回初始学生模型，从而避免误导性监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD 无需外部教师，早期提升显著，但在持续训练中表现不稳定并可能严重退化。作者将这一现象归因于参考条件教师（冻结）与无参考学生（不断演化）之间日益扩大的分布失配：早期二者对齐良好、监督有效，后期教师给出误导性监督，反而损害学生性能。

Method: 提出 Bootstrapped On-Policy Distillation (BOPD) 两阶段框架：(1) 用 OPSD 将参考条件（privileged）监督中的任务相关信息内化到一个中间模型；(2) 将该无参考条件的中间模型蒸馏到一个初始模型的新副本中，从而在蒸馏阶段消除参考条件教师带来的分布失配。

Result: 在多个模型上的实验表明，BOPD 在数学推理和代码生成任务上优于初始模型以及所构建的 OPSD 教师模型，说明 "先内化、再蒸馏" 的策略能更有效地利用特权参考监督。

Conclusion: 特权参考监督的价值应通过两步释放：先将其任务相关信号迁移到任务适配模型中，再从该模型进行无参考条件的蒸馏；这既保留了 OPSD 的知识获取能力，又避开了长时间自蒸馏导致的失配与性能退化。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves language models by distilling teacher predictions on student-generated trajectories, while on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) removes the need for an external teacher by using a reference-conditioned copy of the model as the teacher. Despite its great early improvements, we find that OPSD does not remain consistently effective under continued training and can eventually suffer substantial performance degradation. We study this training behavior and link the degradation to a growing mismatch between the evolving reference-free student and the frozen privileged teacher. In the early stage of OPSD, the privileged teacher remains closely aligned with the student and provides useful guidance for improving the student's generation trajectories. However, as training progresses, the growing distributional mismatch between the privileged teacher and reference-free student can cause the teacher to provide misleading supervision, ultimately degrading student performance. This phenomenon motivates us to first leverage privileged reference supervision to acquire useful knowledge and then transfer it to a fresh student. We introduce Bootstrapped On-Policy Distillation (BOPD), a two-stage framework that first uses OPSD to internalize task-relevant information from reference-conditioned supervision into an intermediate model, and then distills this reference-free intermediate model into a fresh copy of the initial model. Experiments across models show that BOPD improves mathematical reasoning and code generation beyond both the initial models and the constructed OPSD teachers. These results suggest that privileged reference supervision can be more effectively exploited by first transferring its task-relevant signal into a task-adapted model and then distilling from that model without reference conditioning.

</details>


### [72] [FedVP: Federated Learning across Heterogeneous Models by Pooling Locally Trained Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13708)
*Yifan Li,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出联邦验证池（FEDVP），通过共享验证器而非参数聚合实现异构大模型客户端的协作，在五个医疗问答数据集上平均准确率领先最强传统联邦基线1.46–5.79个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习依赖共享模型骨干来聚合客户端更新，要求各客户端模型架构一致，从而限制了部署不同家族、不同规模LLM的机构之间的协作。

Method: 每个客户端用本地数据及其所选LLM骨干生成的回答训练一个验证器；服务器汇集这些独立训练的验证器，通过组合其判断来选择答案，而不合并模型参数。LLM骨干保持冻结，骨干与验证器均可异构（架构与规模不同），协作收益来自更优的答案选择。

Result: 在五个公开医疗问答数据集作为联邦客户端、Qwen与LLaMA骨干（3B–14B参数）的设置下，FEDVP在全部五个客户端上均稳定提升性能，平均准确率比最强传统联邦基线高1.46–5.79个百分点。

Conclusion: 以“共享验证”替代“参数聚合”可作为异构LLM联邦协作的可行新范式，在冻结骨干的前提下取得一致的协作增益。

Abstract: Conventional federated learning relies on the shared model backbone to aggregate client updates, limiting collaboration among institutions that deploy large language models (LLMs) from different families and at different scales. We propose Federated Verifier Pool (FEDVP), a new federated learning paradigm that enables heterogeneous clients to collaborate through shared verification rather than parameter aggregation. Each client trains a verifier using local data and responses generated by its chosen LLM backbone. The server pools these independently trained verifiers and combines their judgments to select answers without merging model parameters. This allows both LLM backbones and verifiers to differ in architecture and size, while keeping the LLM backbones frozen and deriving collaborative gains from improved answer selection. We evaluate FEDVP across five public medical question-answering datasets treated as federated clients, using Qwen and LLaMA backbones ranging from 3B to 14B parameters. FEDVP consistently improves performance across all five clients and outperforms the strongest conventional federated baselines by 1.46-5.79 percentage points in mean accuracy.

</details>


### [73] [TRUER: Test-Time Reward Correction Using an Error-Aware Rectifier for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13714)
*Mingxiang Shen,Jinhao Li,Jiaming Xu,Yibo Han,Guohao Dai,Yijia Zhang,Ningyi Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRUER 用少量标注数据训练"奖励校正器"，在测试时强化学习中纠正多数投票带来的虚假正奖励，从而稳定提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时强化学习依赖模型自身答案派生奖励，但多数投票会在存在正确答案时仍强化错误解；训练审计表明奖励错误以"假阳性"（错误答案获得正奖励）为主。实际场景中大规模获取真实标签困难，但存在少量可用的标注源数据。

Method: 提出 TRUER 框架：(1) 利用有限监督训练奖励校正器，使其能区分"正确性"与"共识性"；(2) 训练时刻意聚焦于"错误多数"与"正确少数"这类共识与正确性不一致的样本；(3) 训练完成后冻结校正器，在后续测试时自适应中用它校正奖励。

Result: 通过提升奖励准确率进而提升模型性能，TRUER 在所有评估配置下的两项指标上均稳定优于朴素 TTRL。

Conclusion: 以少量监督校正测试时奖励是可扩展且有效的方向；消除共识—正确性错位带来的假阳性奖励是关键改进点，未来可进一步降低对标注源集的依赖。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning enables language models to adapt using rewards derived from their own answers. However, majority voting can reinforce incorrect solutions even when correct alternatives are available. Our training audit shows that false positives (incorrect responses receiving positive rewards) dominate reward errors. We focus on a practical regime where ground-truth rewards are difficult to obtain at scale, while a limited labeled source set is available. We introduce TRUER, a framework that uses limited supervision to train a reward rectifier that distinguishes correctness from consensus. To achieve this goal, we train the rectifier with an emphasis on incorrect majorities and correct minorities, targeting cases where consensus and correctness disagree. After that, we freeze the rectifier and use it to correct rewards during subsequent test-time adaptation. By improving reward accuracy and thereby model performance, TRUER consistently outperforms naive TTRL in both metrics across all evaluated configurations.

</details>


### [74] [Weighted-AoI-Driven UAV Trajectory and Reporting for Dynamic CKM Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13726)
*Zeyu Zhao,Yueling Che,Sheng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出W-AoI调度指标与轻量贪心轨迹策略，实现无人机集群对机载二维平面动态CKM的连续在线跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有信道知识图（CKM）构建假设环境准静态，当信道快速去相关时失效；且未测量小区的误差不可观测，使长期误差最小化问题难以求解。

Method: 将问题建模为无人机移动性与上报约束下的长期误差最小化；提出度量驱动的调度方法W-AoI（加权信息年龄），把精确时间陈旧度与2D U-Net预测的空间不确定性相结合；证明top-K W-AoI选择可最大化单时隙误差下降上界；据此设计融合沿路径连续感知的轻量贪心轨迹策略。

Result: 仿真中稳态误差较仅AoI与FIFO基线分别降低3.8 dB²和4.8 dB²，并接近理想oracle性能。

Conclusion: W-AoI为动态CKM在线跟踪提供了有理论保证的调度准则，验证了时空联合不确定度度量的有效性，可在轻量计算下逼近oracle。

Abstract: Existing channel knowledge map (CKM) construction assumes quasi-static environments, failing when channels rapidly decorrelate. This paper investigates continuous online dynamic CKM tracking over an airborne 2D plane via a UAV fleet. We formulate a long-term error minimization problem under UAV mobility and reporting constraints. This problem, however, is intractable with unobservable errors for unmeasured cells. To tackle this, we propose a metric-driven scheduling approach featuring the Weighted-Age of Information (W-AoI). W-AoI evaluates cells by combining exact temporal staleness with spatial uncertainty predicted by a 2D U-Net. We prove that top-$K$ W-AoI selection maximizes the one-slot error reduction bound. Guided by this guarantee, we develop a lightweight greedy trajectory policy incorporating continuous along-path sensing. Simulations demonstrate our method reduces steady-state error by 3.8 dB$^2$ and 4.8 dB$^2$ over AoI-only and FIFO baselines, closely approaching an idealized oracle.

</details>


### [75] [Deterministic, Reproducible Inference in Exact Integer Arithmetic, and Verifiable Distributed Inference as Its Consequence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13730)
*Sayed Amir Karim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: tritify 是一个开源运行时，将真实模型（decoder-only LLM、MoE 解码器、SAM 2.1）前向传播的整条运行时路径改为精确整数运算，浮点仅用于离线量化与验证参考，从而在异构硬件、CPU/GPU 及分布式机器间实现逐比特可复现的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化后的浮点推理不可复现：同一模型在不同硬件、编译器或线程数下会产生不同的比特结果。作者希望以整数算术路径消除这种环境依赖性，并为可验证的分布式推理提供基础。

Method: 构建开源运行时 tritify：浮点只保留在离线量化与浮点参考（用于验证）中，前向传播全部以精确整数算术执行；通过输出 SHA-256 复现、逐层保真度测量（OLMoE-1B-7B 十六层、106B MoE）、以及跨指令集/CPU-GPU/联网机器的分片执行来进行验证。

Result: (1) 整数路径逐比特一致，跨运行、跨 CPU ISA、跨 CPU/GPU 均成立，并以输出 SHA-256 复现；(2) 在测试模型与输入上复现浮点参考的决策，数值例外被量化，十六层逐层无保真度断崖；(3) 计算可跨指令集、CPU/GPU 与联网机器切分而不影响输出比特——GLM-4.5-Air（106,852,251,264 参数、427 GB 整数码）在三台均无法容纳其整数码的机器上生成答案，与单机参考逐比特一致。

Conclusion: 结论是可验证的分布式推理：不可信服务器或设备集群运行模型，而开放标量 oracle 重新推导出精确字节；对不诚实服务器的保证为未来工作。作者明确不主张单设备加速、浮点中间值的比特相等，也不主张机密性。

Abstract: Optimized floating-point inference is not reproducible across environments: the same model on different hardware, compilers or thread counts can produce different bits. We present tritify, an open-source runtime that executes the forward pass of real models -- decoder-only language models, Mixture-of-Experts decoders, and the Segment Anything 2.1 image/video stack -- with the entire run-time path in exact integer arithmetic; floating point is confined to offline quantization and the float reference used for verification. We make three separate claims and verify each. (1) Cross-hardware reproducibility: the integer path produces bit-identical output run to run, across CPU instruction-set architectures, and across CPU/GPU, carried by a reproducing SHA-256 of the output. (2) Decision fidelity: the integer path reproduces the float reference's decision on the models and inputs tested, with the numeric exceptions measured; per-layer fidelity through sixteen layers of OLMoE-1B-7B and of a 106-billion-parameter MoE shows no fidelity cliff. (3) Location independence: a model's computation can be split across instruction sets, CPU and GPU, and networked machines with zero effect on the output bits. GLM-4.5-Air (106,852,251,264 parameters, 427 GB of integer codes) generated its answer across three machines, none of which can hold its integer codes in RAM, bit-identically to a single-host reference. The consequence is verifiable distributed inference: an untrusted server or a fleet of devices runs the model while an open scalar oracle re-derives the exact bytes; a guarantee against a dishonest server is future work. We do not claim a per-device speed-up, bit-equality of floating-valued intermediates, or confidentiality. Code: https://github.com/pelloran-inc/tritify

</details>


### [76] [Plug-and-Play ADMM for Inverse Problems with Flow Matching Denoiser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13742)
*Dibyanshu Kumar,Magda Gregorova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于ADMM的PnP框架以整合流匹配先验，并引入轨迹校正机制，提升反问题重建质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PnP方法中数据保真更新会把迭代点推离流轨迹，导致测量模型与生成先验之间一致性难以维持。

Method: 构建ADMM式PnP框架，通过辅助变量与对偶更新强化测量模型与先验的一致性；并加入轨迹校正机制，用确定性与随机投影组合恢复与流的轨迹一致性。

Result: 实现更合理的流先验集成，获得高质量重建，同时降低计算开销。

Conclusion: ADMM式一致性与轨迹校正能在PnP中更原则性地使用流匹配先验，兼顾重建质量与计算效率。

Abstract: Plug-and-Play (PnP) methods with generative priors have achieved strong performance in inverse problems, with recent flow matching models enabling fast and scalable reconstruction. However, a key challenge in PnP methods is maintaining consistency between data fidelity updates and the learned generative prior, as data-consistency steps can move iterates away from the flow trajectory. We propose an ADMM-based PnP framework for flow matching priors that enforces stronger consistency between the measurement model and the prior through auxiliary variables and dual updates. In addition, we introduce a trajectory correction mechanism that restores consistency with the flow via a combination of deterministic and stochastic projections. Our method provides a more principled integration of flow priors within PnP frameworks, achieving high-quality reconstructions with reduced computational cost.

</details>


### [77] [When Covariance Is Not Enough: Kernel-Based Regularization for Single-Cell Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13748)
*Korbinian Traeuble,Edward de Brouwer,Archit Verma,Doron Haviv,Kipper Fletez-Brant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出线性交叉协方差惩罚无法在存在非线性交互时充分解耦已知/未知因子，提出在概念瓶颈生成模型中加入归一化希尔伯特-施密特独立性准则（NHSIC）的非线性核判据，从而更忠实地实现单细胞反事实编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞反事实编辑（预测某因子改变后细胞的样子）可用于实验不可行的in-silico筛选，但前提是编辑须对个体忠实而非仅对条件忠实；概念瓶颈生成模型通过将细胞表示分解为已知与未知因子使干预效应可解释，而既有方法仅用线性交叉协方差惩罚进行解耦。

Method: 先以受控编辑、已知非线性协变量效应的仿真实验证明线性交叉因子惩罚的不足；再在概念瓶颈生成模型的目标中加入非线性核判据——归一化希尔伯特-施密特独立性准则（NHSIC）以解耦已知与未知维度；最后将该非线性概念瓶颈模型应用于复杂单细胞RNA-seq数据集。

Result: 仿真数据表明一阶（线性）判据在非线性交互下不能完全分离信息，而加入NHSIC后能够恢复真实的干预效应；在真实scRNA-seq数据上，非线性概念瓶颈模型提升了在不同组织环境下生成细胞反事实表达谱的能力。

Conclusion: 解耦概念瓶颈中的已知与未知因子需采用能捕捉高阶依赖的非线性独立性判据；NHSIC型非线性概念瓶颈模型为个体层面忠实的单细胞反事实编辑提供了更有效的手段。

Abstract: Counterfactual editing of single cells, predicting how an observed cell would look had one factor been different, enables in-silico screening where experiments are infeasible, but is useful only if the edit is faithful to the individual and not merely to the condition. Concept bottleneck generative models make the effects of interventions interpretable by decomposing a cell's representation into known and unknown factors. Previous work uses a linear cross-covariance penalty to disentangle known and unknown dimensions. We demonstrate that this first-order criterion does not fully separate information when non-linear interactions are present. We first demonstrate the inadequacy of linear cross-factor penalization with simulation experiments with controlled edits and known non-linear covariate effects. We then demonstrate that adding a nonlinear kernel criterion, the normalized Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion, is able to recover the true intervention effects on simulation data. Finally, we apply the non-linear concept bottleneck model to a complex single-cell RNA-seq dataset and find that it improves the ability to generate counterfactual expression profiles of cells under different tissue environments.

</details>


### [78] [Mixed Signals: Weight-Space Programming for EEG and ECG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13758)
*Dara Varam,Taraneh Aminosharieh Najafi,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验"权重空间编程"（让语言模型直接读取并编辑神经网络权重）在脑电(EEG)与心电(ECG)等生物信号模型上的适用性，发现前沿模型可在不知情的情况下定位并修复植入的特定缺陷，甚至仅凭架构、任务和少量受试者从零写出全部权重；用6倍更少的受试者即达到梯度下降训练的精度，且优于经典手工EEG流程，但仍存在无法改进已训练良好模型、无法仅凭权重识别任务等局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重空间编程使模型可通过"推理"而非梯度下降来完成训练或调试，此前仅在博弈网络、语言模型特定故障及扩散模型上验证。生物信号模型（EEG/ECG）通常依赖大量受试者数据与领域手工特征工程，数据标注昂贵、跨受试者泛化困难，作者希望检验权重空间编程在该领域是否同样有效，并探讨其在边缘设备、可穿戴小模型上的潜力。

Method: 以EEG和ECG训练的网络为对象开展实验。实验一：在生物信号模型中人为植入特定故障，考察前沿语言模型能否在未被说明故障内容的情况下定位并调试修复。实验二：仅给出网络架构、任务描述和极少量训练受试者，要求语言模型从零直接写出网络的全部权重，不使用任何梯度下降步骤。对比基线包括标准梯度下降训练、以及经典手工EEG流程（alpha波段功率；CSP＋LDA）。同时分析方法的局限性。

Result: 语言模型能够在不被告知故障内容的前提下调试生物信号模型中的特定植入缺陷；在部分实验中，仅靠推理即可用6倍更少的训练受试者达到标准梯度下降训练的水平；由模型生成的网络在仅使用相同5名受试者构建时，性能高于经典手工EEG流程（alpha波段功率、CSP＋LDA）。局限在于：无法提升已训练良好的分类模型，且不能仅凭权重可靠识别网络所训练的任务。

Conclusion: 权重空间编程对小型网络（如部署在边缘设备与可穿戴设备上的模型）具有实用价值，可减少对受试者数据的依赖并替代部分手工特征工程；但该方法在改进成熟模型、任务识别等方面仍有不足，距离实际应用尚需进一步研究。

Abstract: Weight-space programming allows language models to read a trained neural network's weights and edit them. This opens the door to ``training'' by reasoning rather than through gradient descent. Prior work has demonstrated its functionality and promise for game-playing networks and fixing specific failures in language models, even extending to diffusion models. In this work, we present its applicability to models trained on biosignal data, specifically EEG and ECG data. We show that frontier models are not only able to debug specific faults planted in biosignal-based models without being told what these faults are, but are even capable of writing all the weights of a network from scratch, given only the architecture, the task, and a minimal set of training subjects. In particular, in one set of experiments, a language model was able to match standard gradient-descent training using $6\times$ fewer training subjects, through reasoning alone and without any gradient descent step. Networks written by Claude Opus 5.5 also scored higher than classic hand-crafted EEG pipelines (alpha band power, and common spatial patterns with linear discriminant analysis) when built from the same 5 subjects. We also highlight some of the limitations, which include the inability of language models to improve already well-trained classification models, and their incapacity to reliably recognize, from the weights alone, the task a network was trained on. Overall, our results indicate that weight-space programming can be useful for small networks, such as those deployed on edge devices and wearables, but the concept still requires further investigation before it can be used in practice.

</details>


### [79] [Beyond Interaction Scores: Selecting Feature Interactions via Independent Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13765)
*Jincheng Gao,Shuo Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LENS是一个模型无关的特征交互选择框架，通过独立复制（两个同算法模型、不相交数据、仅用预测访问）选出可复现的特征交互，并在多重检验校正下仅用两次模型拟合完成筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互得分高可能只是拟合噪声而非可复现模式；现有排序法把截断交给用户，检验法需反复重拟合或假设联合分布，缺乏既有阈值又有可复现性保证的方法。

Method: 用相同学习算法在不相交数据上训练两个模型，仅通过预测访问；对每个特征对，从联合效应面移除主效应以隔离交互模式，按强度排序，并检验第二个模型是否在有意义强度上复现；用单次多重检验校正决定保留哪些对，需两次拟合。理论上证明复制比例单独使用可保留两拟合共享的任意弱模式，加入强度下限可恢复选择一致性，并刻画Friedman H²中归一化何时反转交互排序。

Result: 16个合成设计上匹配TreeSHAP等领先模型特定排序法，恢复91%实质交互（knockoff-based DIAMOND为45%）且无假对；1225候选对且无交互时假发现显著更少；法国车险数据上识别出精算文献记录的交互并恢复全对90%偏差增益；两个信用数据上仅选少量对且预测性能持平或略优于加性基线。

Conclusion: LENS提供了模型无关、计算经济且带统计保证的交互选择方案，兼顾可复现性筛选与可解释性，在合成与真实数据上优于knockoff类方法并保持预测性能。

Abstract: Learning feature interactions is essential for accurate prediction and interpretable modeling, yet a high interaction score may reflect fitting noise rather than a reproducible pattern. Ranking methods score feature pairs but leave the cutoff to the user; testing methods set the cutoff but rely on repeated model refits or a model of the joint feature distribution. We present LENS, a model-agnostic framework for selecting feature interactions through independent replication. LENS trains two models with the same learning algorithm on disjoint data and accesses them only through predictions. For each feature pair, it removes main effects from the joint-effect surface to isolate an interaction pattern, ranks pairs by its strength, and tests whether the second model reproduces it at a meaningful strength. A single multiple-testing correction determines which pairs to retain, using only two model fits regardless of the number of features. We prove that a replication ratio alone can retain arbitrarily weak patterns shared by both fits, whereas a strength floor restores selection consistency, and we characterize when normalization in Friedman's $H^2$ reverses interaction rankings. On 16 synthetic designs, LENS matches leading model-specific ranking methods (e.g., TreeSHAP) and recovers 91% of material interactions, against 45% for the knockoff-based DIAMOND, without selecting false pairs; with 1,225 candidate pairs and no interactions, it makes substantially fewer false discoveries. On large-scale French motor insurance data, it identifies interactions documented in the actuarial literature and recovers 90% of the deviance gain obtained using all pairs. On two credit datasets, LENS selects few pairs while maintaining or slightly improving predictive performance over the additive baseline.

</details>


### [80] [Omni-JEV: Text and Dual-Stream Speech Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13777)
*Shengzhou Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Omni-JEV，将全双工对话中的交互动作决策形式化为在分段端点上的五类动作概率预测任务，采用对齐语音-语音理解骨干网络与双语弱监督（交叉熵微调+RLCD 强化学习），音频与文本测试准确率分别达 84.08% 与 96.55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户话语本身无法唯一决定助手的下一步动作，其后果依赖助手的响应状态与交互历史；语音活动、话语完整性与打断意图等证据需通过显式动作策略才能映射为响应决策。

Method: 在收集的分段端点处形式化任务；构建 Omni-JEV，将文本或带角色索引的用户语音与助手参考语音映射为五类交互动作的概率分布；采用对齐的语音到语音理解骨干分别编码声学角色，并与文本输入共享候选读出；使用双语弱监督先做交叉熵微调，再做面向概率敏感决策的 RLCD 强化学习，并对比同一监督初始化、相同训练曝光下 Gaussian-logit 与 categorical Choice RLCD 两种方案。

Result: Gaussian Omni-JEV 在测试集上取得音频 84.08%、文本 96.55% 的准确率；两类 RLCD 在分类质量与概率质量之间存在不同权衡。

Conclusion: 该统一的动作接口可支撑全双工语音助手在何时响应、保持持续说话、打断或恢复被打断响应等决策，为全双工交互策略提供可复用的建模范式。

Abstract: A user utterance does not uniquely determine the next action in full-duplex dialogue: its consequences depend on the assistant's response state and interaction history. Speech activity, utterance completion, and interruption intent provide related evidence, whose implications for a response require an explicit action policy. We formulate this task at collected-segment endpoints and introduce Omni-JEV, which maps text or role-indexed user and assistant-reference speech to probabilities over five interaction actions. An aligned speech-to-speech understanding backbone encodes the acoustic roles separately and shares its candidate readout with text inputs. Bilingual weak supervision supports cross-entropy fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning for probability-sensitive decisions (RLCD). We compare Gaussian-logit and categorical Choice RLCD from the same supervised initialization under matched training exposure, observing different trade-offs between classification and probability quality. Gaussian Omni-JEV achieves test accuracies of 84.08% for audio and 96.55% for text. This common action interface supports decisions about when a full-duplex voice assistant should respond, preserve ongoing speech, interrupt, or resume an interrupted response.

</details>


### [81] [AnchorMTS: Multi-View Signal Representation and Forecast-Anchored Fusion for Long-Term Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13794)
*Gao Wu,Tingting Xu,Changning Wu,Tengfei Long,Yong Liu,Jiabin Jin,Kexin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AnchorMTS 从观测窗口自对齐生成视觉与文本视图，仅用于精修数值预测；经无参数的输出投影把位移限制在逐变量界内。在六个基准和四个预测步长上取得最佳宏平均 MSE/MAE（0.336/0.367），消融与投影统计显示多视图证据有效且影响可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期时间序列预测器通常只优化单一数值表征，忽略了同一段历史中蕴含的互补几何、循环与语义结构信息。

Method: 提出 AnchorMTS：由每个观测窗口派生自对齐的视觉视图与文本视图，并仅用于精修完整的数值预测。可训练的证据路径生成候选预测，无参数输出投影对其相对数值锚点的位移施加逐变量精确边界约束。

Result: 在六个基准、四个预测步长上，宏平均 MSE/MAE 为 0.336/0.367，在七个有监督基线中最优；对逐单元基线包络在 24 个 MSE 设置中胜出 18 个。移除辅助路径后宏 MSE 升至 0.340。投影使 95.8% 的候选精修保持不变且零边界违规。

Conclusion: 互补的多视图证据能够提升预测性能，同时其对最终输出的影响被显式约束与控制。

Abstract: Long-term time-series forecasters typically optimize a single numerical representation, even though the same history exposes complementary geometry, recurrence, and semantic structure. We propose AnchorMTS, which derives self-aligned visual and textual views from each observed window and uses them only to refine a complete numerical forecast. A trainable evidence path produces a candidate forecast, while a parameter-free output projection enforces an exact per-variable bound on its displacement from the numerical anchor. Across six benchmarks and four horizons, AnchorMTS achieves the best macro MSE/MAE (0.336/0.367) among seven supervised baselines and wins 18 of 24 MSE settings against the cellwise baseline envelope; removing the auxiliary path raises macro MSE to 0.340. The projection leaves 95.8% of candidate refinements unchanged while yielding zero bound violations, showing that complementary multi-view evidence can improve forecasting while its final influence remains explicitly controlled.

</details>


### [82] [JAX-WAVE: A Differentiable Physics-Supervised Framework for Inverse Wave Design in Heterogeneous Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13826)
*Honghui Du,Binyao Guo,Yushu Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JAX-WAVE：用共享神经网络将期望终端波场与已知波速图直接映射为有界初始压力，实现可摊销的逆波设计；训练经可微FDTD求解器，无需优化控制标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非均匀介质中设计指定波场，需要能补偿介质传播响应的初始压力分布；重复数值反演需对每个目标与环境重新求解，代价高昂，缺乏可复用的逆设计流程。

Method: 构建共享神经网络，输入期望终端场与波速图，输出有界初始压力；通过可微FDTD求解器以物理设计目标端到端训练。利用线性传播与对称压力界推导正则最优解的精确符号反演关系，并用共享权重投影强制该关系、保持可行性。同时刻画离散传播算子及其加权伴随，以连接学习、数值反演与最优性评估。

Result: 独立确认集上，三个模型对未见材料分别达到25.24%（几何目标）与4.77%（可达目标）的误差；同容量对比显示符号参数化提升重构效果；在5 ms中位在线预算内，学习映射相对加速数值反演将平均误差降低45%，更长数值求解可获得更高精度。

Conclusion: 结果建立了可复用的逆设计流程，并表明其精度与计算价值取决于目标结构与查询负载。

Abstract: Designing a prescribed wave field in a heterogeneous medium requires an initial pressure distribution that compensates for the medium's propagation response. Repeated numerical inversion solves this task afresh for each target and environment. We introduce JAX-WAVE for amortized inverse wave design: a shared neural network maps desired terminal fields and known wave-speed maps directly to bounded initial pressures. Training through a differentiable finite-difference time-domain solver uses the physical design objective without requiring optimized control labels. Linear propagation and symmetric pressure bounds imply an exact sign-reversal relation for the regularized optimum. A shared-weight projection enforces this relation, preserves feasibility, and satisfies an exact identity for the paired-sign objective. We characterize the discrete propagator and its weighted adjoint to connect learning, numerical inversion, and optimality assessment. On an independent confirmation set, three trained models achieve unseen-material errors of 25.24% for geometric targets and 4.77% for reachable targets. Matched-capacity comparisons show improved reconstruction with the sign parameterization. Within a 5 ms median online budget, the learned map reduces mean error by 45% relative to accelerated numerical inversion on the heterogeneous benchmark; longer numerical solves recover greater accuracy. The results establish a reusable inverse-design procedure and show how its accuracy and computational value depend on target structure and query workload.

</details>


### [83] [Where to Approximate in Neurosymbolic Inference?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13862)
*Samy Badreddine,Emile van Krieken,Luciano Serafini,Antonio Vergari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将概率神经符号方法中的加权模型计数（WMC）统一为"三步自底向上近似编译流水线"，并用张量列（tensor train）分解实例化其中两步，通过精确相乘近似因子再以 SVD 或插值方案重新压缩乘积，得到在数独、算术推理与场景理解六项任务上精度提升数量级的新流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确 WMC 是 #P-hard 问题且扩展性差，现有方法普遍退而求其次采用近似；但近似预算应如何分配到编译流程的各个环节缺乏系统认识与统一框架。

Method: 把已有近似方法统一抽象为三步自底向上的近似编译流水线，借此定位近似预算的分配位置。受张量网络启发，用张量列分解实例化其中两步，并推导出新的流水线：对近似因子进行精确乘法，再通过基于 SVD 或基于插值的重压缩方案压缩其乘积，控制中间表示规模。

Result: 在数独、算术推理和场景理解共六项任务上，随问题规模增大，所提流水线给出的 WMC 估计比先前方法精度高出数个数量级；在依赖符号因子间紧密交互的推理任务上，下游学习效果显著更强，而粗糙近似会忽略这些依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为困难概率推理任务的高效近似编译开辟了新的设计空间，表明把张量分解与精确乘法、重压缩相结合是提升 WMC 近似质量的有效途径。

Abstract: Probabilistic neurosymbolic methods rely on weighted model counting (WMC) to combine neural predictors with symbolic constraints. Exact WMC is #P-hard and typically scales poorly, so many methods resort to approximations. By unifying existing approaches under a three-step bottom-up approximate compilation pipeline, we study where approximation budget is best allocated. Inspired by tensor networks, we instantiate two steps with tensor train decompositions and derive new pipelines that exactly multiply approximated factors and recompress their products via SVD- or interpolation-based schemes. Across six tasks in Sudoku, arithmetic reasoning, and scene understanding, and across increasing problem sizes, our pipelines produce WMC estimates orders-of-magnitude more accurate than prior methods. This translates into stronger downstream learning on tasks where reasoning relies on tight interactions between symbolic factors, where cruder approximations ignore these dependencies. Our pipelines open novel design space for effective approximate compilation of challenging probabilistic reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [84] [Mechanistic Interpretability of Deep Neural Networks: Methods, Frameworks, and Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13868)
*Lorenzo Cirillo,Irene Amerini,Andrea Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，概述面向深度神经网络因果推断的机制可解释性研究进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络广泛用于图像、音频、语言等信号任务，但内部机制难以解释；层间激活是结构化信号，单个神经元常表征多个概念，形成大量相互依赖关系，因此需要可解释手段揭示内部机制。

Method: 综述性方法：梳理机制可解释性（mechanistic interpretability）的思路，即识别激活对特定行为具有因果责任的组件，并围绕深度网络中的因果推断展开归纳。

Result: 摘要仅说明综述覆盖机制可解释性在深度网络因果分析方面的进展，未给出具体实验结果或量化结论。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性为理解深度网络的内部因果机制提供了系统视角，本文对该方向的研究进展进行了总结与展望。

Abstract: Deep neural networks underpin fundamental tasks in a wide range of signal domains, such as image, audio, and language processing. However, their internal mechanisms are notably difficult to interpret. Internal activations are structured signals transformed across layers that may encode a multitude of interdependent relationships, as individual neurons frequently represent multiple underlying concepts. Mechanistic interpretability aims to uncover the internal mechanisms of neural networks by identifying components whose activations are causally responsible for specific behaviors. In such a context, this article provides an overview of advances in mechanistic interpretability for the analysis of causal inference in deep neural networks.

</details>


### [85] [Are Causal Neuron Circuits Unique and Transferable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13885)
*Ömer Tekin Yavuz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文以神经元级因果干预为核心，系统检验"当一组神经元被干预导致模型答案改变时，这一组中究竟有多少是真正必要的"。通过对12个MLP神经元的全部4096个子集进行测量，作者发现完整组极少必要，少数神经元即可翻转答案，且常存在多个互不相交的充分子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性演示（如Qwen3-32B中移除18个神经元使"Scar"变为"Simba"）只证明某组神经元"有影响"，却未说明该组中多少是必要的、是否存在替代组、效应能否泛化到其他输入。作者因此提出核心问题：干预一组神经元改变答案时，该组中有多少是真正必要的。

Method: 对每组选定的12个MLP神经元穷举全部4,096个子集进行干预。任务包括：(1) 二选一首都任务（Qwen3-0.6B），比较零消融（zero-ablating）与跨问题激活替换；(2) 两跳关系链任务，将第一跳问题中的12个神经元激活patch到第二跳，观察答案是否转向桥接实体，并在Qwen3-1.7B、Llama-3.2-1B、Gemma-3-1B上重复，随机神经元组为对照；同时检验重述（rephrasing）与其他链上的迁移性。

Result: 首任务中，零消融12个选中神经元使答案margin下降27%，随机神经元几乎无影响；但用其他问题的相同神经元激活替换几乎无影响，说明其激活跨问题近乎恒定，无证据携带问题特异内容。两跳链上，从第一跳patch 12个神经元使贪心答案以桥接实体开头：Qwen3-1.7B为9/11，Llama-3.2-1B为9/11，Gemma-3-1B为8/10，随机神经元从不产生此效应。完整组极少必要：少数神经元常已足够，许多链中存在互不相交的子集各自单独翻转答案，探索性分析显示这多发生在模型置信度较低时。组效应可迁移到同一链的重述，但在其他测试链上明显减弱。

Conclusion: 粗粒度"组干预"演示高估了组的必要性：效应由少数神经元及多个冗余子集承载，且依赖模型置信度、跨链迁移有限。研究限于神经元级因果干预与替代子集分析，不重构电路的完整连接结构。

Abstract: Interpretability demonstrations often show that intervening on a group of neurons changes a model's answer. A recent example removes an 18-neuron group in Qwen3-32B and the model answers "Simba" instead of "Scar" to a two-hop Lion King riddle. Such a demonstration establishes that the group matters, but not how much of it is necessary, whether other groups could do the same, or whether the effect carries over to other inputs. We ask: when intervening on a group of neurons changes a model's answer, how much of that group is actually necessary? For every selected group we measure all 4,096 subsets of 12 MLP neurons. On a two-alternative capital task (Qwen3-0.6B), zero-ablating 12 selected neurons lowers the answer margin by 27% while random neurons barely change it, yet replacing the same neurons with their values from other questions also barely changes it: their activations are nearly constant across questions, and we find no evidence that they carry question-specific content. On two-hop relation chains, patching 12 neurons with activations from the first-hop question shifts the answer to the bridge entity (from Scar to Simba): the greedy answer starts with the bridge in 9/11 chains for Qwen3-1.7B, 9/11 for Llama-3.2-1B and 8/10 for Gemma-3-1B; random neurons never do. The full group is rarely necessary: a few neurons often suffice, and in many chains disjoint subsets each flip the answer on their own, in an exploratory analysis most often when the model is less confident. Group effects transfer to rephrasings of the same chain but are much weaker on other tested chains. We study neuron-level causal interventions and alternative subsets; we do not recover the full connectivity of circuits.

</details>


### [86] [Conformal Risk Control for QoT-Aware Lightpath Feasibility Under Technology-Driven Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13893)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Omran Ayoub,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种事后共形风险控制（CRC）框架，在保持预训练AI/ML回归器不变的前提下，为回归预测的不确定性带提供有限样本的误覆盖率保证，并将其映射为光网络中QoT感知光路可行性决策的策略规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI/ML回归模型在光网络运营决策中仅给出点预测，缺乏统计意义上可控的可靠性信息；且光纤、放大器、交换升级等引起的域漂移会使源域标定的不确定性估计失效。

Method: 采用事后共形风险控制（CRC）：固定预训练回归器，构建具有有限样本误覆盖率保证的不确定性带；引入特征漂移贡献分数识别在技术过渡中与QoT关系变化最大的光路特征；设计两种策略规则将不确定性带转化为可行性判定，供运营者在保守拒绝与激进接受之间调节。在CONUS拓扑上的开放SDM QoT数据集验证，覆盖17次域漂移、10组校准-测试划分共170次试验。

Result: 目标覆盖率90%时，所提方法在全部170次试验中均达到目标，而仅用源域的量化回归（QR）基线无一达标；在严重漂移下，效率导向（EO）策略将零样本可行性决策准确率由约85%提升至99%以上，接近迁移学习（TL）策略且无需重训练。

Conclusion: 该框架为基于回归的光网络决策支持提供了不确定性带误覆盖率的有限样本控制，可推广至QoT感知光路可行性评估之外的其他应用场景。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence and machine learning (AI/ML)-based regression models support operational decision-making in optical networks but typically expose only point predictions, which carry no statistically controlled information about their reliability. This paper proposes a post-hoc conformal risk control (CRC) framework that keeps a pretrained AI/ML regressor fixed, provides a finite-sample guarantee on uncertainty band miscoverage, and maps the band to operational decisions through policy rules. The guarantee applies to interval miscoverage, while decision-error rates are evaluated empirically. We instantiate the framework for quality of transmission (QoT)-aware lightpath (LP) feasibility assessment, in which a regressor predicts optical signal-to-noise ratio (OSNR) under domain shifts caused by fiber, amplifier, and switching upgrades. Feature shift contribution scores identify the LP features whose relation to QoT changes the most across a technology transition, and two policies translate the bands into feasibility decisions, allowing operators to tune the trade-off between conservatively rejecting or aggressively accepting LP realizations. Experiments on open space-division multiplexing (SDM) QoT datasets over the CONUS topology show that, for a target coverage of 90%, the uncertainty bands meet the target in all 170 trials spanning 17 domain shifts and ten calibration-test partitions, whereas a source-only quantile regression (QR) baseline meets it in none. Under severe shifts, the efficiency-oriented (EO) policy empirically improves zero-shot (ZS) feasibility-decision accuracy from about 85% to above 99%, approaching a transfer learning (TL) policy while avoiding retraining. The framework provides finite-sample control of uncertainty band miscoverage for regression-based optical network decision support, with potential applications beyond QoT-aware LP feasibility assessment.

</details>


### [87] [Target Selection with a Disappearing-Stimulus Mixed-Reality P300 Interface: Feasibility and Object-Distinguishable Versus Uniform Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13906)
*Runqi Pan,Takuto Niinuma,Yunshan Huang,Masato Sugino,Kenta Shimba,Kiyoshi Kotani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在头戴式MR显示上，即使提示刺激在闪烁间消失，纯粹的P300范式的目标选择仍可行：48个严格留出试次中解码正确18次（随机期望1.9，p<1e-12）；但为每个提示赋予稳定的颜色-形状身份相比统一球体并未带来可检测的提升（两种条件下均为9/24，AUC 0.609 vs 0.585）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MR P300接口可直接在用户真实环境中登记可选目标（如轮椅目的地），但当提示在闪烁之间消失时，用户须在目标不可见的情况下保持意图。论文旨在检验该类接口能否支持目标选择，并考察为每个提示赋予稳定的颜色-形状身份是否能改善表现。

Method: 6名健康被试在头戴式MR显示上完成5×5行列任务的4个试次×每种提示条件，采集Fz、Cz、Pz脑电。使用收缩线性判别分析（shrinkage LDA），并采用嵌套验证：完整试次在类别平衡、超参数选择与拟合中被整体留出。

Result: 两种条件均诱发出顶区目标相关正波。48个留出试次中解码单元正确18次（随机期望1.9，二项检验p<1e-12），且3名被试单独亦显著。提示格式无可检测差异：完整目标正确率两条件均为9/24（配对差异0.0个百分点，95% CI [-37, 37]），留出AUC球体0.609、物体线索0.585。

Conclusion: 消失刺激条件下的MR P300选择在严格试次级评估下是可行的；但物体身份带来的收益（若有）过小，本规模试点无法分辨，需更大样本进一步检验。

Abstract: Mixed-reality (MR) P300 interfaces can register selectable destinations, such as wheelchair goals, directly in the user's surroundings. When the cues disappear between flashes, however, the user must retain an intended target that is no longer visible. We tested whether such an interface supports target selection and whether giving each cue a stable colour-shape identity improves it compared with visually uniform spheres. Six healthy adults completed four trials in each cue condition of a 5x5 row/column task on a head-mounted MR display, with EEG from Fz, Cz, and Pz. Shrinkage linear discriminant analysis was evaluated with nested validation in which complete trials were held out from class balancing, hyperparameter selection, and fitting. Both conditions evoked a parietal target-related positivity. The decoded cell was correct in 18 of 48 held-out trials, against 1.9 expected by chance (binomial p < 1e-12), and in three participants individually. Cue format made no detectable difference: complete-target accuracy was 9/24 in both conditions (paired difference 0.0 percentage points, 95% CI [-37, 37]), and held-out AUC was 0.609 for spheres and 0.585 for object cues. Disappearing-stimulus MR P300 selection is therefore feasible under strict trial-level evaluation, but the benefit of object identity, if any, is too small to resolve in a pilot of this size.

</details>


### [88] [Learning from First Discrepancies with Joint Reset and Pilot Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13913)
*Michal Čertík*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper studies how many first-error-stopping runs (each testing a pre-chosen pilot hypotheses on a fresh record of at most D queries) suffice to learn a binary class of VC dimension at most d. A learner is given using Õ((d+1)[D(f+ε)+r]/ε²) runs and Õ(D(1+f/ε)) distinct pilots per history, returning a class member plus an ε-wide interval that with probability ≥1−δ covers both its risk and the optimal risk; matching lower bounds show each order is necessary.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard active/statistical learning assumes queries with correct labels are available throughout, but in the "stopping-at-first-error" setting a run reveals only the queries and labels up to and including its first discrepancy, and nothing after. The paper asks whether such severely truncated data can still support risk estimation and learning, and at what statistical cost—characterizing the number of runs and of distinct pilots required as a function of the class complexity d, record length D, and the infima f of discrepancy probabilities and r of risks.

Method: Model: each run tests a pilot hypothesis chosen in advance from a fixed binary class of VC dimension ≤d on a fresh record of ≤D queries from one unknown distribution independent of the pilot; the terminal (post-error) information is shown only if no discrepancy occurs, and a pre-fixed bounded loss is attached to the stopping position. The learner enumerates label patterns of the class on displayed queries (possibly exponential time), and the analysis—under standard measurability conditions—uses exponential weights over the class, a gain computable from stopped displays, a sparse depth-robust graph for sampling pilots, and a harmonic-mean bound to control the risk interval.

Result: With infima of discrepancy probabilities ≤ f and of risks ≤ r, one learner uses Õ((d+1)[D(f+ε)+r]/ε²) runs and Õ(D(1+f/ε)) distinct pilots on every history, and outputs a class member together with an interval of width ≤ ε that, with probability ≥1−δ, contains both the output's risk and the optimal risk. Unknown ceilings and expected-cost variants are also treated. Lower bounds for finite classes show each order is necessary in the worst case (possibly on different instances), at positive dimension, small accuracy, and fixed confidence.

Conclusion: Truncated, first-error-stopping runs can support learning with essentially optimal statistical resources: the derived upper bounds on runs and distinct pilots are tight up to logarithmic factors in the worst case, the results concern statistical (not computational) resources since the learner may run in exponential time, and the framework extends to unknown ceilings and expected costs.

Abstract: A run that stops at its first wrong decision shows the queries and their correct labels up to and including that decision, and nothing after it. We study how many such runs are needed for learning. Each run tests a hypothesis, the pilot, chosen beforehand from a fixed binary class of VC dimension at most $d$, on a fresh record of at most $D$ queries drawn from one unknown distribution that does not depend on the pilot. The terminal information of a record is shown only if the pilot makes no discrepancy, and a bounded loss, fixed in advance, is attached to the position where a hypothesis stops. If the discrepancy probabilities of members of the class have infimum at most $f$ and their risks have infimum at most $r$, one learner uses $\widetilde O((d+1)[D(f+\varepsilon)+r]/\varepsilon^2)$ runs and $\widetilde O(D(1+f/\varepsilon))$ distinct pilots on every history. It returns a member of the class and an interval of width at most $\varepsilon$ containing, with probability at least $1-δ$, both the output's risk and the optimal risk. We also treat unknown ceilings and expected costs. Lower bounds for finite classes show that each order is necessary in the worst case, possibly on different instances, at positive dimension, small accuracy and fixed confidence. The learner enumerates label patterns of the class on displayed queries, possibly in exponential time; the results, under standard measurability conditions, concern statistical resources only. The proof combines a gain computable from stopped displays, exponential weights, a sparse depth-robust graph and a harmonic-mean bound.

</details>


### [89] [Dual-Core Tensor Wheel Decomposition with Structure-Cached Acceleration for Tensor Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13938)
*Wenchao Xie,Qingsong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出双核张量轮分解（DCTW），将原张量轮分解的高阶中心核分解为两个由共享潜键连接的低阶核，并结合结构缓存加速策略，避免大规模矩阵的显式构造与乘法，在高维数据补全与去噪任务中兼顾重构质量、效率与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始张量轮分解虽能刻画外环因子与高阶中心核之间的复杂相关性，但更新各组件需显式构造并操作大规模矩阵，中心核更新的计算与内存开销尤其高昂，限制了计算效率。

Method: 1）提出DCTW：将中心核分解为两个低阶核，通过共享潜键（latent bond）连接，每个核与不同的外环因子组耦合；2）提出结构缓存加速策略：避免显式大规模矩阵乘法，并缓存张量网络收缩过程中产生的中间张量，使其在分块更新间复用，从而减少冗余计算、避免大矩阵显式构造。

Result: 复杂度分析与实验表明：基于结构缓存的策略相较显式与隐式更新方案可加速优化，同时在秩增大时抑制峰值内存的增长；在彩色视频补全与鲁棒去噪实验中，DCTW在重构质量、计算效率与可扩展性上达到或优于代表性张量网络方法。

Conclusion: DCTW通过中心核的低阶分解与结构缓存加速机制，有效缓解了张量轮分解在大规模更新中的计算与内存瓶颈，是一种兼顾表达力与效率的高维数据表示与优化框架。

Abstract: Tensor wheel decomposition captures complex correlations in high-dimensional data through interactions between outer-ring factors and a high-order central core. However, updating these components requires the explicit construction and manipulation of large-scale matrices, which limits computational efficiency and incurs particularly high computational and memory costs for the central-core update. To address these limitations, we propose dual-core tensor wheel decomposition (DCTW), which factorizes the central core into two lower-order cores linked by a shared latent bond. Each core is coupled to a different group of outer-ring factors. We further propose a structure-cached acceleration strategy that avoids explicit large-scale matrix multi- plications and caches intermediate tensors generated during tensor network contractions for reuse across block updates. This reuse reduces redundant computation and avoids the explicit construction of large matrices. Complexity analysis and experiments show that the strategy based on structural caching accelerates optimization compared with explicit and implicit up- date schemes while limiting the increase in peak memory as the ranks grow. Experiments on color video completion and robust denoising show that DCTW matches or outperforms representative tensor network methods in reconstruction quality, computational efficiency, and scalability.

</details>


### [90] [PARDIG: Global Precipitation Estimates Learned from Sparse Satellite Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13941)
*Guy Shalev,Stephan Rasp,Boris Babenko,Andrew El-Kadi,Mohammed Alewi Hassen,Samier Merchant,Fred Zyda,Dominic Masters,Ferran Alet,Salva Rühling Cachay,Ilan Price,Akib Uddin,Nofar Peled Levi,Ben Gaiarin,Aaron Bell,Peter Battaglia,Alvaro Sanchez-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PARDIG 是一个基于球面图网格 Transformer 的人工智能框架，利用稠密多模态输入（大气状态分析与多光谱静止卫星影像）预测稀疏但高精度的星载雷达降水观测，可在 0.1°、15 分钟分辨率上生成近实时全球降水场，并在多区域雷达网与 3.6 万个地面站评估中全面优于 IMERG V07、MSWEP V3、ERA5 和 HRES。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球尺度降水估计对地球系统科学与灾害响应至关重要，但观测覆盖度与测量精度仍受限；现有产品在技巧、时延、地理覆盖、时间覆盖、时空分辨率等理想特性之间只能权衡取舍，缺乏能同时兼顾多目标的高质量近实时全球降水数据。

Method: 提出 PARDIG：基于球面图-网格（graph-mesh）Transformer 架构的 AI 框架。训练阶段以稠密多模态输入（大气状态分析场 + 多光谱静止卫星影像）为预测变量，以稀疏、高精度的星载雷达降水测量为监督目标；推理阶段将训练好的模型在全球范围内稠密应用，得到 0.1° 空间分辨率、15 分钟时间分辨率的完整近实时降水场（2016 年至今），并提供回溯至 1999 年的历史版本。

Result: 在美国、欧洲和日本三个独立雷达网络以及全球逾 36,000 个地面站的评估中，PARDIG 在不同降水阈值与区域上一致优于 IMERG V07（Final 与 Early）、MSWEP V3、ERA5 与 HRES，且改进幅度在极端强度上最大；极端降水事件个例分析表明其能成功刻画事件的空间结构与发生时段。改进还延伸至预报：全球天气模型 WeatherNext 3 在以 PARDIG 为预测目标训练后，降水预报技巧提升。

Conclusion: PARDIG 表明以稠密多模态输入融合稀疏高精度雷达监督的球面图网格 Transformer 方案，可同时实现高技巧、低时延、全球覆盖与高时空分辨率的降水估计，衍生历史与近实时数据集均以 CC BY 4.0 许可开放获取，并可作为下游天气预报模型的训练目标以提升降水预报能力。

Abstract: Estimating precipitation at the global scale is vital for Earth system science and disaster response, yet observational coverage and measurement precision remain constrained. Existing solutions provide options that trade off desirable traits such as skill, latency, geographical coverage, temporal coverage, and spatial and temporal resolution. We present PARDIG, an artificial intelligence framework based on a spherical graph-mesh transformer architecture that produces near-real-time global precipitation estimates. PARDIG is trained on dense multi-modal inputs, including atmospheric state analyses and multi-spectral geostationary satellite imagery, to predict sparse, high-precision precipitation measurements from spaceborne radar. At inference, the trained model is applied densely across the globe at $0.1^\circ$ spatial and 15-minute temporal resolution, producing complete near-real-time precipitation fields from 2016 to the present, alongside a historical variant extending the record back to 1999. Evaluation against three independent radar networks (over the US, Europe, and Japan) and against over 36,000 surface stations worldwide shows that PARDIG consistently outperforms IMERG V07 (Final and Early), MSWEP V3, ERA5, and HRES across precipitation thresholds and regions, with the largest improvements at extreme intensities. Case studies of extreme precipitation events demonstrate that PARDIG successfully captures their spatial structure and timing. The improvements also extend to forecasting: WeatherNext 3, a global weather model, produces more skillful precipitation forecasts when trained to predict PARDIG. Both the historical and near-real-time PARDIG datasets are openly available under a CC BY 4.0 license.

</details>


### [91] [Chem World: A Large-Scale Benchmark and Physics-Aware Framework for Trustworthy Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13945)
*Tianyou Bai,Huan Wang,Mingchen Gao,Tongrui Yang,Fangyue Lin,Pinze Ren,Zhenlin Zhao,Siming Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出大规模分子性质预测基准 Chem World（整合17个公开数据集、超80万条分子与化学体系实例、10个性质任务），并在此基础上提出物理感知组合建模方法 PACM，实验表明分子语言模型具备强可迁移表示能力，PACM 在多类化学体系中稳定提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子性质预测基准在数据集、性质类型与评测协议上高度碎片化，难以系统评估模型的泛化能力；同时缺少对多组分化学体系与领域物理约束的统一建模测试平台。

Method: 构建 Chem World 基准：统一整合17个公开数据集，覆盖10个性质任务轨道，涵盖单分子与多组分体系（含药物溶解度）。在此基础上提出 PACM（Physics-Aware Compositional Modeling），将分子表示与组成感知建模、领域特定物理约束相结合；并基于该基准训练药物性质预测 Agent。

Result: 大规模实验显示：分子语言模型能提供强且可迁移的分子表示；PACM 在多样化学体系的预测任务中持续带来性能提升。

Conclusion: Chem World 与 PACM 驱动的药物性质预测智能体为可泛化的化学建模提供了统一基础，可支撑 AI 辅助的生物医学研究与药物制剂开发。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction is fundamental to computational chemistry and drug formulation research, yet existing benchmarks remain fragmented across datasets, properties, and evaluation protocols, limiting systematic evaluation of model generalization. We introduce Chem World, a large-scale benchmark integrating 17 public datasets with over 800,000 molecular and chemical-system instances across 10 property tracks, covering single-molecule and multi-component systems, including drug solubility. Chem World provides a unified testbed for evaluating molecular representations and compositional modeling across diverse chemical properties. Building on the benchmark, we propose PACM (Physics-Aware Compositional Modeling), which combines molecular representations with composition-aware modeling and domain-specific physical constraints. Extensive experiments show that molecular language models provide strong and transferable representations, while PACM consistently improves prediction across diverse chemical systems. Chem World and our PACM-powered drug-property prediction Agent support generalizable chemical modeling and offer a foundation for AI-assisted biomedical research and drug formulation development.

</details>


### [92] [Geometry-Dependent Approximation and Stability Properties of Shallow ReLU$^k$ Networks on Bounded Euclidean Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13952)
*Yuqing Liu,Tong Mao,Jinchao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在有界欧氏区域上研究隐藏参数几何（以填充距离h与对径分离距离h̲刻画）对浅层ReLU^k网络的影响，给出依赖几何的逼近速率、饱和型下界、Gram与刚度矩阵谱估计、Bernstein与Marcinkiewicz–Zygmund不等式、离散最小二乘恢复及逆逼近等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层ReLU^k网络的逼近与稳定性不仅取决于网络宽度，还受隐藏参数（频率、方向、偏移）几何分布支配；因此需要量化参数几何如何同时决定逼近精度、系数表示的稳定性、离散恢复与收敛速率中所含的规律性信息。

Method: 采用光滑局部化技术与二进Fourier估计；以填充距离h与对径分离距离h̲参数化隐藏参数几何；对指定网络空间L_n^k推导逼近上界（利用目标至临界阶(d+2k+1)/2的规律性，在内子域Ω'上）与饱和度型下界（对更光滑的固定目标渐近成立，在较大区域Ω上）；进而建立几何相关的Gram矩阵与刚度矩阵谱估计、Bernstein与Marcinkiewicz–Zygmund型不等式，并研究基于函数值的离散最小二乘正则化恢复与逆逼近。

Result: 得到逼近估计：inf_{g_n∈L_n^k}‖f−g_n‖_{L²(Ω')} ≲ h^r‖f‖_{H^r(Ω)}，以及下界 inf_{g_n∈L_n^k}‖f−g_n‖_{L²(Ω)} ≳ h̲^{(d+2k+1)/2}‖f‖_{L²(Ω')}；上界利用临界阶之前的规律性，下界渐近作用于比该阶更光滑的固定目标。另获表明条件数与系数稳定性的几何相关谱估计、范数离散化与由函数值进行正则化恢复的结果，并在系数或分离条件下由逼近速率反推目标规律性。

Conclusion: 参数几何系统性地支配浅层ReLU^k网络的逼近精度、系数表示的稳定性、离散恢复能力以及收敛速率中所携带的规律性信息；h与h̲分别控制上逼近精度与饱和下界，共同刻画了网络空间的表达能力与数值稳定性权衡。

Abstract: On bounded Euclidean domains, we study how hidden-parameter geometry affects shallow ReLU$^k$ networks through results on approximation rates, saturation-type lower bounds, Gram and stiffness matrix spectra, Bernstein and Marcinkiewicz--Zygmund inequalities, discrete least-squares recovery, and inverse approximation.
  Our analysis uses smooth localization and dyadic Fourier estimates. We quantify parameter geometry by the fill distance $h$ and antipodal separation distance $\underline h$. For the prescribed network space $L_n^k$, representative approximation estimates take the form \begin{equation*} \begin{aligned} \inf_{g_n\in L_n^k}\|f-g_n\|_{\mathcal L^2(Ω')}&\lesssim h^r\|f\|_{\mathcal H^r(Ω)},\\ \inf_{g_n\in L_n^k}\|f-g_n\|_{\mathcal L^2(Ω)}&\gtrsim \underline h^{\frac{d+2k+1}{2}}\|f\|_{\mathcal L^2(Ω')}. \end{aligned} \end{equation*} The upper bound exploits target regularity up to the critical order $\frac{d+2k+1}{2}$, while the lower bound applies asymptotically to fixed targets smoother than this order. The upper estimate holds on an interior subdomain $Ω'$, whereas the lower estimate concerns the larger domain $Ω$.
  We also obtain geometry-dependent spectral estimates that quantify conditioning and coefficient stability. Further results establish norm discretization and regularized recovery from function values, and infer target regularity from approximation rates under coefficient or separation conditions. Altogether, these results describe how parameter geometry governs approximation accuracy, the stability of coefficient representations, discrete recovery, and the regularity information contained in convergence rates.

</details>


### [93] [RB-LDC: Redundancy-Balanced Latent Coding for Noise-Robust Diffusion Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13955)
*Hyunseok Jeong,Jaeho Jeon,Young-Sik Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究潜空间扩散模型中解码器对各方向扰动敏感度不均的问题：通过在白化潜空间与扩散过程之间插入一个可逆线性层，为每个方向分配不同的log-SNR，从而在能量预算约束下最小化解码器敏感度加权的LMMSE风险，并提出RB-LDC方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 白化后的前向扩散对所有方向施加相同SNR的噪声破坏，但解码器对各方向的敏感度并不相等，因此"等SNR"编码并非最优；作者希望按解码器敏感度重新分配噪声预算以提升含噪潜变量的重建与生成质量。

Method: 构造编码器与扩散过程之间的可逆线性层，偏移各方向的log-SNR。对任意白化潜分布给出LMMSE恢复风险的精确谱表达式；在能量预算与保证可逆性的下界约束下，最小化沿扩散调度积分、按解码器敏感度加权的LMMSE风险，得到唯一最优分配。该层与解码器的期望拉回度量对齐，并使各方向的边际风险下降相等，由确定性谱求解器计算。大预算下分配服从敏感度的幂律，指数由损失加权的低SNR尾部决定（噪声预测1/2，数据预测在余弦调度下2/3，截断调度存在交叉）。以基于雅可比的敏感度估计实例化为RB-LDC。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256上，使用SD-VAE与VA-VAE，训练后的RB-LDC在四项指标、五种编码配置下均优于预算匹配的各向同性编码（15个SNR与3个流水线种子的平均）；VA-VAE块局部编码下平均扰动FID降低17.1，同时保持干净重建；也报告了扰动FID变差的中间SNR区间。在冻结扩散模型下，采样期加噪的12种采样进度×信道SNR组合中，RB-LDC的FID点估计均更低。

Conclusion: 按解码器敏感度定向分配扩散噪声方向（log-SNR偏移的可逆线性层）是提升潜空间编码鲁棒性的有效原则，其最优分配有闭式谱刻画与幂律规律，RB-LDC在实践中一致改善含噪潜变量的重建与采样鲁棒性。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models decode approximately correct latents, making decoder sensitivity to perturbations relevant to image quality. After whitening, forward diffusion corrupts every direction at the same signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), despite unequal decoder sensitivities. We study an invertible linear layer between the encoder and diffusion process that shifts each direction's log-SNR. Its linear minimum mean-square-error (LMMSE) recovery risk has an exact spectral expression for any whitened latent distribution. Under an energy budget and a floor ensuring invertibility, minimizing decoder-sensitivity-weighted LMMSE risk integrated over the diffusion schedule yields a unique optimal allocation. The layer aligns with the decoder's expected pullback metric and equalizes marginal risk reduction across directions, computed by a deterministic spectral solver. At large budgets, the allocation follows a power law in sensitivity whose exponent is determined by the low-SNR tail of the loss weighting: one half for noise prediction and two thirds for data prediction under the cosine schedule, with a crossover on truncated schedules. We instantiate this principle as RB-LDC using Jacobian-based sensitivity estimates. On ImageNet at 256 x 256 resolution with SD-VAE and VA-VAE, trained RB-LDC improves reconstruction from noisy latents over budget-matched isotropic coding in all four metrics and five coding configurations, averaged over 15 SNRs and three pipeline seeds. It reduces average perturbed FID by 17.1 for VA-VAE block-local coding while preserving clean reconstruction. We also report intermediate SNRs where perturbed FID worsens. With a frozen diffusion model, RB-LDC yields lower FID point estimates in all 12 tested combinations of sampling progress and channel SNR under corruption during sampling.

</details>


### [94] [Universal Approximation Theorem for Metaplectic Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13991)
*Ahmed Abdeljawad,Malek Yacine Rezoug*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 证明了元 plectic 神经算子在非空有界 Lipschitz 域上的通用逼近性：单隐藏层、单非局部分支即可在 C^s→C^r 与 H^s→H^r 上实现紧致一致逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将神经算子通用逼近理论从 Fourier 乘子类推广到更一般的元 pletic 乘子类，以覆盖 Fourier 与固定非退化分数阶 Fourier 乘子等更广的非局部算子族，同时追求极简网络结构（一层、单分支）。

Method: 以带零延拓与限制的全空间元 pletic 乘子作为非局部分支，乘子取任意固定自由辛矩阵；在光滑、全局 Lipschitz、非多项式激活下，证明关键工具是精确的 chirp 共轭平均恒等式与导数估计，并与平均神经算子理论结合。

Result: 对任意非负整数 s、r 和有限输入输出通道维数，从 C^s 到 C^r、从 H^s 到 H^r 的连续算子可被紧致一致逼近；Sobolev 情形需紧致输入集额外在 L^∞ 中一致有界；单个标量符号原子与有限个位置特征由变换与域独立确定，不依赖目标算子、紧集与精度。

Conclusion: 元 pletic 神经算子具备通用逼近能力，结论囊括 Fourier 与固定非退化分数阶 Fourier 乘子构造，为辛结构类非局部算子提供了统一逼近框架。

Abstract: We prove universal approximation for metaplectic neural operators on nonempty bounded Lipschitz domains. The nonlocal branches are whole-space metaplectic multipliers with zero extension and restriction, associated with any fixed free symplectic matrix. Assuming a smooth, globally Lipschitz, nonpolynomial activation, we obtain compact-uniform approximation of continuous operators from $C^s$ to $C^r$ and from $H^s$ to $H^r$ in the respective output norms, for arbitrary nonnegative integers $s,r$ and finite input and output channel dimensions. In the Sobolev setting, compact input sets must additionally be uniformly bounded in $L^\infty$. One hidden operator layer with one nonlocal branch suffices. A single scalar symbol atom and finitely many positional features are prescribed by the transform and domain independently of the target operator, compact set and accuracy. The proof combines averaging neural operator theory with an exact chirp-conjugated averaging identity and derivative estimates. The result includes Fourier and fixed nondegenerate fractional Fourier multiplier constructions.

</details>


### [95] [Temporal Behavior Trees for Reinforcement Learning: Specifications as Rewards, Curricula, and Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13993)
*Vladimir Krsmanovic,Bernd Finkbeiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TBTCL框架，以时序行为树（TBT）作为规范引导强化学习的统一接口，单一TBT同时充当任务规范、密集奖励、课程与诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规范引导RL将任务定义、奖励塑形、课程设计与策略诊断视为彼此独立的数学对象，导致需额外转换步骤，并丢弃规范中已有的结构信息（子任务边界、顺序约束、定量度量）。

Method: 改造TBT语法与语义以适配规范引导RL，给出比LTL更具表达力且天然分层的形式化；提出从TBT语义自动提取课程DAG、直接导出基于鲁棒性的密集奖励的算法；利用TBT轨迹分段构建可理解的能力画像，并自动将奖励向瓶颈任务调整。

Result: 在离散与连续域共六个规范上评估TBTCL，验证时序行为树可同时作为规范、奖励、课程与诊断使用。

Conclusion: TBT可作为规范引导RL的统一载体，消除模块间转换开销并保留规范结构，兼具表达力、分层性与可解释性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from formal specifications often separates task definition, reward shaping, curriculum design, and policy diagnostics into different mathematical objects. This fragmentation requires additional conversion steps and discards structure already present in the specification, such as subtask boundaries, ordering constraints, and quantitative measures. We introduce TBTCL (Temporal Behavior Tree Centric Learning), a framework that uses Temporal Behavior Trees (TBTs) as a unified interface for specification-guided reinforcement learning. A single TBT specifies the task, induces dense robustness-based rewards, defines a curriculum, and supports better understanding of learned policies. By adapting TBT syntax and semantics for specification-guided reinforcement learning, we provide a formalism that is more expressive than popular specification languages such as LTL and inherently hierarchical. We present algorithms to automatically extract a curriculum DAG and derive dense, robustness-based rewards directly from TBT semantics. Furthermore, we utilize TBT trace segmentation to build understandable competence profiles and automatically adapt the reward toward bottlenecks. We evaluate TBTCL on six specifications across discrete and continuous domains and show that Temporal Behavior Trees can serve as a specification, a reward, a curriculum, and a diagnostic.

</details>


### [96] [BioXArena-Pro: a research-grade benchmark for autonomous biomedical machine learning agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13994)
*Loka Li,Duzhen Zhang,Xingbo Du,Leonard Song,Kun Zhang,Le Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BioXArena-Pro：来自八个生物医学领域的 64 个研究级建模任务，均从已发表研究重建，多数按物种或队列等完整生物学单元留出以防止信息泄漏；最强 agent 配置仅得 55.1/100，且 scaffold 与模型本身同等重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 coding agent 用于生物医学建模的基准规模小、接近饱和，且存在信息泄漏风险，无法可靠评估 agent 的真实建模能力。

Method: 构建 64 个研究级任务覆盖八个生物医学领域，逐任务依据已发表研究重建，多数留出整类生物学单元（物种、队列）以避免泄漏；评测 22 种 agent 配置；对每个任务重跑已发表方法作对照；并进行预训练模型（含生物基础模型）禁用消融；任务经多轮 AI 辅助审计与最终人工复核。

Result: 最佳 agent 得分 55.1/100；scaffold 影响与模型选择接近；在已发表流程可直接套用的任务上 agent 与方法打平，而在重建任务上方法落后 8.2 分；在最强 agent 使用预训练模型的任务上禁用这些模型使其得分下降 9 分。

Conclusion: 生物医学 coding agent 仍远未饱和，其优势主要来自对新数据的适应性；scaffold 设计与预训练模型（尤其是生物基础模型）的可获得性显著影响表现，基准设计需严格控制信息泄漏并经多轮审计。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly proposed as autonomous builders of biomedical models, but existing benchmarks are small, near saturation or open to leaked information. We present BioXArena-Pro, 64 research-level modelling tasks from eight biomedical domains, each rebuilt from a published study; most hold out whole biological units such as species or cohorts. Across 22 agent configurations, the best scores 55.1 of 100, and the scaffold matters almost as much as the model. We re-ran a published method for every task: where the published recipe applies unchanged, the best agent and the methods tie; on rebuilt tasks the methods trail by 8.2 points, suggesting that agents gain by adapting to new data. Pretrained models (including biological foundation models) matter where the strongest agent uses them: forbidding them decreases its score on those tasks by 9 points. Every task was audited in several AI-assisted rounds and a final human review.

</details>


### [97] [Coding Agent Memory Post-Training II: Unlocking the Memory Potential of Pre-trained File Operations for Long-Horizon Tasks via On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14009)
*Lirui Luo,Kelong Mao,Xinwei Yang,Rongqing Li,Luyu Chen,Kieran Wong,Yudong Guo,Xinrui Wang,Jiayin Zhu,Gu Simiu,Sulong Xu,Cong Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出 CAMG-II 长程智能体强化学习环境套件与 CAMP-II 训练方法，通过全异步 on-policy 蒸馏让单一策略在五个环境中学习基于文件的记忆行为，收益达最强 OPD 基线的 2.97 倍，并泛化到 Terminal-Bench 2.1 与 SWE-Bench Pro V2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务的交互历史超出模型有效上下文，智能体需自主决定写入、读回哪些记忆以及何时操作。现有工作用 on-policy 蒸馏（OPD）把记忆控制纳入策略，但依赖模型预训练中不存在的记忆工具与压缩协议，导致双向削弱：教师对未使用过的接口无法给出有效指导，学生采样的有效记忆动作过少，指导难以生效。

Method: 构建 CAMG-II 环境套件，覆盖 Shop、Coding、AutoResearch、General、Visual 五类长程智能体 RL 环境；每个环境在原生任务接口之外提供普通 Bash 访问与跨回合持久化工作区，使策略能在上下文替换之间创建、修改、检索和复用文件作为记忆。提出 CAMP-II，以完全异步的 OPD 在五个环境上联合训练单一策略，教师为具备文件操作先验的冻结模型，直接从教师学习文件式记忆行为。

Result: 在 CAMG-II 上，CAMP-II 取得的收益为最强 OPD 基线的 2.97 倍；所学记忆行为可迁移至 Terminal-Bench 2.1 与 SWE-Bench Pro V2 两个外部基准，成功率均有提升。

Conclusion: 以通用 Bash 与持久化工作区替代专用记忆工具接口，配合具备文件先验教师的全异步 OPD 联合训练，可有效解决记忆控制蒸馏中的接口失配与动作稀疏问题，所得记忆行为具备跨环境与跨基准的泛化能力。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks whose interaction histories exceed the model's active context. They must therefore decide what to write down and what to read back, and when to do either. Recent work has begun to use on-policy distillation (OPD) to make memory control part of the policy, relying on memory tools and compaction protocols that lie outside either model's pre-training. However, this weakens the distillation on both sides: the teacher cannot give useful guidance on an interface it has never used, and the student samples too few meaningful memory actions for that guidance to take effect. To study this problem, we introduce Coding Agent Memory Gym II (CAMG-II), a suite of long-horizon agentic-RL environments spanning Shop, Coding, AutoResearch, General, and Visual. Alongside each environment's native task interface, CAMG-II provides ordinary Bash access and an episode-persistent workspace, enabling the policy to create, revise, search, and reuse files as memory across context replacements. We also introduce Coding Agent Memory Post-Training II (CAMP-II), which trains a single policy jointly across all five environments with fully asynchronous OPD to learn this file-based memory behavior directly from a frozen teacher with a prior for file operations. On CAMG-II, CAMP-II obtains 2.97 times the gain of the strongest OPD baseline. The memory behavior CAMP-II learns generalizes to two external benchmarks, Terminal-Bench 2.1 and SWE-Bench Pro V2, with improved success rates.

</details>


### [98] [Margin-Guided Token Credit for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14015)
*Chi Liu,Xin Chen,Yuchuan Weng,Srinivasan Manoharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MCPO 将强化学习的最终答案奖励按策略下一 token 分布的 top-2 间隔（犹豫程度）重新分配到各 token，在多个数学基准上稳定优于 GRPO，并伴随显著更长的回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 把可验证奖励均等地分配给解中的每个 token，忽略了不同 token 的贡献差异。作者认为应把信用放在模型"犹豫"的位置（即下一 token 分布中 top-1 与 top-2 概率接近之处），从而更精细地分配信用。

Method: 用 top-2 概率间隔 M = p₁ − p₂ 度量犹豫程度；通过两个固定阈值把 M 映射为 token 分数；按该分数把整条解的 advantage 分摊到各 token，并做归一化保持均值不变。间隔来自训练器已有的前向计算，无需额外模型评估，也不依赖批次统计量。实验：MiniCPM5-2B-Base + LoRA，训练 200 步，使用 2,048 道 DAPO-Math 题目，在六个数学竞赛基准上评测；并用 Qwen3-4B-Base 复现。

Result: 在五个数据种子上均优于未修改的 GRPO 基线，macro avg@32 平均提升 6.55 分（95% CI [4.1, 9.0]）；Qwen3-4B-Base 复现在三个种子上均提升（+4.75）。增益在 GRPO 仅能部分解出的题目上最大，并随 pass@k 的 k 增大而增大，pass@32 时达 13.8 分。

Conclusion: 基于边际信用（margin-based credit）的 token 级信用分配是一种低成本、免额外前向的 GRPO 改进，效果稳健且跨模型迁移。但 MCPO 模型的回答长度为 GRPO 的 2–3 倍，且回答更长者得分更高；当前实验未能将"信用分配"与"长度增长"两种效应区分开。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, a language model is rewarded for a correct final answer, and GRPO passes that reward to every token of the solution in equal measure. We instead place the credit where the model hesitated, in a method we call MCPO (Margin-Credit Policy Optimization). We measure hesitation by the top-2 margin $M=p_1-p_2$ of the policy's next-token distribution, map it to a score with two fixed thresholds, and spread each solution's advantage over its tokens so that the mean stays unchanged. The margin is read from a forward pass the trainer already performs, so the method needs no extra model evaluation and no batch statistics. We train MiniCPM5-2B-Base with LoRA for 200 steps on 2,048 DAPO-Math problems and evaluate on six competition-math benchmarks. MCPO beats an unmodified GRPO baseline for all five data seeds, by 6.55 points of macro avg@32 on average (95\% CI $[4.1, 9.0]$), and a Qwen3-4B-Base replication improves for all three seeds ($+4.75$). The gain is largest on problems that GRPO solves only some of the time and grows with $k$ in pass@$k$, reaching 13.8 points at pass@32. MCPO models also write two to three times longer answers, and runs that write longer score higher; our experiments do not separate the two effects.

</details>


### [99] [ScaleBrain: A Scale-Consistent Framework for EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14035)
*Minyu Zhou,Yajing Wu,Yongqiang Tang,Wang Jia,Wensheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ScaleBrain，一个"尺度一致"的 EEG 基础模型预训练框架，从模型输入与优化目标两侧协同多尺度表示：输入端采用多尺度 EEG tokenization（不同时间跨度、共享时间锚点的分析窗），目标端采用尺度特异的掩码重建以对齐多尺度结构，并设计轴向时空注意力融合多尺度表示。在 6 个公开 BCI 基准上对比 12 个基线，5 个数据集取得最高平均平衡准确率、1 个排名第三，参数量仅 5.0M。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EEG 基础模型虽表明固定长度 patch 的多尺度表示能改进表示学习，但通常仅在特征层面构造时间尺度，导致学习到的特征与原始 EEG 信号的尺度特异特性之间的对应关系是隐式的；此外，当自监督目标与多尺度输入表示不对齐时，某些时间尺度上的信息在预训练中监督不足。

Method: 1) 多尺度 EEG tokenization：用不同时间跨度、共享时间锚点的分析窗表征同一 EEG 片段，使 token 与原始信号的时间尺度显式对应；2) 尺度特异的掩码重建目标：与多尺度输入结构对齐，在不同时间分辨率上提供监督；3) 轴向时空注意力（axial spatio-temporal attention）：沿空间与时间维度对融合后的多尺度表示进行上下文建模。

Result: 在 6 个公开 BCI 基准数据集上与 12 个代表性基线比较，ScaleBrain 在 5 个数据集上取得最高平均平衡准确率（Balanced Accuracy），在剩余 1 个数据集上排名第三；模型参数量仅 5.0M，兼具较强性能与良好参数效率。

Conclusion: 从输入表示与优化目标两侧显式对齐多尺度信息，是提升 EEG 基础模型预训练效果的有效途径；尺度一致的 tokenization 与尺度特异重建能在小参数量下取得领先的 BCI 解码性能。

Abstract: Foundation models pre-trained on large unlabeled electroencephalography (EEG) corpora have emerged as a transformative paradigm for Brain-Computer Interface (BCI) applications. Recent EEG foundation models have shown that multi-scale representations derived from fixed-length patches can improve EEG representation learning. Existing frameworks commonly construct temporal scales through feature-level operations. However, these operations leave the correspondence between learned features and scale-specific characteristics of the original EEG signals implicit. Moreover, when the self-supervised objective is not aligned with the multi-scale input representations, information at some temporal scales may receive insufficient supervision during pre-training. In light of this, we propose ScaleBrain, a scale-consistent pre-training framework that explicitly coordinates multi-scale representations of EEG from both the model input and optimization objective sides. Specifically, ScaleBrain introduces multi-scale EEG tokenization to represent the same EEG segment through analysis windows with different temporal spans and shared temporal anchors, and pairs these representations with scale-specific reconstruction to align with the multi-scale input structure and provide supervision across temporal resolutions during self-supervised learning. We further develop an axial spatio-temporal attention mechanism to contextualize the fused multi-scale representation along spatial and temporal dimensions. Extensive evaluations on six public BCI benchmarks against 12 representative baselines show that ScaleBrain achieves the highest mean Balanced Accuracy on five datasets and ranks third on the remaining one, with only 5.0M parameters, demonstrating strong performance and favorable parameter efficiency.

</details>


### [100] [Structured Confidence Perturbation via Fisher Geometry for Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14046)
*Chenlong Wei,Hanling Wang,Chong Zhang,Qinkai Yu,Fangyu Wu,Xiaobo Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对对抗训练中普遍存在的"伪攻击"（扰动仅抬升损失却未真正改变模型置信度结构）问题，提出两种互补的"攻击—防御"方法：一是融合交叉熵与KL散度的混合目标，二是基于置信流形的几何重构对抗训练，理论证明最优扰动方向与Fisher信息矩阵主特征向量对齐，并给出高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对抗训练以最大化损失为目标，易产生"伪攻击"：扰动显著提升损失值，但模型的置信度结构（预测分布关系）基本未变，导致鲁棒性提升有限。作者认为应从"置信度结构形变"角度重新定义攻击与防御目标。

Method: 提出两条互补路径：(1) 在损失最大化框架下引入混合目标，联合交叉熵与KL散度，使对抗样本同时破坏预测正确性与分布一致性；(2) 几何视角重构对抗训练，在置信流形上生成使分布距离最大的扰动。理论分析表明最优扰动方向与Fisher信息矩阵的主特征向量一致，并设计高效算法规避高维矩阵运算。

Result: 在多个数据集与模型架构上的实验表明，该方法在对抗鲁棒性与鲁棒迁移性上优于现有方法，尤其在高置信区域的结构性扰动场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 以置信度结构形变为核心目标的对抗训练，能够缓解伪攻击问题并带来更实质的鲁棒性增益；Fisher信息矩阵视角为扰动方向提供了可解释的几何刻画，且所提高效算法具备实际可行性。

Abstract: Adversarial training is often plagued by "pseudo-attacks", perturbations that significantly increase the loss value but fail to truly alter the model's confidence structure. To address this, we propose two complementary approaches to directly attack and defend against confidence structure deformation. First, building upon the traditional loss maximization perspective, we introduce a hybrid objective that integrates cross-entropy and KL divergence, encouraging adversarial examples to simultaneously disrupt prediction accuracy and distribution consistency. Second, we geometrically reconstruct adversarial training by generating perturbations that maximize the distribution distance on the confidence manifold.
