Theoretical analysis shows that the optimal perturbation direction aligns with the principal eigenvectors of the Fisher information matrix. We also propose an efficient algorithm to avoid high-dimensional matrix computations. Experiments on multiple datasets and model architectures demonstrate that our method outperforms existing methods in adversarial robustness and robust transferability, particularly excelling in structural perturbations within high-confidence regions.

</details>


### [101] [Generalizing Causal Decision-Making: Markov Equivalence, Transportability and Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14066)
*Min Woo Park,Sanghack Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文把结构因果bandit扩展到部分已知因果结构、跨环境信息迁移与可实现反事实动作三种设定，给出可能最优最小干预集的图准则与搜索算法、基于部分可迁移性的目标奖励界与优势剪枝、以及反事实结构因果bandit的最小性与可能最优性图刻画，并通过实验验证这些约简与迁移界对学习的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结构因果bandit假设因果结构已知，但现实中往往只有部分因果知识（如Markov等价类）、可借助其他环境的数据，且可选干预受实验能力限制。因此需要明确：在因果知识不完整、存在不可操纵变量、动作集受限时，哪些动作值得探索、如何剪枝并保证不丢失最优动作。

Method: (1) 在Markov等价下给出最小干预集的图准则，针对极大祖先图刻画可能最优最小干预集，并提出在部分祖先图所表示的至少一个图中可能最优的集合搜索算法；(2) 通过部分可迁移性从源分布推导目标奖励界，处理不可操纵变量，并利用优势关系进一步界定动作以剪枝与限制探索，在有效先验界下给出regret保证；(3) 在指定实验能力支持的动作上建立反事实结构因果bandit，给出最小性与可能最优性的图刻画及去除等价/被支配动作而保留最优动作的算法；(4) 在指定结构因果模型上实验，考察约简与迁移界对学习的影响。

Result: 给出部分祖先图下可能最优最小干预集的图准则与搜索算法；给出基于部分可迁移性的目标奖励界及优势关系剪枝，并在有效先验界下具有regret保证；给出反事实结构因果bandit中最小性与可能最优性的图刻画及保持最优性的动作约简算法；实验表明这些约简与迁移界会改变并影响学习过程。

Conclusion: 因果知识与实验能力共同决定了哪些动作值得探索；通过对等价与被支配动作的图化约简以及跨环境奖励界迁移，可在保留最优动作的前提下压缩探索空间并引导在受限动作空间内的学习。

Abstract: Structural causal bandits use causal knowledge to guide exploration when reward mechanisms are unknown. This paper extends the approach to partially known causal structure, information from other environments, and realizable counterfactual actions. First, we develop graphical criteria for minimal intervention sets under Markov equivalence and characterize possibly-optimal minimal intervention sets for maximal ancestral graphs. A search algorithm finds sets that are possibly optimal in at least one graph represented by a partial ancestral graph. Second, we derive target reward bounds from source distributions through partial transportability, accounting for non-manipulable variables. Dominance relations provide further bounds for action pruning and restricted exploration, with a regret guarantee under valid prior bounds. Third, we formulate counterfactual structural causal bandits over actions supported by specified experimental capabilities. Graphical characterizations of minimality and possible optimality yield algorithms that remove equivalent or dominated choices while preserving an optimal action. Experiments with specified structural causal models examine how these reductions and transferred bounds affect learning. Together, these results clarify how causal knowledge and experimental capabilities determine which actions merit exploration and guide learning within the retained action space.

</details>


### [102] [Opportunity-Cost Option-Critic: Adaptive Switching Costs for Robust Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14099)
*Khaled Ammoura,Aya Kayal,Naseem Daher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出机会成本选项评论家（OC²）框架，用当前选项与最佳备选之间的正价值差作为状态相关的"深思成本"，实现自适应的选项持续与切换，在非平稳环境下优于固定成本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层强化学习中的深思成本（deliberation cost）用于防止选项坍缩为原始动作，但现有方法依赖固定惩罚或基于时长的自适应，二者都只是选项效用的间接代理，无法反映"放弃当前行为"的真实代价。

Method: 将选项持续性建立在放弃当前行为的机会成本上：定义状态相关的深思成本为活跃选项与其最佳备选之间的正价值差，当前选项明显更优时鼓励持续、出现更好选项时允许快速切换；并指出直接学习该成本会退化为零成本解，因此引入辅助目标使学习到的成本与价值差目标对齐；在Four Rooms与Pinball环境中，考察平稳、目标漂移与几何漂移三种条件。

Result: 在Pinball目标漂移任务中，OC²框架前十回合成功率为52%，固定成本基线为18%；整体结果表明基于机会成本的持续性提升了非平稳环境下的适应能力。

Conclusion: 以机会成本（价值差）替代固定或时长型惩罚作为选项持续性的依据是可行且有效的，能显著改善非平稳环境中的适应速度与切换行为。

Abstract: Deliberation costs are commonly used in hierarchical reinforcement learning (HRL) to prevent options from collapsing to primitive actions. Existing approaches rely on fixed penalties or duration-based adaptations, both of which are indirect proxies for an option's utility. This paper argues that option persistence should instead depend on the opportunity cost of abandoning the current behavior. We propose the Opportunity-Cost Option-Critic ($OC^2$) framework, which defines a state-dependent deliberation cost using the positive value gap between the active option and its best alternative. This encourages persistence when the current option is clearly preferable and allows rapid switching when a better option emerges. We show that directly learning such costs leads to a degenerate zero-cost solution and introduces an auxiliary objective that aligns the learned cost with the value-gap target. The approach is evaluated in Four Rooms and Pinball domains under stationary, goal-shift, and geometry-shift conditions. In the Pinball goal-shift task, the $OC^2$ framework achieves a 52\% first-ten-episode success rate compared with 18\% for a fixed-cost baseline. The results suggest that opportunity-cost-based persistence improves adaptation in non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [103] [Hierarchical Graph Representation Learning with Pooling-Induced Substructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14126)
*Luca Sbicego,Xiaowen Dong,Dorina Thanou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Pooled-Substructure-Aware GNNs（PSA-GNN），在图池化中显式保留并处理池化诱导的子结构，理论上提升 WL 表达力，并给出两个可控层次结构的合成数据集与层次模式普遍性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 GNN 难以处理需在超出局部邻域的子结构上进行推理的层次化图层级任务。图池化通过将图粗化为层次表示来应对，但现有方法往往不能显式保留其诱导出的子结构，导致经验性能不稳定；同时缺乏具有显式层次结构的基准，评测困难。

Method: 提出 PSA-GNN：在基于结构的池化方案中显式保留池化所诱导的子结构，并对这些子结构单独处理（作为池化簇所对应子图的显式表示），再与粗化图的信息结合；理论上从 Weisfeiler-Lehman（WL）测试角度分析其表达力增强。评测方面构造两个层次结构可控的合成数据集，并提出一个刻画真实图级基准中层次模式普遍性的度量指标。

Result: 在具有相关层次结构的任务上优于匹配的池化基线（参数/设置对齐的 pooling 基线），在结构更简单的基准上保持竞争力；理论上证明相较池化类 GNN 具备更强的 WL 表达力。

Conclusion: 显式保留池化诱导子结构是提升池化类 GNN 表达力与层次任务性能的有效设计；所提合成数据集与层次模式度量弥补了评测缺口，代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Hierarchical graph-level tasks that require reasoning over substructures that extend beyond local neighborhoods are posing challenges for standard Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). While graph pooling aims to address this by coarsening graphs into hierarchical representations, existing methods often fail to explicitly preserve the substructures they induce, leading to inconsistent empirical performance. Moreover, evaluating such approaches remains challenging due to the lack of benchmarks with explicit hierarchical structure. We propose Pooled-Substructure-Aware GNNs, an approach to graph pooling that explicitly preserves and processes substructures uncovered by structure-based pooling schemes. We show that this design enhances the expressive power of pooling-based GNNs in the sense of the Weisfeiler-Lehman (WL) test. To enable principled evaluation, we introduce two new synthetic datasets with controllable hierarchical structure and propose a metric to characterize the prevalence of hierarchical patterns in real-world graph-level benchmarks. On tasks with relevant hierarchical structure, our approach outperforms matched pooling baselines, while remaining competitive on structurally simpler benchmarks. Code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/lsbicego/PSA-GNN/.

</details>


### [104] [Right for the Wrong Reasons? Evaluating Plausibility and Behavioral Change in Explanation-Guided Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14134)
*David Dembinsky,Adriano Lucieri,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统检验“解释与人类理由的一致性提升是否意味着决策策略真正改变”，发现解释引导学习（EGL）的效果高度依赖于具体目标：基于理由的数据增强带来最明显的鲁棒性提升，归因约束类目标主要提升解释合理性但行为纠正较弱，且二者都不能可靠保证扰动保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EGL 宣称能让模型既准确又依赖任务相关证据，但“解释与人类理由更一致”是否等价于“模型决策策略发生改变”尚不清楚。作者希望在受控条件下分离解释层面与行为层面的效果，检验二者是否可互相推断。

Method: 构建带有人为引入捷径的合成基准数据集，覆盖局部化、背景式与全局线索三类捷径，模拟真实应用场景；在此受控设置下比较不同 EGL 目标函数，并沿行为轴（准确率、前景依赖）与解释轴（重要性质量 Importance Mass、扰动保真度）两个维度分别评估。

Result: EGL 效果强烈依赖目标类型。基于理由的数据增强在捷径证据可与任务相关前景分离时鲁棒性增益最清晰，平均准确率最高提升 +4.8 个百分点，同时前景依赖增强；归因约束类目标主要提升解释合理性，Importance Mass 平均最高提升 +14 个百分点，但行为纠正较弱且不稳定，准确率增益至多 +1.6 个百分点；此外，鲁棒性或合理性的提升都不能可靠地对应更高的扰动保真度。

Conclusion: EGL 有一定价值，但其成功不能仅凭理由一致性来推断，应在行为层面与解释层面这两类互补维度上分别评估。作者公开全部实验结果以支持后续分析。

Abstract: Explanation-Guided Learning (EGL) promises models that are not only accurate but also rely on task-relevant evidence. However, whether improved agreement between model explanations and human-provided rationales also implies a changed decision strategy remains unclear. We study this tension under controlled synthetic shortcut introduction using a constructed shortcut dataset. Inspired by real-world applications, our benchmark covers shortcuts of different kinds, including localized, background-based, and global cues. This allows us to evaluate whether different EGL objectives produce behavioral changesand explanatory changes. Across these axes, we find that the effects of EGL are strongly objective-specific. Rationale-based data augmentation provides the clearest robustness gains when shortcut evidence can be separated from task-relevant foreground information, reaching average accuracy improvements of up to +4.8 percentage points (pp) together with increased foreground reliance. Attribution-constraining objectives primarily improve explanation plausibility, increasing Importance Mass by up to +14 pp on average, but provide weaker and less consistent behavioral correction, with average accuracy gains of at most +1.6 pp. Moreover, neither improved robustness nor improved plausibility reliably implies higher perturbation-based fidelity. Overall, our results show that EGL can be useful, but its success cannot be inferred from rationale agreement alone and should instead be evaluated along complementary behavioral and explanation-level axes. We publicly release all experimental results to enable further analysis (https://right-for-wrong-reasons.dfki.de/).

</details>


### [105] [HaiRuo: Forecasting Ocean Eddies as Fields and Objects with a Satellite-Altimetry Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14136)
*Xukun Qin,Haochen Sun,Yuyang Tian,Ge Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HaiRuo 是一个统一"场+对象"双视角的中尺度涡旋预报框架：以 30 天卫星海平面高度图为输入，单次前向推理即输出 15 天海平面与地转速度场、逐日涡旋掩膜以及当前所有存活涡旋的轨迹。其由无标签预训练的 1.05B 参数基础模型 HaiRuo-1B 与拉格朗日对象解码器 XuanJi 构成，在统一的全球预注册评测中于全部端点超越 14 种对比方法（15 天中心误差 25.2 vs 30.6 km，-18%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中尺度涡输运了海洋绝大部分动能并跨海盆输送热、盐与营养盐，但其两周后的位置目前仍依赖海洋数值模式配合事后追踪启发式（post-hoc tracking heuristics）获得，缺乏端到端、同时刻画欧拉场与拉格朗日对象演化的预报手段。

Method: 1) HaiRuo-1B：1.05B 参数、开源权重的基础模型，在 24 年全球卫星测高观测上无标签预训练，提供 15 天物理场与涡旋掩膜；2) XuanJi：拉格朗日对象解码器，在每个涡旋的随动极坐标（co-moving polar frame）中对演化预报采样，并经由三轮"前瞻"（look-ahead）迭代精修 15 天路径，其中引入对象注意力机制；3) 采用统一的全球预注册评测协议，并与固定追踪器耦合进行流水线级评测。

Result: 相同解码器下，HaiRuo-1B 带来 +0.14 场技巧与 +1.1 mIoU 点提升；冻结骨干降低 15 天中心误差 1.2 km（全量重训为 1.35 km）。XuanJi 在 HaiRuo-1B 上再降 2.5 km（联合微调 2.9 km），在六个已发表骨干上借助对象注意力降低 2.5–3.3 km。全球预注册评测中全部端点优于 14 种方法（15 天中心误差 25.2 vs 30.6 km，-18%），在有标签区域外及 ENSO 两个相位下均保持优势，并复现西向漂移、50–60°S 东向逆转及气旋—反气旋向极偏转。经固定追踪器的十天预报中，HaiRuo 预报场使 WenHai 与 XiHe 的涡心误差下降 37–43%（-2.8 km，而二者自身退化 +4.3–8.7 km），对象轨迹使流水线误差全球降低 77–78%。

Conclusion: 涡旋预报可在单一模型中同时以欧拉场和拉格朗日对象两种表征端到端完成，且基础模型式预训练与对象级解码器的"分工式"组合在该任务上显著优于既有方法；模型在分布外区域、ENSO 不同相位及下游追踪流水线中均具稳健增益，并已开源权重、代码与项目页。

Abstract: Mesoscale eddies carry most of the ocean's kinetic energy and move heat, salt and nutrients across basins, yet where an eddy will be two weeks ahead is still read off ocean models with post-hoc tracking heuristics. We introduce HaiRuo, which forecasts eddies as fields and as objects at once: from 30 days of satellite sea-level maps, one forward pass returns 15 days of sea level and geostrophic velocity, daily eddy masks and the trajectory of every eddy alive today. HaiRuo pairs two components with a clear division of labour: (1) HaiRuo-1B, an open-weight 1.05-billion-parameter foundation model pretrained without labels on 24 years of global satellite-altimetry observations, which supplies the 15-day physical fields and masks (+0.14 field skill and +1.1 mIoU points under the same decoder) and further lowers 15-day centre error by 1.2 km frozen (1.35 km over training from scratch); and (2) XuanJi, a Lagrangian object decoder that samples the evolving forecast in each eddy's co-moving polar frame and refines the 15-day path across three look-ahead rounds, removing 2.5 km on HaiRuo-1B (2.9 km with joint fine-tuning) and 2.5-3.3 km via its object attention across six published backbones. Under one pre-registered global evaluation over the whole ocean, HaiRuo improves on fourteen methods on every endpoint (25.2 vs. 30.6 km 15-day centre error, -18%), holding its margin outside the labelled regions and through both phases of the El Nino-Southern Oscillation (ENSO), while reproducing westward drift, 50-60 deg S eastward reversal and poleward cyclone-anticyclone deflection. Over ten days through one fixed tracker, HaiRuo's forecast field cuts the eddy-centre error of WenHai and XiHe by 37-43% (-2.8 km vs. day 0 while they degrade +4.3-8.7 km), and its object trajectories cut pipeline error by 77-78% globally. Open-weight checkpoints, code and project page: https://github.com/xkqin/HaiRuo

</details>


### [106] [Deviation-Aware Pretraining for Cardiovascular Disease Detection from Electrocardiograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14141)
*Siyuan Yan,Haibo Zheng,Feng Lu,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于近百万条无标签12导联ECG的自监督特征提取框架，含偏离定位嵌入(DLE)与关联重建嵌入(ARE)，在多公开数据集上AUROC最高提升6.5%、F1提升12.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习ECG模型多为有监督、任务特定的训练，难以跨疾病类型、人群和采集设置泛化，且依赖大量标注数据。

Method: 在近100万条无标注12导联ECG上进行自监督预训练，设计两个模块：DLE检测波形相对正常模式的偏离；ARE以偏离为引导进行重建，建模跨导联关联，从而同时捕获细微偏离模式及其跨导联上下文。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上，即使不对预训练特征提取器额外微调，AUROC最高提升6.5%、F1提升12.8%；嵌入可视化显示学到的特征可形成有意义的疾病分组与异常识别。

Conclusion: 该框架降低了对标注数据的依赖，有望辅助不同医疗场景下的临床工作者，改善心血管疾病的早期检测与监测，促进更可及、公平的心脏健康照护。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) signals are critical for cardiovascular disease (CVD) detection. Most current deep learning models rely on supervised, task-specific training, which limits their ability to generalize across diseases, patient populations, and acquisition settings. This paper presents a self-supervised ECG feature extraction framework that learns from nearly one million unlabeled 12-lead ECG recordings. The framework includes two modules: Deviation Localization Embedding (DLE), which detects waveform-level deviations from normal patterns, and Association Reconstruction Embedding (ARE), which models cross-lead associations through deviation-guided reconstruction to capture abnormal patterns expressed across multiple leads. These modules enable the framework to capture subtle deviation-related patterns and their cross-lead context. The framework achieves up to 6.5% AUROC and 12.8% F1 improvements over prior methods on multiple public datasets, even without additional fine-tuning the pretrained feature extractor. Embedding visualizations show that the learned features support meaningful disease grouping and anomaly identification. The approach reduces the need for labeled data and can support clinicians in diverse healthcare settings. The framework has potential to improve early detection and monitoring of cardiovascular diseases, contributing to more accessible and equitable heart care.

</details>


### [107] [Unipolar Neuron Networks Trained by Recirculation for Reward-Based Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14153)
*Florin Leon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于无极性神经元（单根神经突分支成树，兼具输入与输出突触）的神经网络模型及"再循环"(recirculation)学习规则，仅依赖奖励信号学习，并通过任务编码与巩固机制抑制序贯学习中的遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头足类及多数无脊椎动物的主导神经元是无极性神经元，其单根神经突分支成树，同一树既承载输入突触又承载输出突触，并以分级局部输出和单点全局输出传递信息。现有主流神经网络模型基于其他神经元形态与反向传播学习，缺少针对这种生物结构的网络与学习规则的建模，且深度网络在序贯任务中遗忘严重。

Method: 将每个神经元建模为无源分段构成的树，其激活值精确地取为线性系统的稳态解；网络仅从奖励中学习，采用recirculation规则：每次前向传播后，第二次传播沿相同连接回传输出的"请求变化量"；每个突触仅利用其局部可得的信息调整权重。由于每个神经元的线性系统对称，该更新近似于预测奖励误差的梯度。为防止灾难性遗忘，引入两种机制：任务编码在每项任务中抑制不同分支子集；巩固机制降低近期权重变化较大突触的学习率。

Result: 在MNIST与Iris任务序列上评估，该网络保持高准确率且遗忘很低；而对同一网络采用基于梯度的训练，则几乎完全遗忘先前任务。

Conclusion: 受生物无极性神经元启发的树状无源分段网络配合recirculation局部学习规则，可在仅用奖励信号的条件下有效学习，并通过任务编码与巩固显著缓解序贯学习中的灾难性遗忘，其性能明显优于同一网络上的梯度训练。

Abstract: Unipolar neurons are the predominant type of nerve cell in the octopus and in most other invertebrates. A single neurite (a fiber extending from the cell body) branches into a tree that carries both input and output synapses. The branches send graded local outputs to nearby cells, and one site on the main neurite sends a global output to distant targets. This paper proposes a network of such neurons and a learning rule for it. Each neuron is modeled as a tree of passive segments, whose activations are computed exactly as the steady state of a linear system. The network learns from reward alone, with a rule called recirculation. After each forward pass, a second pass sends requested changes of the outputs back through the same connections. Each synapse then adjusts its weight using only quantities available at its own location. Because the system of each neuron is symmetric, this update closely follows the gradient of the error in the predicted reward. Two mechanisms limit forgetting when tasks are learned in sequence: a task code inhibits a different subset of branches in each task, while consolidation lowers the learning rate of synapses that changed strongly in the recent past. The model is evaluated on a sequence of MNIST and Iris tasks. The network maintains high accuracy with low forgetting, whereas gradient-based training of the same network forgets earlier tasks almost completely.

</details>


### [108] [Tree-Ising: Repairing Language Models with Bit-Level Ising Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14210)
*Hadi Salloum,Ammar Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tree-Ising将LLM权重修复表述为模型自身BF16权重上的稀疏离散优化问题，按输出行精确分解为独立QUBO并经GPU模拟退火/模拟分岔求解，在MATH-500上达54.6%，超越MIT智能体编辑结果（51.7%），且修改权重更少、仅需4次前向请求、零LLM token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIT的Takehi等提出的权重空间编程需前端LLM智能体读取网络权重并写出修复，代价高昂（158轮交互、8970万token、50次前向请求）。作者希望以更直接、更廉价的稀疏离散优化替代智能体式修复流程。

Method: 利用前向统计构造目标函数：削弱循环（loop）方向、强化收敛方向，并在有效token上保持该层输出。在状态树表示下（无损BF16存储为其特例），允许的编辑为保持共享组指数与尾数不变条件下的符号翻转与指数平移。该目标被精确分解为逐输出行独立的QUBO（Ising）子问题，在GPU上用模拟退火或量子启发式模拟分岔求解；另在IBM Heron处理器上用QAOA求解一个8变量子问题作概念验证。

Result: 智能体编辑的两个层上，Tree-Ising在MATH-500达54.6%（智能体51.7%，基座7.9%）；模拟分岔给出统计上不可区分的55.1%。两种求解器在全部五个数学基准上均优于智能体编辑，经多重比较校正后MATH-500与GSM8K改善显著。修改权重数少13%–17%，BF16位翻转减少2.2–2.3倍；仅需4次前向请求、零LLM token，退火方案GPU时间约0.90美元。Qwen3.5-2B上（仅用基座模型选层与剂量）贪心GSM8K从21.6%升至69.4%。

Conclusion: 将权重修复重构为结构化稀疏离散优化（QUBO/Ising）可在效果上超越LLM智能体式权重编辑，同时大幅降低计算、token与位翻转成本，并具备向量子求解器迁移的可行性；其编辑空间由BF16无损表示约束自然刻画，具备可扩展性。

Abstract: Weight-space programming, introduced in recent MIT work by Takehi et al., enables a frontier LLM agent to read a network's weights and write a repair. Their GPT-6 Astra agent corrected the reasoning loops of Qwen3.5-0.8B without gradients, using 158 agent turns, 89.7 million tokens, and 50 forward-pass requests. Our method, Tree-Ising, casts this repair as a sparse discrete optimization problem over the model's own BF16 weights. Forward statistics define an objective that weakens a loop direction, strengthens a concluding direction, and preserves the layer's output on productive tokens. Within a state-tree representation, where lossless BF16 storage acts as a special case, the admissible edits are sign flips and exponent shifts that preserve all shared group exponents and mantissas. We decompose this objective exactly into independent QUBO (Ising) problems, one per output row, which we solve on a GPU using simulated annealing or quantum-inspired simulated bifurcation. On the agent's two layers, Tree-Ising achieves 54.6% on MATH-500, outperforming the agent's edit (51.7%) under the same evaluation protocol, while the base model scores 7.9% as reported. The simulated bifurcation solver yields a statistically indistinguishable repair at 55.1%. Both solvers outperform the agent edit across all five math benchmarks, with statistically significant improvements on MATH-500 and GSM8K after correcting for multiple comparisons. Our approach modifies 13% to 17% fewer weights and reduces BF16 bit flips by a factor of 2.2 to 2.3. It requires only four forward-pass requests and zero LLM tokens, with the annealing recipe costing about $0.90 in GPU time. On Qwen3.5-2B, with layers and dose selected using only the base model, the same recipe increases greedy GSM8K accuracy from 21.6% to 69.4%. As a proof of concept, we execute an eight-variable repair subproblem using QAOA on an IBM Heron processor.

</details>


### [109] [Verifiable Agentic Traffic Class: A Policed Quality-of-Service Mechanism for Agentic AI Traffic, Evaluated from Simulation to Real-Kernel Adversarial Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14211)
*Naveen Babu Narayanan Nair,Sajay Kurup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VATC 为 LLM 智能体流量设计了可验证的差异化服务类别：用预留 DSCP 声明身份、路由器侧分类器将矛盾流降级、并配合限速优先队列。稳态性能等同 DiffServ，突发场景下智能体排队时延降 98%（人类时延 +12%），内核伪造洪泛下人类时延仅为 DiffServ 的 1/11；但分类器仍会漏检模仿智能体模式的伪造者，且时序特征在引入时延后失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 驱动的软件智能体产生的流量与人类 Web 流量特征不同，但现有路由器没有对应类别，也无法核验发送方"我是智能体"的声明，因此既无法对其做差异化调度，也无法防止标记滥用。

Method: 提出 VATC：①预留 DSCP 值用于声明智能体流量；②路由器侧分类器检测与声明相矛盾的流，并将其降级到"尽力而为之后"的类别；③限速优先队列控制智能体流的占比。在两个模拟器与真实 Linux 内核上，与静态 DiffServ、信任标记的专用类、以及 fq_codel 公平队列调度器对比；并实现一个含四特征的在线分类器，测试突发、伪造洪泛、模拟网络时延与真实智能体执行框架下的表现。

Result: 稳态下 VATC 与 DiffServ 相当。构造突发中，假设即时判决，智能体平均排队时延相对 DiffServ 降低 98%，人类时延增加 12%，且增益主要来自独立类别。内核伪造洪泛下，限速使人类时延比 DiffServ 低 11 倍。缺少验证时，DiffServ 与紧配置的 fq_codel 给合法智能体的时延优于 VATC。四特征分类器将全部 20 个批量型伪造者降级（智能体获得 0.30 ms），但漏检 6 个复制智能体模式的伪造者；引入模拟时延后，时序特征失去区分能力。在拥塞链路的真实智能体框架中，路由器排队占任务时间的 13%–63%。

Conclusion: VATC 能在不牺牲人类流量的前提下为智能体提供低时延，并有效抑制粗暴伪造；但其收益依赖分类准确度，对模式模仿型伪造仍脆弱，时序特征不具鲁棒性，且实验流量为合成或沙箱规模，真实部署效果待验证。

Abstract: Software agents built on large language models generate traffic unlike human web traffic, yet routers have no class for it and cannot verify a sender's claim to be an agent. This paper proposes the Verifiable Agentic Traffic Class (VATC), which combines a reserved Differentiated Services Code Point (DSCP) value declaring agent traffic, a router-side classifier that demotes flows contradicting the declaration to a class served after best-effort traffic, and a rate-limited priority queue. VATC was compared with static Differentiated Services (DiffServ), a dedicated class that trusts the marking, and fq_codel, a per-flow fair-queuing scheduler, in two simulators and on a real Linux kernel. VATC matched DiffServ under steady load. In a constructed burst it cut mean agent queuing delay by 98% relative to DiffServ in simulation (assuming an immediate verdict), at a 12% increase in human delay; most of the gain came from the separate class. Under a spoofed flood in the kernel, the rate limit held human latency 11 times below DiffServ's. Without verification, DiffServ and a tightly configured fq_codel gave legitimate agents lower latency than VATC; a live four-feature classifier then demoted all 20 bulk-shaped spoofers and gave agents 0.30 ms, but missed six spoofers that copied the agent pattern. With emulated network delay, a timing feature of the classifier stopped separating agent from human flows. In real agent harnesses on a congested link, router queueing took 13% to 63% of task time, depending on harness and assumed model latency. The traffic was synthetic or sandbox-scale.

</details>


### [110] [SinkState: On-the-Fly SVD of Recurrent States for Efficient Hybrid LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14246)
*Haolin Jiang,Weihang Li,Zhongzhu Zhou,Jinwen Pan,Santosh Pandey,Hang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SinkState，利用线性注意力状态的低秩特性，将循环状态以自适应低秩形式存储与更新，降低混和模型解码时的显存占用与访存量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合模型用线性注意力把历史压缩为固定大小的循环状态以平衡质量与效率，但线性注意力的压缩并未达到极限：作者发现线性层的 Q、K、V 集中于稳定的低维子空间（称为 linear attention sink），使每个状态实质上是低秩的，且秩随状态变化。然而现有推理系统仍以稠密形式保存这些冗余状态，每个解码步都整体读写，导致访存量随批大小增长并主导解码开销。

Method: SinkState 在推理全程以自适应低秩形式存储每个循环状态；采用带缓冲的更新策略，并仅在顶部子空间上做局部 SVD 以控制分解开销；其余分量则异步在 CPU 上整合。

Result: 在四个含 Mamba-2 与 Gated DeltaNet (GDN) 层的混合模型（4B 至 120B 参数）上，相比 Mamba-SSM 与 FLA，循环状态峰值显存最多降低 2.6×（平均 1.9×），解码速度最多提升 1.58×（平均 1.25×），同时保持精度。

Conclusion: 线性注意力状态的可压缩性可被系统性利用：通过自适应低秩状态存储与增量维护，可在不损失精度的前提下显著降低混合模型服务的显存与解码延迟。

Abstract: Hybrid models combine softmax attention with linear attention, which compresses history into fixed-size recurrent states to balance model quality and inference efficiency. However, linear attention has not pushed this compression to its limit: we find that the queries, keys, and values of linear layers concentrate in a stable, low-dimensional subspace, a phenomenon we call the linear attention sink, so each state is effectively low-rank, with a rank that varies across states. Yet existing serving systems keep these redundant states dense, reading and rewriting them in full at every decoding step, so their memory traffic grows with the batch size and dominates the decoding cost. Thus SinkState stores each recurrent state in an adaptive low-rank form throughout inference with buffered updates and a local SVD restricted to the top subspace. SinkState further consolidates the remaining components asynchronously on the CPU. On four hybrid models with Mamba-2 and Gated DeltaNet (GDN) layers, from 4B to 120B parameters, SinkState reduces the peak memory of recurrent states by up to 2.6x (1.9x on average) and speeds up decoding by up to 1.58x (1.25x on average) over Mamba-SSM and FLA, while maintaining accuracy.

</details>


### [111] [Chain of Physics: Supporting Fault Diagnosis with Engineering-Inspired Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14263)
*Wenqi Zhou,Leonardo Baldo,Kai Goebel,Manuel Arias Chao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Chain of Physics (CoP)框架：自动从资产参考文档构建约束知识图谱(CKG)，存储故障模式—物理量变化—监测传感器的诊断链；推理时检索语义相似图路径作为信息与上下文示例，并对候选故障模式逐一生成"故障—观测"链，最终与选项比对决策。在FailureSensorIQ上跨4类资产、3个LLM相比直接提示提升7.8–19.6个百分点，相比常规RAG提升11.7–18.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统故障诊断需将物理量观测变化与故障机理关联，依赖工程师经验与故障传播知识。LLM具备推理能力，但其诊断效果受限于资产专用工程知识的可得性及其表示与使用方式；现有直接提示与常规RAG缺乏结构化的故障传播知识，难以完成故障—传感器的关联。

Method: 1) 从资产专用参考文档自动生成约束知识图谱CKG，节点/边显式连接故障模式、物理量变化与监测传感器，形成诊断链；2) 推理阶段按问题检索语义相似的图路径，同时作为信息依据与上下文示例；3) 从问题中抽取候选故障模式，对每个候选用独立LLM调用生成合理的"故障→观测"链；4) 决策模块将生成链与备选答案比对给出最终判断。

Result: 在FailureSensorIQ基准的四个工业资产类别与三个LLM上评估：相比直接提示，准确率提升7.8–19.6个百分点；相比常规RAG，提升11.7–18.2个百分点，且在所有评估模型上均有提升。

Conclusion: 结构化的工程知识（约束知识图谱）在结合图检索与故障传播的上下文示例时，可有效增强LLM的故障诊断推理能力，说明知识的表示形式与检索—示例结合方式是提升诊断性能的关键。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis in complex systems requires linking observed changes in physical quantities to the failure mechanisms that produce them. Experienced engineers perform this task by combining sensor information with knowledge of system behavior and fault propagation. Large Language Models (LLMs) can support this process, but their diagnostic reasoning depends on the availability of asset-specific engineering knowledge and on how this knowledge is represented and used during inference. This work introduces Chain of Physics (CoP), an LLM-based fault-diagnosis framework built around a Constrained Knowledge Graph (CKG) generated automatically from asset-specific reference documentation. CoP addresses failure-sensor association from textual engineering knowledge. The CKG stores diagnostic chains connecting failure modes, changes in physical quantities, and monitoring sensors. During inference, semantically similar graph paths are retrieved for each question and used as both information and in-context examples for the reasoning process. Candidate failure modes are extracted from the question and analysed independently through dedicated LLM calls, which generate plausible failure-to-observation chains. A final decision module compares these chains with the available answer choices. CoP is evaluated on the FailureSensorIQ benchmark across four industrial asset classes and three LLMs. Compared with direct prompting and conventional Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), CoP improves diagnostic accuracy across all evaluated models, with gains ranging from 7.8 to 19.6 percentage points and from 11.7 to 18.2 percentage points, respectively. The results show that structured engineering knowledge can improve LLM-based diagnostic reasoning when graph retrieval is combined with in-context examples of fault propagation.

</details>


### [112] [Physics-Constrained Autoencoder for Interpretable Health Representation Learning in Turbofan Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14266)
*Leonardo Baldo,Jokin Alcibar Ibarzabal,Daniel Cisneros Acevedo,Kai Goebel,Manuel Arias Chao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种物理约束自编码器，将系统物理知识同时作为解码器结构先验与损失函数学习偏置，使潜在变量对应可解释的物理量，从而构建可解释、可分解的健康指标（HI）。在N-CMAPSS上R²=0.9993、MAE=0.0036，HI与真实退化量的Spearman相关为0.9791、RMSE=0.0286、MAE=0.0233；单一模型跨四个数据集仍保留区分压缩侧与膨胀侧退化的部件级特征，并支持在部件图上可视化退化趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自编码器的HI学习多仅以重构为目标，潜在变量缺乏物理含义，可解释性与诊断价值受限；本文希望把系统知识注入表征学习，使HI既保留数据驱动的表征能力，又具备物理可解释性和部件级分解能力。

Method: 在解码器架构中嵌入可微物理方程，以潜在变量与工况为输入，结构性地约束每个潜在变量表征预定义物理量；同时在损失函数中加入物理一致性项作为学习偏置。先进行工况修正，再将部件级物理量聚合为系统级HI，并在N-CMAPSS上与其他表征方法基准对比，另用单一模型跨四个不同退化场景数据集评估，最终在部件图上可视化退化趋势。

Result: 重构R²=0.9993、MAE=0.0036，与纯数据驱动模型相当；工况修正后推断物理量能跟踪退化，聚合HI与真值Spearman相关0.9791、RMSE=0.0286、MAE=0.0233；单一模型在四个数据集上无需诊断标签即可保留区分压缩侧与膨胀侧退化的部件级特征；可实现部件图上的退化趋势可视化。

Conclusion: 将物理约束作为归纳偏置嵌入自编码器，可在保持重构精度的同时赋予潜在变量明确物理意义，得到高相关性、可分解、可解释的HI，并具备跨退化场景的泛化性与面向领域专家的可视化能力，适用于PHM的诊断与预测任务。

Abstract: Health Indices (HIs) summarize degradation from operational data and support diagnostic and prognostic tasks in Prognostics and Health Management (PHM). Their usefulness depends on capturing degradation and interpreting system condition. Autoencoders learn such representations from unlabelled monitoring data; however, when training is driven only by reconstruction, the physical meaning of latent variables remains undefined, limiting their interpretation and diagnostic value. This work proposes a physics-constrained autoencoder that embeds system knowledge as an inductive bias in the decoder architecture and as a learning bias in the loss function. The decoder contains differentiable physical equations that receive latent variables and operating conditions as inputs, structurally guiding each latent variable to represent a predefined physical quantity and enabling component-level analysis before aggregation into a system-level HI.The methodology is benchmarked on the N-CMAPSS dataset against alternative representations. Reconstruction reaches an $R^2$ of 0.9993 and an MAE of 0.0036, comparable to fully data-driven models. After operating-condition correction, the inferred physical quantities track degradation, and their aggregation produces an HI with a Spearman correlation of 0.9791 with the ground truth, an RMSE of 0.0286, and an MAE of 0.0233. The methodology is further evaluated using a single model trained across four datasets representing distinct degradation scenarios. This model preserves component-level signatures that distinguish compression-side from expansion-side degradation without diagnostic labels.Finally, the proposed framework enables degradation trends to be visualized on component maps, providing a health representation in a form familiar to engine operators and domain experts.

</details>


### [113] [Federated Learning for Cross-Regional Epidemic Forecasting with Asynchronous Outbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14272)
*Ruibin Liu,Francisco Fernandes,Xuanfeng Li,Zige Liu,André Salgado,Chitin Hon,Zifeng Yang,Arlindo Oliveira,Ana Teresa Freitas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出并验证了一个跨区域 COVID-19 联邦学习（FL）预测框架：在广东、澳门、葡萄牙三地疫情异步暴发、干预异质的条件下，基于 LSTM 经 Flower 框架联邦训练，归一化 RMSE 达 0.028，较本地模型（0.240）改善 88.3%，接近集中式训练，且保持数据主权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疫情在各地区间往往异步暴发，跨区域协同预测具有潜在价值，但受隐私法规限制与公共卫生干预异质性的双重阻碍，数据难以集中共享。

Method: 采用 WHO 与中国 CDC 的日新增确诊病例数据（三地区），以 LSTM 为基模型，通过 Flower 联邦学习框架在各地本地训练并仅聚合模型参数，各站点超参数保持一致；以本地化训练与集中式训练为基线，用 RMSE、MAE、SMAPE 评估短期预测性能；因仅使用公开聚合病例数，可计算集中式基准作为参照。

Result: 数据集为 2021-11-24 至 2023-01-08 共 411 天、1,233 条日观测。葡萄牙较早的 Omicron 波提供了可迁移的传播动态，提升了澳门、广东等后发地区的预测效果。FL 模型归一化 RMSE 为 0.028，相对本地模型（0.240）改善 88.3%，与集中式模型相当；通信开销主要由模型规模与聚合轮数决定。

Conclusion: FL 在保持数据不出域的前提下取得接近集中式训练的短期预测精度，开销可接受；利用区域间疫情异步性可作为隐私保护协同预测的可行路径，但仍需前瞻性验证。

Abstract: Objective: Epidemic outbreaks are often asynchronous across regions, yet collaborative forecasting is hampered by privacy laws and heterogeneous public health interventions. We developed and evaluated, as a proof of concept, a federated learning (FL) framework for cross-regional COVID-19 forecasting under asynchronous outbreaks among Guangdong, Macau, and Portugal, while maintaining data sovereignty.
  Methods: Using daily case data from WHO and China CDC for the three regions, whose outbreaks were asynchronous and interventions heterogeneous, we trained Long Short-Term Memory networks via FL for short-term forecasting, benchmarked against localized and centralized training using Root Mean Square Error, Mean Absolute Error, and Symmetric Mean Absolute Percentage Error. All experiments used the Flower FL framework with consistent hyperparameters across sites. Publicly available, aggregated case-count data were used so that the centralized benchmark could be computed as a reference.
  Results: The dataset comprised 1,233 daily observations (411 days) of new confirmed COVID-19 cases from November 24, 2021, to January 8, 2023. The earlier Omicron wave in Portugal supplied transmission dynamics that improved forecasts in the later-onset regions of Macau and Guangdong. The FL model achieved a normalized Root Mean Square Error of 0.028, an 88.3% improvement over the local model (0.240) and comparable to the centralized model, with network overhead mainly determined by model size and aggregation rounds.
  Conclusion: FL achieved short-term forecasting accuracy close to centralized training while maintaining data residency, with acceptable overhead. Leveraging asynchronous outbreaks across regions may offer a practical path to privacy-preserving collaborative forecasting, though prospective validation is required.

</details>


### [114] [MMDM: Measuring Massive Multitask Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14309)
*Junhao Shen,Qing Zong,Yue Guo,Zhanqiu Zhang,Yiwen Guo,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MMDM 基准，含 16 类任务、5,448 道高质量题目，覆盖单/多/软标签决策，选项空间 2–1,024、输入长达 64,817 tokens，用于评估 System-1 决策模型在长上下文、大选项空间与复杂逻辑依赖下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: System-1 决策模型（单次前向直接输出结构化概率决策）的现有评测多依赖既有基准，模型可能已接触过测试材料，难以反映真实泛化；同时现有人工基准未能同时刻画长上下文、大选项空间与复杂逻辑依赖这三大现实挑战。

Method: 构建 MMDM 基准：5,448 题、16 类任务，结合新构造任务与基于来源材料的重组/改写，在全新上下文中提供单选、多选与软标签决策；场景含智能体状态追踪、工具与技能选择、偏好判断、评分量表打分；选项数 2–1,024，输入最长 64,817 tokens。基于该基准对 28 个决策模型做定量评测。

Result: 模型在世界知识类常见问题上表现较好，但在相互依赖选项、约束决策与多选任务上明显下降：Perplexity Decider-V1.2 在 Hard Single-select 世界知识上达 78.75%，而在选项逻辑仅 53.95%、约束规划仅 47.95%；全部模型最佳 Hard Multi-select Accuracy 仅 36.81%。

Conclusion: 联合推理、约束感知选择与多选完整召回是决策模型发展的关键方向；MMDM 为衡量此类进展提供可复用的测试平台，弥补现有基准在防污染与任务综合难度上的不足。

Abstract: System-1 Decision Models directly make structured probabilistic decisions over query-specified options given a state in a single forward pass, without autoregressively generating reasoning trajectories or textual responses. Recent evaluations cover diverse tasks but often rely on existing benchmarks, where prior exposure to test material can obscure generalization. Moreover, real-world decision making often involves long contexts, large option spaces, and complex logical dependencies, challenges that existing benchmarks do not adequately capture together. Thus, we introduce MMDM (Massive Multitask Decision Making), a benchmark of 5,448 high-quality questions across 16 task categories, combining newly constructed tasks with source-grounded recompositions and rewrites for single-select, multi-select, and soft-label decision making in fresh contexts. It covers realistic scenarios such as agent state tracking, tool and skill selection, preference judgments, and rubric scoring, with option spaces ranging from 2 to 1,024 and inputs extending to 64,817 tokens. With MMDM, we conduct a comprehensive quantitative evaluation of 28 decision models, showing that many perform well on common knowledge yet struggle with interdependent options, constrained decisions, and multi-select tasks. For example, Perplexity Decider-V1.2 reaches 78.75% Hard Single-select Accuracy on world knowledge, but only 53.95% on option logic and 47.95% on constraint planning, while the best Hard Multi-select Accuracy across all models is 36.81%. These results highlight joint reasoning, constraint-aware selection, and complete multi-select recovery as key priorities for decision model development, with MMDM providing a testbed for measuring this progress.

</details>


### [115] [PSL-RGHA: Parallel Split Learning with Reference Gradient Half-Space Alignment for Mitigating Gradient Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14312)
*Han Li,Zheng Lin,Miao Yang,Zehang Lin,Jing Yang,Zihan Fang,Qiyang Zhang,Zhaolu Kang,Jianmin Li,Lin Li,Mingwei Hong,Shunzhi Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PSL-RGHA框架，通过参考梯度半空间对齐机制缓解并行拆分学习在异构数据下的训练发散问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络复杂度上升，使联邦学习在资源受限设备（尤其图像分类、语音识别等多媒体场景）上难以部署。并行拆分学习（PSL）通过逐层模型划分将大部分计算卸载到服务器，适配资源受限场景，但缺少客户端侧聚合，在异构数据分布下易出现严重训练发散甚至收敛失败。

Method: PSL-RGHA包含两个组件：参考梯度渐进构造（RGPC），评估客户端梯度方向一致性，并通过Weiszfeld更新逐步合成稳健参考梯度，近似全局下降方向；梯度半空间对齐（GHA），将客户端梯度约束在RGPC定义的参考半空间内，以缓解梯度冲突并修正本地更新。

Result: 大量实验表明，PSL-RGHA在多种设置下均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 梯度对齐机制可有效抑制PSL在异构数据下的训练发散，为资源受限场景下的拆分学习提供了有效的收敛性改进方案。

Abstract: The escalating complexity of deep neural networks hinders the deployment of federated learning (FL) on resource-constrained devices, especially for multimedia applications such as on-device image classification and speech recognition. Parallel split learning (PSL) has emerged as a promising solution by offloading a substantial portion of the computational workload to the server through layer-wise model partitioning, making it particularly suitable for resource-limited visual and speech intelligence scenarios. However, the absence of client-side aggregation renders PSL sus ceptible to severe training divergence and potential convergence failure under heterogeneous data distributions. To address this challenge, we propose PSL-RGHA, a gradient-aligned PSL framework that employs reference gradient half-space alignment mechanism to mitigate training divergence among client devices. PSL-RGHA comprises two novel components: reference gradient progressive construction (RGPC) and gradient half-space alignment (GHA). Specifically, RGPC evaluates the directional consistency of client gradients and progressively synthesizes a robust reference gradient via Weiszfeld updates, effectively approximating the global descent direction. Then, GHA constrains client gradients within the reference half-space defined by RGPC to mitigate gradient conflicts and rectify local updates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our PSL-RGHA framework outperforms existing baselines in various settings.

</details>


### [116] [Auditing the Judge: Internal Signals for LLM Reasoning Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14323)
*Mayank Gulati,Gerhard Wunder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文比较了语言模型“判决词分数”与基于注意力头激活训练的有监督正确性探针在固定预算下的错误排序能力，发现探针在域内能显著提升固定审查预算下的错误召回，但跨数据集迁移性差，并非通用置信度估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型系统需通过自我判断决定答案的发布、复核或重算，而干预成本高昂，因此保障分数必须在实际可用预算内有效区分错误，而非仅在全排序上表现好（AUROC 只反映全预算平均召回）。作者据此主张以固定预算下的错误召回与残余风险为核心指标来评估这类判断信号。

Method: 提出用精确的恒等关系将固定预算错误召回与残余风险联系起来，并指出 AUROC 等价于在所有预算上对召回取平均。实验上，在同一提示下比较两种信号：（1）判定词上的连续分数（判决分数）；（2）利用注意力头激活训练的有监督正确性探针。在三个 7–12B 开源模型、GSM8K 与 MATH 上评估 5% 与 10% 预算下的审查/路由表现，并做双向跨数据集迁移测试。

Result: 在全部六个“模型–数据集”设定中，5% 审查预算下探针比判决分数多检出 1.3–22.5 个百分点的错误（中位数 7.4 点）。在 10% 路由预算下，探针引导的分配在两个 GSM8K 实验中均优于等成本随机分配，但仅对较弱判决模型优于判决引导路由。跨数据集迁移在双向、所有模型上都降低了探针 AUROC；由于数据集、权重精度与生成预算同时变化，该结果反映的是联合协议偏移而非纯数据集效应。

Conclusion: 内部信号可改善有监督的、域内的固定预算分配，但并非域通用的置信度估计器；评估保障分数应以目标预算下的错误召回/残余风险为准，而非仅报告 AUROC。

Abstract: Language-model systems use self-judgment to decide which answers to release, review, or recompute. Because intervention is costly, an assurance score must rank errors well at the available budget, not merely over the full ranking. We compare the judge's continuous verdict-token score with a supervised correctness probe trained on attention-head activations from the same prompt. Exact identities link fixed-budget error recall to residual risk and show that AUROC averages recall over all budgets. Across three open 7-12B models on GSM8K and MATH, the probe finds 1.3-22.5 additional percentage points of errors at 5% review in all six model-dataset settings (median 7.4 points). At 10% routing, probe-guided allocation improves accuracy over equal-cost random allocation in both GSM8K experiments, but beats judge-guided routing only for the weaker judge. Cross-dataset transfer lowers probe AUROC for every model in both directions relative to target-domain training. Because dataset, weight precision, and generation budget change together, this measures a joint protocol shift rather than a dataset-only effect. Internal signals can improve supervised, in-domain budget allocation, but are not domain-general confidence estimators.

</details>


### [117] [Forward-Value Discretization: Faster Stochastic Diffusion Sampling Without High-Order Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14328)
*Yuchen Jiao,Na Li,Changxiao Cai,Yuting Wei,Gen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明随机扩散采样器即使采用高阶离散化仍是"一阶收敛"（给出匹配的上界与最坏情形下界），据此提出无需训练的"前向取值离散化"框架，构造F-DPMSolver与F-DDPM，在每次迭代仅一次NFE下达到一阶收敛保证，并在多个数据集上以前向DDPM实现同NFE预算下更优FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机扩散采样要达到与确定性采样相当的样本质量，往往需要多得多的神经函数评估（NFE）。确定性采样可通过高阶离散化加速，但高阶离散化对随机采样的收益此前并不清楚；作者希望厘清其收敛阶本质，并在不改训练的前提下缩小随机与确定性采样之间的实际效率差距。

Method: 1) 理论分析：建立匹配的上界与最坏情形下界，证明即使得分函数精确，带有高阶近似的随机采样器仍只能保持一阶收敛，因此提升近似阶数一般不改善收敛阶。2) 提出forward-value discretization（前向取值离散化）训练无关框架：基于先前预测构造lookahead估计，并在该点上评估数据/噪声预测模型。3) 确定性采样：得到Forward DPMSolver（F-DPMSolver），其基于DDIM的变体在低NFE预算下提升样本质量（但仍为一阶收敛）。4) 随机采样：提出Forward DDPM（F-DDPM），在经后续噪声注入校正后的更"干净"lookahead点评估模型，该估计同时复用上一次模型预测与上一次注入的噪声；两者均在一次模型评估/迭代下具备一阶收敛保证。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、ImageNet、FFHQ与LSUN上，F-DDPM在相同NFE预算下相较所评估的随机采样基线持续获得更低FID；当NFE为确定性基线的1.5倍时达到有竞争力的样本质量，从而缩小随机采样与确定性采样之间的实际效率差距。

Conclusion: 高阶离散化无法普遍提升随机扩散采样的收敛阶，这是其效率劣势的根源之一；前向取值离散化是一种免训练、每次迭代仅一次NFE且具一阶收敛保证的通用改进手段，在确定性与随机采样两条路线上均有效，显著提升了随机采样的NFE效率。

Abstract: Stochastic diffusion samplers often require substantially more neural function evaluations (NFEs) than deterministic samplers to achieve comparable sample quality. Although higher-order discretization accelerates deterministic sampling, its benefits for stochastic sampling remains unclear. We establish matching upper and worst-case lower bounds showing that stochastic samplers with higher-order approximations remain first-order convergent, even with exact score functions. Thus, increasing the approximation order does not generally improve the convergence order. Motivated by this limitation, we introduce forward-value discretization, a training-free framework that evaluates the data/noise prediction model at a lookahead estimate constructed from previous predictions. For deterministic sampling, the resulting Forward DPMSolver (F-DPMSolver), improves sample quality at low NFE budgets through its DDIM-based variant, despite remaining first-order convergent. For stochastic sampling, we develop Forward DDPM (F-DDPM), which evaluates the model at a cleaner lookahead point adjusted for subsequent noise injection. This estimate reuses both the previous model prediction and the previously injected noise. Both variants admit first-order convergence guarantees with one model evaluation per iteration. Across CIFAR-10, ImageNet, FFHQ, and LSUN, F-DDPM consistently achieves lower FID than the evaluated stochastic baselines at matched NFE budgets. With 1.5 times as many NFEs as deterministic baselines, it achieves competitive sample quality, narrowing the practical efficiency gap between stochastic and deterministic sampling. Code is available at https://github.com/Na-Li66/F-DDPM.

</details>


### [118] [The Unused Input: The Initial State as a Conditioning Interface for Recurrent TSFMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14357)
*Bernhard Voggenberger,Christian Ganhör,Levente Zólyomi,Sebastian Böck,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究循环式时间序列基础模型中"初始循环状态"这一被常规忽略的输入，通过冻结主干、仅训练小型头部将上下文向量写入初始状态，证明频谱摘要与文本嵌入注入均可带来稳定但小幅的零样本预测提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环主干的时间序列基础模型（如xLSTM-based TiRex）每步计算代价恒定、支持长上下文且自带状态，但预测时仅使用观测上下文窗口，初始循环状态按惯例被闲置。作者希望验证初始状态能否被有效利用，且希望将精度变化归因于状态注入本身而非权重适配。

Method: 以TiRex为案例，冻结主干，仅训练一个小的头部将上下文向量写入初始循环状态。方案一：对输入窗口做频谱摘要，一次性学习后对所有序列通用；方案二：编码与序列绑定的文本嵌入。对照实验包括参数匹配的LoRA适配器与可学习的静态状态；评估指标为GiftEval MASE与六个MoTime数据集，并做模块/上下文长度的消融分析。

Result: 频谱摘要状态使GiftEval平均MASE降低0.175%，且所有随机种子均改善，参数匹配LoRA与可学习静态状态均无法复现；作为逐数据集适配面，静态状态相对零样本基线降低MASE 0.04%–6.96%（相当于全量微调增益的8%–70%）；作为外生信息通道，文本条件状态在六数据集中的四个上进一步降低0.15%–0.41%，其中一个数据集上与LoRA结合后超过无文本输入的全量微调。效应集中于mLSTM块，且在短上下文时最强。

Conclusion: 初始循环状态在循环式TSFM中是可以被利用且具有信息价值的接口；作者强调结果尚属初步、提升幅度较小，但为后续研究指明了初始状态这一方向。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) forecast unseen series without task-specific training. Increasingly they are built on recurrent backbones, whose constant per-step cost makes long-context inference cheap, and which, unlike transformers, carry a state. A recurrent backbone computes its prediction from two inputs: the observed context window and the initial recurrent state. In practice only the first is utilised and the initial state is unused by convention. We ask whether the initial state can be utilised in recurrent TSFMs. Using the xLSTM-based zero-shot forecaster TiRex as a case study, we freeze the backbone and train only a small head that writes a context vector into the initial state, so that any change in accuracy is attributable to the injected state rather than to adapted weights. A spectral summary of the input window, learned once and applied unchanged to every series, lowers mean GiftEval MASE by 0.175%, with every seed improving, which neither a parameter-matched LoRA adapter nor a learnable static state reproduces. An embedding of text attached to each series, evaluated on six MoTime datasets, reveals two roles for the state. As a per-dataset adaptation surface, a static state lowers MASE by 0.04-6.96% of the zero-shot baseline (8-70% of the gain of full fine-tuning). As a channel for exogenous information, the text-conditioned state lowers it by a further 0.15-0.41% of the zero-shot baseline on four of six datasets and, combined with a LoRA adapter, exceeds full fine-tuning, which has no access to the text, on one of them. In TiRex, the effect concentrates in the mLSTM blocks and is strongest at short context. The results are preliminary and the improvements small, but they point to the initial state as a direction for further research.

</details>


### [119] [Uncertainty-Aware Hybrid Offline Reinforcement Learning with Online Action Selection Enabled by Sparse Gaussian-Process Critics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14374)
*Marco Chan,Christos Evripidou,Liqiu Dong,Benoit Chachuat,Mehmet Mercangöz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文考察 HOFLON 中 AAE 惩罚项的作用，并用稀疏变分高斯过程（sGP）critic 替代树集成 critic 与 AAE，在聚合反应器开车基准上验证不确定性调节系数 κ 对安全性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开车与牌号切换仍主要依赖操作员，而为基于模型的控制开发并维护动态模型需大量工程投入；同时需明确 HOFLON 在线目标中 AAE 惩罚项是否必要、以及能否用更 principled 的不确定性建模替代。

Method: 基于历史转移数据的一步离线强化学习选动作；对照实验：(1) 去掉 AAE 惩罚；(2) 分析惩罚与树集成 critic 输出波动的量级及其对近似等价候选动作的影响（配对运行）；(3) 用 Matérn-5/2 与平方指数 sGP critic 替代原 critic，动作最大化 μ−κσ（一次动作后沿历史操作实践的预测回报），扫描 κ∈{0,3} 及核/诱导输入设置。

Result: 去掉 AAE 后，五个 critic 共 15 次运行中 4 次开车失败；但按发表权重，AAE 惩罚的波动比树集成 critic 小四个数量级，仅在候选预测回报几乎相等时改变动作，配对运行从第一步即分叉。Matérn-5/2 sGP 下 κ=0 时 15 次中 13 次失败，κ=3 时 15 次零失败；回报中位数/最小值为 13,464/13,398（HOFLON 为 13,393/12,411）。学习诱导输入的平方指数 sGP 需 κ≥3 才能保持在稳态数据经验支撑内，随后稳定于 2.0 K 温度偏差。

Conclusion: AAE 惩罚的作用不在量级而在于打破近似平局时的决策并列，具有实际安全价值；用 sGP 显式不确定性惩罚（μ−κσ）可替代它，且 κ 是关键安全超参数——κ 过小导致高失败率，κ=3 可完全避免失败并取得更高且更集中的回报。

Abstract: Start-ups and grade changes remain largely operator-driven, while developing and maintaining dynamic models for model-based control requires substantial engineering effort. Hybrid Offline Learning and Online Optimisation (HOFLON) uses one-step offline reinforcement learning to select actions from historical transition data. In its original implementation, the online objective combines the predicted return from a tree-ensemble critic with an adversarial-autoencoder (AAE) penalty for unfamiliar state-action pairs. We examine the effect of this penalty and replace the critic and AAE with a sparse variational Gaussian-process (sGP) critic. Actions maximise $μ- κσ$, an uncertainty-adjusted estimate of the return from one action followed by the historical operating practice. On the HOFLON polymerisation-reactor benchmark, removing the AAE from HOFLON led to 4 failed start-ups in 15 runs over five critics. At its published weight, the variation in the AAE penalty is four orders of magnitude smaller than the variation in the tree-ensemble critic. Nevertheless, it changes actions when the predicted returns of competing candidates are nearly equal, and paired runs with and without the penalty diverged from the first move. With a Matérn-5/2 sGP critic, $κ= 0$ led to failure in 13 of 15 runs, whereas with $κ= 3$ none of 15 runs (five critics, three noise realisations) failed. The median and lowest returns were 13,464 and 13,398 (HOFLON: 13,393 and 12,411). A squared-exponential sGP critic with learned inducing inputs needed $κ\geq 3$ to remain within the empirical data support in steady state, and then settled with a temperature offset of 2.0 K.

</details>


### [120] [A Margin Perspective on LoRA: Robustness to Catastrophic Forgetting and Adapter Merging (MaLoRA)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14397)
*Ziqing Xu,Hancheng Min,Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,Salma Tarmoun,Enrique Mallada,Weijie Su,Rene Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从margin角度理论解释了LoRA的两大经验性质（抗灾难性遗忘、可合并），并在多类线性分类、近正交任务与ℓ2正则下刻画最优LoRA适配器，据此提出免训练的合并规则MaLoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA被观察到具有对灾难性遗忘的鲁棒性以及可将独立训练的适配器合并为多任务模型的特性，这两点对持续学习与多任务学习至关重要，但缺乏理论理解。

Method: 在多类线性分类、近正交任务设定下，结合ℓ2正则分析LoRA，按正则强度分区间刻画最优适配器：中间正则区间内最优适配器对齐微调数据上的最大间隔（max-margin）解；进而推导预训练与微调数据上margin的闭式表达，刻画保持与适应的权衡；并分析适配器合并，证明合并模型在各任务上具有正margin，导出使各任务margin均衡且在并集上最大化margin的最优混合系数，形成免训练合并规则MaLoRA。

Result: 得到预训练/微调数据margin的闭式表达，揭示正则参数控制保持与适应的权衡；证明合并模型在各任务上margin为正，并给出平衡各任务margin、最大化并集margin的最优混合系数；在现代架构与真实数据集的视觉、语言分类任务上实验验证理论预测，MaLoRA匹配或优于多种适配器合并基线。

Conclusion: 基于margin的统一视角可解释LoRA抗遗忘与可合并性，并直接导出简单、免训练的合并方法MaLoRA，理论与实验一致。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is the de facto method for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large neural networks, achieving strong task adaptation with minimal trainable parameters and low optimization cost. Beyond this efficiency, practitioners have observed two striking properties: \textit{robustness to catastrophic forgetting} and the ability to \textit{merge independently trained adapters} into a single model that performs competitively across multiple tasks. These properties are central to continual and multi-task learning, yet remain poorly understood. In this work, we provide a theoretical explanation through a unified \textit{margin-based perspective}. We analyze LoRA in multiclass linear classification under a near-orthogonal task regime with $\ell_2$-regularization, and characterize the optimal LoRA adapter across regularization regimes. Our analysis shows that, in an intermediate regime of regularization parameters, the optimal adapter aligns with the \emph{max-margin solution} on the fine-tuning data. Building on this characterization, we derive two key consequences. First, we obtain closed-form expressions for the margins on pre-training and fine-tuning data, revealing a precise margin trade-off: the regularization parameter controls the balance between retention and adaptation. Second, we analyze adapter merging, proving that merged models achieve positive margin on each task and deriving optimal mixing coefficients that balance margins across tasks and maximize the margin over their union. These results lead to a simple, training-free merging rule, which we term \textit{margin-based LoRA merging} (MaLoRA). Experiments on modern architectures and real datasets validate our theoretical predictions, showing that MaLoRA matches or outperforms several adapter-merging baselines across a range of vision and language classification tasks.

</details>


### [121] [REAL Loss: Optimizing Real Expected Acceptance Length for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14399)
*Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出REAL——一种"真实期望接受长度(EAL)"的训练损失，用路径权重实现无偏估计、用Rao-Blackwell化降方差，在不改架构与推理流程的前提下提升投机解码的接受长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码的加速比由期望接受长度(EAL)决定，但交叉熵、KL散度、全变差等标准训练目标只关注分布匹配或单步重叠；近期的块级训练目标虽试图直接优化EAL，却仍是代理量而非EAL的精确形式。例如某些目标在目标分布连续段上对后续接受率取平均，而非按被接受token所选的验证分支进行，产生协方差间隙，甚至可能使梯度方向反转。

Method: 先分析上述协方差间隙，据此推导出REAL损失：以路径权重对EAL做无偏估计，并引入Rao-Blackwell化降低估计方差。仅修改训练损失，草稿模型架构与推理过程保持不变。实验在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B目标模型与Domino草稿模型上做受控的损失级对比，与WTV、VSD、DBLast及静态LK基线比较概率化服务下的接受长度，覆盖9个任务子集、5个评估种子。

Result: 在9个任务子集与5个评估种子上，REAL在概率化服务接受长度指标上优于WTV、VSD、DBLast与静态LK基线。

Conclusion: 精确建模EAL并修正代理目标的偏差可带来稳定的接受长度增益，且实现代价低（仅改损失函数），说明"真实EAL训练"具有实际可用性；作者开源了REAL损失参考实现。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by having a draft model propose multiple tokens for the target model to verify in one pass. The speedup is governed by the expected acceptance length (EAL), yet standard training objectives such as cross-entropy, KL divergence, and total variation focus only on distribution matching or single-step overlap. Recent studies instead propose block-level training objectives, aiming to optimize EAL directly. However, these objectives remain surrogates rather than exact formulations of EAL. For example, some average later acceptance over target-distributed continuations rather than the verification branches selected by accepted tokens, introducing a covariance gap that can reverse the gradient direction. We analyze this gap and derive REAL, a real EAL training loss that uses path weights for unbiased estimation and Rao-Blackwellization for variance reduction. In a controlled loss-level comparison with a Qwen3.6-35B-A3B target and a Domino drafter, REAL outperforms WTV, VSD, DBLast, and the static LK baseline on probabilistic serving acceptance length across 9 task subsets and 5 evaluation seeds. These gains require only a change to the training loss, with no modification to the drafter architecture or inference procedure, demonstrating the practicality of real EAL training. A reference implementation of the REAL loss is available at https://github.com/King-ty/speculators/tree/real-loss.

</details>


### [122] [Discovering Equations for the Thermodynamics of South Asian Monsoon Precipitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14401)
*Savannah L. Ferretti,Jane W. Baldwin,Michael S. Pritchard,Tom Beucler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究用"符号蒸馏"（神经网络+符号回归）从21年夏季再分析资料中发现可解释的南亚季风降水方程，其解释方差为常用浮力诊断的1.5倍，以约千分之一的参数恢复约85%的神经网络技巧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南亚季风降水依赖低层大气的热力结构，但这种依赖在垂直方向上是非局地的（nonlocal in the vertical），各要素如何组合决定降水仍不清楚，缺乏可解释且物理一致的降水诊断关系。

Method: 构建"符号蒸馏"框架：先用神经网络学习与降水最相关的垂直热力结构，将每个变量压缩为不绑定特定垂直网格层的单一可解释特征；再由符号回归结合这些特征与地表状态，推导闭式（closed-form）降水方程；并与浮力诊断、神经网络进行技巧与参数效率对比，检验方程各项响应的物理一致性。

Result: 最佳方程解释的降水方差为广泛使用的浮力诊断的1.5倍，在参数少约三个数量级的情况下恢复神经网络约85%的技巧。与将水汽与不稳定度相加的浮力诊断不同，方程将二者视为相互竞争的控制因子，最强降水出现在低层自由对流层湿润时；在海洋上，感热加热主要在当前近地面空气温暖湿润时才伴随额外降水，并改善降水及其日循环的预测。

Conclusion: 尽管特征仅为降水预测而优化，方程对各特征的响应仍大体符合对流物理并与再分析一致。这些响应把季风降水对整个垂直气柱的依赖约化为少数简单、可检验的约束，可用于指导数据驱动降水模型，使其更具物理一致性。

Abstract: South Asian monsoon precipitation depends on the thermodynamic structure of the lower troposphere, but how its ingredients combine to shape precipitation remains unclear, partly because this dependence is nonlocal in the vertical. We use symbolic distillation, which combines neural networks and symbolic regression, to discover interpretable precipitation equations from 21 years of June-August reanalysis data. Neural networks first learn the vertical thermodynamic structure most relevant to precipitation, condensing each variable into a single interpretable feature not tied to a particular vertical grid. Then, symbolic regression derives closed-form equations from these features and the surface state. Our best equation explains 1.5 times as much precipitation variance as a widely used buoyancy-based diagnostic and recovers about 85% of neural network skill while using about a thousand times fewer parameters. Unlike the buoyancy diagnostic, which adds moisture and instability, the equations treat them as competing controls, with the heaviest precipitation occurring when the lower free troposphere is humid. Over ocean, sensible heating is associated with additional precipitation mainly when near-surface air is warm and moist, improving predicted precipitation and its diurnal cycle. Although the learned features were optimized only for precipitation prediction, the equations largely respond to each feature as expected from convective physics, consistent with the reanalysis. These responses reduce the dependence of monsoon precipitation on the entire vertical column to a few simple, testable constraints that could guide data-driven precipitation models to make them more physically consistent.

</details>


### [123] [Causal Concept Explanations for Deep Neural Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14407)
*Josh Rountree,Pulkit Verma,Oswin So,Chuchu Fan,Julie A. Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CCW-Net，首次将干预式中介分析作为训练目标，使基于概念的解释对模型输出具备必要性、充分性与独立性，并可作为量化因果归因的评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经模型的概念解释仍停留在相关性层面，缺少引导与量化概念-输出因果关系的工具，导致某些概念解释看似正确却既非输出所必需也非充分，具有误导性。

Method: 将经济学、医学、流行病学中的干预式中介分析引入作为训练目标：通过对概念表示进行反事实干预，构造必要性（无关概念效应趋于零）、充分性（相关概念表示可完整重构输出）与独立性（总效应在概念间线性可分解）三条因果对齐准则，约束模型的concept representation；同时把中介分析作为评估工具，按概念标签组合量化各概念解释对模型输出的因果贡献比例。

Result: 在飞行器控制、自动驾驶与图像分类任务上对CCW-Net进行了评估，模型的概念表示满足上述因果对齐准则，并可用中介分析量化各概念的因果归因。

Conclusion: CCW-Net使概念解释不仅是可解释的，而是可量化因果的，有助于透明可信神经模型的安全评估、认证与部署。

Abstract: Concept-based explanations in neural models are important for the development, certification, and adoption of autonomous systems. Yet, interpretability remains correlational. Without methods to guide and quantify causal relationships between explanations, a concept explanation can be provided without being necessary or sufficient for the observed output thereby appearing correct while misleading. We introduce Causal Concept Wrapper Network (CCW-Net), the first use of interventional mediation analysis from fields such as economics, medicine, and epidemiology as a training objective to align causal effects of concept-based explanations with a model's output. We further apply mediation analysis as an evaluation tool to quantify, per concept label combination, how much of any model's output is causally attributable to each concept explanation. As a training objective, CCW-Net shapes a model's concept representations such that they are both necessary and sufficient for the outputs they are credited with and independent of other concepts' contributions. Counterfactual concept representations anchor a set of causal alignment criteria: necessity, sufficiency, and independence. Necessity drives irrelevant concept effects toward zero, sufficiency ensures relevant concept representations fully reconstruct a model's output, and independence enforces the total effect decomposes linearly across concepts. We evaluate CCW-Net across aircraft control, driving, and image classification. CCW-Net provides a step toward concept-based explanations that are not merely interpretable, but quantifiably causal, supporting safer evaluation, certification, and deployment of transparent and trustworthy neural models.

</details>


### [124] [DnNP: Denoising Input Uncertainty in Neural Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14424)
*Fatemeh Tohidian,Chengzhi Shi,Soomi Lee,Matthew Elia,Amy Mueller,Stratis Ioannidis,Jennifer Dy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出去噪神经过程（DnNPs），在标准神经过程框架中显式建模输入噪声，通过变分推断联合学习去噪与元学习，从而在含噪数据上获得更优的预测与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经过程（NP）假设输入无噪声，但实际中传感器噪声与测量误差不可避免，模型假设与真实数据的差异会显著损害预测质量与不确定性估计。因此需要将输入不确定性纳入NP框架。

Method: 提出DnNP，作为NP框架的原理性扩展，显式建模输入不确定性；采用变分推断进行训练，使模型联合学习对含噪输入去噪并完成元学习；同时给出理论分析：证明含噪输入下的贝叶斯最优预测器属于DnNP模型类，且固定观测方差的plug-in模型存在不可消除的KL散度差距，而DnNP可证明地避免该差距。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上均优于标准NP，预测与不确定性量化一致改善；在真实空气质量传感器的PM2.5空间插值任务上，相较标准NP，RMSE最多降低30%，NLL最多降低73%。

Conclusion: 显式建模输入不确定性可使神经过程对含噪训练数据更具鲁棒性，并在理论与实证上均优于标准NP，为含噪输入场景下的元学习与不确定性量化提供了有理论基础的方法。

Abstract: Neural Processes (NPs) are flexible meta-learning models capable of uncertainty quantification across diverse applications. However, the standard NP framework assumes access to noise-free inputs. This is unrealistic in many practical settings, where sensor noise or measurement errors are unavoidable. This discrepancy between model assumptions and real-world data can significantly degrade prediction quality and uncertainty estimates. We introduce Denoising Neural Processes (DnNPs), a principled extension of the NP framework that explicitly models input uncertainty. We train DnNPs via variational inference. DnNPs jointly learn to denoise corrupted inputs and perform meta-learning, making them robust to noisy training data. We demonstrate DnNPs' effectiveness on both synthetic and real-world datasets, showing consistent improvements in prediction and uncertainty quantification compared to standard NPs. On PM2.5 spatial interpolation from real-world air quality sensors, DnNP reduces RMSE by up to 30% and NLL by up to 73% relative to standard NPs. We also provide theoretical grounding: we show that the Bayes-optimal predictor under noisy inputs belongs to the DnNP model class, and that plug-in models with fixed observation variance incur an irreducible KL divergence gap that DnNP provably avoids.

</details>


### [125] [Quantifying the Sampling Frequency Invariance of Time-Series Foundation Models for Sensor Data Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14425)
*Sourish Gunesh Dhekane,Abinaya Kumar,Torsten Woertwein,Yan-Bo Lin,Thomas Ploetz,Shabnam Ghaffarzadegan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将采样频率不变性（SF-invariance）置于时间序列基础模型（TSFM）评估的核心，对 MOMENT、Chronos-2、Moirai-MoE、TimesFM 四个模型进行压力测试，并证明该性质应被视为可度量、依赖信号与模型组合的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器应用中采样频率（SF）随设备与工作模式（如省电）变化，而 TSFM 被期望在 SF 变化时仍保持有效。既有评估缺乏对 SF 不变性的系统量化，也未区分性能下降究竟源于信息带宽损失还是模型能力局限。

Method: 对 4 个主流 TSFM（MOMENT、Chronos-2、Moirai-MoE、TimesFM）开展沿三个维度的压力测试：(i) 嵌入空间漂移、(ii) 预测性能退化、(iii) 下游任务性能退化；并分别提出三个新指标 FSI、FDI、CDI 予以量化。实验设计以信号原生 SF 与任务所需带宽之间的裕度为控制变量。

Result: 刻画三点结论：(i) SF 不变性取决于信号原生 SF 与任务所需带宽之间的裕度；(ii) 量化了该不变性中有多少可由 TSFM 选型解释；(iii) 分解了低 SF 下性能退化中有多少来自信息损失、多少来自模型能力限制。

Conclusion: SF 不变性应被重新定义为（信号×TSFM）组合的可度量、信号感知属性，而非模型的固定特性；研究结果为从业者判断 TSFM 何时具备 SF 不变性提供指导。

Abstract: Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) are increasingly used in sensor applications, where sampling frequency (SF) varies across sensors, devices, and operating modes (e.g., power saving). Ideally, TSFMs must remain effective during such SF changes, provided the SF retains task-critical bandwidth. In this paper, we stress-test 4 prominent TSFMs: MOMENT, Chronos-2, Moirai-MoE, and TimesFM, to quantify their SF-invariance and investigate whether their behavior tracks the resulting loss of information bandwidth rather than the SF value itself. Our stress-test probes 3 dimensions: (i) the shift in embeddings, (ii) the degradation in forecasting performance, and (iii) the degradation in downstream performance, quantified by three novel metrics: FSI, FDI, and CDI, respectively. To our knowledge, this is the first study to place SF-invariance at the core of TSFM evaluations. Our results characterize: (i) how SF-invariance depends on the margin between signal's native-SF and the bandwidth its task requires, (ii) how much of that invariance the choice of TSFM explains, and (iii) how much of TSFM's performance degradation under a lower SF reflects information loss rather than a limitation of their abilities. These findings reframe SF-invariance as a measurable, signal-aware property of the (signal X TSFM) pair, and guide practitioners on when TSFMs display SF-invariance.

</details>


### [126] [Accurate predictions of properties of energetic materials with tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14462)
*Vagn Krister Nes Glomsrud,Mathias Bynke,Lars Henry Berge Olsen,Dennis Christensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将表格基础模型 TabPFN 用于含能材料的撞击感度、分解温度和晶体密度三项性质预测，无需调参即在三项任务上超越已发表最优模型及 XGBoost、随机森林、高斯过程等主流机器学习模型，并具备内置不确定性量化与 Shapley 值可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含能材料的关键性质（撞击感度、分解温度、晶体密度）预测对安全与设计至关重要，但现有机器学习方法通常依赖繁琐的超参数调优与人工特征工程，且缺乏可靠的不确定性量化手段，因此需要评估新兴表格基础模型能否以零调参方式取得更优且更可信的预测。

Method: 以 TabPFN 为核心模型，分别在手工分子描述符与 Chemprop 消息传递神经网络学习到的表示（embedding）上训练，预测撞击感度、分解温度与晶体密度；与已发表最优模型、调优的 XGBoost、随机森林、高斯过程等基线对比，考察精度与运行速度；利用 TabPFN 输出的预测分布进行不确定性量化，并与重复测量化合物的经验分布对照；最后用 Shapley 值对特征重要性进行解释。

Result: TabPFN 在无需任何超参数调优的情况下，对三项性质均优于已发表的最佳模型，并显著优于各类成熟机器学习模型；在手工描述符与 Chemprop 嵌入两种输入下结论一致；尤其在三项性质上，基于 Chemprop 嵌入的 TabPFN 甚至比 Chemprop 自身预测更准确；同时 TabPFN 是所考察模型中最快的方法之一，速度比 XGBoost 快一个数量级以上；其输出的完整预测分布所给出的不确定性量化与重复测量经验分布具有可比性。

Conclusion: TabPFN 可作为含能材料性质预测的高精度、免调参、快速且自带不确定性量化的通用方案，配合 Shapley 值可提供特征层面的可解释性，显示出表格基础模型在小样本材料数据场景中的实用价值。

Abstract: We use TabPFN, a recently developed tabular foundation model, to predict three properties of energetic materials: impact sensitivity, decomposition temperature and crystal density. Without any hyperparameter tuning, TabPFN outperforms the best published models for all three properties. It also substantially outperforms a wide range of established machine-learning models, including tuned gradient boosting (XGBoost), random forests and Gaussian processes. This holds both when the models are given hand-crafted molecular descriptors and when they are given learned representations from Chemprop's message-passing neural network. In particular, for all three properties, TabPFN applied to Chemprop's own embedding is more accurate than Chemprop's predictions. TabPFN is also among the fastest models we consider, more than an order of magnitude faster than XGBoost. As TabPFN predicts the entire distribution of the target rather than just a single value, it offers built-in uncertainty quantification, which we compare with the empirical distributions of compounds with repeated measurements. Finally, we interpret the predictions using Shapley values, which rank the features by their importance.

</details>


### [127] [A Theory of Policy Optimization in Post-Training: Coverage Collapse and Reference Resets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14467)
*Audrey Huang,Nan Jiang,Sham M. Kakade,Sadhika Malladi,Akshay Krishnamurthy,Dylan J. Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视语言模型后训练的策略优化理论基础，提出"覆盖保持"(coverage-preserving)算法概念，揭示REINFORCE类方法(PPO/GRPO基础)存在覆盖崩塌问题，并通过APMD元算法与参考模型重置给出首个全局收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略优化是语言模型后训练的核心，但缺乏原理性指导。作者希望识别一类算法：其总样本量与梯度步数由基座模型的"响应覆盖度"(即基座模型生成最优响应的容易程度)所决定，从而捕捉"放大基座模型采样可得能力"的目标。

Method: 1) 分析REINFORCE的覆盖崩塌现象：即使奖励提升，最优响应概率也可能趋近于零，收敛需指数级步数，且在精确梯度与KL正则下依然存在；2) 提出参考重置干预，形式化为近似策略镜像下降(APMD)——近似求解一系列KL正则化RL子问题的元算法；3) 针对预训练特征上的softmax线性策略，用APMD建立全局收敛保证。

Result: 证明APMD使更强正则化成为可能(子问题更易求解)，而参考重置保证收敛到全局最优；首次证明PPO、GRPO变体(显式KL正则+参考重置)以及Kimi模型所用的平方损失类算法均属覆盖保持算法。

Conclusion: 结果厘清了正则化、裁剪(clipping)与目标函数选择如何影响后训练的采样成本与优化成本，为后训练算法设计提供理论依据。

Abstract: Policy optimization is central to language model post-training, yet principled guidance remains limited. We revisit its theoretical foundations to identify algorithms that learn near-optimal policies using total samples and gradient steps governed by the base model's response coverage, a measure of how readily it generates optimal responses. We call such algorithms coverage-preserving. This guarantee captures the goal of amplifying capabilities accessible through sampling from the base model.
  (1) Coverage collapse. REINFORCE, the basis for PPO and GRPO, can drive the probability of optimal responses to near zero, even as reward improves. This coverage collapse stalls optimization, and convergence can require exponentially many steps despite good initial coverage. The failure arises even with exact gradients and persists under KL regularization to the base model.
  (2) Reference resets. We show that repeatedly resetting the KL reference to the most recent policy mitigates coverage collapse. We formalize this intervention in Approximate Policy Mirror Descent (APMD), a meta-algorithm that approximately solves a sequence of KL-regularized RL subproblems. APMD enables stronger regularization, making each subproblem easier to solve, while reference resets allow convergence to global optimality.
  (3) Coverage-preserving algorithms. For softmax linear policies over pretrained features, we use APMD to establish the first global convergence guarantees showing that widely used families of post-training algorithms are coverage-preserving. These include stylized PPO and GRPO variants with explicit KL regularization and reference resets, and squared-loss algorithms that implement them implicitly, including those used to train the Kimi models.
  These results clarify how regularization, clipping, and objective choice affect the sampling and optimization costs of post-training.

</details>


### [128] [TimeSeek: Multi-Task and Multi-Domain Pretraining for Generalized Time Series Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14491)
*Peng Chen,Yihang Wang,Zhongwen Rao,Yang Shu,Yuying Qiu,Yuxuan Chen,Lujia Pan,Bin Yang,Chenjuan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimeSeek is a generalized time series foundation model that, under a "selective information sharing under domain and task heterogeneity" principle, uses a Multi-Domain Feature Extractor and multi-task collaborative pre-training to unify forecasting, anomaly detection and classification, achieving SOTA in few-shot and zero-shot settings with good inference efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TSFM 存在两大问题：(1) 主要靠堆叠 Transformer 层扩大参数量，缺乏从多领域数据中自适应建模复杂时序模式的能力；(2) 多为特定任务预训练（如预测或异常检测），少数支持多任务的模型未能充分利用任务间的特异性与共性，性能次优。

Method: 核心思想为领域与任务异质性下的选择性信息共享。多领域数据视角：提出 Multi-Domain Feature Extractor，依据领域特定特征自适应捕获时间依赖与通道相关性，包含渐进式多尺度建模（progressive multi-scale modeling）与自适应槽通道建模（adaptive slot channel modeling）。任务多样性视角：提出多任务协同训练（multi-task collaborative training），通过任务定制与跨任务训练利用任务特异性和共性，在预训练中统一多任务。

Result: 在大规模时序数据上预训练后，TimeSeek 在预测、异常检测和分类任务上，于 few-shot 与 zero-shot 场景均取得 SOTA 性能，并具有良好推理效率。

Conclusion: 通过以统一预训练同时建模多领域时序模式并统筹多任务，TimeSeek 证明了"选择性共享"原则在通用时序分析基础模型上的有效性，兼顾多任务通用性、下游适应性与推理效率。

Abstract: Time series analytics has been widely applied across various domains. While existing time series foundation models (TSFMs) have demonstrated promising results, they still face two major challenges: 1) Most TSFMs mainly rely on stacking Transformer layers to increase parameter size, lacking the ability to adaptively model complex time series patterns from multi-domain data. 2) Current TSFMs are primarily pre-trained for specific tasks, e.g., forecasting or anomaly detection, and a few models support multiple tasks, but they fail to fully leverage task specificity and commonality, resulting in suboptimal performance. To address these challenges, we propose TimeSeek, a novel generalized time series analytics model that adaptively models time series patterns from multi-domain data and unifies multiple tasks in pre-training. Conceptually, TimeSeek is guided by selective information sharing under domain and task heterogeneity. From the perspective of multi-domain data, we propose a Multi-Domain Feature Extractor that adaptively captures temporal dependencies and channel correlations based on domain-specific characteristics, including progressive multi-scale modeling and adaptive slot channel modeling. From the perspective of the diversity of tasks, we propose multi-task collaborative training, which leverages task specificity and task commonality through task customization and cross-task training, effectively unifying multiple tasks in pre-training and better adapting to diverse downstream scenarios. After pre-training on large-scale time series data, TimeSeek achieves state-of-the-art performance in forecasting, anomaly detection, and classification tasks across both few-shot learning and zero-shot situations, together with favorable inference efficiency.

</details>


### [129] [Emergent Biological Capabilities in a Foundation Model for Molecular Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14504)
*Adi Mashiach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bonbon 是面向分子相互作用的多模态生物学基础模型，基于约3.2万亿token的蛋白-配体序列对训练；单一表征空间在功能、残基、键三个层级呈现零样本涌现能力，并在1.6B规模出现跳跃式涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型中"无标注大规模语料预训练产生涌现能力"的范式推广到多模态生物学，探索仅凭蛋白-配体序列、无需任务监督与结构坐标，能否获得多层级分子相互作用理解能力，并检验基于损失的缩放律是否足以预测涌现发生。

Method: 在约3.2万亿token的配对蛋白-配体序列上预训练统一表征空间；开展42M–1.6B共六个规模的受控缩放实验；以零样本方式评估功能（作用机制）、残基（结合位点定位、正构/别构判别）与键（共价/非共价）三类任务，并在分布外复合物上检验泛化；采用冻结表征进行结合与亲和力预测及前瞻性湿实验验证（九个疾病靶点、七个蛋白家族）。

Result: 三类零样本能力均可泛化至分布外复合物；在最大规模处连续指标出现明显跃升，而预训练损失平滑下降，说明单靠损失缩放律无法预测涌现时点；能力在参数:token≈1:2000的比例下涌现以适配配对数据稀疏性；冻结表征的结合/亲和力预测达SOTA，速度较共折叠方法快约五个数量级；九个靶点湿实验中从头设计化合物剂量-反应命中率61.5%，并获得结构新颖、此前未被报道可抑制这些蛋白的分子，含分布外靶点。

Conclusion: 仅序列预训练即可支撑多层级分子相互作用的零样本涌现，涌现呈"阈值式"跃迁而非随损失连续改善，因此需以能力导向而非损失导向的缩放评估；该范式兼具高效性（冻结表征、高速推理）与实用价值（高湿实验命中率、新颖分子），为多模态生物学基础模型提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Foundation models trained on large unlabeled corpora develop emergent capabilities, tasks that go beyond the training objective. We extend this paradigm to multimodal biology with Bonbon, a foundation model for molecular interactions trained on approximately 3.2 trillion tokens of paired protein-ligand sequences. From a single representation space, Bonbon exhibits three zero-shot emergent capabilities at hierarchical levels of resolution: functional (mechanism of action), residue (binding site localization, including orthosteric/allosteric discrimination), and bond (covalent versus non-covalent engagement). All three capabilities generalize to out-of-distribution complexes. Bonbon was trained on protein-ligand sequences alone, without any task-specific supervision and without structural coordinates. We conducted controlled scaling experiments across six model scales from 42M to 1.6B parameters; every capability evaluated shows a sharp jump at the largest scale on continuous metrics, even as pretraining loss decreases smoothly. This demonstrates that loss-based scaling laws alone are insufficient to predict when zero-shot emergent capabilities become available. The capabilities reported here emerge after training at a 1:2,000 parameter-to-token ratio to accommodate the sparsity of paired biological interaction data. The same frozen representations achieve state-of-the-art binding and affinity prediction at five orders of magnitude greater speed than co-folding methods. In prospective wet-lab validation across nine disease targets spanning seven protein families, Bonbon achieves a 61.5% dose-response hit rate on de novo compounds, yielding structurally novel molecules not previously reported to inhibit these proteins, including for out-of-distribution targets.

</details>


### [130] [Continual-ARC: Measuring Skill Acquisition over a Working Lifetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14505)
*Daniel Lenton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Continual-ARC 基准：由三版 ARC-AGI 任务构成终身学习流，任务在休眠间隔后以新实例重现、新任务持续到达，统一在同一反馈预算下评测，使基于记忆与基于权重的方法可在同一尺度比较。实验中，harness 持久化在无专家指导时几乎不能提升记忆；多数 harness 方法不如"缓存演示"这一下界，且均无法泛化到未见任务，而基于权重的学习取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习/智能体基准存在三大局限：只关注单一应用领域；每个实例仅被服务一次；假定单一学习者家族，导致基于记忆与基于权重的系统难以在同一尺度上比较。作者希望构建一个无需领域知识、可重复访问同一任务、并对两类学习范式统一计分的终身学习评测流。

Method: 构建 Continual-ARC：任务取自三版 ARC-AGI，无需领域知识；同一任务在休眠间隔后以全新实例重现，同时不断有新任务加入。学习者在每个实例上可付费购买演示、提交答案或在 harness 内行动，错误提交可重试但同样付费；实例得分依据其消耗的反馈预算比例。学习者可跨实例保留任意状态，因此记忆型与权重型系统计分口径一致。流结束后冻结学习者，分别在已见任务的新实例（记忆）与未见任务（泛化）上测试。实验在 Qwen3.5-9B 与 GLM-5.3 Flash 上运行冻结权重的 agent harness（含/不含持久化），并与两个提示下界、一个程序归纳方法以及模仿 GLM-5.3 Flash 训练的 Qwen3.5-9B 权重模型对比。

Result: ① 无显式专家指导时，harness 的持久化通常不提升记忆表现；② 在所有测试模型中，多数基于 harness 的方法学习效果低于"直接缓存每个任务演示"的简单下界；③ 在未见任务上没有 harness 方法取得增益，而基于权重的学习显示出显著增益。

Conclusion: 当前 agent harness 作为持续学习机制效果有限，记忆式持久化不足以支撑真正的学习与泛化。结果为未来基于权重的持续学习指明方向——训练模型学会在新领域中驱动 harness。

Abstract: Agent harnesses have emerged as a promising mechanism for continual learning, alongside test-time parametric learning. However, the current benchmarks have several limitations: they generally focus on a single applied domain, serve each instance only once, and assume a single family of learner, so memory-based and weight-based systems are rarely compared on the same scale. We propose Continual-ARC, a lifelong stream built from the three ARC-AGI editions, whose tasks need no domain knowledge. The same tasks return after dormant gaps with fresh instances, while new tasks keep arriving. On each instance, the learner may pay for demonstrations, submit answers, or act inside the harness, with retries permitted on incorrect submissions, which are paid for. Each instance is scored based on the share of this feedback budget consumed. Since a learner may keep anything between instances, memory-based and weight-based systems are scored on the same scale. After the stream, the learner is frozen and tested on fresh instances of the tasks it met, which measures memorization, and on unseen tasks, which measures generalization. We run an agent harness with frozen weights, with and without persistence, on Qwen3.5-9B and GLM-5.3 Flash, comparing them with two prompting floors, a program-induction method and Qwen3.5-9B's weights trained to imitate GLM-5.3 Flash. Persistence in the harness generally does not improve memorization unless provided with explicit expert guidance. Most harness-based methods still learn less than a floor that simply caches each task's demonstrations, across all models we tested. No harness-based method gains on unseen tasks, whereas weight-based learning shows substantial gains. This opens the door to future weight-based continual learning that could teach models to drive harnesses in new domains.

</details>


### [131] [When Does the Real Wiring Win? Testing connectome structure-function claims against unmeasured physiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14508)
*Yeyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 连接组约束模型常被用来证明真实布线优于打乱布线，但该比较混淆了布线本身与模型调参方式。作者区分三种估计量（P1生理在前件迁移、P2各自调参、P3受独立数据约束的生理），用合成真值、安慰剂零假设与三个果蝇回路模型量化机器学习已知偏差（欠优化基线、继承权重、赢家诅咒）对生物学结论的影响。部分结论稳健（奖赏/惩罚多巴胺分离提升学习、头朝向拓扑为持续性bump所必需），部分不稳健：蘑菇体中真实Kenyon细胞输出布线在P1/P2下优于跨亚型身份打乱0.09，但在P3下反而劣于其0.09，判决取决于alpha'beta'细胞占比。可信的结构性主张需匹配优化、数据约束生理与在该设置下仍可工作的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接组约束模型被广泛用于论证"结构决定功能"：即实测布线图优于打乱或重连布线。然而此类比较存在混杂——连接组只规定谁与谁相连，不规定兴奋性、增益、可塑性率等参数，而建模者往往反复调这些参数直到真实布线模型"可用"为止。这使真实布线获得隐性的参数优势（欠优化的对照基线、从真实模型继承的权重、赢家诅咒等选择偏差），因此需要系统评估这些偏差对生物学结论的实际影响，厘清哪些结论是真实的、哪些是调参伪影。

Method: 作者提出三种估计量以分离布线效应与调参效应：P1为在真实布线上调好生理参数后迁移到零假设布线；P2为每种布线各自单独调参；P3为P2但限于与独立实验数据一致的生理参数范围。使用三类材料检验偏差大小：合成真值（已知正确答案）、安慰剂零假设（应无效应）、以及三个果蝇回路模型（蘑菇体Kenyon细胞输出、flyvis、头朝向系统）。通过置信区间、保持集复跑、多倍重训（十倍、五十倍）及镜像/空间打乱比较来判断结论稳健性。

Result: 部分结论在所有协议下均稳健：奖赏与惩罚多巴胺通路分离提升学习；头朝向拓扑是维持持久bump的必要条件。部分结论不稳健：蘑菇体中真实Kenyon细胞输出布线在P1和P2下优于跨亚型身份打乱0.09，但在P3下反劣0.09（95% CI排除零，保持集复跑确认）。该判决由活跃Kenyon细胞中alpha'beta'细胞占比决定——P1选择低占比；按成像暗示的约44%占比时，真实布线模型无法学习，说明是模型不适定性而非果蝇布线缺陷。flyvis中P1惩罚镜像连接组，而镜像连接组具有构造解，其误差中位数仅比真实高0.07，且在50/50成员中均优于空间打乱；该排序在十倍重训后仍保持，但在五十倍时依赖检查点。

Conclusion: 连接组优于打乱布线的常见论证在很大程度上受调参协议驱动，而非纯粹由结构决定功能。可信的结构性主张必须同时满足三个条件：对照与真实布线获得匹配的优化程度、生理参数受独立数据约束（如P3）、以及模型在该约束下仍能正常工作。建议将P1/P2/P3三估计量作为报告标准，以避免欠优化基线、继承权重与赢家诅咒导致的虚假结构结论。

Abstract: Connectome-constrained models are often used to argue that a measured wiring diagram outperforms shuffled or rewired wiring, and hence that structure shapes function.
