Such comparisons can confound wiring with how the models are tuned: a connectome fixes who connects to whom, not the excitability, gains or plasticity rates the model also needs, and modellers often tune these until the real-wiring model works. We separate three estimands: physiology tuned on the real wiring and transferred to nulls (P1), each wiring tuned on its own (P2), and P2 restricted to physiology consistent with independent data (P3). The biases involved are known in machine learning (under-tuned baselines, inherited weights, winner's curse); we measure what they do to biological conclusions, using synthetic ground truth, placebo nulls and three Drosophila circuit models. Some claims survive every protocol we test: segregated reward and punishment dopamine improves learning, and head-direction topography is required for a persistent bump. Others do not. In the mushroom body, the real Kenyon-cell output wiring beats a cross-subtype identity shuffle by 0.09 under P1 and P2 but loses to it by 0.09 under P3 (95% CIs exclude zero; held-out runs confirm). The verdict is set by the share of alpha'beta' cells among active Kenyon cells, which P1 selects low; at the share implied by imaging (about 44%) the real-wiring model fails to learn, exposing a model inadequacy rather than a deficit of the fly's wiring. In flyvis, P1 penalises a mirrored connectome, which has a constructed solution within 0.07 (median) of the real error, more than a spatially shuffled one in 50 of 50 members; the ranking survives ten-fold longer retraining but depends on the checkpoint at fifty-fold. A credible structural claim needs matched optimisation, data-constrained physiology and a model that still works there.

</details>


### [132] [Pyrite: Building and Benchmarking Protein-Ligand Pose Ranking Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14511)
*Megha Srivastava,Michael H. Lim,Allison M. Keys,Pranav Murugan,Nate Gruver*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pyrite——一个包含1,500个位姿对的分子对接位姿成对排序基准，并构建PyriteRep结构化表示与两个LLM智能体（PyriteReasoner直接推理、PyriteFeaturizer生成代码构造特征），最高分别达67%和74%准确率，超过专家表现；LLM引导的特征发现优于直接推理，GRPO可提升开源模型至前沿闭源模型水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代共折叠（cofolding）模型能生成多样候选位姿，但可靠地排序/甄别这些候选仍很困难；化学家借助3D可视化与物理打分也仅接近随机水平，亟需研究自动化位姿排序方法，并探索如何解释与整合计算化学工具的输出。

Method: 构建Pyrite成对位姿排序基准（1,500个位姿对，覆盖多样蛋白-配体复合物），并让具备3D可视化工具与物理评分的专家化学家进行评测作为人类基线；提出PyriteRep结构化表示（分子几何、蛋白-配体相互作用、物理化学评分）；开发两个基于LLM的智能体：PyriteReasoner以文本推理直接比较位姿，PyriteFeaturizer生成可执行代码构造特征供逻辑回归使用（可选融入LLM子调用派生特征）；并采用GRPO强化学习优化开源权重模型。

Result: 专家化学家表现接近随机；PyriteReasoner与PyriteFeaturizer最高准确率分别约67%与74%，均超过专家；LLM引导的特征发现总体优于直接推理；GRPO可提升开源权重模型，使其在评测中达到或超过闭源前沿模型。

Conclusion: Pyrite确立了位姿排序作为一项具有挑战性的能力测试基准；LLM引导的特征发现是解释并融合计算化学工具输出的有前景途径。

Abstract: Predicting how a ligand binds to its target is central to small-molecule drug discovery. Modern cofolding models can generate diverse candidate poses, but reliably ranking these candidates remains challenging. We introduce Pyrite, a pairwise pose-ranking benchmark comprising 1,500 pose pairs across diverse protein-ligand complexes. Expert chemists equipped with 3D visualization tools and physics-based scores perform near chance, highlighting the difficulty of interpreting available chemical signals. To investigate automated ranking, we introduce PyriteRep, a structured representation of molecular geometry, protein-ligand interactions, and physicochemical scores, and develop two LLM-based agents: PyriteReasoner, which directly compares poses through text-based reasoning, and PyriteFeaturizer, which generates executable code to construct features for logistic regression, optionally incorporating features derived from LLM subcalls. These agents achieve up to 67% and 74% accuracy, respectively, exceeding observed expert performance. LLM-guided feature discovery generally outperforms direct reasoning, while Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) improves open-weight models, enabling them to match or surpass closed-source frontier models in our evaluations. These results establish Pyrite as a challenging test of pose-ranking ability and highlight LLM-guided feature discovery as a promising approach to interpret and combine the outputs of computational chemistry tools.

</details>


### [133] [AutoScaffold: AI-Guided Scaffolding for Reinforcement Learning of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14515)
*Zijian Zhang,Rizhen Hu,Jiangweizhi Peng,Shiyang Li,Sujay Bhatt,Sihan Zeng,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AutoScaffold：用冻结教师模型根据学生训练经验自动诊断瓶颈、生成并动态调整训练期脚手架（跨任务策略+单任务提示），评测时移除脚手架，在 ALFWorld、检索式QA、数学推理、GPU kernel 生成四个领域均超过对应 RL 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 产生丰富训练经验，但结果奖励把每次尝试压缩为单一标量，绝大部分信息被浪费；训练期脚手架可将这些信息转化为指导，但现有脚手架依赖人工设计且高度领域专用，缺乏通用性与自动适应性。

Method: 统一的 AI-for-AI 框架：冻结教师周期性检视学生训练经验、诊断瓶颈，并以两种形式设计脚手架——(i) 跨一类任务共享的策略，(ii) 针对个别困难任务的提示；随学生能力提升动态调整脚手架的内容与支持量；所需领域信息极少，训练中无需人工干预，评测时完全移除脚手架。

Result: 无脚手架评测下，四领域均优于对应 RL 基线：ALFWorld 成功率 +7.2 点，检索式QA 平均精确匹配 +4.0 点，GPU kernel 生成中"正确且快于 PyTorch 参考"的任务比例最多 +5.0 点，数学上以约三分之一学生训练步数超过 QuestA 报告精度。分析显示：教师对每类任务识别出不同失败模式，并随学生进步将脚手架从分步流程转向针对性修补；修订后的脚手架比沿用旧脚手架或无脚手架带来更多学习收益。

Conclusion: 训练期脚手架可作为把 RLVR 结果奖励之外的经验信息转化为自适应指导的有效通道，且能由 AI 自动完成诊断与设计；其价值在于脚手架被移除后仍提升学生自身能力，说明收益来自能力内化而非评测时的提示辅助。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) produces rich training experience, yet outcome rewards reduce each attempt to a single number and leave most of this information unused. Training-time scaffolds, such as strategies or hints added to the prompt during training, offer a natural way to turn this information into guidance, but their design and adaptation are largely left to humans and tailored to specific domains. We introduce AutoScaffold, a unified AI-for-AI framework that turns a student's training experience into adaptive scaffolds, with minimal domain-specific information and no human intervention during training. A frozen teacher periodically examines this experience, diagnoses bottlenecks, and designs scaffolds in two forms: (i) strategies shared across a class of tasks and (ii) hints for individual hard tasks, adjusting the content and amount of support as the student learns. The scaffolds are removed at evaluation. We apply the same framework to ALFWorld, search-based QA, mathematical reasoning, and GPU kernel generation. When evaluated without scaffolds, students trained with AutoScaffold outperform those trained with the corresponding RL baselines in all four domains, with gains of 7.2 points in ALFWorld success and 4.0 points in average QA exact match. On GPU kernel generation, AutoScaffold increases the fraction of tasks with a correct kernel that runs faster than the PyTorch reference by up to 5.0 points. On math, our student surpasses QuestA's reported accuracy using approximately one-third as many student training steps. Analyses show that the teacher identifies a different failure in each task class and shifts its scaffolds from step-by-step workflows to targeted fixes as the student improves, and that its revised scaffolds help the student learn more than keeping the previous scaffold or using no scaffold.

</details>


### [134] [TorchCurves: Differentiable Parametric Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14516)
*Alex Shtoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TorchCurves：将向量值参数曲线作为可学习原语的 PyTorch 库，支持 B 样条与 Legendre 多项式基、对输入与系数双向可微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习框架缺乏将参数曲线作为一等可学习原语的通用抽象，而连续特征嵌入、KAN、函数型回归等应用需共享同一套曲线求值与可微能力。

Method: 提供可训练模块、可复用基模块与函数式接口；批量标量输入映射到曲线点，采用经典求值算法配合向量化 PyTorch 算子实现，无需定制硬件内核，强调可读性与可维护性。

Result: 计时实验将曲线求值与 NumPy、SciPy 对比，将 KAN 训练与 EfficientKAN 对比，覆盖多种问题规模；结果刻画出该通用实现保持计算可行性的区间以及开销显著增大的情形。

Conclusion: 曲线原语可统一支撑多类应用，通用向量化实现以可接受的开销换取灵活性与可维护性；库已开源。

Abstract: We present TorchCurves, a PyTorch library that treats vector-valued parametric curves as learnable primitives. Its central operation maps batches of scalar inputs to points on curves represented in B-spline or Legendre polynomial bases, with differentiation through both inputs and coefficients. Curve coefficients can be optimized jointly with the surrounding model or produced by another network. The library provides trainable modules, reusable basis modules, and a functional interface, supporting applications ranging from continuous feature embeddings to Kolmogorov-Arnold networks and functional regression. Its implementation combines classical evaluation algorithms with vectorized PyTorch operations, emphasizing readability and maintainability without custom hardware kernels. Timing experiments compare curve evaluation against NumPy and SciPy and KAN training against EfficientKAN across a range of problem sizes. The results illustrate where this generality remains computationally practical and where overhead is substantial. Library is available on GitHub at https://github.com/alexshtf/torchcurves.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Time Running Backwards, and Where It Stops: Superoscillations of Proper Time and the Memory of the Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12971)
*Yakir Aharonov,Avshalom C. Elitzur*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究"量子时间平移机"（弱值放大时间平移）能多大程度地逆转不可逆的原子衰变过程；结论是：可逆性受限于谱带宽而非熵，双分支事后选择仅在环境记忆时间内有效，超越记忆时间需多分支、严格有界谱环境且事后选择概率指数下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aharonov、Anandan、Popescu、Vaidman 提出的量子时间平移机通过事后选择将固有时叠加转化为时间膨胀因子的超振荡，弱值 α_w 可大于1、为零甚至为负。作者希望定量回答：这样的机器能把一个不可逆过程"倒回"多远，并澄清可逆性的真正瓶颈（熵 vs 带宽）。

Method: （1）将固有时叠加实现为世界线或引力势的叠加，各分支以相同动力学演化不同的时长；（2）对设定固有时长的控制量子比特进行事后选择，得到有效弱值时移 α_w t；（3）测试系统为一个"密封腔"：激发态原子、其辐射场与吸收壁作为一个整体量子对象处理；（4）采用投影式判据：在事后选择子系综中，检验实验前已开始衰变的原子是否比插入时更激发；（5）解析推导该判据，证明其精确结果仅依赖原子的存活幅度；（6）分别考察双分支情形（与两分支间腔内容差异、原子与场的总能量展宽相关）与多分支情形（要求严格有界谱的环境，且事后选择概率随需撤销的环境振荡数目指数下降）；（7）提出四种实现时钟速率叠加的方案，从空心大质量壳层到惯性速度。

Result: 投影式见证是精确的，且只由原子的存活幅度决定。在双分支情形下，该见证仅在衰变时长短于环境的记忆时间时成立；超过记忆时间后发射变为马尔可夫型，事后选择放大的是两分支中腔内容之间的微小差异（由原子及其场的全部能量展宽而非线宽决定），见证随之失效。要把衰变逆转至记忆时间之外，需要多分支、具有严格有界谱的环境，并且事后选择概率随需撤销的环境振荡数目呈指数衰减。

Conclusion: 逆转不可逆过程的障碍不是熵，而是带宽——正是这种带宽最初使发射成为不可逆的。文中还给出四种叠加时钟速率的具体方案（空心大质量壳层至惯性速度）。

Abstract: A quantum system prepared in a superposition of proper times - a clock whose worldline is superposed, or a clock at rest in a superposed gravitational potential - evolves, in each branch, by the same dynamics for a different duration. Post-selecting the control that sets the proper time turns the superposition into a superoscillation of the time-dilation factor: the system is found to have evolved for a proper time $α_w t$, where the weak value $α_w$ may exceed unity, vanish, or be negative - the quantum time-translation machine of Aharonov, Anandan, Popescu and Vaidman. We ask how far back such a machine can run an irreversible process. The test system is a sealed cell - excited atoms, the radiation field they emit into, and the absorbing walls, carried as one quantum object - and the witness is projective: atoms that had begun to decay before the run are found, in the post-selected sub-ensemble, more excited than when inserted. The answer is exact and depends only on the atoms' survival amplitude. With two branches the witness holds only while the decay is younger than the memory time of the environment, after which the emission becomes Markovian. Beyond it, the post-selection amplifies the minute difference between the contents of the cell in the two branches, set by the full energy spread of the atoms and their field rather than by the linewidth, and the witness fails. Reversing a decay past the memory time requires many branches, an environment with a strictly bounded spectrum, and a post-selection probability that falls exponentially with the number of environmental oscillations to be undone. The obstacle to reversal is therefore not entropy but bandwidth - the same bandwidth that makes the emission irreversible in the first place. We give four ways to superpose a clock rate, from hollow massive shells to inertial velocities.

</details>


### [136] [Second-level termination of the PPT entanglement-cost hierarchy for two qutrits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12993)
*Wei Song,Xiao-Lan Zong,Ming Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明对任意两qutrit量子态，PPT纠缠成本的半定规划下界层级在第二层终止，从而零误差渐近纠缠成本可由仅含三个厄米矩阵变量的半定规划求得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPT entanglement cost 的 semidefinite programming 下界层级（hierarchy）此前只知逐层给出下界，且已知在所有维度上不存在统一的终止层；两qutrit 这一最小双体系统是否存在统一终止层尚不明确。

Method: 通过对偶可行链（feasible dual chain）缩短一层的思路，将精确有理算术可验证的有限证书（finite certificates）与参数域边界附近的解析估计（analytic estimates）相结合，完成两qutrit 情形的终止性证明。

Result: 对任意两qutrit 态，SDP 下界层级在第二层终止；因此其在保持正部分转置（PPT）的操作下的零误差渐近纠缠成本可由只含三个厄米矩阵变量的半定规划精确给出。

Conclusion: 两qutrit 系统存在统一的终止层（第二层），这与全维度上不存在统一终止层的结论形成对比；方法上，精确证书加边界解析估计的组合可用于此类层级终止性证明。

Abstract: We prove that for arbitrary two-qutrit quantum states, the semidefinite programming lower-bound hierarchy for the PPT entanglement cost terminates at the second level. Therefore, under operations preserving positive partial transpose, its zero-error asymptotic entanglement cost can be obtained by a semidefinite program involving only three Hermitian matrix variables. The key to the proof is to shorten the feasible dual chain by one level; to this end, we combine finite certificates verified by exact rational arithmetic with analytic estimates near the boundary of the parameter domain. This result shows that a unified terminating level exists for two-qutrit systems, whereas no unified terminating level exists for all dimensions.

</details>


### [137] [Quantum simulation of real-time bosonic dynamics with binary and unary position encodings on superconducting and trapped-ion processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13079)
*Masanori Hanada,Jiwoo Seo,Seok-Kyun Son,Junggi Yoon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了量子比特硬件上模拟玻色自由度的二进制编码与一元（unary）编码：在一维对称双势阱隧穿问题上，一元编码经精确压缩后仅需约两百个双比特门即可在IBM超导与IonQ离子阱处理器上复现隧穿，而需数千双比特门的二进制乘积公式方案未能复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子比特硬件上模拟玻色自由度必须离散化其位置空间，而格点的量子比特编码方式直接决定了实现实时动力学的线路结构。作者希望厘清编码选择（二进制 vs 一元）与编译优化如何共同影响实际硬件上可实现的动力学保真度。

Method: 对比二进制与一元两种位置格点编码：二进制编码用量子傅里叶变换实现动能项；一元编码每个格点对应一个量子比特，势能项用单比特旋转、动能项用保持激发数的交换门。对单自由度，一元动力学等价于自由费米子问题，其乘积公式可精确压缩为固定规模的最近邻线路。在超导（IBM）与离子阱（IonQ）处理器上运行对称双势阱隧穿实验，并进行无噪声仿真对照，考察压缩优化与编码各自的贡献；辅以耦合振子基准测试。

Result: 约两百个双比特门的一元线路在两种处理器上均分辨出隧穿：右阱概率在IBM上由0.97降至0.06，在IonQ上由0.96降至0.12；而需数千双比特门的二进制乘积公式线路未能复现该布居转移。无噪声仿真表明，这一对比同时源于编译与编码：若不做固定规模优化，能捕捉该转移的一元乘积公式同样需要数千双比特门。对单激发子空间的后选择可消除泄漏；由于对称格点的每个能量本征态对两阱布居均等，测得的布居反转给出了本征态间相干性的下界。

Conclusion: 编码与编译共同决定量子模拟的可行性：在小规模单自由度问题上，经精确压缩的一元编码在门数与硬件表现上显著优于二进制编码。但在寄存器间相互作用使精确压缩失效的耦合振子基准中，最优编码取决于格点规模与演化时间，因此不存在普遍最优的编码方案。

Abstract: Simulating a bosonic degree of freedom on qubit hardware requires discretizing its position space, and the qubit encoding of the resulting lattice determines the circuits that realize its real-time dynamics. We compare binary and unary encodings of a position lattice on superconducting (IBM) and trapped-ion (IonQ) processors. Binary encoding applies the kinetic term through the quantum Fourier transform; unary encoding uses one qubit per site, single-qubit rotations for the potential and excitation-conserving exchange gates for the kinetic term. For a single degree of freedom the unary dynamics is a free-fermion problem, and its product formula compresses exactly into a nearest-neighbor circuit of fixed size. With about two hundred two-qubit gates, these circuits resolve tunneling in a symmetric double well on both processors, with the right-well probability falling from 0.97 to 0.06 on IBM and from 0.96 to 0.12 on IonQ, whereas binary product-formula circuits with several thousand two-qubit gates do not reproduce the transfer. Noiseless emulation indicates that this contrast reflects the compilation as well as the encoding: without the fixed-size optimization, a unary product formula that captures the transfer also requires several thousand two-qubit gates. Post-selection on the single-excitation subspace removes leakage, and because every energy eigenstate of the symmetric grid populates both wells equally, the measured population reversal bounds the coherence between energy eigenstates from below. Coupled-oscillator benchmarks, where inter-register interactions prevent this exact compression, show that the preferred encoding depends on the grid size and the evolution time.

</details>


### [138] [Field-induced phase-matching in Josephson traveling wave parametric amplifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13084)
*Daniil E. Bazulin,Simon Gandorfer,Niklas Bruckmoser,Kedar E. Honasoge,Leon Koch,Maria-Teresa Handschuh,Matthias Hirschbichler,Kirill Shulga,Achim Marx,Michael Haider,Stefan Filipp,Kirill G. Fedorov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验演示了一种基于磁場非均匀性的相位匹配方法，利用均匀约瑟夫森元非线性介质实现超导行波参量放大器的宽带高增益放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JTWPAs对超导量子比特单发读出、宽带真空压缩和微波簇态制备至关重要，但信号与泵浦模式间的相位匹配是主要工程难题，通常需在非线性介质中集成谐振器以裁剪色散关系，结构复杂。

Method: 采用基于约瑟夫森元件的均匀非线性介质，通过引入磁场非均匀性来调节色散，避免集成谐振器；实验研究了该非均匀场对三波混频非线性符号的影响。

Result: 实验表明磁场非均匀性可使三波混频非线性发生符号反转，并稳定参量放大过程，实现了相位匹配的放大效果。

Conclusion: 该磁场非均匀性方法可作为传统谐振器色散裁剪相位匹配方案的可替代选择，简化器件结构并实现稳定参量放大。

Abstract: Superconducting Josephson traveling wave parametric amplifiers (JTWPAs) enable broadband, quantum-limited amplification of weak microwave signals. JTWPAs are essential for the single-shot readout of superconducting qubits, broadband vacuum squeezing, or the generation of microwave cluster states. Phase-matching between signal and pump modes in such JTWPAs represents a major engineering challenge, which is typically solved by integrating resonators in a nonlinear medium in order to tailor electromagnetic dispersion relations and achieve a broadband, high-gain amplification. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a phase-matching approach using a homogeneous nonlinear medium based on Josephson elements by exploiting a magnetic field inhomogeneity. We show how the latter may lead to the sign reversal of the 3-wave mixing nonlinearity and stabilization of the parametric amplification. We envision this technique as a viable alternative to conventional phase-matching approaches.

</details>


### [139] [Time Direction Entanglement Entropy of Free Fermion Ground States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13085)
*Xinyu Sun,Bowei Liu,Joshua Yi,Biao Lian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了自由费米子量子态在时间方向区间上的纠缠熵（TDEE）的标度行为，基于Toeplitz矩阵的Fisher–Hartwig猜想建立了离散时间步下的解析框架，并讨论了连续时间极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间子区域的纠缠熵已被广泛研究，但时间方向上量子态的纠缠熵缺乏恰当定义且研究不足，需要明确其定义并刻画其标度规律。

Method: 利用"极小支撑时间区间内全部时间演化算符"的子希尔伯特空间的约化密度矩阵定义TDEE；基于Toeplitz矩阵的Fisher–Hartwig猜想发展解析框架，处理离散时间步τ；对无无序1D系统（无能隙费米子、能带绝缘体）解析求各参数区间标度，对1D Anderson绝缘体进行数值计算，并考察τ→0极限。

Result: 无能隙/能带绝缘体在不同能隙、带宽与τ区间下分别呈面积律（非零能隙）、对数律或体积律；1D Anderson绝缘体呈类Page曲线行为，短时增长、超过由局域化决定的交叉时间后幂律衰减；τ→0时三者分别呈对数律、面积律、次面积律。

Conclusion: TDEE在时间长度上呈现丰富的标度律；τ→0极限下时间区间的子希尔伯特空间趋于稳定，可理解为Lieb–Robinson因果钻石希尔伯特空间的子空间。

Abstract: While the entanglement entropy of a spatial subregion has been extensively studied, a properly defined entanglement entropy of quantum states in a time direction interval remains underexplored. We investigate the scaling behaviors of the recently defined time direction entanglement entropy (TDEE) for free fermion quantum states, which is computed from the reduced density matrix of a sub-Hilbert space that minimally supports all the time-evolved operators in the time direction interval. Based on the Fisher--Hartwig conjecture for Toeplitz matrices, we develop an analytical framework for understanding the TDEE in a time interval with discrete time step $τ$. For disorderless one-dimensional (1D) systems (gapless fermions or band insulators), in different regimes of gap, bandwidth and $τ$, we find the TDEE can exhibit area law (for nonzero gap), logarithmic law or volume law in the length of time. For 1D Anderson insulators, we numerically find the TDEE shows a Page-curve like behavior, growing at short time, and decaying in power-law at long time exceeding the crossover time determined by localization. In the continuum time limit $τ\rightarrow 0$, the TDEE of disorderless gapless fermions, band insulators and Anderson insulators exhibit logarithmic law, area law and sub-area law, respectively. We also show that the sub-Hilbert space of a time-direction interval stabilizes in the $τ\rightarrow 0$ limit, which is understandable as a subspace of the Hilbert space of the Lieb-Robinson causal diamond.

</details>


### [140] [Kinematic Quantum Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13089)
*Bernardo Barrera,Nishchhal Verma,Raquel Queiroz,Anatoli Polkovnikov,Anushya Chandran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出"运动学量子几何"(kinematic quantum geometry)框架：快速自由度绝热跟随由慢自由度坐标与速度共同参数化的量子态，推广了仅依赖坐标的Born-Oppenheimer几何，引入混合Berry曲率并修正慢自由度有效理论；并在磁性织构中自旋粒子与石墨烯/L‑C电路耦合的狄拉克电子两个体系中验证其预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Born-Oppenheimer(绝热)几何只考虑慢自由度的坐标参数化，忽略了慢自由度速度对快自由度量子态的影响。作者希望将几何描述扩展到坐标+速度的参数空间，以刻画由此产生的混合Berry曲率对慢自由度有效动力学(质量、力、电容、电流响应)的修正，并检验其在大速度、非微扰区域的适用性。

Method: 构造以慢自由度坐标与速度为参数的"运动学"快自由度态空间，定义相应的量子几何量(含混合Berry曲率)，并推导其对慢自由度有效理论的修正；在两类模型上做具体计算：(1)自旋粒子在磁性织构(可实现共面织构)中运动；(2)石墨烯中狄拉克电子与LC电路耦合。同时与微扰极限下已有结果(外电磁场中的电子动力学、赝自旋织构)做对比。

Result: 体系(1)：运动学量子几何给出速度依赖的有效质量张量，并产生与速度非线性相关的类洛伦兹力，该力即使在共面磁性织构中也可出现。体系(2)：运动学量子几何重整化电路电容，并产生关于电场非线性的霍尔电流。小速度极限下复现了外电磁场电子动力学及赝自旋织构中的近期结果。

Conclusion: 运动学量子几何是Born-Oppenheimer几何的自然推广，可统一解释速度依赖的质量修正与非线性类洛伦兹力/非线性霍尔响应等效应；在运动学量子态变化足够缓慢的前提下，该框架可突破微扰区，在大速度下仍保持有效。

Abstract: Kinematic quantum geometry describes fast degrees of freedom (DOFs) that adiabatically follow states parametrized by both the coordinates and the velocities of slow DOFs. It extends Born-Oppenheimer geometry, which depends on coordinates alone, introduces mixed Berry curvatures, and modifies the effective theory governing the slow DOFs. We test its predictions in two setups: spinful particles moving in magnetic textures, and Dirac electrons in graphene coupled to an LC circuit. In the first, kinematic quantum geometry produces a velocity-dependent effective mass tensor and generates a Lorentz-like force nonlinear in velocity that can arise even in coplanar textures. In the second, it renormalizes the circuit capacitance and generates a Hall current nonlinear in the electric field. For small velocities, it reproduces recent results for electronic dynamics in external electromagnetic fields and in pseudospin textures. However, the framework can remain valid at large velocities beyond the perturbative regime, provided the kinematic states are sufficiently slowly varying.

</details>


### [141] [PINNverse meets Jordan--Wigner: learning the Hamiltonian of long spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14023)
*Gubio G De Lima*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于Jordan-Wigner变换的PINNverse框架，将网络输出从4^N降至N^2，并结合时间依赖Hartree-Fock闭合处理寄生ZZ耦合，实现最多50量子比特自旋链哈密顿量参数的高精度反演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆物理信息神经网络（PINNverse）可从期望值测量中学习量子哈密顿量参数，但网络输出随量子比特数N按4^N指数增长，限制了其在多比特体系中的可扩展性。

Method: 针对具有最近邻交换相互作用的自旋链，利用Jordan-Wigner变换使动力学闭合在N×N的单体密度矩阵上，网络仅需N^2个输出，数据为在五种全局测量设置下测得的局域可观测量的期望值；寄生ZZ耦合（串扰）通过时间依赖Hartree-Fock闭合纳入模型。

Result: 在合成数据上，同一网络先求解运动方程、再反演哈密顿量参数，成功恢复多达50量子比特链的参数，耦合学习精度优于0.1%；长链局域频率的残差误差可通过以网络估计为初值的直接最小二乘拟合消除。

Conclusion: Jordan-Wigner变换可有效消除PINNverse的指数标度瓶颈，使其在多比特自旋链参数反演中兼具可扩展性与高精度，配合最小二乘后处理可进一步提高长链精度。

Abstract: Inverse physics-informed neural networks (PINNverse) learn the parameters of a quantum Hamiltonian from measured expectation values, but the network output grows as $4^N$ with the number of qubits $N$. We show that, for spin chains with nearest-neighbor exchange interactions, the Jordan-Wigner transformation removes this exponential cost: the dynamics closes on an $N\times N$ one-body density matrix, so the network needs only $N^2$ outputs and the data are local observables measured in five global settings. Parasitic $ZZ$ couplings (crosstalk) are included through a time-dependent Hartree--Fock closure. With synthetic data, the same network first solves the equation of motion and then recovers the Hamiltonian parameters of chains of up to $50$ qubits, with couplings learned to better than $0.1\%$. Errors in the local frequencies of long chains are removed by a direct least-squares fit started from the network estimate.

</details>


### [142] [Frustration-free control for quantum-state preparation: an exact first-passage theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13094)
*T. Dörstel,T. C. Lang,T. Iadecola,J. H. Wilson,M. Buchhold*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对基于局域测量与反馈制备Dicke态的受控耗散机制，建立了一套精确理论：动力学严格封闭在N_q(N_q-1)/2个单态对投影子上，等价于经典首达过程，并将指数大的多体Liouvillian约化为多项式规模的生成元，从而得到精确弛豫时间下界、末态闭式解及涨落闭合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域测量加反馈可在无阻挫（frustration-free）框架下自主制备与稳定纠缠多体态，其目标流形由局域约束指定，违反量由测量检测、反馈移除。但该机制一般难以解析，需要针对具体目标态（Dicke态）给出精确可解的理论，以理解制备、加速、鲁棒性与涨落的统一机制。

Method: 以N_q量子比特的Dicke态（置换对称子空间，每个磁化扇区一个）为目标，用局域单态投影子作为约束以探测对置换对称性的偏离；证明动力学精确封闭于单态对投影子张成的空间，并映射为经典首达过程（测量移动对端点，反馈在测得键上吸收对）；在一维情形下闭式求解该过程；再通过第二精确闭合确定任意初态对应的末态，并引入修正输运、受限反馈与物理误差的扩展；最后用精确二阶矩闭合刻画系综涨落。

Result: (1) 封闭使指数规模的多体Liouvillian约化为多项式规模生成元；(2) 一维过程可闭式求解，最慢速率2[1−cos(π/N_q)]（以测量速率为单位）是精确的Liouvillian本征值；(3) 对该类方案，弛豫时间下界为反馈所作用的对构型占比的倒数；局域反馈仅覆盖O(N_q)个（共O(N_q²)个）构型，故时间至少随N_q线性增长；(4) 第二闭合给出从任意初态可达的末态；(5) 扩展得到精确的输运—吸收体渡越（crossover）、几何制备规律与稳态泄漏上界；(6) 二阶矩闭合表明测量噪声是保守的，而反馈产生移除型散粒噪声与对关联。

Conclusion: 制备、加速、鲁棒性与涨落均由同一个精确可解的物理过程——局域约束违反量的输运与吸收——所支配；该框架将指数复杂度问题化为多项式复杂度，并为受限（局域）反馈下的时间下界与稳态泄漏提供定量判据。

Abstract: Quantum circuits based on local measurements and feedback can autonomously prepare and stabilize entangled many-body states. In frustration-free control, the target manifold is specified by local constraints, whose violations are detected by measurement and removed by feedback. We develop an exact theory of this mechanism for the preparation of Dicke states of $N_q$ qubits. Dicke states span the permutation-symmetric subspace, one per magnetization sector. Local singlet projectors define the constraints and detect departures from permutation symmetry. The resulting dynamics closes exactly on the span of the $N_q(N_q-1)/2$ singlet-pair projectors and is equivalent to a classical first-passage process: measurements move the pair endpoints, while feedback absorbs pairs on measured bonds. The closure reduces the exponentially large many-body Liouvillian to a generator of polynomial size. In one dimension we solve this process in closed form. Its slowest rate, $2[1-\cos(π/N_q)]$ in units of the measurement rate, is an exact Liouvillian eigenvalue. For any scheme of this class, the relaxation time is bounded below by the inverse fraction of pair configurations on which feedback acts; local feedback reaches $O(N_q)$ of $O(N_q^2)$ configurations, requiring a time at least linear in $N_q$. A second exact closure determines the final state reached from any initial condition. Extensions to modified transport, restricted feedback, and physical errors yield an exact transport-to-absorber crossover, geometric preparation laws, and a bound on the stationary leakage. An exact second-moment closure determines the ensemble fluctuations: the measurement noise is conserving, while feedback generates removal shot noise and pair correlations. Preparation, acceleration, robustness, and fluctuations are thus governed by one exactly solvable process: the transport and absorption of local constraint violations.

</details>


### [143] [Surface codes with coherent errors: optimal decoding and finite-size crossovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13098)
*Xu-Dong Dai,Zhou-Quan Wan,Xi Dai,Guo-Yi Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究均匀相干Z旋转后接完美综合征测量下表面码的有限尺寸行为，识别出两个特征尺度（弹道交叉尺度与重整化群尺度），给出复制Majorana理论中的四次相互作用与class-D非线性sigma模型，并提出标度变量X_4=β_R^4 ln d，支持热力学极限下θ_c=π/4的Pauli解码阈值及ML解码的量子最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀相干Z旋转下的表面码存在强烈有限尺寸效应，使得从数值模拟中提取渐近解码阈值变得困难；需要理解这些有限尺寸漂移的微观起源与标度结构。

Method: 结合Pauli解码度量与相干信息的有限尺寸分析；在复制Majorana理论中推导O(β_R^2)的层间四次相互作用，导出class-D非线性sigma模型及边缘RG流；以X_4=β_R^4 ln d为标度变量进行自由费米子模拟；并给出偶d下最大似然Pauli解码最优性的严格证明。

Result: 发现两类特征长度：代数型弹道交叉尺度d_ba~1/β_R^α（奇d α=1，偶d α=2）与远大于它的RG尺度d_RG~exp(C/β_R^4)；中间区d_ba≪d≪d_RG内出现显著有限尺寸漂移与近似平台；模拟显示X_4下解码度量近似塌缩。

Conclusion: 交叉图像具有微观基础，结果与热力学极限下θ_c=π/4的Pauli解码阈值一致；对任意偶d，最大似然Pauli解码在所有确定性综合征条件量子恢复中最大化纠缠保真度，其阈值达到量子最优极限并与相干信息定义一致。

Abstract: We study the surface code under uniform coherent $Z$ rotations $\exp{(iθZ)}$ followed by perfect syndrome measurements, a setting known to exhibit strong finite-size effects that complicate the extraction of the asymptotic decoding threshold. We identify finite-size behavior of both Pauli decoding measures and the coherent information with two characteristic lengths: an algebraic ballistic-crossover scale $d_{\mathrm{ba}}\sim 1/β_R^α$ ($α=1$ for odd $d$ and $α=2$ for even $d$) and a much larger renormalization-group scale $d_\mathrm{RG}\sim \exp{(C/β^{4}_R)}$, where $β_R=-\tfrac{1}{2}\ln\tanθ$ and $d$ is the system size. As $θ$ approaches the ballistic SWAP point $π/4$, the two length scales diverge at very different rates, leaving a broad intermediate regime $d_{\mathrm{ba}}\ll d\ll d_\mathrm{RG}$ in which these quantities show substantial finite-size drift and approximate plateaus. To establish the microscopic basis of this crossover picture, we derive a leading inter-flavor quartic interaction of order $β_R^2$ in the replicated Majorana theory, which provides a route to the class-D nonlinear sigma model. The resulting marginal RG flow motivates the scaling variable $X_4=β_R^4\ln d$, in which free-fermion simulations show approximate collapse of decoding measures. These results are consistent with a Pauli-decoding threshold at $θ_c=π/4$ in the thermodynamic limit. For any even $d$, we prove that maximum-likelihood Pauli decoding maximizes entanglement fidelity over all deterministic syndrome-conditioned quantum recoveries, and that its decoding threshold attains the quantum-optimal limit, coinciding with the one defined by coherent information.

</details>


### [144] [Third-Order Quantum Information from Distributed Randomized Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13099)
*Giovanni Scala,Corrado Cosimo Mattiacci,Gniewomir Sarbicki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究分布式随机测量下三方局域幺正不变量的可提取性，利用置换对称性将重构问题化为3×3矩阵的符号求逆，实现无需指数级矩阵求逆的非线性信息提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机测量可在不重构完整密度矩阵的情况下探测量子态的非线性性质；但在远距离实验室间共享的多体态中，分布式测量协议通常涉及指数级规模的矩阵，如何判断哪些三阶局域幺正不变量组合可被保留、哪些无法区分，并高效重构，是待解决的问题。

Method: 对同一局域设置下测得的三个结果概率之积，在独立局域随机幺正变换上取平均；针对任意粒子数，分析分布式测量协议诱导的置换对称性，确定平均后可保留的、局域幺正不变的三阶组合；并利用该对称性把重构问题约化为3×3矩阵的符号求逆。

Result: 确定了任意粒子数下哪些三阶局域幺正不变量组合可被平均值保留、哪些无法分离；可获取信息被编码为量子态的代数不变量；重构问题无论测量结果数目多少，均归结为一个3×3矩阵的符号求逆。

Conclusion: 分布式随机测量协议中的置换对称性使非线性信息可被高效提取，避免了分布式多体量子系统中通常出现的指数级大矩阵求逆，为无需全态层析的非线性性质探测提供了可扩展途径。

Abstract: Randomized measurements offer a way to probe nonlinear properties of quantum states without reconstructing their full density matrices. For states shared between distant laboratories, we study products of three outcome probabilities measured at the same local settings and then averaged over independent local random unitaries. We determine, for an arbitrary number of parties, which third-order local-unitary invariant combinations these averages retain and which they cannot separate. The accessible information is encoded in algebraic invariants of the quantum state and is reconstructed by analysing the permutation symmetries induced by the distributed measurement protocol. Exploiting these symmetries, the reconstruction problem collapses, regardless of the number of measurement outcomes, to the symbolic inversion of a 3 x 3 matrix. As a result, nonlinear information can be extracted without inverting the exponentially large matrices that typically arise in distributed multipartite quantum systems.

</details>


### [145] [Which data samples matter when training a quantum model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13103)
*M. Emre Sahin,Cenk Tüysüz,David Galvin,Oscar Wallis,Alex Gregory,Paul Edwards,Christa Zoufal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出量子兼容版本的V-usable information（V可用信息）指标，从以数据为中心的视角度量量子模型能从数据集中提取的信息量，并据此筛选对学习贡献最大的数据子集，提升量子机器学习的训练数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子机器学习研究多集中于模型视角（表达能力、损失景观、泛化行为），而对特定量子模型与具体数据集的兼容性缺乏了解，缺少可对模型与数据进行直接、以数据为中心比较的度量工具。

Method: 建立与量子模型兼容的V-usable information形式化定义，用该指标量化模型可从数据集中提取的信息；利用该指标分析哪些数据点对模型学习过程贡献最大，并据此设计有依据的数据子集预选策略。

Result: 该指标能够直接对比不同量子模型在给定数据集上的可用信息；基于数据点贡献分析的预选策略可显著提升训练流程的数据效率。

Conclusion: V-usable information可作为学习动态的有力诊断工具，并为数据高效的量子机器学习提供一条实用路径。

Abstract: The expressivity, loss landscapes, and generalization behavior of quantum machine learning models have been studied extensively, yet mainly from a model-centric point of view. Little is known about the compatibility of particular quantum models with specific datasets. In this work, we study quantum machine learning models with a data-dependent focus by establishing a quantum-compatible form of the V-usable information. This metric quantifies the information a model can extract from a dataset, enabling a direct, data-centric comparison of different models. We employ this metric to study which data points contribute most significantly to a model's learning process. This enables us to develop an informed preselection strategy for data subsets that can significantly improve the data efficiency of the training pipeline. This work establishes the V-usable information as a powerful diagnostic tool for learning dynamics and a practical route to data-efficient quantum machine learning.

</details>


### [146] [Finite Measured Entropy Production Does Not Imply Finite Microscopic Irreversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13110)
*Hieu Nguyen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个退化参量振荡器系统，证明在有限效率光子计数这一粗粒化测量下，观测到的相对熵（时间箭头）始终有限且严格为正，而微观熵产生在真空浴中发散，揭示"有限且具信息量的熵产生测量"可与"无限微观不可逆性"共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗粒化测量只给出熵产生的下界，但其可能掩盖多少微观不可逆性此前缺乏定量刻画。作者希望厘清：当热浴趋于真空（吸收被禁止、正反向轨迹法则支撑集不再重叠）时，可观测的时间不对称性是否会随之无条件发散。

Method: 构造驱动量子系统：阈值以下的退化参量振荡器，通过重复相互作用与玻色辅助粒子耦合至热库，并向真空浴维持有限正热流。以含有限收集效率、门控时间窗口与独立暗计数的光子计数器定义正反向对齐轨迹法则的公共粗粒化，并解析计算观测相对熵随浴温T_b的行为。

Result: 对固定观测时长T与探测器参数，证明观测相对熵在T_b→0时一致有界，且真空下严格为正；被隐藏的贡献满足Σ_hid(T,T_b)=T·Q̇₀/(k_BT_b)+O(1)，其中Q̇₀>0为稳态真空热流。

Conclusion: 暗计数等误报在掩盖支撑集失配的同时仍保留可测的时间不对称性；因此有限而具信息量的熵产生测量可以与无限微观不可逆性同时存在，粗粒化下界对真实不可逆性的估计可能严重低估。

Abstract: Coarse-grained measurements provide a lower bound on entropy production, but how much microscopic irreversibility can they conceal? We construct a driven quantum system in which an imperfect counter registers a finite, strictly positive arrow of time while the microscopic entropy production is infinite. The system is a degenerate parametric oscillator below threshold, coupled to a thermal reservoir through repeated interactions with bosonic ancillas. The oscillator sustains a finite, positive heat current into the vacuum bath, where absorption is forbidden and forward and reverse trajectory laws have different supports. A photon counter with finite collection efficiency, gated time bins, and independent dark counts defines a common coarse-graining of the aligned trajectory laws. For fixed observation duration $\mathcal T$ and detector parameters, we prove that the observed relative entropy remains uniformly bounded as the bath temperature $T_b$ vanishes and is strictly positive at vacuum. The hidden contribution obeys $Σ_{\mathrm{hid}}(\mathcal T,T_b)=\mathcal T\dot Q_0/(k_BT_b)+O(1)$, where $\dot Q_0>0$ is the stationary vacuum heat current. False positives conceal the support mismatch while preserving measurable time asymmetry. Thus, finite and informative measurements of entropy production can coexist with infinite microscopic irreversibility.

</details>


### [147] [FusionQ: A Generic and Optimal Decoding Framework for Ultra-Low-Latency Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13111)
*Junyu Zhou,Sean Garner,Aarthi Sundaram,Gushu Li,Yue Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 FusionQ 融合式解码框架：以触发探测器簇为中心构建开放边界局部 DEM，按需扩展足迹，将局部解码结果融合为单一全局纠正，并在精确后端下与整体 MLE 等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 QEC 解码依赖固定解码实例（整体时空 DEM 或预定时间窗），需等到足够综合征数据后才交给后端，难以让解码与硬件采样重叠，且所有 shot 被迫走同一全局问题。

Method: 围绕已触发探测器簇构造开放边界局部 DEM；当局部最优解使用虚拟边界误差时扩展对应足迹；调度器仅需局部解与边界使用标志，故可兼容精确求解器、图状 DEM 上的匹配式解码器与启发式解码器。

Result: 证明组合最优性定理：精确最小权局部后端且无边界发布时，融合局部结果得到原 DEM 的全局 MLE。实验表明精确后端下 FusionQ 在每个 shot 上与整体 MLE 一致；各后端下通过将局部解码与综合征采集重叠，显著降低解码延迟。

Conclusion: FusionQ 以"由综合征导出局部任务再融合"取代固定窗口范式，兼顾最优性与后端通用性，并支持流式重叠解码。

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) decoding has traditionally been organized around fixed decoding instances: a monolithic space-time detector error model (DEM), or a predetermined temporal window, is handed to a backend decoder after enough syndrome data has arrived. This paper presents FusionQ, a fusion decoding framework that changes this paradigm by deriving local decoding tasks from the observed syndrome and then fusing their results into a single global correction. Instead of forcing every shot through the same global or windowed problem, FusionQ builds open-boundary local DEMs around fired detector clusters and expands the corresponding footprints only when a local optimum uses virtual boundary errors. FusionQ is generic in the decoder backend: the scheduler requires only a local solution and a boundary-use flag, so exact solvers, matching-style decoders on graphlike DEMs, and heuristic decoders can share the same runtime. We prove a compositional optimality theorem: with an exact minimum-weight local backend and boundary-free publication, fusing the published local decoding results yields a global most-likely error (MLE) solution of the original DEM. Our evaluation shows that, with an exact backend, FusionQ matches the monolithic MLE result on every shot. Across decoder backends, FusionQ substantially reduces decoding latency by overlapping local decoding with hardware syndrome acquisition.

</details>


### [148] [Rational quantum spectral filtering by repeat-until-success protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13154)
*Maksim A. Gavreev,Aleksey K. Fedorov,Evgeniy O. Kiktenko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出有限记忆的重复直至成功(RUS)框架，用量子线路实现有理谱滤波R(X)，突破QSP/QSVT仅能做多项式变换的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QSP/QSVT只能实现多项式谱变换，难以高效逼近有理函数（如正则化预解式、洛伦兹谱线），固定深度多项式加后选择方案查询成本高。

Method: 交错调用厄米算子X的块编码查询与中途测量（接受/拒绝/继续，不重置量子态）；协议可运行任意轮次，但其终止统计由有限维Lyapunov方程确定；在一致终止与相位反转对称条件下刻画可实现的接受谱滤波集，并给出任意可行R的构造性编译器、记忆与期望查询成本界，用谱相干核刻画接受的量子操作。

Result: 精确刻画：在接受时R可为[-1,1]上无极点且取值在[0,1]的任意实有理函数；给出记忆与期望查询成本上界；在正则化预解式与洛伦兹光谱应用中获低阶精确实现，相比固定深度QSVT多项式后选择基线具查询成本优势（成本含所有重复与拒绝后独立重启）。

Conclusion: RUS有限记忆框架将可实现的谱变换从多项式扩展到有理函数，且成本更优，为有理谱滤波提供通用编译方案。

Abstract: We introduce a finite-memory repeat-until-success (RUS) framework for implementing rational spectral filters on a quantum computer, extending spectral filtering beyond the polynomial transformations implemented by quantum signal processing (QSP) and quantum singular value transformation (QSVT). Our approach interleaves queries to a block encoding of a Hermitian operator $X$, whose spectrum lies in $[-1,1]$, and mid-circuit measurements that either accept, reject, or continue the computation without resetting the quantum state. Although the protocol may run for arbitrarily many rounds, its terminal statistics are determined by a finite-dimensional Lyapunov equation. Under uniform termination and phase-inversion symmetry, we characterize exactly the spectral filters $R(X)$ realizable upon acceptance: $R$ can be any real rational function with no poles on $[-1,1]$ and values in $[0,1]$. We provide a constructive compiler for any admissible $R$, with bounds on memory and expected query cost, and characterize the accepted quantum operation through a spectral coherence kernel. Applications to regularized resolvents and Lorentzian spectroscopy yield exact low-degree realizations and query-cost advantages over the fixed-depth QSVT polynomial-postselection baseline considered here, with all repetitions and independent restarts after rejection included in the cost.

</details>


### [149] [Long-range correlated quantum errors from diffuson-correlated dephasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13156)
*N. R. S. van Venrooij,M. E. Flatté*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出"扩散子关联退相位"(diffuson-correlated dephasing)机制：无序导体中的低能扩散模式会对共享该导体的多个量子比特施加空间关联的退相位噪声，其噪声核随比特间距平方反比衰减并随比特对取向变号，二维情形下单比特与其余阵列的关联噪声随阵列尺寸对数增长，从而破坏容错阈值定理所依赖的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电学固态量子比特的初始化、操控与读出普遍依赖扩散导体。既有研究多关注单个比特与环境的局域噪声，而对"多个比特共享同一扩散导体"时可能出现的空间关联噪声缺乏定量刻画；若这类关联噪声随阵列规模增长，将直接威胁大规模量子计算的容错性，因此需要从环境软模出发给出可计算、可实验检验的噪声理论。

Method: 以描述无序导电区域的 Keldysh 非线性 sigma 模型为起点，将导体与二能级系统(TLS)作电容耦合，对扩散子自由度求迹/积分，得到闭式的噪声核(noise kernel)；据此解析分析其随比特间距的标度、随比特对取向的符号变化，以及二维下噪声在全阵列上的累积行为，并对照容错阈值定理的适用条件。

Result: 噪声核以比特间距平方反比的规律衰减，并随比特对取向改变符号，故等间距的比特对可呈相关或反相关——这构成明确的实验判据；在二维情形、最坏情况下，单个比特与阵列其余部分共享的关联噪声随阵列尺寸对数增长而非收敛；因此关联噪声下容错的充分条件被违反，标准阈值定理不再保证大比特阵列对该信道的保护。

Conclusion: 这种退相位源自环境的软模(扩散子)结构，而非任何具体门操作细节，故凡固态量子比特或传感器共享无隙导电介质之处，都可能出现此类关联退相位信道；结果是：将电学量子比特架构扩展到大规模时，必须把关联扩散子退相位计入错误预算，或通过设计(如抑制导体软模、屏蔽耦合、间隔与几何排布)加以规避。

Abstract: Electrical solid-state qubits are generally initialized, manipulated and read out through diffusive conductors. We show that the disorder-induced low energy modes of such a conductor --- diffusons --- impose spatially correlated dephasing on any qubits that share it, a mechanism we term diffuson-correlated dephasing. Starting from a Keldysh nonlinear sigma model for the disordered conductive region, coupled capacitively to two-level systems, we obtain the noise kernel in closed form. It decays as the inverse square of the qubit separation and changes sign with the orientation of the pair, so that qubit pairs at equal separation may be correlated or anticorrelated, an unambiguous experimental signature. In two dimensions, the correlated noise a single qubit shares with the rest of the array, in the worst-case scenario, grows logarithmically with array size rather than converging. The sufficient condition for fault tolerance under correlated noise is therefore violated, so standard threshold theorems no longer guarantee protection against this channel for large qubit arrays. As this dephasing originates from the soft-mode content of the environment rather than on any specific gate detail, we expect correlated dephasing channels of this kind wherever solid-state qubits or sensors share a gapless conductive medium.

</details>


### [150] [How wide must an eavesdropper be? Leakage of a toric-code logical bit to a strip and the Nishimori point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13174)
*Konstantinos Karagiannis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文证明：在任意区域、任意Pauli噪声（含关联噪声）下，观察者学习一个逻辑比特的最优优势恰为 1−2P_fail，其中 P_fail 是该区域受限最大似然解码的失败概率；由此得出"几何而非噪声提供保护"、"宽度不是风险的良好指标"等分区解码与复用读出设计准则，并在 IBM Heron 处理器上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算机把每个逻辑量子比特的部分信息分发给独立解码器模块与共享读出线，单个模块究竟泄露多少逻辑信息此前缺乏精确刻画，亟需一个可量化的信息泄露度量以指导分区解码与复用读出设计。

Method: 建立信息泄露与解码失败概率的精确恒等式：观察者最优优势 = 1 − 2P_fail（P_fail 为区域受限的最大似然解码失败率），适用于任意区域与任意Pauli噪声（含关联噪声）；用该恒等式把条带区域的操作阈值映射为随机键Ising模型的Nishimori点，并用宽度至16的Walsh-Hadamard转移矩阵数值定位该点；在 d=3、5 的两台 IBM Heron 处理器上以模拟的"条带窃听者"进行实验，并考察非均匀量子比特错误下训练攻击者的表现。

Result: 缺失单列码的区域在任何噪声水平下都学不到任何信息；横跨整个码的区域仅在翻转率 10^-3 时需 d>350 这类不切实际的距离才有保护。i.i.d. 噪声下两行条带与单行一样几乎无泄露，但阈值以下窃听者所需宽度仅随 ln d 增长。Walsh-Hadamard 转移矩阵将条带阈值（Nishimori点）定位到千分之几精度。在 IBM Heron (d=3,5) 上，模拟条带攻击者在宽度三处出现预测的跃变；非均匀量子比特错误使训练攻击者能从均匀噪声下无用的第二行获益。

Conclusion: 逻辑信息泄露由区域几何而非噪声强度决定，宽度不能作为风险指标；所证恒等式统一了信息论泄露、解码失败率与统计物理阈值，可直接转化为分区解码与复用读出的设计规则，并给出了硬件层面的实验验证与开源代码数据。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computers hand pieces of each logical qubit to separate decoder modules and shared readout lines. How much does a piece reveal? We prove that for any region and any Pauli noise, including correlated noise, an observer's optimal advantage in learning a logical bit is exactly 1 - 2 P_fail, where P_fail is the failure probability of maximum-likelihood decoding confined to that region. Two lessons follow. Geometry, not noise, provides the protection: a region that misses a single column of the code learns exactly nothing at any noise level, while a region that spans the code is protected by noise only at impractical distances (d > 350 for one row at a 10^-3 flip rate). And width is a poor guide to risk: under i.i.d. noise a two-row strip learns exactly as little as one row, yet below threshold an eavesdropper needs a width growing only as ln d. Through the identity, the strip's operational threshold is the Nishimori point of the random-bond Ising model, and a Walsh-Hadamard transfer matrix up to width 16 locates it to within a few thousandths. On two IBM Heron processors (d = 3, 5), an emulated strip adversary shows the predicted jump at width three, and non-uniform qubit errors let a trained attacker profit from the second row that uniform noise makes useless. We turn these results into design rules for partitioned decoding and multiplexed readout. Code and data are included.

</details>


### [151] [Quantized memory of measurement records near the light cone of quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13175)
*Jamal Slim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在砖墙型随机电路中，沿光锥边缘的测量记录构成两态马尔可夫链；光锥内部第 d 个格点的测量统计仅由 ⌊d/2⌋+1 个随光锥移动的量子比特决定，其余比特充当无记忆热浴，从而给出任意规模、任意双比特门下的精确可解参考，并在 156 比特超导处理器上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子处理器已能在电路中途测量比特，测量结果流（误差校正、基于测量的态制备、受监测动力学）是重要原始输出；但对扰乱电路而言，预测该结果流的统计等同于模拟处理器本身，一旦器件超出经典可模拟规模，就缺乏精确参考来校验。作者希望找到一种在任意系统尺度下都严格可解、可作为基准的判决性结构。

Method: 在最大混态背景中置入一个偏振比特的砖墙型电路里，分析光锥边缘附近测量结果的相关结构；对任意双比特门推导"记忆量子化"结论：边缘处记录为两态马尔可夫链，距边缘 d 个格点处的测量由 ⌊d/2⌋+1 个随光锥移动的比特生成，其余比特构成无记忆浴。进一步由同一因果结构分析硬件噪声的可探测性并给出消除方案，以及无需层析的双酉性见证量；最后在 156 比特超导处理器上按预先设定的分析方案进行验证。

Result: 理论上得到记忆量化的严格界（边缘 1 比特，每偏移一格至多多 1 比特，一般门可达到该界）；指出记录能"看到"哪些硬件噪声、可在不增加门开销下消除该敏感性，并给出免层析的双酉性见证。实验中在光锥边缘内侧 1 个和 2 个格点处观测到预测的记忆量，而在理论禁止的位点上未观测到记忆。

Conclusion: 该工作为动态电路提供了任意系统规模下的精确参考，使超出经典模拟规模的器件仍可被严格检验；同时表明开放系统理论中的碰撞模型（collision model）精确地涌现于混沌动力学内部。

Abstract: Quantum processors now measure qubits in the middle of a circuit, and the stream of outcomes is the raw output of error correction, measurement-based state preparation and monitored dynamics. For a scrambling circuit, predicting the statistics of this stream is as hard as simulating the processor, so there is no exact reference to check it against once the device outgrows classical simulation. We show that near the light cone the problem becomes exactly solvable, for any two-qubit gate. In a brickwork circuit with one polarized qubit in a maximally mixed background, measurements along the edge of the light cone form a two-state Markov chain, and measurements $d$ sites inside the edge are generated by $\lfloor d/2\rfloor+1$ qubits that move with it, while the rest of the chain, however large, acts on them as a memoryless bath. The memory of the record is therefore quantized, one bit on the edge and at most one more for every site of displacement, a bound that generic gates attain. The same causal structure determines which hardware noise the record can see, removes that sensitivity at no gate cost, and yields a tomography-free witness of dual-unitarity. On a 156-qubit superconducting processor, a demonstration with its analysis specified in advance finds the predicted memory one and two sites inside the light cone, and none where the theory forbids it. This gives dynamic circuits an exact reference at any system size, and shows that the collision models of open-system theory arise exactly inside chaotic dynamics.

</details>


### [152] [Exceptional points in non-Markovian quantum-state diffusion: From coefficient dynamics to state signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13221)
*Yusui Chen,Hang Liu,Kaiqi Xiong,Quanzhen Ding,Eric Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在精确非马尔可夫量子态扩散（NMQSD）框架下，本工作区分了"系数方程的例外点"与"量子态可观测量的例外点"，论证例外点签名依赖于因果初始化、态重构与噪声平均三个环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 例外点（EP）通常在系数（记忆核）动力学层面被定义，但实验或理论上的"可观测量"往往是对量子态的特定读出。作者希望澄清：系数动力学中出现的高阶极点（缺陷模式）是否能、以及在何种条件下能完整地传递到具体的量子态可观测量中，并厘清非马尔可夫噪声平均在其中扮演的角色。

Method: 1) 采用精确NMQSD，沿着从系数动力学到噪声平均所得非马尔可夫主方程的同一算符闭合结构进行分析；2) 构造有限维齐次提升（homogeneous lift）识别因果初始化所到达的Jordan链；3) 通过态重构确定哪些贡献在指定读出中存活。研究两类模型：(a) 限制在零/单激发子空间的共同浴量子比特，取噪声无关的降算符形式O，得到闭合主方程，并分析缺陷衰减模式向"无条件激发–参考相干"的极点阶数传递，包括一个已知的三阶构型（二次–指数型相干及其在另一正交分量中最高阶项的抵消）；(b) 固定光子数的光机械扇区中位置耦合振子，其精确闭合含标量噪声历史项；在浴衰减率低于振子频率的规则可归一化分支上，考察两个二阶系数块。

Result: (a) 共同浴量子比特模型：每个缺陷衰减模式将其完整极点阶数传递给合适的无条件激发–参考相干；三阶构型同时表现出二次–指数型相干，以及另一正交分量中最高阶项的抵消。(b) 光机械模型：在长制备时龄、固定延迟下，条件位移对NMQSD噪声历史的响应出现二重极点；纯粹的代数系数尾项在条件高斯宽度中抵消；完整噪声平均改变主方程系数，并将条件宽度与态中心的加权展宽合并。

Conclusion: 系数例外点并不自动等同于可观测量例外点。其物理意义由三要素共同决定：因果制备（决定进入哪条Jordan链）、态重构（决定存活贡献）、噪声平均（决定最终主方程系数与读出形式）。因此"例外点"的阶数与可观测特征必须相对于特定读出与平均流程来陈述。

Abstract: An exceptional point of a memory-coefficient equation need not have the same signature in every quantum-state observable. We examine this relation within exact non-Markovian quantum-state diffusion (NMQSD), following the same operator closure from its coefficient dynamics to the non-Markovian master equation obtained by noise averaging. A finite homogeneous lift identifies the Jordan chains reached by the causal initialization, while state reconstruction determines which contributions survive in a specified readout. Two models expose the distinction. For common-bath qubits restricted to zero and one excitation, a noise-independent lowering-form $O$ operator gives a closed master equation. Every defective decaying mode transfers its full pole order to a suitable unconditional excitation--reference coherence. A known third-order configuration illustrates both a quadratic--exponential coherence and cancellation of its highest-order term in another quadrature. For a position-coupled oscillator in a fixed-photon-number optomechanical sector, the exact closure contains a scalar noise-history term. On the regular normalizable branch studied for bath decay rates below the oscillator frequency, two second-order coefficient blocks lead to a double pole in the conditional displacement response to the NMQSD noise history, at long preparation age and fixed lag. The purely algebraic coefficient tail cancels from the conditional Gaussian width. Complete noise averaging changes the master-equation coefficients and combines conditional width with the weighted spread of state centers. Together, the models show how causal preparation, state reconstruction, and averaging give coefficient exceptional points a precise, readout-dependent meaning.

</details>


### [153] [A Compiler for Fault Tolerant Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13301)
*Narasinga Rao Miniskar,Elaine Wong,Seyong Lee,Vicente Leyton-Ortega,Jeffrey S. Vetter,Travis S. Humble*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出开源 MLIR 方言 FTQC，将逻辑量子比特、综合征、魔法态与 Pauli 帧建模为带类型的 SSA 值，实现纠错感知的类型系统、两级 lowering 与 IR 内资源分析，可降至 OpenQASM 3、QIR、Stim 与 IQM 硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算（FTQC）的编译中间表示需在分析、模拟与硬件降级过程中保留纠错码选择、综合征信息及非 Clifford 资源需求，而现有 IR 缺乏对这些信息的显式类型化表达。

Method: 构建 MLIR 方言 FTQC：以纠错感知类型系统将逻辑量子比特、综合征、魔法态、Pauli 帧表示为静态单赋值（SSA）值；采用两级 lowering 设计降低至 OpenQASM 3、QIR、Stim 与 IQM 硬件；并将资源分析驻留于 IR 中。以三个纠错码族开展案例研究。

Result: 在橡树岭国家实验室 20 量子比特 Pathfinder 平台上，解码使逻辑读出错误降低 3.3 倍；QFT-3 案例显示魔法态工厂的峰值量子比特需求为编码电路的 5 倍，揭示隐藏的架构开销。

Conclusion: 类型化元数据使码相关的设计决策可验证、可重定向，为跨软硬件目标的容错编译变换开发与评估提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) needs compiler intermediate representations (IRs) that preserve error-correcting-code choices, syndrome information, and non-Clifford resource requirements through analysis, simulation, and hardware lowering. We report practical experience building FTQC, an open-source Multi-Level Intermediate Representation (MLIR) dialect that makes logical qubits, syndromes, magic states, and Pauli frames typed static single assignment (SSA) values. Its error-correction-aware type system, two-level lowering design, and IR-resident resource analysis provide one inspectable pipeline to Open Quantum Assembly Language~3 (OpenQASM~3), the Quantum Intermediate Representation (QIR), Stim, and IQM hardware. Case studies across three code families show that typed metadata makes code-specific decisions verifiable and retargetable: on the 20-qubit Pathfinder at Oak Ridge National Laboratory, decoding reduced logical readout error by 3.3 times. They also expose hidden architectural costs: for a three-qubit Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT-3), a magic-state factory requires five times the peak qubits of the encoded circuit. These experiences demonstrate a practical foundation for developing and evaluating fault-tolerant compiler transformations across software and hardware targets.

</details>


### [154] [Early State Exclusion for $5\times5$ Jacobi Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13315)
*Jasmin G. Enriquez,Anastasiia Minenkova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文解决了一维量子链（n=5个量子比特）中"在完美态传输首次出现之前量子态完全消失"这一开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算中的态传输问题：对奇数 n>4 的一维量子链，可能出现待传输量子态在完美态传输首次发生前就完全消失（概率为零）的现象，此前仅作为开放问题提出，缺乏解答。

Method: 针对 n=5 的情形给出构造性数学证明；证明仅需三角学与微积分知识，建模部分用到线性代数中的特征值理论与内积。

Result: 给出了 n=5 时该问题（态提前完全消失）的确定性解答与完整证明。

Conclusion: 为奇数长度一维量子链中"先消失后传输"现象提供了首个可验证实例，且证明门槛较低，便于学生理解与后续推广。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a problem that arose from quantum computing, when the quantum state to be transferred completely vanishes before the first occurrence of the perfect state transfer. It was posed as an open problem for 1D quantum chains with $n$ of qubits, where $n>4$ is an odd number. We present the solution for $n=5$. The proofs are accessible to students who studied trigonometry and calculus. As for the set-up the knowledge of eigentheory and inner products from linear algebra is needed.

</details>


### [155] [The wave equation and two-electron atomic wavefunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13320)
*John Lekner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 三维经典波动方程的轴对称解与"两电子动能算符"（在相对固定原子核的坐标下表达）的零模之间存在一一对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示经典波动方程的轴对称解与量子多体问题中特定动能算符（两电子相对固定核坐标下的动能算符）零模之间的深刻数学结构联系，从而可在两个看似不同的方程之间建立映射。

Method: 通过坐标变换与算符对应，建立轴对称波动方程解空间与两电子动能算符核空间之间的显式一一映射（构造性对应关系）。

Result: 证明了二者解空间之间存在一一对应（同构），即波动方程的轴对称解可用该动能算符的零模函数刻画，反之亦然。

Conclusion: 该对应为求解相关方程提供了可迁移的工具，并提示经典波方程与多电子动能算符零模共享相同的数学结构。

Abstract: Axisymmetric solutions of the classical wave equation in three spatial dimensions are shown to be in one-to-one correspondence to functions which annihilate the two-electron kinetic energy operator expressed in coordinates relative to a fixed nucleus.

</details>


### [156] [Computational Power of Clifford and Ising Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13347)
*Sawyer Dobson,Zhenghan Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将Ising量子计算机的高效经典模拟从编织(braiding)推广到测量(measurement)，并研究不同"高效Clifford量子计算"定义下（取决于电路生成器能力）的计算能力：当生成器可看到完整输入时，高效经典模拟将导出P与PSPACE的分离；当生成器仅看到输入长度时，Clifford量子计算机恰好判定所谓仿射语言(affine languages)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的高效经典模拟结果主要针对Ising量子计算中的编织操作，缺少对测量这一基本操作的处理；同时"高效Clifford量子计算"在文献中定义不一，其定义细节（尤其电路生成器的能力）如何影响计算复杂度归属尚不清楚，需要系统澄清。

Method: 考察若干种不同强度的电路生成器，据此给出多种"高效Clifford量子计算"的定义（生成器可见整个输入 vs. 仅见输入长度），先把编织情形的高效经典模拟技术扩展到测量，再结合复杂性类分离的论证与语言刻画进行分析。

Result: 若电路生成器能看到完整输入，则对这类计算的高效经典模拟将蕴含 P ≠ PSPACE（即导致两个复杂度类的分离）；若生成器只能看到输入长度，则Clifford量子计算机所判定的语言类恰好是作者定义的仿射语言类。

Conclusion: Clifford（Ising）量子计算的计算能力对"高效"定义中生成器的可见信息高度敏感；该工作把经典模拟从编织拓展到测量，并给出复杂度分离条件与语言类的精确刻画，为理解Clifford量子计算的经典可模拟边界提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We extend the efficient classical simulation of Ising quantum computers from braidings to measurements. We investigate several different definitions of efficient Clifford quantum computation by varying the power of the circuit generator. In case the circuit generator can see the entire input, we give a detailed proof that efficient simulation of classical computation would imply the separation of complexity classes $\mathbf{P}$ and $\mathbf{PSPACE}$. If the circuit generator sees only the input length, we prove that Clifford quantum computers decide what we call affine languages.

</details>


### [157] [Syndrome extraction for quantum LDPC codes in silicon: Distributed entanglement and mobile ancillas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13371)
*William Chow,Jonathan Baugh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出在硅自旋节点网络上构建"单重态织物"（singlet fabric）来执行双变量自行车（bivariate-bicycle）qLDPC码的长程校验，并用硬件约束调度与电路级存储仿真比较分布式GHZ态、隐变量隐形传态与CAbLECAR（移动隐变量直接传输）三类综合征提取电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qLDPC码能降低每个逻辑量子比特所需的数据量子比特数，但其校验涉及平面器件上的长程相互作用，难以直接实现。作者希望评估在以自旋单重态为纠缠资源的模块化硅节点网络上，何种综合征提取方案最为可行、可靠。

Method: 1) 在穿梭通道上产生自旋单重态，并在节点间分发，由局域操作消耗单重态以测量非局域稳定子；2) 构造两种提取电路：基于分布式GHZ态与基于隐变量隐形传态；3) 采用受硬件约束的调度与电路级存储仿真进行评估；4) 与CAbLECAR（在每条校验的数据量子比特间运送移动隐变量）对比；5) 进一步考察把移动隐变量置于所设计节点晶格上的效果、固定数据访问顺序的影响，以及源标记（heralding）与相位纯化等资源提纯手段。

Result: 在原始布局上，当传输过程中同时包含自旋翻转与退相位时，隐形传态可优于CAbLECAR；在节点晶格上，[[144,12,12]] Gross码的平均调度路径由350 μm缩短至153 μm，逻辑错误率在所采样的比特翻转区间内最多降低50倍。直接传输在每个采样点上的逻辑错误率均低于两种单重态电路。当存在频繁传输比特翻转、且双比特门与读出错误率降至约4–5×10⁻⁴（约为本文假设值的一半）以下时，GHZ电路反超直接传输。固定隐变量的数据访问顺序会形成"更短路径"与"隐变量故障引起的关联错误"之间的权衡。源标记对两种单重态电路均有改善；相位纯化仅在依赖噪声的交叉点以上对GHZ电路有益，而对隐形传态方案始终有害。

Conclusion: 物理布局与相互作用顺序的重要性可与提取电路的选择相当；更纯净的纠缠资源并不必然带来更可靠的逻辑存储。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes can reduce the number of data qubits required per logical qubit, but their checks require long-range interactions on a planar device. We develop a singlet fabric for bivariate-bicycle codes on a silicon node network. Spin singlets are generated on shuttling lanes and distributed between nodes, where local operations consume them to measure non-local stabilizers. We construct two syndrome-extraction circuits, based on distributed GHZ states and ancilla teleportation, and evaluate them using hardware-constrained schedules and circuit-level memory simulations. We compare these circuits with CAbLECAR, which transports a mobile ancilla between the data qubits of each check. Teleportation can outperform CAbLECAR on its original layout when transport includes spin flips as well as dephasing. Placing the mobile ancilla on our node lattice, however, shortens the mean scheduled route for the [[144,12,12]] Gross code from 350 to 153 $μ$m and lowers its logical error rate up to 50-fold in the sampled bit-flip regime. Direct transport then achieves a lower logical error rate than either singlet circuit at every sampled Gross-code point. With frequent transport bit flips, the GHZ circuit overtakes direct transport once two-qubit gate and readout errors fall below about $4$-$5\times10^{-4}$, roughly half the value assumed here. Fixing the ancilla's data-visit order reveals a trade-off between shorter routes and the correlated errors produced by ancilla faults. Source heralding improves both singlet circuits, whereas phase purification helps the GHZ circuit only above a noise-dependent crossover and worsens teleportation throughout. These results show that the physical layout and interaction order can be as consequential as the choice of extraction circuit, and that cleaner entangled resources do not necessarily produce a more reliable logical memory.

</details>


### [158] [PriorTilt: Decoding quantum LDPC codes with sparse perturbations of belief propagation priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13373)
*Min Ye,Nicolas Delfosse*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 PriorTilt：在保持 BP 消息传递规则不变的前提下，选择性扰动少量先验对数似然比（由近期后验与出边消息统计确定偏置），在三个基准码的电路级解码中全面优于 Relay-BP 与 beam search。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BP 是经典 LDPC 码的主流译码器，但在量子场景下（如量子 LDPC / 量子纠错码的电路级噪声）精度受限，需要在不改变其消息传递框架、不显著增加复杂度的前提下提升译码性能。

Method: PriorTilt 作为 BP 的变体：每轮仅对一小部分先验对数似然比施加偏移扰动，偏移量由最近的后验概率与出边消息统计量确定，消息传递规则与原 BP 完全一致；在三个基准码（含 [[144,12,12]] Gross code BB144）上与 Relay-BP、beam search 及标准 min-sum BP 在电路级噪声下对比。

Result: 在所有测试数据点上 LER 均低于两个基线；BB144 在 p=1e-3 时 LER 相对 Relay-BP 降低 4.3×、相对 beam search 降低 3.1×；同噪声下平均译码时间低于 Relay-BP（三个码均如此），相对 beam search 平均加速约 2.6×–3.0×；BB144 在 p=1e-3、相同迭代预算下 LER 较标准 min-sum BP 低约 610×，且每轮平均扰动先验比例不足 0.5%。

Conclusion: 以极小的先验扰动（<0.5%/轮）即可显著改善量子 BP 解码的准确率与速度，PriorTilt 在精度与效率上同时优于 Relay-BP 与 beam search，是一种低开销、易集成的量子码 BP 改进方案。

Abstract: Although belief propagation (BP) is a widely adopted decoder for classical low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes, its accuracy is limited in the quantum setting. We introduce \PriorTilt{}, a variant of BP obtained by selectively perturbing a small fraction of its prior log-likelihood ratios using offsets determined by recent posterior and outgoing-message statistics, while preserving the same message-passing rules. We compare \PriorTilt{} with Relay-BP and beam search decoders for circuit-level decoding of three benchmark codes. \PriorTilt{} achieves a lower logical error rate (LER) than both baselines at every tested data point. For the $[[144,12,12]]$ Gross code BB144 at noise rate $p=10^{-3}$, the LER reductions reach $4.3\times$ relative to Relay-BP and $3.1\times$ relative to the beam search decoder. At $p=10^{-3}$, \PriorTilt{} has lower mean decoding times than Relay-BP on all three codes and achieves mean decoding speedups of approximately $2.6\times$--$3.0\times$ over the beam search decoder. For BB144 at $p=10^{-3}$, \PriorTilt{} achieves an LER approximately $610\times$ lower than the standard min-sum BP decoder with the same iteration budget, while perturbing fewer than $0.5\%$ of its priors per round on average.

</details>


### [159] [Sample complexity of learning fermionic and bosonic Gaussian states with single-copy and entangled measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13428)
*Ron Rubin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明单副本测量学习混合费米高斯态的样本复杂度上界为 Θ(m³/ε²)（无对数因子），并系统填充了费米/玻色高斯态在单副本、k-副本与集体测量下的样本复杂度表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知集体测量下费米高斯态学习需要 Θ(m²/ε²) 副本，而单副本测量的最优速率存在猜想与空白，且费米/玻色高斯态样本复杂度表有大量未定项，需要统一刻画单副本、k-副本与集体测量的差别。

Method: 构造与度量在各占据数尺度上匹配噪声的单副本测量；对 k-副本采用协变最高权测量并以 Weyl-位移估计器读出；对玻色态给出基于协方差界与占据数分布的测量方案；对无能量/压缩界情形采用非高斯测量。

Result: 单副本费米高斯态 Θ(m³/ε²)（解决 arXiv 2609.23189 猜想 10.1，ε≤1/3600 下界）；k-副本在 Frobenius 范数下为 Θ(max{m³/k,m²}/ε²)，迹距离下在距纯态边界固定距离的类上成立，纯边界外获 1/k 增益（k≤m）；玻色态在协方差有界时达 O((m²+r³+Σ_{i,j>r}1/(n_i+n_j))/ε²)，为 dyadic-区间类上的极小极大速率；无界情形下居中单模态 O(ε⁻²)、居中纯 m-模态 O(m²/ε²)、一般 m-模态 O(m³/ε²)，且无 log log E 项。

Conclusion: 单副本与集体测量间存在本质复杂度差距；该工作基本完成费米与玻色高斯态样本复杂度表的刻画，并显示非高斯测量可消除 log log E 开销。

Abstract: With single-copy measurements, every mixed fermionic Gaussian state on $m$ modes, pure modes included, is learned to trace distance $ε$ from $Θ(m^3/ε^2)$ copies, with no logarithmic factor, against the known $Θ(m^2/ε^2)$ for collective measurements. We prove the upper bound, resolving Conjecture 10.1 of arXiv:2609.23189 (lower bound for $ε\le1/3600$), and fill most open entries of the sample-complexity table of fermionic and bosonic Gaussian states, for single-copy, $k$-copy and collective measurements. The single-copy measurement's noise is matched to the metric at every occupation scale. With blocks of $k$ copies, the covariant highest-weight measurement of arXiv:2611.07271, read out through the Weyl-shift estimator, is exactly unbiased for every state; it gives $Θ(\max\{m^3/k,m^2\}/ε^2)$ in Frobenius norm on all states and in trace distance on every class at a fixed distance from the pure boundary. The gain $1/k$ ($k\le m$) away from the pure boundary, against $1/\sqrt k$ on cold number-conserving states, is therefore attained. For bosonic Gaussian states with bounded covariance, any displacement and squeezing, and occupations $n_1\le\dots\le n_m$, $O((m^2+r^3+\sum_{i,j>r}1/(n_i+n_j))/ε^2)$ single-copy measurements suffice, $r$ the largest index with $r\,n_r\le2$; this is the minimax rate on dyadic-interval classes, with no spectral gap or cold-mode count assumed, and holds with no covariance bound for displaced passive states. With no bound on energy or squeezing, non-Gaussian measurements learn centred one-mode states, mixed included, from $O(ε^{-2})$ and centred pure $m$-mode states from $O(m^2/ε^2)$ single copies, free of the $\log\log E$ term of Gaussian measurements, and $m$-mode states from $O(m^3/ε^2)$ copies, entangled only in a first round on blocks of $4m+6$.

</details>


### [160] [Double-quantum enhanced coherent control and magnetic sensing of spin defects in hexagonal boron nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13447)
*Shihao Ru,Abdullah Rasmita,Jiakai Chen,Yuzhe Yang,Xiaodan Lyu,Robert Cernansky,Edwin Hang Tong Teo,Jesús Zúñiga-Pérez,Fedor Jelezko,Weibo Gao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在hBN的VB⁻系综中实现双量子相干控制，提升AC磁测量信噪比，并将双量子弛豫通道用作电场与应变噪声的差分探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前hBN中VB⁻实验主要单独利用两个自旋三重态基态单量子跃迁，未充分利用Δm_s=2流形，且需要更高灵敏度的AC磁测量与区分噪声来源的手段。

Method: 同时共振微波驱动两个基态单量子跃迁，实现Δm_s=2双量子流形的相干控制；在相同实验条件下与单量子协议对比，并利用双量子弛豫通道作为电场/应变噪声的差分探针。

Result: 双量子协议提高光学读出对比度，在AC磁测量中获得2.6倍信噪比提升；弛豫数据分析表明与相关表面或体分布涨落模型相容，而短程非相关表面噪声模型在分析假设下不被支持。

Conclusion: 确立了hBN自旋系综中的双量子相干控制与频率分辨AC磁测量方法，并指出需依赖样品相关测量来确定主导弛豫通道的微观起源。

Abstract: The negatively charged boron vacancy ($V_{\mathrm{B}}^{-}$) in hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) is a promising spin defect for quantum sensing in two-dimensional material systems. Previous hBN experiments have mainly exploited the two spin-triplet ground-state single-quantum transitions separately. Here we implement a double-quantum driving scheme based on simultaneous resonant microwave excitation of both ground-state single-quantum transitions, establishing coherent control of the $Δm_s=2$ manifold in a $V_{\mathrm{B}}^{-}$ ensemble. We compare the resulting double-quantum response with the corresponding single-quantum protocol under identical experimental conditions. The double-quantum protocol enhances the optical readout contrast and yields a 2.6-fold signal-to-noise improvement in AC magnetometry. We further use the double-quantum relaxation channel as a differential probe of electric-field and strain noise, showing that the present data are compatible with correlated-surface or volume-distributed fluctuations, while short-range uncorrelated surface-noise models are disfavored within the assumptions of our analysis. These results establish double-quantum coherent control and frequency-resolved AC magnetometry in hBN spin ensembles, and identify the sample-dependent measurements needed to determine the microscopic origin of the dominant relaxation channels.

</details>


### [161] [Real-time spectral combs from delayed quantum feedback in multimode cavity QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13465)
*Diego Aranda-Lozano,Héctor M. Castro-Beltrán,José Récamier,Ricardo Román-Ancheyta,Carlos A. González-Gutiérrez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究原子置于电磁腔中心、辐射经往返时间 $t_r$ 返回后再激发所形成的延迟反馈系统中光的含时物理谱，揭示其表现为间隔 $2\pi/t_r$ 的瞬态频率梳，梳齿位于由共振反射原子形成的两个等效半腔的共振频率上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对腔量子电动力学中原子—腔耦合的研究多关注布居数回复或稳态谱（如缀饰态谱），而缺乏对含时"物理谱"（即经有限带宽滤波后实际可测的谱）在连续发射—再吸收循环下如何演化的定量理解，尤其是反馈延迟、反馈相位与原子位置信息在谱上如何体现尚不清楚。

Method: 考虑二能级原子与有限个离散腔模耦合，在耦合强度于原子线宽内近似的条件下，将发射动力学约化为 Bixon–Jortner 型延迟微分方程；借助其以 Laguerre 多项式给出的解析解，计算含时物理谱（引入带宽 $\Gamma$ 的滤波器），并与严格对角化结果对比验证缀饰态谱。

Result: 物理谱在每个布居回复期间出现瞬态频率梳，间隔为 $2\pi/t_r$，源于相继发射阶段间的量子干涉；梳齿不落在腔模上而落在共振反射原子所构成的两个等效半腔的共振处；梳可见度按 $e^{-\Gamma t_r}$ 随滤波带宽 $\Gamma$ 衰减；长时极限下频率梳收敛到缀饰态谱，解析结果与严格对角化定量一致；物理谱还能分辨布居数不敏感的反馈相位，并给出原子位置的频率分辨特征。

Conclusion: 含时物理谱是探测延迟反馈量子光学中反馈相位与原子位置等隐蔽信息的有效工具；结果可在超导 transmon（人工原子）与微波腔及声表面波谐振腔耦合的两类可行实验平台上检验。

Abstract: We study the time-dependent physical spectrum of light for a two-level atom coupled to a large, but discrete, number of modes of an electromagnetic cavity. Radiation emitted by the atom, placed at the center of the cavity, returns after the round-trip time $t_r$, re-excites it, and is re-emitted, producing a sequence of atomic population revivals. For a coupling approximately flat over the atomic linewidth, the emission dynamics reduces to a delay-differential equation of Bixon--Jortner type. Using its analytical solution in terms of Laguerre polynomials, we investigate how successive emission and reabsorption cycles shape the time-dependent spectrum. The physical spectrum exhibits revivals during which a transient frequency comb of spacing $2π/t_r$ develops. The comb results from the quantum interference between successive emission stages; its teeth sit not at the cavity modes but at the resonances of the effective two half-cavities created by the resonantly reflecting atom. The visibility scales as $e^{-Γt_r}$ with the filter bandwidth $Γ$, and at long times the comb converges to the dressed-mode spectrum, which we also obtain analytically in quantitative agreement with exact diagonalization. We show that the physical spectrum resolves the feedback phase, to which the atomic population is insensitive in the regime of well-separated emission stages, and provides additional frequency-resolved signatures of the atomic position. We discuss two feasible experimental implementations of our results with transmons (artificial atoms) coupled to microwave and surface-acoustic-wave resonators.

</details>


### [162] [Packet-wise multi-hop quantum routing of hybrid quantum-classical packets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13625)
*Tae Yeong Park,Youn Chang Jeong,Myeong Soo Kang,Kwang Yong Song,Paul G. Kwiat,Hee Su Park*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于光纤的新型量子路由器，以更透明的操作方式减少对混合数据包（经典头+量子载荷）的扰动和时延，无需再生经典头即可实现双跳路由，并将时间仓量子比特的BB84诱骗态QKD包成功分送至三个接收端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现可扩展量子网络需要在多节点间动态路由量子信息。现有方案依赖波长分束器和光开关分别访问经典与量子成分，并在发送前重新封装、再生经典头，会引入额外扰动与延迟，且不适用于多级级联路由。

Method: 设计基于光纤的量子路由器，对携带经典头和量子载荷的混合数据包进行更透明（passive/低干预）的处理，避免经典头再生；实验搭建两级级联路由器，实现双跳路由至三个不同接收端；并采用时间仓量子比特与两诱骗态BB84协议进行QKD应用验证。

Result: 无需再生经典头即完成两级级联的双跳路由并分发至三个接收端；三个接收端均获得非零安全密钥率，验证了路由后QKD包仍可用于密钥生成。

Conclusion: 研究表明通过多级路由节点互联的大规模量子网络具有可行性，透明化路由是降低扰动和时延、支撑网络扩展的有效途径。

Abstract: The ability to dynamically route quantum information across multiple nodes is a key requirement for realizing versatile and scalable quantum networks. Quantum routers are devices to achieve packet-level routing beyond static point-to-point connections by handling hybrid packets combining classical headers and quantum payloads. Previously proposed methods have selectively accessed the classical and quantum components of the packets utilizing wavelength splitters and optical switches, and repacked the hybrid packets before transmission through the outgoing link with regenerating classical headers. This work reports a novel optical-fiber-based quantum router that minimizes the disturbance and latency on the hybrid packets through more transparent operation. We experimentally demonstrate double-hop routing via two cascaded routers to three distinct receivers without regeneration of the headers. As a practical application, quantum key distribution packets based on time-bin qubits and a BB84 protocol with a two-decoy-state method are successfully delivered to the three receivers achieving nonzero secret key rates. These results demonstrate the feasibility of extensive quantum networks interconnected by multiple-step router nodes.

</details>


### [163] [Machine-learning decoders for fusion-based quantum computing in real time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13630)
*Daniel Barter,Jacob C. Bridgeman,Chris Dawson,Terry Farrelly,Shane Fleming,Mercedes Gimeno-Segovia,Paul Hartke,Timothy Hein,Sean Howell,Gregory Hyegang Jun,Siddhartha Kamalakara,Yehua Liu,Jose M. Monsalve Diaz,Eddie Richter,Sam Roberts*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 PRISM，一种基于 Transformer 的机器学习解码器，利用擦除位置的已知信息，在表面码、色码和量子 LDPC 码上（码距至 24）达到与整数规划解码器相同的逻辑错误率，同时速度显著更快；其 FPGA 实现可对距离 8 的逻辑块实现 6.59 微秒延迟与 1.56 微秒的均摊解码时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算要求解码器同时具备准确性、吞吐量、低延迟和可扩展性。在基于光子融合的架构中，光子损耗和概率性融合测量所带来的擦除是主导错误机制，因此需要在高擦除率下仍保持准确的解码器。

Method: 设计 PRISM——基于 Transformer 的机器学习解码器，显式利用协议中擦除位置的先验信息，同时处理 Pauli 错误与擦除错误；在表面码、色码与量子 LDPC 码等多类容错协议上进行基准测试，并对解码器及其实时硬件实现进行针对性优化。

Result: 在码距至 24 的各类基准上，PRISM 的逻辑错误率与基于整数规划的解码器相当，且在阈值 50% 以下的高错误率区间速度明显更快；在 AMD VP1902 FPGA 上的优化实现可对时空距离均为 8 的逻辑块完成解码，延迟 6.59 微秒，均摊解码时间 1.56 微秒/逻辑块。

Conclusion: 机器学习解码可为以擦除为主导的容错量子架构提供准确且低延迟的推理，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computation requires decoders that combine accuracy, throughput, low latency, and scalability to large numbers of physical qubits. In photonic fusion-based architectures, erasures from photon loss and probabilistic fusion measurements are a dominant error mechanism, motivating decoders that remain accurate at high erasure rates. We introduce PRISM, a transformer-based machine-learning decoder designed to exploit the known locations of erasures in protocols subject to both Pauli and erasure errors. We benchmark PRISM across a wide range of fault-tolerant protocols based on surface codes, color codes, and quantum low-density parity-check codes, with distances up to 24. Across these benchmarks, PRISM matches the logical error rates of integer-programming-based decoders while being significantly faster, including at error rates up to 50% of threshold. We further optimize the decoder and its real-time hardware implementation to reduce latency while maintaining accuracy. Our optimized implementation on an AMD VP1902 FPGA can decode logical blocks of distance 8 in both space and time, demonstrating a latency of 6.59 microseconds and an amortized decoding time of 1.56 microseconds per logical block is possible. These results show that machine-learning decoding can provide accurate, low-latency inference for erasure-dominated fault-tolerant quantum architectures.

</details>


### [164] [An Efficient Quantum Algorithm for Preparing Uniform Superpositions over Arbitrary Integer Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13658)
*Alok Shukla,Prakash Vedula*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究在n量子比特寄存器中精确制备任意整数区间上的均匀量子态，给出无辅助比特、仅含单比特门和单控制单比特门的电路构造，最坏情况门数精确为5n-9（n≥3），并证明Ω(n)下界，达到宽度上的渐近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀叠加态制备是量子算法的基础原语，但现有方法多针对全零到全一的完整区间的均匀态（如Hadamard门），对任意非空整数区间[a, a+M)上的均匀量子态的精确制备缺乏门数最优、无辅助比特的解决方案，需要刻画其精确门复杂度与下界。

Method: 对每个0≤a<a+M≤2^n，构造仅使用单比特门和单受控单比特门的无辅助比特电路（直接构造）；给出恶化的精确门数界（n≥3时为5n-9，n=1和n=2的精确最大值分别为1和3）与Ω(n)下界；推导依赖于实例的界O(log2 M+ε)，其中ε由二进制Hamming重量定义的偏移相关参数；进一步给出反射式构造与经典程序以选取门数更低的精确电路，并分析O(n)逻辑深度上界；最后将方法推广到区间的按位异或像及间距为2的幂的等差数列。

Result: 直接构造的最坏情况精确门数为5n-9（n≥3），n=1、2时分别为1和3；Ω(n)下界说明其在寄存器宽度上渐近最优；存在实例依赖的门界O(log2 M+ε)；直接构造具有O(n)逻辑深度上界；反射式构造与经典选择程序可进一步降低门数；结论可推广至位异或像与2的幂间距等差数列。

Conclusion: 论文完整刻画了区间均匀态精确制备的门复杂度：构造达到最坏情况5n-9的紧确界并与Ω(n)下界匹配，兼具实例自适应性、线性逻辑深度及向异或像和等差结构的可推广性。

Abstract: We consider exact preparation of the uniform quantum state supported on an arbitrary nonempty interval of integer labels in an $n$-qubit register. For every $0\leq a<a+M\leq2^n$, we give an ancilla-free circuit using only one-qubit gates and singly controlled one-qubit gates. In the logical gate model specified in the paper, the direct construction has exact worst-case gate count $5n-9$ for $n\geq3$; its exact maxima for $n=1$ and $n=2$ are $1$ and $3$, respectively. A worst-case lower bound of $Ω(n)$ establishes asymptotic optimality in the register width. In addition, the gate complexity admits an instance-dependent bound $O(\log_2 M+ε)$, where $ε$ is an offset-dependent parameter defined in terms of binary Hamming weights. We also derive a reflected construction and a classical procedure that selects the lower-count exact circuit. The direct construction admits an $O(n)$ logical-depth upper bound. We also treat bitwise-XOR images of intervals and arithmetic progressions with power-of-two spacing.

</details>


### [165] [Observation of strong electric dipole-dipole interactions between two atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13665)
*Yu-Kun Lu,Jiahao Lyu,Wolfgang Ketterle*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在密度超过 $10^{16}/\rm{cm}^3$（即 $n\bar\lambda^3>10$）的原子对样品中研究光与原子相互作用，发现相干偶极-偶极作用超过耗散部分，单光子发射可向原子传递超过十倍反冲动量的力矩，并首次在双原子体系中观测到由近场偶极作用主导的两体 Lamb 位移共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往光诱导偶极-偶极相互作用仅在远场平均场（many-body）区域被观测，双原子近场主导的共振位移缺乏实验证据；同时高密度区间 $n\bar\lambda^3>10$ 中相干与耗散偶极作用的相对关系及动量传递行为尚不清楚。

Method: 制备密度超 $10^{16}/\rm{cm}^3$ 的原子对，用非共振激光将原子修饰出约 1 Debye 的电偶极矩使其相干耦合；通过单光子发射测量动量传递，并通过双原子共振谱测量两体 Lamb 位移。

Result: 相干偶极-偶极作用大于耗散部分；单光子发射可传递大于十倍单光子反冲的动量；双原子共振位移由偶极相互作用的近场部分主导，与以往仅观测到远场主导的多原子情形形成对比。

Conclusion: 工作为光缔合过程提供新认识，并为研究强电偶极-偶极作用驱动的量子体系奠定基础。

Abstract: We have prepared pairs of atoms at densities exceeding $ 10^{16}/\rm{cm}^3$ corresponding to $n\lambdabar^3>10$ and studied how they interact with light. In this regime, the coherent part of the electric dipole-dipole interaction is larger than the dissipative part, and a single photon emission can transfer large momenta (larger than ten times the single photon recoil) to the atoms. For off-resonant laser light, the atoms are dressed with an electric dipole moment of $\approx$ 1 Debye and interact coherently. This light-induced dipole-dipole interaction has been previously observed only in the far-field mean-field regime. For two atoms, the resonance is shifted by the two-body Lamb shift which is dominated by the near-field part of the dipolar interaction. Dipolar resonance shifts have been observed before only for many atoms interacting with the far-field part of the dipolar interaction. Our work sheds new light on photoassociation processes and paves the way for studying driven quantum systems with strong electric dipole-dipole interactions.

</details>


### [166] [Quantum-Assisted Generative Anomaly Detection: A Dimension-Controlled Study of Latent Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13671)
*Thesath Wijayasiri,Omar Bacarreza,William Clements,Kar Wai Fok,Vrizlynn L. L. Thing*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于SAGEGAN风格式良性样本检测器，比较三种潜在先验分布（512维高斯、48维高斯、ORCA PT-2光子量子处理器采样的48维分布）对异常型恶意软件检测的影响，并考察贪心与强化学习式表征精修的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常型检测器依赖良性数据的潜在表征，而潜变量先验通常默认取高斯分布；作者质疑先验分布的选择是否重要，以及量子硬件产生的先验是否与经典先验在行为上有差异。

Method: 以SAGEGAN（仅用良性数据训练的风格式检测器）为基线，训练三种潜分布版本：原始512维高斯、48维高斯、量子处理器采样的48维分布；随后用贪心方法和强化学习方法，在冻结表征上做精修；并在十个随机种子下重复实验。

Result: 量子先验模型表现出更丰富的内部结构，有效秩更高、潜在相似性更好，并且从精修中获益最多；但其最终检测准确率与精修后的高斯潜变量模型相近。

Conclusion: 先验分布的选择会显著影响潜空间几何结构与精修行为，即便最终检测精度相近；量子硬件先验在潜表征质量上展现出可测量的差异，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Anomaly-based malware detectors learn what benign files look like and flag deviations. Generative approaches to this problem can make use of a "latent" or "prior" data representation, which is often chosen to be a Gaussian distribution. We ask whether the choice of prior distribution matters, and whether one produced by quantum hardware behaves differently from a classical one. Building on SAGEGAN, a style-based detector trained only on benign data, we compare three latent distributions: the original 512-dimensional Gaussian, a 48-dimensional Gaussian, and a 48-dimensional distribution sampled from an ORCA Computing PT-2 photonic quantum processor. We then refine each trained model's frozen representation using greedy and reinforcement-learning methods. Across ten random seeds, the quantum-derived model shows richer internal structure with better effective rank and latent similarity. It also benefits most from refinement. Though final detection accuracy is similar to that of the refined Gaussian latent, these findings show differences in latent geometry and refinement behavior between the trained configurations.

</details>


### [167] [A Hardware-Trained Generative Quantum Eigensolver for Sample-Based Quantum Diagonalization of Organotin Hydroxides up to 70 Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13755)
*Kunal Kumar,Kimberlee Keithley,Toshiya Iwamori,Shu Kanno,Ikko Hamamura,Takao Kobayashi,Akira Koseki,Qi Gao,Naoki Yamamoto,Hiroshi Horii,Yuri Kobayashi,Borja Peropadre,Kouhei Nakaji,Toru Imai,Takayuki Osogami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出用70比特量子硬件在训练回路中训练生成量子本征求解器（GQE）策略，为基于采样的量子对角化（SQD）生成态制备电路，在46–70比特任务上以远少于LUCJ电路的双比特门数达到与DMRG参考相差15 mHa以内的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确经典模拟难以为大规模电路生成器提供反馈训练信号，因此需要直接利用量子硬件的采样作为训练回路内的反馈，以突破经典模拟可达规模，并降低高价量化方法对量子资源的依赖。

Method: 训练以分子描述符为条件的Transformer策略（GQE），输出SQD的态制备电路；先在无噪声模拟器上训练，再以IBM Heron处理器ibm_kobe在回路中训练，规模46与70比特、四组主训练共1.2×10^8次采样。CCSD(T)参考能量仅用于准入判据与两项辅助奖励项，并引入奖励惩罚防止策略塌缩到仅采样Hartree-Fock行列式的电路。将在过渡金属配合物上训练的策略零重训迁移到Sn(OH)3-Me及其水合物（32–70比特）。

Result: 一轮硬件采样后，仅做经典排序并用缓存计数与分子哈密顿量恢复能量、不再调用QPU：46–70比特的28次运行中27次与DMRG参考相差在15 mHa内（中位4.5 mHa）；8次未训练对照中训练策略均更准确；56–70比特下所选电路的双比特门数比同设备转译的LUCJ电路少34–72倍。

Conclusion: 结果证明电路生成器可在70比特量子硬件上直接训练，且训练所得策略具备跨体系迁移能力与显著的门数/资源效率优势，为量子硬件在回路中的生成式变分方法提供了可行范式。
