Abstract: Quantum hardware can provide feedback for training circuit generators beyond the reach of exact classical simulation. We train generative quantum eigensolver (GQE) policies, transformers conditioned on molecular descriptors, to generate the state-preparation circuits for sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD). Training runs first on a noiseless simulator and then with the IBM Heron processor ibm_kobe inside the loop, at 46 and 70 qubits ($1.2 \times 10^8$ shots for the four main training runs). A classical CCSD(T) reference energy of the training molecules enters only an admission gate and two auxiliary reward terms. During hardware training, a reward penalty prevents collapse to circuits that sample only the Hartree-Fock determinant. Policies trained on transition-metal complexes are applied without retraining to Sn(OH)$_3$-Me and its hydrates, minimal models of organotin photoresists for extreme-ultraviolet lithography, from 32 to 70 qubits. After one round of hardware sampling, followed by classical ranking and recovery of the cached counts with the molecular Hamiltonian and no further QPU calls, 27 of 28 runs at 46-70 qubits are within 15 mHa of the density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) reference (median 4.5 mHa), and in all eight runs with an untrained control the trained policy is more accurate. At 56-70 qubits the selected circuits use 34-72 times fewer two-qubit gates than the local unitary cluster Jastrow (LUCJ) circuit transpiled for the same device. These results demonstrate that circuit generators can be trained with quantum hardware at 70 qubits.

</details>


### [168] [Polynomial Quantum Recurrent Channels for Matrix Inverse Actions: Deferred-Reset Dynamics and Chebyshev Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13778)
*Vahid Tavakkoli,Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文构建完全正、保迹的多项式递推信道用于量子解态制备，并推导不动点与时变跟踪界；实验显示递推方法仅在部分配置下提升精度与降低成本，无普遍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索以多项式（Chebyshev）递推信道替代"每次重新制备"的量子解态制备方案，辨析"驻留递推"与"新鲜递推"在精度与成本上的相对优劣，并检验其在逻辑噪声下的可行性边界。

Method: 构造完全正、保迹（CPTP）的多项式递推信道；理论推导不动点界与时变跟踪界；在2,880组合成配置上比较驻留Chebyshev递推与基线的精度通过快照数；另设360个场景的成本研究，对比驻留与新鲜递推的成本及可行性；基于精确约化密度演化仿真与设定成本模型评估。

Result: 精度方面：驻留递推在608例中通过快照数更多，2,271例持平，仅1例落后。成本方面：在184个联合可行场景中新鲜递推均更便宜（驻留/新鲜成本比中位数3.12），而驻留递推在74例中唯一满足精度要求。在逻辑噪声率1e-4下，两种递推方法均不达标。

Conclusion: 结果表明递推信道具有条件性跟踪优势，但不存在普遍端到端加速或总成本收益；结论依赖精确约化密度仿真与假设的成本模型。

Abstract: We develop completely positive, trace-preserving polynomial recurrent channels for quantum solution-state preparation and derive fixed-point and time-varying tracking bounds. Across 2,880 synthetic configurations, resident Chebyshev recurrence improves accuracy-passing snapshot counts in 608 cases, ties in 2,271, and loses in one. A separate 360-scenario cost study shows that fresh recurrence is cheaper in all 184 jointly feasible scenarios (median resident-to-fresh cost ratio: 3.12), while resident recurrence uniquely satisfies accuracy requirements in 74 cases. At a logical noise rate of $10^-4$, neither recurrent method qualifies. Based on exact reduced-density simulations and assumed cost models, our results demonstrate conditional tracking advantages but no general end-to-end speedup or total-cost benefit.

</details>


### [169] [Eigenmode and field imaging of a compact superconducting resonator array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13791)
*Benedek Gaál,Vincent Jouanny,Simone Frasca,Christophe Galland,Pasquale Scarlino,Valentin Goblot*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将激光扫描显微（LSM）与NV色心磁强计联用，对33个集总元件谐振器构成的蜂窝晶格超导谐振腔阵列进行空间分辨表征，成像拓扑边缘态并测量其亚晶格极化，给出约0.3%的格点频率无序上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导谐振器阵列可定制量子信息处理的光子环境，但传统基于端口的微波谱无法分辨其模式丰富的空间结构，缺乏对拓扑边缘态等的空间表征手段。

Method: 对二维蜂窝晶格超导谐振器阵列（33个集总元件谐振器）同时施加激光扫描显微成像本征模式，与NV色心磁强计绘制局域微波磁场幅度分布，二者互补；利用所得空间信息进行实验验证与参数提取。

Result: 实验确认所制备晶格与设计一致；成功成像拓扑边缘态并测得亚晶格极化；给出约0.3%的相对格点频率无序上限。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了一套面向紧凑超导谐振器阵列的联合空间表征方法，可服务于混合自旋—超导量子系统等应用。

Abstract: Superconducting resonator arrays can tailor the photonic environment for quantum information processing, but conventional port-based microwave spectroscopy alone does not resolve the rich spatial structure of their modes. Here, we apply laser scanning microscopy (LSM) and nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center magnetometry to a two-dimensional superconducting resonator array consisting of 33 lumped-element resonators arranged in a honeycomb lattice hosting topological edge states. We demonstrate the complementarity of the two techniques: LSM images the eigenmodes, while NV magnetometry maps the local microwave magnetic field amplitude. Using this spatial information, we experimentally validate that the realized lattice matches the design, we image the topological edge state and measure its sublattice polarization, and place an upper bound of $\approx0.3\%$ on the relative on-site frequency disorder in the lattice. Our work establishes a combined spatial characterization approach for compact superconducting resonator arrays with applications in hybrid spin--superconducting quantum systems.

</details>


### [170] [Analog quantum approximate optimization algorithm for solving the weighted MaxCut problem on three-dimensional single-atom Rydberg array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13796)
*Junxi Chen,I. I. Beterov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论研究基于三维囚禁里德堡原子阵列的QAOA模拟实现，用于求解带权MaxCut问题，通过将图权重编码到原子间相互作用中，并结合离散量子退火与图神经网络热启动两种参数优化策略，在无噪声情形下几乎全部实例优于Goemans-Williamson舍入算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带权MaxCut是组合优化中的经典难题，而中性原子平台具有可编程的几何构型与长程相互作用，适合模拟式实现QAOA。作者希望探索如何将问题权重映射到原子排布，并寻找高效的参数优化策略以提升解质量。

Method: 采用由全局激光脉冲驱动、在里德堡共振附近施加位点相关失谐的三维独立囚禁里德堡原子阵列。以20个随机加权的3-正则图（各12个顶点）为基准，通过选择合适的三维原子位置将权重直接编码为原子间相互作用能。比较离散量子退火与基于GNN的热启动初始化两种参数优化策略，并分析采样统计与总脉冲时间成本，最后在Aquila中性原子处理器的现实误差模型下用量子轨迹模拟优化后调度方案的表现。

Result: 组合优化策略在无噪声情况下几乎在所有实例上优于GW舍入算法；同时给出了两种方法在采样统计与脉冲时间成本方面的对比，并在现实误差模型下评估了优化调度方案的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明三维里德堡原子阵列的模拟式QAOA结合混合参数优化策略是求解带权MaxCut的有效途径，并为在现有中性原子硬件上评估其实际可行性提供了参考。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate an analog implementation of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) for the weighted maximum cut problem (MaxCut) problem. The protocol uses a three-dimensional array of individually trapped Rydberg atoms driven by globally addressed laser pulses, with site-dependent detunings applied near the Rydberg resonance. As benchmarks, we consider twenty randomly weighted 3-regular graphs with 12 vertices each. Problem weights are encoded directly in the interatomic interaction energies by choosing appropriate three-dimensional atom positions. We compare two parameter-optimization strategies: discretized quantum annealing and warm-start initialization using a graph neural network (GNN). The combined optimization strategy creates a portfolio that outperforms the Goemans-Williamson (GW) rounding almost in all instances in the noiseless case. We analyze the sampling statistics and the total pulse-time cost of both approaches. Finally, we emulate the performance of the optimized schedules on quantum trajectories using a realistic error model of the Aquila neutral-atom quantum processor.

</details>


### [171] [Coherent Optical Readout of Neutral-Atom Qubits via Homodyne Detection of Resonant Fluorescence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13799)
*Minho Jang,Jaeyong Hwang,Minhyeok Kim,Sanghyo Park,Donggyu Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出用光电二极管平衡零差探测实现中性原子量子比特的相干共振荧光读取，解析模型与三维蒙特卡洛模拟显示100 μs内保真度超99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复采样、mid-circuit测量和容错中性原子量子处理都需要快速、非破坏性的量子比特读取，但基于相机的荧光探测存在超出荧光收集时间的电子学延迟。

Method: 用光电二极管进行平衡零差探测，对态依赖的共振荧光做相干读取；建立解析模型量化原子运动对时间积分正交信号与噪声的影响（简谐运动导致相位平均、光子反冲引起依赖轨迹的信号涨落），并以三维蒙特卡洛模拟验证。

Result: 解析模型表明信号累积与运动退相干之间的竞争决定最优探测时间；三维蒙特卡洛模拟验证模型，并在实验可行参数下预测100 μs内态探测保真度超过99%。

Conclusion: 给出了中性原子量子比特快速相干荧光读取的读取条件与物理约束。

Abstract: Fast, non-destructive qubit readout is essential for repeated sampling, mid-circuit measurement, and fault-tolerant neutral-atom quantum processing, but camera-based fluorescence detection introduces electronic latency beyond fluorescence-collection time. We propose coherent readout of the state-dependent resonant fluorescence using balanced homodyne detection with photodiodes. An analytic model quantifies the effect of atomic motion on the time-integrated quadrature signal and noise, showing that harmonic motion causes phase averaging while photon recoils produce trajectory-dependent signal fluctuations; the competition between signal accumulation and motion-induced decoherence sets an optimal detection time. Three-dimensional Monte Carlo simulations validate the model and predict state-detection fidelities exceeding 99% within 100 $μ\mathrm{s}$ for experimentally realistic parameters. These results establish the readout conditions and physical constraints for rapid coherent fluorescence readout of neutral-atom qubits.

</details>


### [172] [Adaptive Policy-Guided Error Mitigation (APGEM): A Unified Learning-Based Framework for Noise Resilience in Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13815)
*Bisma Majid,Shabir Ahmad Sofi,Mir Mohammad Yousuf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出广义自适应策略引导误差缓解(APGEM)，用单个上下文赌博机控制器依据变分算法自身反馈在线选择误差缓解技术，在80种算法-噪声条件下达oracle的95.5%，显著优于六种固定策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实践中误差缓解方法通常被一次性手工选定并在整个实验周期内固定使用，而QML训练过程中电路与噪声特性持续漂移，单一固定策略在不同阶段常失效，因此需要按步骤自适应选择缓解技术。

Method: 将缓解技术选择建模为在线决策问题；单一上下文赌博机控制器观测变分算法本就产生的信号（保真度、熵、奖励/损失、梯度行为等），在ZNE、PEC、测量误差缓解、动态解耦与电路优化中逐步选择；因仅依赖共享反馈接口，框架可通用监督、无监督、强化及混合量子-经典学习。

Result: 在五种范式、80种算法-噪声条件下，自适应选择达oracle的95.5%，显著优于全部六种固定策略（Friedman χ²=92.18, p≈1.1e-17；成对Wilcoxon p<4e-6）；学到的策略具噪声感知性，偏好技术随去极化与读出误差升高而转移；在八台真实IBM设备的噪声模型上达oracle效用的99.9%，三到八量子比特下开销约1.3e4 shots且基本恒定。

Conclusion: 将误差缓解选择视为基于算法反馈的在线决策，可跨范式通用地逼近最优策略，且开销可控，为噪声硬件上的变分量子算法提供了实用、可迁移的缓解框架。

Abstract: Error mitigation stands between variational quantum algorithms and useful results on today's noisy hardware, yet in practice a mitigation method is usually chosen once, by hand, and applied unchanged for the life of an experiment. This is a poor fit for quantum machine learning (QML), where the circuit and the noise drift as training proceeds, so the best strategy in one regime is often wrong in another. We present a generalized form of Adaptive Policy-Guided Error Mitigation (APGEM), which treats the choice of mitigation technique as an online decision made from an algorithm's own feedback. A single contextual-bandit controller observes signals any variational algorithm already produces, such as fidelity, entropy, reward or loss, and gradient behavior, and selects among Zero-Noise Extrapolation, Probabilistic Error Cancellation, Measurement Error Mitigation, Dynamical Decoupling, and circuit optimization per step. Because it depends only on this shared feedback interface, the framework applies unchanged across supervised, unsupervised, reinforcement, and hybrid quantum-classical learning. Evaluated on five paradigms across 80 algorithm-and-noise conditions, adaptive selection reaches 95.5% of an oracle that always picks the best technique and significantly outperforms all six fixed strategies (Friedman $χ^{2}=92.18$, $p\approx1.1\times10^{-17}$; pairwise Wilcoxon $p<4\times10^{-6}$). The learned policy is noise-aware: its favored technique shifts as depolarizing and readout error rise. On noise models from eight real IBM devices, APGEM tracks the oracle almost exactly (99.9% of oracle utility) at a flat cost of roughly $1.3\times10^{4}$ shots from three to eight qubits.

</details>


### [173] [Correlations carried and controlled by coupled non-Markovian environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13900)
*Stuart Flannigan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出局域纯态层级方法，用于模拟与空间关联非马尔可夫环境耦合的量子系统，实现环境记忆与空间关联的统一处理，并展示环境输运与局域反馈对远距自旋关联的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境既保留量子信息又能在空间分离的系统间传递信息，描述其联合作用需同时考虑环境记忆（非马尔可夫性）与空间关联，现有方法难以二者兼顾且缺乏数值精确的随机纯态框架。

Method: 构建空间局域的自适应纯态层级（local hierarchy of pure states），处理模式间可交换激发的空间关联非马尔可夫环境；应用于局域耦合的Rabi自旋—腔链（腔QED情境），其中被消去腔环境的光子跳跃诱导自旋间关联与纠缠；对中间腔进行监测并将测量记录反馈至对应自旋；另用张量网络模拟XXZ与Heisenberg链。

Result: 光子跳跃在被隔离的自旋间产生关联与纠缠；对中间腔监测并反馈可增强未直接受反馈的端点自旋间关联；反馈启动时的延迟响应及停止后纠缠的持续增长，支持相干环境动力学将先前反馈效应传递至端点自旋的机制；张量网络模拟显示环境输运改变多体关联模式。

Conclusion: 耦合环境与局域反馈可调控远距自旋间关联；所建立的数值精确随机纯态框架为研究相互作用非马尔可夫多体系统的相干与耗散控制开辟了途径。

Abstract: An environment can retain quantum information and carry it between spatially separated systems. Describing the combined influence of these processes requires a treatment of both environmental memory and spatial correlations. Here, we formulate a local hierarchy of pure states for quantum systems coupled to spatially correlated, non-Markovian environments whose modes themselves exchange excitations. We use this method to describe the reduced spin dynamics of a chain of locally coupled Rabi spin--cavity pairs motivated by cavity-QED implementations, where photon hopping in the eliminated cavity environment generates correlations and entanglement between otherwise isolated spins. Monitoring intermediate cavities and feeding their measurement records back onto the corresponding spins further enhances correlations between endpoint spins that receive no direct feedback. The delayed response when feedback starts, and continued entanglement growth after it stops, support a mechanism in which coherent environmental dynamics carry the effects of earlier feedback to the endpoint spins. Finally, tensor-network simulations of XXZ and Heisenberg chains show how environmental transport changes many-body correlation patterns. These results show how coupled environments and local feedback can control correlations between distant spins. We provide a numerically exact stochastic pure-state framework for simulating these processes, opening up avenues to explore the coherent and dissipative control of interacting non-Markovian many-body systems.

</details>


### [174] [Reservoir-induced Quantum Phase Transitions in spin ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13934)
*Giuseppe Musolino,Daniele Lamberto,Gabriele Orlando,Omar Di Stefano,Salvatore Savasta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究热力学极限下N个二能级原子与玻色库耦合的开放量子系统，发现在无抵消项（缺少亚稳极小）情形下，系统可经历由库结构而非相干内部动力学决定的量子相变，并用精确量子朗之万方程给出临界点定位及反射/透射谱的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨开放量子系统中量子相变的驱动机制：究竟源自系统内部相干动力学，还是可由库（环境）结构所设定；并关注临界附近过阻尼区（损耗率可超过软化模式共振频率）中常规近似失效的问题。

Method: 考虑热力学极限下N个二能级原子集合与玻色库耦合；在无抵消项、系统-库相互作用不存在亚稳极小的条件下，采用在临界附近过阻尼区仍严格成立的精确量子朗之万方程，通过复激发谱定位临界点，并推导闭式的反射谱与透射谱。

Result: 确定了由库结构决定的临界点；所得反射与透射谱呈闭式形式，在接近相变时表现出模式软化以及逐渐增强的非对称线型。

Conclusion: 在缺乏亚稳极小的无抵消项情形下，开放原子系综的量子相变可由库结构单独驱动；精确量子朗之万方程框架可有效处理过阻尼临界区，复激发谱与谱线非对称性可作为相变的可观测判据。

Abstract: In this work, we present a comprehensive analysis of an open quantum system consisting of a collection of N two-level atoms in the thermodynamic limit coupled to bosonic reservoirs. Our results demonstrate that, when the system-bath interactions lacks a metastable minimum, as in the counterterm-free case, the system can undergo a quantum phase transition set by the reservoir structure rather than by coherent internal dynamics. Using exact quantum Langevin equations, valid even in the overdamped regime near criticality, where the loss rates can exceed the resonance frequency of the softening mode, we locate the critical point via the complex excitation spectrum and obtain closed-form reflection and transmission spectra that display the mode softening and an increasingly asymmetric lineshape near the transition.

</details>


### [175] [Multiparameter quantum estimation of circulant networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13948)
*Maria Popovic,Silvia Cassina,Ilaria Gianani,Claudia Benedetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究连续时间量子行走在循环网络中多参数估计耦合强度，给出量子Fisher信息矩阵、最优探测态与量子极限，并对比位置测量与粗粒化测量的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络是描述相互作用、传输与通信架构的自然框架，准确刻画其耦合结构是量子技术的关键挑战，需系统刻画量子行走网络推断中耦合估计的基本与实用精度极限。

Method: 以连续时间量子行走探测循环网络，推导耦合强度的量子Fisher信息矩阵，确定最优探测态及量子测量可达极限（含随未知耦合数目的标度）；再计算位置测量的经典Cramér-Rao界，比较最优态与局域态；最后用粗粒化位置测量建模有限分辨率探测。

Result: 得到量子Fisher信息矩阵与最优探测态，明确量子极限及其随耦合数目的标度；位置测量的经典界在最优态与局域态下与量子极限有差异；粗粒化通常降低精度，但某些情形下低分辨率测量反而优于更细空间分辨率的测量。

Conclusion: 系统刻画了基于量子行走的网络推断中耦合估计的基本与实践极限，并表明有限分辨率探测并非总是劣势，为网络耦合结构的精度优化测量设计提供依据。

Abstract: Networks provide a natural description of interaction patterns, transport media, and communication architectures. Accurately characterizing their coupling structure is a key challenge in quantum technologies. We investigate the multiparameter quantum estimation of coupling strengths in circulant networks probed by continuous-time quantum walks. We derive the quantum Fisher information matrix, identify the optimal probe states and determine the ultimate precision allowed by quantum measurements, together with its scaling with the number of unknown couplings. We further analyze the precision achievable through position measurements of the walker, comparing the corresponding classical Cramér-Rao bounds for optimal and localized probe states with the quantum limit. Finally, we consider finite-resolution detection modeled by coarse-grained position measurements. Although coarse graining generally reduces estimation precision, we find that lower-resolution measurements can, in some instances, outperform other measurements with finer spatial resolution. Our results provide a systematic characterization of the fundamental and practical limits of coupling estimation in quantum-walk-based network inference.

</details>


### [176] [Quantum propagation of vibrational excitation in harmonically confined trapped-ion crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13967)
*Takumi Yuri,Yu-Xuan Chen,Kenji Toyoda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在一维10离子阱量子晶体中实验研究局域声子激发的传播，发现离子间距不均匀导致相邻跳跃与整体漂移等特征行为，实验结果与数值模拟细致吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阱中离子的振动激发（声子）是量子模拟与量子计算的有用资源，局域声子可用于模拟在系统中传播的量子粒子，因此需要实验上刻画其在离子晶体中如何传播。

Method: 将10个离子约束于谐波势阱中形成一维离子晶体，在其中制备局域振动激发并观测其传播；同时进行数值模拟，将实验数据与模拟结果逐项对比。

Result: 观测到离子间距的不均匀性引起相邻跳跃（adjacent hopping）和整体移动（global movement）等特征行为；实验与数值模拟在细节层面达到一致。

Conclusion: 该平台可用于扩展至玻色子多体系统以及更复杂的准粒子研究。

Abstract: Vibrational excitations, or phonons, in trapped ions serve as useful resources for quantum information processing, including quantum simulation and computing. Localized excitations of ions' vibrational motion (local phonons) can be used for simulating quantum particles that propagate over a system. Here, we experimentally investigate the propagation of a localized vibrational excitation in a one-dimensional trapped-ion crystal. A 10-ion crystal confined in a harmonic potential is used. It is found that inhomogeneity in the spacing between the ions leads to characteristic behaviors such as adjacent hopping and global movement. A comparison of the experimental results with numerical simulations shows agreement up to fine details. The system explored here may be extended to many-body systems of bosonic particles as well as more complex quasiparticles.

</details>


### [177] [Maximal Violation of Mermin Inequality for Commuting von Neumann Algebras and Multipartite Vacuum Entanglement in QFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13981)
*Kan He,Jinchuan Hou,Shuyuan Yang,Bingke Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将 Summers–Werner 关于两体 CHSH 不等式在相对论量子场论真空态中极大违背的判据（强稳定性）推广到三个及以上相互交换的 von Neumann 代数，聚焦多体 Mermin 不等式，证明三体情形的极大违背等价于存在公共的渐近 GHZ 稳定子序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Summers 与 Werner 已证明两体 CHSH 不等式在量子场论真空中的极大、普遍违背与代数的强稳定性等价，但该判据只针对"一个代数与其交换子"的两体结构，缺少对三个及以上相互交换的 von Neumann 代数的相应刻画，本文旨在填补这一多体情形下的理论空白。

Method: 将研究框架从 CHSH 泛函替换为多体 Mermin 泛函；对三体情形，证明"每个正规态均极大违背"当且仅当存在公共的渐近 GHZ 稳定子序列；分析局部因子强稳定性的必要性，并以嵌入无穷 GHZ 张量积作为充分条件；最后通过反例检验充分性边界，并将结论推广至 n≥3 体。

Result: 三体 Mermin 泛函在每个正规态上达到极大违背，等价于存在公共的渐近 GHZ 稳定子序列；每个局部因子的强稳定性是极大违背的必要条件，联合嵌入无穷 GHZ 张量积是充分条件；反例表明，即便各单独代数强稳定且为 III_1 型、并额外满足三体对偶性，仍不足以保证极大违背。

Conclusion: 两体情形中强稳定性即等价于极大违背，而在三体及以上情形该等价性失效：强稳定性仅为必要条件，需辅以公共渐近 GHZ 稳定子结构才能刻画 Mermin 不等式的极大、普遍违背；相应结论可推广到 n≥3 体相互交换 von Neumann 代数。

Abstract: Summers and Werner proved that Bell inequalities are violated maximally and generically in the vacuum of a relativistic quantum field theory in [S. J. Summers and R. Werner, Commun. Math. Phys. 110 (1987), 247-259]. Their criterion concerns two-party case, that is, there are two algebras of quantum observables: one algebra and its commutant. In this case, the maximal CHSH value is converged to in every normal state exactly when the algebra is strongly stable. In the paper, we study the corresponding question for three and more mutually commuting von Neumann algebras. We focus on the multipartite Mermin inequalities instead of CHSH ones. For the tripartite Mermin functional we show that maximal violation in every normal state is equivalent to the existence of a common sequence of asymptotic GHZ stabilizers. Strong stability of each local factor is an necessary condition for maximal violation, and a joint embedding of an infinite GHZ tensor product is sufficient. Counterexamples show that strong stability and type III_1 of the individual algebras, even together with tripartite duality, do not suffice to arrive at the maximal violation. The results are also extended to n>=3 parties.

</details>


### [178] [Pool-free, residual-triggered ansatz growth for variational imaginary-time evolution: when a fixed operator order beats adaptive screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13998)
*Yu-Hao Chen,Renata Wong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种无需算子池的变分虚时演化（VITE）方案：预先生成由哈密顿量嵌套对易子构成、按对称性与粒子数分组的固定层，当相对McLachlan残差超过阈值时追加下一层；在五个非可积系统上以1.6–5.4倍更少的线路数达到1e-4相对误差，显著优于6–8种自适应变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADAPT-VQE、AVQITE 等自适应变分算法在每一步增长时都需对算子池进行筛选，ADAPT-VQE 还要重新优化全部参数，筛选与再优化构成主要开销。作者希望探索一种免算子池的替代方案，以避免这种成本。

Method: 面向变分虚时演化（VITE）的免池方案：层结构提前固定，由哈密顿量的嵌套对易子生成并按对称性和粒子数分组；以相对 McLachlan 残差作为判据，超过阈值时追加下一层，而非每步从池中筛选。

Result: 在五个非可积体系（混合场 Ising 链与环面模型、拉伸 H4）上，动态 VITE 以1e-4相对误差为目标，所用线路数比六到八种自适应变体（单 Pauli 串池、量子比特激发池、批量 ADAPT-VQE 等）中最便宜者少1.6–5.4倍；在链上优势从8格保持到16格。分析表明自适应方法的成本主要来自筛选与再优化；纵向场扫描显示该优势依赖可积性破缺（此时自适应筛选代价高）。在横场 Ising 模型、更严目标（1e-6）以及调度前置弱层的情形下优势消失。

Conclusion: 免池的固定层+残差触发追加策略可在非可积、低精度目标区间有效替代自适应筛选，优势来源于规避筛选与再优化开销并依赖可积性破缺；其适用范围受模型类型、目标精度与层调度顺序限制。

Abstract: Adaptive variational algorithms such as ADAPT-VQE and AVQITE screen an operator pool at every growth step, and ADAPT-VQE also re-optimizes all parameters. We study a pool-free alternative for variational imaginary-time evolution (VITE). The layers are fixed in advance by nested commutators of the Hamiltonian, grouped by symmetry and particle number, and the next layer is appended when the relative McLachlan residual exceeds a threshold. On five non-integrable systems (the mixed-field Ising chain and tori, and stretched H$_4$), this dynamic VITE reaches a relative error of $10^{-4}$ with 1.6-5.4 times fewer circuits than the cheapest of six to eight adaptive variants, including single-Pauli-string and qubit-excitation pools and batched ADAPT-VQE. On the chain the advantage persists from 8 to 16 sites. Screening and re-optimization account for most of the adaptive methods' cost. On the spin models, a longitudinal-field scan shows that the advantage requires broken integrability, which makes adaptive screening expensive. The advantage is lost on transverse-field Ising models, at tight targets (relative error $10^{-6}$) and where the schedule front-loads weak layers.

</details>


### [179] [Normalizing Flows for Classical Simulation of Quantum Circuits in Phase Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14006)
*Edward Jiang,Timothy Heightman,Marcin Płodzień*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多体系统相空间表述的量子线路表示学习框架，将酉门参数化为概率分布间的归一化流，用于生成模型驱动的量子线路模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N量子比特态可表示为光滑2N维流形上的概率分布，这一相空间视角为用量子线路模拟引入生成模型提供了新思路，可望突破现有神经网络方法的规模瓶颈。

Method: 在相空间表述下，将N量子比特态映射为2N维光滑流形上的概率分布，并把酉门参数化为这些分布之间的归一化流（normalizing flow）。

Result: 在三类常见量子线路族上评测，大系统规模下精度优于当前最先进的神经网络方法。

Conclusion: 相空间+归一化流为量子线路模拟提供了有效且可扩展的新途径，在大规模系统上展现出优势。

Abstract: We present a novel framework to learn a representation of quantum circuits based on the phase space formulation of many-body systems. Given that $N$-qubit states can be represented as probability distributions on a smooth $2N$-dimensional manifold, we show how unitary gates can be parameterized as normalizing flows between these distributions, opening a new avenue for quantum circuit simulation using generative models. We evaluate our method on three common families of quantum circuits, and show that its accuracy exceeds that of current state-of-the-art neural network methods for large system sizes.

</details>


### [180] [CV-TF-QKD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14044)
*Stefano Pirandola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出连续变量版本的孪生场量子密钥分发（CV-TF-QKD），采用独立高斯调制相干态与非可信单光子中继，在固定弱输入过量噪声下密钥率随√η标度，并在指定器件参数下超越无中继器界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散变量孪生场协议已展示突破无中继器容量界限的"孪生场优势"，但连续变量（高斯调制相干态）体系尚未纳入该框架；同时需在集体攻击与一般相干攻击下给出严格安全性，并考虑有限码长与器件非理想性。

Method: 提出CV-TF-QKD：两臂使用独立高斯调制相干态，经非可信单光子中继（单光子干涉测量）产生关联；采用全记录协调（full-record reconciliation）与二值相位披露协议；对相位披露方案，通过制备强度界限检验与符号奇偶互补性（sign-parity complementarity）证明组合安全（composable security）以抵御一般相干攻击；并进行含探测器低效率、暗计数、相位抖动与参考系开销的有限码长计算。

Result: 全记录协调与二值相位披露在集体攻击下达到近乎相等的渐近密钥率；在每臂固定弱输入过量噪声时，两种方案的每中继密钥率均按√η标度（η为端到端透过率）；在指定器件参数下，有限码长密钥率超过纯损耗界限与匹配热损耗中继器无中继界限。

Conclusion: 将孪生场优势推广至高斯调制连续变量量子密码，表明CV-TF-QKD在实用参数与有限码长条件下可超越无中继器界限，拓展了TF-QKD的适用体系。

Abstract: We introduce a continuous-variable version of twin-field quantum key distribution, using independent Gaussian-modulated coherent states and an untrusted single-photon relay. Full-record reconciliation and a binary phase-disclosure protocol achieve nearly equal asymptotic key rates against collective attacks. At fixed weak input-referred excess noise per arm, both rates per relay use scale as $\sqrtη$, where $η$ is the end-to-end transmissivity. For phase disclosure, we establish composable security against general coherent attacks using a bounded test of preparation intensities and sign-parity complementarity. For specified apparatus parameters, finite-key calculations incorporating detector inefficiency, dark counts, phase fluctuations, and reference overhead predict rates exceeding the pure-loss and matched thermal-loss repeaterless bounds. These results extend the twin-field advantage to Gaussian-modulated continuous-variable quantum cryptography.

</details>


### [181] [Dynamical GKP codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14081)
*Jonathan Pelletier,Baptiste Royer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"动力学GKP码"，将连续变量晶格编码与时变平移测量动力学结合，并给出计算其校正子与距离的高效方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学（Floquet）码已成功推广稳定子形式，但此前仅针对量子比特系统；而GKP码作为将量子比特编码于振子连续相空间的候选方案，尚无对应的动力学形式，二者之间存在空白。

Method: 先用缩放辛晶格将GKP码推广为子系统GKP码；在此基础上移植动力学量子比特码中的祖先（ancestry）、探测器（detector）与良性错误（benign-error）机制；再借助"持久稳定子晶格"与"有界推断窗口"，构造通用且高效的动力学GKP码校正子与距离计算方法；并以双模方-六角Floquet GKP码为例加以演示。

Result: 该框架可高效计算给定测量序列的更新规则与码距；双模方-六角Floquet GKP码相比方型与六角GKP码测量权重更低，却兼具两者的逻辑门集合，更易实现完整的单比特Clifford群；具有无理辛重叠的测量在单模情形下退化为破坏性零差探测。

Conclusion: 本工作把重要的量子比特码工具推广到连续变量系统，并在离散稳定子测量与连续零差探测之间建立了新的桥梁。

Abstract: Dynamical codes protect quantum information with sequences of measurements, generalizing the stabilizer formalism of static codes. However, the dynamical formalism has been developed exclusively for qubit systems. While Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes are promising candidates for quantum computing that encode qubits in the continuous phase space of oscillators, no dynamical formalism has been developed for them. In this work, we bridge this gap and introduce dynamical GKP codes, combining continuous-variable lattice encodings with time-dependent translation measurement dynamics. To obtain the update rules and the distance of a given measurement sequence, we first generalize GKP codes to subsystem GKP codes using scaled symplectic lattices. Building on this structure, we adapt the ancestry, detector, and benign-error machinery originally developed for dynamical qubit codes. From this construction, we obtain a general and efficient method to compute the syndrome and distance of a dynamical GKP code from a persistent stabilizer lattice and a bounded inference window. We illustrate the framework with the example of the two-mode square-hexagonal Floquet GKP code. This code has reduced measurement weight compared to both the square and hexagonal GKP codes, while combining their set of logical gates, resulting in easier implementation of the full single-qubit Clifford group. Finally, we show that measurements with irrational symplectic overlap, which have no analog in discrete qubit codes, reduce to destructive homodyne detection in the single-mode case. These results extend an important qubit-code tool to continuous-variable systems and identify a new bridge between discrete stabilizer measurements and continuous homodyne detection.

</details>


### [182] [Cross-Platform Benchmarking of GHZ-State Generation in Quantum Processors via Cloud Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14114)
*Sergio Hernández,Unai Aseguinolaza,David Aguirre,Marta Gili,Joseba Hernández,Guillermo Valverde,Uxue Ballarin Manso,Uxue Auzmendi,Iker Pastor,Pablo García Bringas,Ane Blázquez García,Rubén Peña,Mikel Sanz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用 QuSquare 套件中的多体纠缠基准，对来自 5 家厂商的 9 台商用超导与离子阱量子处理器进行跨平台基准测试，以 GHZ 态保真度估计最大可达的真正多体纠缠（GME）规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件平台多样化，需要基准测试来识别各处理器优势、比较性能并为应用选择提供依据；且缺乏反映终端用户实际体验（默认编译、无误差缓解、云访问）的跨平台对比基线。

Method: 通过远程云服务、各厂商默认编译设置且不启用误差缓解，运行 QuSquare 的多体纠缠基准：用直接保真度估计（DFE）测量 GHZ 态保真度，并在统计误差范围内寻找满足 GME 阈值 F=1/2 的最大 GHZ 态规模 N*。

Result: 物理比特数与通过基准的最大 GHZ 规模之间存在显著差距：最佳超导处理器达到 N*=27 和 N*=24（不足其寄存器规模的四分之一），而 12 比特离子阱处理器以全寄存器通过。中位数校准数据对基准结果预测能力差；结论对统计判定规则高度敏感——若要求"认证 GME"，在所有测试规模上成功的设备从 9 台降至 4 台。

Conclusion: 物理比特数并非有效纠缠规模的良好指标，校准元数据不能替代端到端基准；判定规则需明确报告。该工作为未来跨平台量子处理器基准测试提供了基线。

Abstract: Quantum computing has rapidly evolved into a diverse ecosystem of hardware platforms that differ in their underlying technologies and operational characteristics. Consequently, benchmarking has become essential for identifying the strengths of quantum processors, comparing their performance, and enabling informed decisions when selecting the most appropriate quantum computing platform for specific applications. In this work, we benchmark nine commercially accessible quantum processors from five vendors, spanning superconducting and trapped-ion technologies, using the multipartite entanglement benchmark from the QuSquare suite. All experiments were performed through remote cloud-access services, with the default compilation of each provider and without error mitigation, reflecting the performance experienced by end users. The benchmark estimates the fidelity of Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) states by direct fidelity estimation and searches for the largest GHZ state whose fidelity is compatible, within the statistical error, with the genuine multipartite entanglement (GME) threshold $F=1/2$. Our results reveal a substantial gap between the total number of physical qubits available on a processor and the size of the largest GHZ state passing the benchmark: the best superconducting processors reach $N^*=27$ and $N^*=24$ qubits, less than a quarter of their registers, whereas the 12-qubit trapped-ion processor passes with its full register. We further show that median calibration data are poor predictors of the benchmark outcome, and that the reported sizes depend strongly on the statistical decision rule: requiring certified GME reduces the number of devices that succeed at any tested size from nine to four. These results provide a baseline for future cross-platform benchmarking of quantum processors.

</details>


### [183] [Coherent-Dissipative Crossover of Quantum Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14139)
*Li Liu,Niu Wang,Xun-Wei Xu,Jie-Qiao Liao,Hui Jing,Le-Man Kuang,Changliang Ren*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立了由快速中介耦合的两个量子振子的一般框架，通过绝热消融得到相干交换与集体耗散两条同步通道，指出同步由相干耦合、耗散与库诱导涨落的联合平衡决定，并证明对共享输出场的连续监测可将库信息转化为资源、在相干-耗散过渡区最大幅增强条件同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放系统中的量子同步既可由相干相互作用产生，也可由集体耗散产生，但二者相互作用的机制尚不清楚；作者希望给出统一描述并厘清两种通道的竞争与协作。

Method: 研究由快速中介耦合的两个量子振子，对中介模式作绝热消融，导出相干交换与集体耗散两种同步通道，得到连接耗散主导与相干主导区间的解析描述；并分析对共享输出场的连续监测（条件动力学）。

Result: 同步并非仅由相干耦合决定，而取决于相干耦合、耗散与库诱导涨落三者的联合平衡；连续监测共享输出可将库信息转化为资源，显著增强条件同步，且增强在相干-耗散交叉区最强。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了相干-耗散量子同步的一般机制，并展示了开放量子系统中"测量赋能"的同步调控途径。

Abstract: Quantum synchronization in open systems can arise from both coherent interactions and collective dissipation, yet their interplay remains poorly understood. Here we develop a general framework for two quantum oscillators coupled through a fast mediator. Adiabatic elimination reveals two distinct synchronization channels, coherent exchange and collective dissipation, and yields an analytical description connecting dissipation and coherence-dominated regimes. We show that synchronization is governed by the joint balance of coherent coupling, dissipation, and reservoir-induced fluctuations, rather than by coherent coupling alone. Continuous monitoring of the shared output further converts reservoir information into a resource, substantially enhancing conditional synchronization, with the strongest gain near the coherent-dissipative crossover. These results establish a general mechanism for coherent-dissipative quantum synchronization and its measurement-enabled control in open quantum systems.

</details>


### [184] [Multiparticle entanglement in a time-dependent electron--ion--photon system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14142)
*Axel Stenquist,Jan Marcus Dahlström*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文以冯·诺依曼熵与对数负性量化光电离中电子—离子—光子复合体系的纠缠动力学，揭示纠缠由电子—离子经真正多体纠缠态转移到电子—光子，并发现瞬态离子—光子纠缠及光子能量连续谱关联的高维纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光电离为实现时域纠缠转移提供自然场景：出射电子可与剩余离子纠缠，随后自发辐射可能把这些关联传递给光子；但该三元体系中纠缠如何在各方之间产生、传递与分配，尤其涉及多体纠缠、两两纠缠的瞬态行为及连续谱维度，尚缺乏系统的定量刻画。

Method: 对整个电子—离子—光子复合体系，采用一对其余（one-versus-rest）二分的冯·诺依曼熵描述整体纠缠动力学；再用对数负性（logarithmic negativity）解析两两纠缠，分辨不同子系统的配对关联；并借助其对弱占据施密特模的增强敏感性探测光子能量连续谱所对应的高维纠缠。

Result: 初始纠缠的电子—离子体系在演化中先经历真正的多粒子纠缠态，随后趋近纠缠的电子—光子态；对数负性进一步给出两两纠缠结构，特别是瞬态的离子—光子纠缠，同时揭示与光子能量连续谱相关的高维纠缠。

Conclusion: 光电离—自发辐射过程可作为时域纠缠转移与多体纠缠生成的可行机制；单一冯·诺依曼熵不足以刻画内部纠缠分配，需辅以对数负性等两两度量；连续谱自由度支持高维纠缠，为高维量子信息应用提供依据。

Abstract: Photoionization provides a natural setting for entanglement transfer in the time domain: an outgoing electron can become entangled with the residual ion, while subsequent spontaneous emission may transfer these correlations to an emitted photon. We quantify the entanglement dynamics of the composite electron--ion--photon system using the von Neumann entropy of the one-versus-rest bipartitions, showing that the initially entangled electron--ion system evolves through a genuinely multiparticle-entangled state before approaching an entangled electron--photon state. Logarithmic negativity further resolves the pairwise entanglement, revealing in particular transient ion--photon entanglement. Further, its enhanced sensitivity to weakly populated Schmidt modes additionally exposes high-dimensional entanglement associated with the photon-energy continuum.

</details>


### [185] [Secure Information Flow in Quantum Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14147)
*Zhicheng Zhang,Mingsheng Ying,Lirong Xia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文为量子程序建立信息流安全的语义基础与验证方法：提出基于非干扰和基于模拟两种安全定义并证明其等价，进而基于量子霍尔逻辑构建证明框架，用"自共轭"技术替代经典的自组合技术以克服不可克隆型障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息流安全旨在防止程序执行泄露机密信息，但量子程序的状态可能涉及叠加与纠缠、执行中经典与量子变量交互复杂，使得该问题本质上更为微妙；而经典的自组合验证技术在量子情形下因类似不可克隆定理的障碍而失效，缺乏可用的验证方法。

Method: 1）给出两种信息流安全定义：一是自然推广经典情形的非干扰定义，二是显式建模攻击者、具清晰操作语义的模拟式定义，并证明二者等价。2）基于量子霍尔逻辑建立证明框架；针对标准自组合技术失效的问题，提出"自共轭"（self-conjugation）新技术，借助延迟测量原理与酉操作性质实现安全性的霍尔式刻画。

Result: 证明了非干扰式与模拟式两种安全定义的等价性；所提出的基于量子霍尔逻辑与自共轭的证明框架可有效验证量子程序的信息流安全，并在量子一次性密码、量子纠错、匿名纠缠生成等实例上得到演示。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子程序信息流安全提供了统一的语义基础（两种定义等价）与可操作的验证逻辑方法，自共轭技术绕开了量子情形下自组合的不可克隆型障碍，适用于典型量子密码与纠错协议的安全性验证。

Abstract: Information-flow security aims to prevent the leakage of confidential information through program execution. This problem becomes fundamentally subtler for quantum programs, because their states may involve superposition and entanglement, and their executions may involve intricate interactions between classical and quantum variables. This paper develops semantic foundations and verification methods for secure information flow in quantum programs. We formulate two notions of information-flow security. The first is a noninterference-based definition that naturally generalizes its classical counterpart. The second is a simulation-based definition that explicitly models an attacker, providing a clear operational interpretation of security. We prove that these two notions are equivalent.
  To verify the information-flow security of quantum programs, we develop a proof framework based on quantum Hoare logic. A key challenge is that the standard self-composition technique, which expresses information-flow security in Hoare logic by composing a program with a renamed copy of itself, fails in the quantum setting due to an obstruction analogous to the no-cloning theorem. To overcome this challenge, we introduce self-conjugation, a novel technique that leverages the deferred measurement principle and properties of unitary operations. We demonstrate our framework on examples drawn from the quantum one-time pad, quantum error correction, and anonymous entanglement generation.

</details>


### [186] [Quantum Advantage in Assisted Work Distillation from Complementary Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14173)
*Chellasamy Jebarathinam,Debarshi Das,Ray-Kuang Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在热操作资源理论中建立微观热力学功提取与非经典关联刻画之间的操作性桥梁：把可信接收方的存储划分为独立量子比特池，证明互无偏基下的并发关联（"work gain"）可作为局部功提取的显式能量资源，并据此导出基于功的EPR steering不等式与半设备无关的work inequalities，用于见证superunsteerability、激活弱纠缠态的隐藏热力学效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子热力学中的功提取与非经典关联（纠缠、EPR steering）此前多被分开研究；本文旨在寻找一个统一的操作性框架，使关联本身可被量化为可提取的功，从而把电子/量子热力学增强效应转化为可实验检验的关联见证，并处理弱关联态在标准场景中不可见的问题。

Method: 采用热操作资源理论（thermal operations）与assisted work distillation的广义框架：将可信接收方系统分割为多个独立、分离的粒子存储池，在互无偏基集合上测量并发信息论关联，定义基于功增益的关联度量；以该paradigm导出基于功的EPR steering不等式，并在单边半设备无关信任框架下限制不可信方维度，构造动态缩放的work inequalities。

Result: （1）互无偏基下测量的并发关联可定量作为局部功提取的能量资源；（2）新一类基于功的EPR steering不等式，其违反可认证单边设备无关的量子热力学增强；（3）单边半设备无关框架下维度受限的动态work inequalities，可见证superunsteerability；（4）能够激活并认证弱关联纠缠态在标准无约束场景中完全不可见的热力学效用。

Conclusion: 研究表明非经典关联可作为热力学功的可操作资源，并给出由功不等式构成的关联见证层级（steering、半设备无关、superunsteerability），为弱关联态提供可检测的热力学价值判据。

Abstract: We establish an operational bridge between microscale thermodynamic work extraction and the foundational characterization of nonclassical correlations in bipartite quantum systems. Within the resource theory of thermal operations, we analyze a generalized framework of assisted work distillation where the trusted receiver's system is divided into separate, independent particle storage pools. We demonstrate that concurrent information-theoretic correlations measured across sets of mutually unbiased bases act as an explicit, quantifiable energy resource for local work extraction. Based on this paradigm, we derive a novel class of work-based Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen steering inequalities whose violations certify a one-sided device-independent quantum thermodynamic enhancement. Furthermore, by restricting the dimension of the untrusted party under a one-sided semi-device-independent trust framework, we introduce dynamically scaling work inequalities capable of witnessing superunsteerability. This allows us to activate and certify the hidden thermodynamic utility of weakly correlated entangled states that are otherwise completely invisible in standard, unconstrained scenarios.

</details>


### [187] [Quantum-Enhanced QSAR for DYRK1A: Evaluating Complementary and Utility QPU-Derived Molecular Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14179)
*Alejandro Giraldo,Daniel Ruiz,Mariano Caruso,Hamed Mohammadbagherpoor,Vladyslav Bohun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在DYRK1A激酶命中发现任务中，以同一RBF-SVM分别基于分子描述符表格表示与量子处理器实测向量进行评分，两种模型给出的待实验化合物集合不同，且各自剔除了对方纳入的已知活性化合物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室筛选能力有限，命中识别的选择直接决定哪些化学系列进入后续研究；未被检测的命中不会进入确证与类似物系列。作者因此关注两种表示下的模型是否提出同一批待测化合物，以及这种预测分歧带来的实际代价。

Method: 使用相同的RBF-SVM，对同一批测试分子分别在两种表示上打分：分子描述符的表格表示，以及在量子处理器上测量得到的向量；随后对比两模型提出的待实验化合物集合，报告入选化合物、其标签，以及已发表化学文献是否已有提及。

Result: 两模型提出的化合物不同：量子处理器向量模型纳入了表格表示遗漏的化合物（其中包含已有标签的活性化合物），表格表示也纳入了量子向量模型遗漏的活性化合物。这些化合物均落在两种表示共同覆盖的邻域内，差异仅在于哪个模型将其标记为活性。

Conclusion: 作者不主张形式化量子优势，也不解释两种向量分离行为不同的机制；仅指出预测分歧的代价——两个模型会把不同的化学系列送入实验评估。

Abstract: In drug discovery, hit identification selects a limited set of compounds for experimental evaluation from a larger collection of candidates. Because laboratory screening capacity is limited, that choice determines which chemical series continue into further investigation. A hit that is not assayed does not enter confirmation or the analog series, and that chemical series is not opened. For the DYRK1A kinase, the same test molecules were scored from a tabular representation of molecular descriptors and from a vector measured on a quantum processor, with the same RBF-SVM. The question is whether the two models propose the same set for experimental evaluation: they propose different compounds. The model on the vector measured on the quantum processor includes compounds that the tabular representation leaves out, and among them are already-labeled actives. The tabular representation includes actives that the vector measured on the quantum processor leaves out. What is reported is that set: who enters, with what label, and whether published chemistry already mentions it. No formal quantum advantage is claimed, nor a mechanism for why the two vectors separate differently. Those compounds fall within the neighborhood that both representations already cover; what changes is which model marks them active. From that follows the cost of predictive disagreement: each model would send a different chemical series to experimental evaluation.

</details>


### [188] [Quantum-Informed Graph Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14180)
*Matthew Brunet,Leah Prepchuk,Rebekah Herrman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子增强图分解算法QEGD，以QAOA为子程序通过局部顶点合并稀疏化图，在MaxCut上取得比GDOA及其他QAOA变体更高的近似比且时间成本低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAOA求解组合优化问题时精度与资源需求随问题规模或结构恶化，实用性受限；GDOA虽通过迭代缩减问题规模来缓解，但需解含指数级变量的整数规划，且因松弛项可能导致次优解。

Method: 提出启发式QEGD算法，其新颖性在于将QAOA用作子程序，通过局部顶点合并（local vertex-merging）对图进行稀疏化，再将稀疏化后的MaxCut问题交由QAOA求解。

Result: 在随机5-正则与6-正则图上，所有测试图的近似比均高于GDOA；在Erdős-Rényi图集合上，平均近似比高于QAOA²和ParaQAOA，在稀疏图上高于DC-QAOA、在较稠密图上与之相当，而平均墙钟时间仅为DC-QAOA的0.4%–5%。

Conclusion: QEGD通过以QAOA驱动的局部顶点合并实现图稀疏化，在保证较高近似比的同时显著降低求解时间，是相较GDOA与多种QAOA变体更具实用优势的方案。

Abstract: The Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) is a near-term quantum algorithm for solving combinatorial optimization problems, but its accuracy and resource requirements may scale poorly with problem size or structure, reducing its utility in practice. Approaches such as the Graph Decomposition for Optimization Algorithm (GDOA) seek to address this by iteratively reducing an input problem until it reaches a size where it can be solved using QAOA while maintaining the same optimal solution. This algorithm, however, requires solving integer programs that have exponentially many variables, and can result in suboptimal solutions, as the integer programs may contain slack terms. In this work, we propose a heuristic called the Quantum Enhanced Graph Decomposition (QEGD) algorithm. Its novelty lies in its use of QAOA as a subroutine for sparsifying a graph through local vertex-merging. The algorithm, when used to sparsify MaxCut problems that are then solved using QAOA, achieves higher approximation ratios across all tested graphs than GDOA for random 5-regular and 6-regular graphs. We then compare the time-to-solution and approximation ratio of QEGD to other QAOA variants a collection of Erdös-Rényi graphs. QEGD has higher approximation ratios on average than QAOA$^2$ and ParaQAOA on all tested graphs. It also results in higher average approximation ratios than DC-QAOA on sparse Erdös-Rényi graphs and comparable average approximation ratio on denser Erdös-Rényi graphs. However, its average wall-clock time is between 0.4\% and 5\% of the average DC-QAOA wall-clock time.

</details>


### [189] [Quantum-Assisted Counterfactual Regret Minimization for Bounded-Payoff Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14182)
*Gabriel Maresch,Erik Schulze,Jnan Yalla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子辅助的反事实遗憾最小化（QA-CFR）框架，利用量子振幅估计降低有界收益不完全信息博弈中遗憾更新的计算复杂度，同时保持与经典蒙特卡洛CFR相同的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分级奖励结构的不完全信息博弈中，均衡计算需在指数级大的策略空间上估计收益期望，计算挑战巨大。CFR虽能有效逼近纳什均衡，但其蒙特卡洛变体从采样博弈树轨迹估计反事实值时产生大量采样开销。作者希望借助量子振幅估计缓解这一瓶颈。

Method: 将量子振幅估计（QAE）嵌入CFR的反事实价值估计与遗憾更新过程，构建QA-CFR框架；对所得可利用性（exploitability）界进行理论分析；并讨论兼容NISQ的振幅估计变体及其资源需求结构。

Result: 给出了QA-CFR的可利用性理论上界，论证其保持经典对应方法的收敛性，并阐明实现复杂度降低所需的量子资源结构；同时指出仍需实证验证潜在优势的后续工作。

Conclusion: 量子振幅估计有望在保持收敛性的前提下降低CFR遗憾更新的采样复杂度，为不完全信息博弈均衡求解提供潜在加速路径，但其实际优势尚待NISQ硬件上的实验验证。

Abstract: Imperfect-information games with a graded-reward structure present significant computational challenges for equilibrium computation, since the payoff expectations must be estimated across exponentially large strategy spaces. Counterfactual Regret Minimization over the years has proven effective for approximating Nash equilibria, but Monte Carlo variants create a large overhead of sampling costs when the counterfactual values are estimated from sampled game-tree trajectories. We propose a novel Quantum-Assisted Counterfactual Regret Minimization, a framework that uses Quantum Amplitude Estimation to reduce the computational complexity of the regret updates in bounded-payoff imperfect-information games, while preserving the convergence of its classical counterpart. We give a theoretical analysis of the resulting exploitability bound, discuss NISQ-compatible amplitude-estimation variants, the structure of their resource requirements, and outline what is needed to validate the potential advantage empirically.

</details>


### [190] [Low-energy spectral-window truncation for compact U(1) lattice gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14188)
*Yi Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明了原始纯紧致U(1)格点哈密顿量在整个真空相对低能窗口上的非渐近电场通量截断界：在三维周期立方格点、θ=0、规范不变零全局通量扇区中，对窗口中任意归一化向量成立"阶乘平方"形式的尾部范数估计，且无需谱隔离、窗口边缘能隙或非简并假设，也不含窗口维度因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点规范理论的量子模拟与数值计算需要把无限维电场自由度截断为有限维，但以往截断误差多依赖谱隔离、能隙或窗口维度等强假设，缺乏对整个低能窗口一致且非渐近的严格保证，尤其缺少对有限时间演化和可观测读出误差的控制。

Method: 在三维周期立方格点（每方向N≥3个格点）、θ=0、规范不变零全局通量扇区中，对原始纯紧致U(1)哈密顿量的真空相对低能窗口内的任意归一化向量，推导电场基截断后的尾部范数界（阶乘平方衰减形式）；并将其应用于同一初态归一化投影的有限时间硬壁演化，辅以一个解析的有限体积强耦合例子进行验证。

Result: 得到对窗口中所有态一致成立的阶乘平方尾部范数估计，不含窗口维度因子，无需物理本征值隔离、窗口边缘能隙或非简并假设；可控制有限时间硬壁演化误差；强耦合解析例子认证了非真空能带及被指定有限截断所保持的序一量级元格信号变化；同一估计给出精度依赖的充分截断、基于极分解的线性等距编码（适用于参考纠缠输入）以及局域电场平方读出的误差界。

Conclusion: 在固定正裸耦合、物理体积、窗口宽度与演化时长下，取截断按β log N/log log N（β>2）随格点细化增长，即可使态截断误差与有界读出截断误差在格点细化极限下趋于零。

Abstract: We prove a nonasymptotic electric-flux truncation bound for the entire vacuum-relative low-energy window of the original pure compact $U(1)$ lattice Hamiltonian. On a three-dimensional periodic cubic lattice with $N\geq3$ sites per direction at $θ=0$, in the gauge-invariant zero-global-flux sector, the factorial-squared tail-norm estimate holds for every normalized vector in the window, without physical eigenvalue-isolation, window-edge gap, or nondegeneracy assumptions and without a window-dimension factor. It controls finite-time hard-wall evolution from the normalized projection of the same initial state. An analytic finite-volume strong-coupling example in this model certifies a nonvacuum band and an order-one plaquette signal change preserved by a specified finite cutoff. The same estimate yields precision-dependent sufficient cutoffs, an alternative linear isometric encoding by polar decomposition for reference-entangled inputs, and local electric-square readout bounds. At fixed positive bare coupling, physical volume, window width, and duration, cutoffs scaling as $β\log N/\log\log N$ with $β>2$ make state and bounded-readout truncation errors vanish under lattice refinement.

</details>


### [191] [Classical phase unwrapping enables large-time-step unitary quantum Krylov subspace diagonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14214)
*Tushar Kumar Jain,Rahul Maitra,Nicholas J. Mayhall*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出经典后处理方法 CURE-QKSD，在 UQKSD 框架下利用大时间步长改善 Krylov 基病态问题，并通过相位一致性景观从混叠能量中恢复真实本征值，实现小 Krylov 维度下的高精度分子能量计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UQKSD 无需深电路与 Pauli 采样即可计算分子能量，但小时间步 τ 下 Krylov 向量近乎线性相关导致重叠矩阵病态，增加 Krylov 向量不提升精度，且能量对截断阈值敏感；增大 τ 可改善条件数、降低所需维度 M，却使能量仅知模 2π/τ，即出现混叠。

Method: CURE-QKSD 为纯经典后处理：不改动 UQKSD 电路与广义本征值问题，利用一次（必要时两次）UQKSD 运行的相位一致性景观（phase-consistency landscapes）推断丢失的 2π/τ 整数倍，从而"解缠"混叠本征值。

Result: 在 12–20 量子比特的 6 个分子、28 个目标能量的无噪声测试中，全部在混叠区被成功恢复，最终误差 ≤1.1×10⁻⁷ Ha；在 σ=10⁻⁴ 高斯噪声下，大混叠时间步长、M=20–65 时基态或第一激发态中位误差达化学精度，而未校正的主值支能量偏差达 0.7–115 Ha。

Conclusion: CURE-QKSD 使混叠时间步长可用于 UQKSD 计算，从而支持更小、条件数更好的 Krylov 基，提升早中期容错硬件上分子能量计算的实用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: For computing molecular energies on early fault-tolerant hardware, unitary quantum Krylov subspace diagonalization (UQKSD) avoids both the deep circuits of quantum phase estimation and the Pauli-term sampling of variational methods by extracting the energies from a projected real-time propagator. At a small time step $τ$, nearly linearly dependent Krylov vectors make the overlap matrix ill-conditioned. Our tests at fixed total evolution time show that additional Krylov vectors then add no accuracy, while the thresholded energy becomes increasingly sensitive to the choice of cutoff. A larger time step improves the conditioning and reduces the Krylov dimension $M$ needed for a target accuracy, but the energies are then known only modulo $2π/τ$. We present Classical Unwrapping for Resolving Eigenvalues (CURE-QKSD), a classical post-processing method that leaves the UQKSD circuits and generalized eigenvalue problem unchanged and recovers the missing multiples of $2π/τ$ from phase-consistency landscapes of one or, when needed, two UQKSD runs. In noiseless tests on six molecules with 12--20 qubits, all 28 targeted energies are recovered in their aliased regimes with final errors of at most $1.1\times10^{-7}$~Ha. Under Gaussian noise with $σ=10^{-4}$, the median error of the ground or first excited state reaches chemical accuracy at large aliased time steps with $M=20$--$65$, whereas uncorrected principal-branch energies are off by $0.7$--$115$~Ha. CURE-QKSD therefore makes aliased time steps usable for UQKSD calculations with smaller, better-conditioned Krylov bases.

</details>


### [192] [Reading long-range information from a separable quantum record requires entangled measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14295)
*Qingtian Miao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 局域生成的、完全可分的探针记录中仍可储存长程信息，但只有具有纠缠效应的测量才能读出，从而在可分测量的读出成功率与联合读出之间给出严格分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究“记录不含纠缠”是否意味着“记录不含长程信息”，即区分记录的纠缠性与记录所携带信息可被何种测量读取，界定量子信息存储与读出资源之间的差别。

Method: 构造量子比特探针记录：每个探针通过局域相互作用编码一对近邻、初始无关联的Ising自旋的相对取向，用重叠量1-p的两个态表示一个键标签，所得记录完全可分。接收方获得一份拷贝，判断两个远距离自旋是否同向。分析两类读出：可分测量效应（含所有LOCC），以及效应在每个探针切割下部分转置仍保持正定的测量；并与联合读出对比。

Result: 在方格子上，当0<p≤p0=1-1/√2时，最佳可分测量读出的成功概率随自旋间距增大趋近1/2；对上述部分转置正定的测量族结论相同。当p*=0.29181≤p≤1时，对同一记录的联合读出可将成功概率保持在1/2以上，且超出量不随距离衰减。

Conclusion: 重叠区间[p*,p0]构成可分读出与联合读出之间的严格分离：局域产生且无纠缠的记录也能承载长程信息，但唯有含纠缠效应的测量才能提取，说明记录的纠缠性与可读信息量并非等价。

Abstract: Qubit probes record the relative orientation of neighboring, initially uncorrelated Ising spins through local interactions. Each probe encodes one bond label in two states with overlap $1-p$. The resulting probe record is fully separable. A receiver gets one copy and guesses whether two distant spins point in the same or opposite directions. On the square lattice, the best readout with separable measurement effects approaches success probability $1/2$ as the spin separation grows, for $0<p\le p_0=1-1/\sqrt2$. This includes all local operations with classical communication. The same conclusion holds for every measurement whose effects remain positive under partial transposition across every cut of the probes. For $p_*=0.29181\le p\le1$, a joint readout of the same record maintains a success probability above $1/2$ by a positive amount independent of distance. The overlap $[p_*,p_0]$ gives a strict separation between the readouts. A record that is created locally and contains no entanglement can therefore hold long-range information that only measurements with entangled effects can read.

</details>


### [193] [Electrical Stark Tuning and In Situ Field Sensing of $V_{2}$ Silicon Vacancies in a 4H-SiC a-Plane Lateral pin-Diode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14308)
*Jannik H. Schwarberg,Fedor D. Hrunski,Maximilian Hollendonner,Wolfgang Knolle,Mathias Rommel,Fabian J. Magerl,Jan F. Dick,Jörg Schulze*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在a面4H-SiC衬底上用CMOS工艺制备横向pin二极管，实现V2硅空位的电学控制与光学寻址，零偏下光学线宽<80 MHz，可用于低温局域电场原位测量与原位调节ZPL以实现发射体光谱不可区分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统c面上4H-SiC的谐振光激发受样品几何限制，实验方案不便；a面衬底允许通过晶圆表面实现更灵活的谐振光接入。同时量子网络需要不同发射体的光谱不可区分性，而器件集成后的色心质量与电学调控能力尚待验证。

Method: 在a面4H-SiC晶圆上用可扩展CMOS工艺制作横向pin二极管，将V2（立方格位）硅空位置于本征区；测量零偏下光学跃迁线宽；利用Stark效应在低温下原位测量局域电场，并与宏观TCAD模拟对比；通过反向偏压Stark效应对单个发射体的ZPL进行精确调谐。

Result: 集成色心经完整半导体工艺后仍保持良好光学质量，零偏线宽低于80 MHz；量子传感器测得局域电场，与TCAD对比揭示高度局域化的场集中现象；反向偏压可调谐单个发射体ZPL并补偿不同发射体间的光谱差异，在瞬态电荷涨落弛豫后光谱稳定性超过4小时。

Conclusion: a面4H-SiC结合CMOS集成是可靠的量子平台，兼具原位电场传感与光谱调谐能力，为未来干涉实验和量子光子集成电路奠定基础。

Abstract: Silicon carbide has emerged as a highly promising platform for scalable quantum technologies, with the silicon vacancy serving as a robust spin qubit and quantum sensor. However, conventional c-plane 4H-SiC restricts the experimental scheme and resonant optical excitation to the sample's geometry. The usage of a-plane substrates enables more versatile resonant optical access to the color center through the wafer surface. Here, we demonstrate the electrical control and optical addressability of $V_{2}$ (cubic lattice site) silicon vacancies within the intrinsic region of lateral pin-diodes fabricated on a-plane 4H-SiC wafers using a scalable complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor (CMOS) process. Despite extensive semiconductor processing, the integrated color centers exhibit good optical quality, evidenced by narrow optical transition linewidths below 80 MHz at zero bias. These embedded quantum sensors are utilized for in situ measurement of local electric fields at cryogenic temperatures via the Stark effect. Comparisons with macroscopic technology computer-aided design simulations reveal the value of these integrated quantum sensors to help understand cryogenic device behavior, for example in predicting highly localized field crowding. Furthermore, to address the spectral indistinguishability required for quantum networks, we precisely tune the zero-phonon line (ZPL) of individual emitters via the reverse-bias Stark effect. This successfully compensates for the spectral variations of distinct emitters. Following the relaxation of transient charge fluctuations, the Stark-tuned emitters exhibit spectral stability over measurement periods exceeding four hours. Our results establish a-plane 4H-SiC as a robust platform for future interference experiments and quantum photonic integrated circuits.

</details>


### [194] [Error Resilience and Unitary 2-Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14314)
*Zheng-Hang Sun,Fabian Ballar Trigueros,Xhek Turkeshi,Piotr Sierant*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将编码-出错-解码电路的恢复保真度与编码器集合对 Haar 二阶矩（unitary 2-design）的偏离联系起来，导出普适不等式，并证明非高斯门掺杂的 matchgate 电路只有在接近 unitary 2-design 时才具备稳定的误差保护相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有结果指出，从 unitary 2-design 集合抽取编码器时，编码-出错-解码电路在阈值错误强度以下存在误差保护相。但实际量子多体编码器（如 matchgate、哈密顿量演化）通常并不满足 Haar 二阶矩统计，这种偏离如何影响误差保护相尚不清楚。

Method: 以编码酉、错误层、逆酉解码与辅助比特后选择构成电路；用恢复保真度刻画性能，引入第二酉框架势（second unitary frame potential）量化编码器集合与 unitary 2-design 的接近程度，导出二者的普适不等式；并以掺杂可调数量非高斯门的 matchgate 电路为范例，结合热力学极限下的解析论证与哈密顿量编码器（含实验可实现的 all-to-all 自旋模型）进行验证。

Result: 导出的普适不等式表明恢复保真度受第二酉框架势约束；未掺杂 matchgate 电路在热力学极限下不存在稳定的误差保护相；随非高斯门掺杂增加，编码器集合趋近近似 unitary 2-design，误差保护机制得以恢复；哈密顿量编码器与 all-to-all 自旋模型呈现相同机制。

Conclusion: 恢复保真度可作为量子多体系统中偏离 unitary 2-design 行为的诊断量；稳定的误差保护相要求编码器集合在二阶矩意义上接近 Haar 分布。

Abstract: Encoding-error-decoding circuits provide a setting for studying protection of quantum information against errors. In these circuits, an encoding unitary is followed by an error layer, decoding by the inverse unitary, and ancilla postselection. For encoders drawn from unitary 2-design ensembles, these circuits exhibit an error-protecting phase below a threshold error strength in which the fidelity between the input and recovered logical states approaches unity. Here, we show how departures from Haar second-moment statistics of the encoder can destabilize the error-protecting phase and derive a universal inequality linking recovery fidelity to the second unitary frame potential, which quantifies the ensemble's proximity to a unitary 2-design. This connection is exemplified by matchgate circuits doped with a tunable number of non-Gaussian gates. We analytically show that undoped circuits lack a stable error-protecting phase in the thermodynamic limit and that increasing the doping restores the error protection mechanism as the encoder ensemble approaches an approximate unitary 2-design. Further examples of Hamiltonian encoders, including an experimentally motivated all-to-all spin model, follow the same mechanism. Our results identify recovery fidelity as a diagnostic of departures from unitary 2-design behavior in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [195] [Dynamical Casimir emission from Floquet-driven Landau polaritons: Gauge invariance forbids a superradiant phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14339)
*S. Hughes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文建立了周期驱动Landau极化激元的精确理论，证明磁场调制不能实现Floquet超辐射相变，取而代之的是动力学Casimir效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献预测仅调制磁场可增强周期平均耦合并诱导Floquet超辐射相变（SRPT），这与规范不变性对线性体系SRPT的禁令相矛盾，因此需要严格检验该预测。

Method: 构建周期驱动Landau极化激元的精确理论：计入调制磁通所感应的电场（法拉第定律），借助Kohn定理将问题严格约化为电子质心模与腔模的耦合，其哈密顿量在每一时刻均为平方和；用Krein定理证明在可计算的Krein频率以上系统全阶稳定，并给出自包含证明；并证明排除任意能带结构（含Kane非抛物性）下Floquet光子凝聚的规范定理，导出广义TRK求和规则。

Result: 高于Krein频率时系统对所有阶稳定，高频驱动下电子感受到平均场加有质动力势阱（Fock–Darwin问题）而非耦合增强；低于Krein频率时仅在共振舌内不稳定，对应真空产生粒子对（动力学Casimir效应）。原预测的相变源于在固定Landau能级基下对耦合取平均，所得有效模型违反TRK求和规则，且忽略了规范不变性所要求的法拉第感应场。磁调制实际产生动力学Casimir发射：低阈值下的极化激元–磁等离子体激元对，以及具有5–10 dB双模压缩的双色光子对。

Conclusion: 正确规则是对最小耦合哈密顿量本身进行调制与平均，而非对其投影参数取平均；该规则适用于任何线性最小耦合体系。磁调制不产生SRPT，而是产生动力学Casimir辐射。

Abstract: Ultrastrong light--matter coupling of linear systems, such as Landau polaritons formed by a two-dimensional electron gas in a terahertz cavity, is described by the bosonic Hopfield model, and gauge invariance forbids a superradiant phase transition (SRPT). Modulating the magnetic field alone has been predicted to enhance the cycle-averaged coupling and induce a Floquet SRPT. We develop an exact theory of periodically driven Landau polaritons. Including the electric field induced by the modulated flux (Faraday's law), the problem reduces exactly, by Kohn's theorem, to the electronic center-of-mass mode coupled to the cavity, whose Hamiltonian is a sum of squares at every instant. A theorem of Krein then guarantees stability to all orders above a computable drive frequency, the Krein frequency, for which we give a self-contained proof. Below it, the system is stable except within resonance tongues where the drive creates pairs from the vacuum, the dynamical Casimir effect. At high drive frequency the electrons experience the mean field plus a ponderomotive trap (the Fock--Darwin problem), not an enhanced coupling. A gauge theorem excludes a Floquet photon condensate for any band structure, including Kane nonparabolicity, for which we derive a generalized Thomas--Reiche--Kuhn (TRK) sum rule. The predicted transition arises from averaging the coupling in a fixed Landau-level basis, which yields an effective model that violates the TRK sum rule; under driving, gauge invariance also requires the induced Faraday field. The rule to modulate and average the minimal-coupling Hamiltonian, not the parameters of its projection, applies to any linear, minimally coupled system. Instead of an SRPT, magnetic modulation produces dynamical Casimir emission: Faraday-driven polariton--magnetoplasmon pairs at low threshold, and two-color photon pairs with up to 5--10~dB of two-mode squeezing.

</details>


### [196] [High-Speed Control of a Fluxonium Qubit via Picosecond Pulses from Josephson Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14358)
*M. V. Bastrakova,D. S. Pashin,V. A. Vozhakov,N. V. Klenov,I. I. Soloviev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于约瑟夫森数字电路产生的皮秒级单通量量子(SFQ)脉冲，为低频fluxonium量子比特开发控制策略：提出高频SFQ脉冲包(仅磁通通道)与相位偏移磁通+电荷双通道两种互补协议，多能级模拟显示单比特门保真度均超过99.99%，其中前者简单鲁棒、后者显著缩短门时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有为transmon设计的SFQ控制协议在低频fluxonium上效率低下：该区间内磁通耦合与电荷耦合的不对称性会导致要么泄漏过大、要么门时间过长，因此需要专门的控制方案。

Method: 以约瑟夫森数字电路产生的皮秒级单通量量子脉冲为驱动源，设计两种互补协议：(1)通过磁通通道施加高频SFQ脉冲包；(2)将相位偏移的磁通脉冲序列与电荷脉冲序列组合，以增强共振驱动、缩短门时长。采用多能级数值模拟评估保真度、鲁棒性与门时间。

Result: 多能级模拟给出单比特门保真度高于99.99%；较简单的磁通脉冲包协议在比特参数与磁通偏置中等波动下仍保持鲁棒，组合协议在保持高保真度的同时大幅加快门操作。

Conclusion: 基于SFQ的低温控制是fluxonium量子比特快速、精确且可扩展操纵的一条现实可行路线。

Abstract: We develop a control strategy for fluxonium qubits based on picosecond single-flux-quantum (SFQ) pulses generated by Josephson digital circuits. A major challenge is that SFQ protocols originally designed for transmon control become inefficient for low-frequency fluxonium. In this regime, the asymmetry of flux and charge couplings leads either to excessive leakage or excessively long gate times. We show that this limitation can be overcome by two complementary protocols. The first protocol employs high-frequency SFQ pulse packets applied through the flux channel, offering a simple, analytically transparent, and robust approach for high-fidelity single-qubit control. The second protocol combines phase-shifted flux and charge pulse sequences to enhance the resonant drive and reduce the gate duration. Multilevel simulations demonstrate single-qubit gate fidelities above $99.99\%$; the simpler protocol remains robust under moderate variations of qubit parameters and flux bias, while the combined protocol preserves high fidelity while substantially accelerating the gate. These results establish SFQ-based cryogenic control as a realistic route to fast, accurate, and scalable manipulation of fluxonium qubits.

</details>


### [197] [Invariance can prevent overfitting and shortcut learning in Quantum Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14368)
*Giuseppe D'Ambruoso,Giovanni Gramegna,Domenico Pomarico,Giuseppe Magnifico,Cosmo Lupo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在严格受控条件下，论文隔离出"不变性"本身对等变量子神经网络的价值：四个结构、参数量与动力学李代数完全相同的八比特模型中，具有D₄不变性的模型泛化更好、不受方向捷径影响，且在真实噪声模型下优势依然保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变量子神经网络按设计内嵌旋转/反射对称性，但通常对比的架构在门、结构或规模上也存在差异，因此"对称性本身带来多少收益"难以单独识别。作者希望在控制变量的实验设置中，直接考察不变性对泛化的作用。

Method: 选取标签在二面体群D₄下不变的图像分类任务；构建四个八比特模型，编码方式、线路结构、可训练参数数量完全一致，且具有等价的动力学李代数，仅"是否满足不变性"不同；在四个二分类任务（MNIST、星系与红细胞两个科学数据集）上从泛化误差、对捷径学习的鲁棒性、对真实噪声模型的抵抗力三方面进行基准测试，并通过类别相关的角落水印实验检验方向捷径。

Result: 仅用数十张训练图像时，不变模型的泛化优于非不变模型；面对方向捷径（类别相关角落水印），非不变模型会学到水印，一旦移动水印准确率常常跌至随机水平，而不变模型对此免疫；在真实噪声模型下，不变模型带来的准确率提升依然存在。

Conclusion: 在变量受控的对比中，不变性独立地贡献了泛化能力、抗捷径鲁棒性与噪声容忍度，说明按对称性设计等变量子线路具有可测量的实际价值，而非仅由架构或规模差异所致。

Abstract: Many image classification tasks have labels that are unchanged by rotations and reflections. Equivariant quantum neural networks incorporate such symmetries by design, but the specific benefit of symmetry is difficult to isolate. Equivariant quantum circuits are usually compared with architectures that also differ in gates, structure or size. Here, we investigate the role of invariance in a controlled setting, classifying images whose labels are invariant under the dihedral group $D_4$. We compare four eight-qubit models with identical encoding, circuit structure and number of trainable parameters, and equivalent dynamical Lie algebras, and benchmark invariant implementations against non-invariant ones in terms of generalization error, robustness to shortcut learning and resilience to a realistic noise model. On four binary tasks, including MNIST and two scientific data sets of galaxies and red blood cells, the invariant models trained on a few tens of images show better generalization than non-invariant ones. Invariance also makes the models immune to orientation shortcuts: when trained with a class-dependent corner watermark, the non-invariant models learn the watermark and often drop to chance level when it is moved. We show that the gain in accuracy of invariant models persists also under a realistic noise model.

</details>


### [198] [Operational limits of reference renewal for retained quantum-service results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14376)
*Roberto Fernández-Barrios,Iker Pastor-López,Amaia Pikatza-Huerga,Pablo García Bringas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用预先设定的协议实施"引用更新"（reference renewal），重新考察已保留的物理量子服务结果：12个转移各保留6项结果，10个完成更新、8个达到预设终点、5个仅凭保留结果即成功；精确单侧p=0.0546875不足以支持恢复假设。离线重建可从保留计数恢复判定，回顾性"绑定+oracle"校验在不做更新的情况下接受全部60个可用转移结果，故本研究未能证明引用更新带来数值正确性上的增量效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有量子服务结果的保留与"恢复"机制缺乏严格、可复现的验证框架；作者希望以预设定协议检验：在不重新执行实验的前提下，仅靠引用更新能否恢复并确认原有结果的有效性，并厘清其操作层面的边界。

Method: 采用预设定协议的引用更新流程，对12个转移×6项结果共60项可用结果进行再考察；以精确单侧检验评估恢复假设；记录未完成引用、绑定失败与顺序等待等过程性障碍；并进行离线重建（由保留计数复原判定）与回顾性"绑定+oracle"校验作为对照。

Result: 10/12转移完成更新，8/10达到预设终点，5项仅凭保留结果即成功；精确单侧p=0.0546875，未达显著。离线重建可完整复原判定；回顾性绑定+oracle校验接受全部60项可用转移结果而无需更新；由于oracle同时提供校准分数，更新未显示出数值正确性上的增量价值。

Conclusion: 在引入精确oracle与回顾性校验的条件下，引用更新并未带来可测的数值正确性提升，仅体现为操作流程成本；结果保留与恢复的可靠性更依赖离线重建与绑定校验，而非更新本身，故恢复假设未获支持。

Abstract: We implement reference renewal to reconsider retained physical quantum-service results under a prespecified protocol. Twelve transitions retained six results each; ten completed renewal, eight met the prespecified endpoint, and five succeeded using retained results alone. The exact one-sided p=0.0546875 does not establish the recovery hypothesis. Incomplete references, binding failures and sequential waits expose operational limits. Offline reconstruction recovers decisions from preserved counts. Retrospective binding-plus-oracle checking accepts all 60 usable-transition results without renewal. With the exact oracle also supplying calibration scores, the study does not demonstrate incremental numerical-correctness utility of renewal.

</details>


### [199] [Implementing rapid reset of superconducting qubits with on-chip dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14391)
*Feiyu Li,Yang Liu,Zhen Yang,Zhenxing Liu,Jiayu Ding,Yang Gao,Xuehui Liang,Weijie Sun,Yanjun Ma,Chuhong Yang,Guangming Xue,He Wang,Fei Yan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验演示了一种基于片上耗散单元（谐振腔耦合片上电阻负载）的可扩展多比特重置与泄漏消除方案，实现约48 ns的中位重置时间常数、30 mK有效浴温，并显著降低残余布居。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速高保真比特重置是量子态初始化与量子纠错的前提。传统超导比特重置依赖连接到外部电路的耗散元件，使比特暴露于更高温环境的热噪声，且难以扩展。

Method: 采用由谐振腔与片上电阻负载组成的专用耗散单元，对多个transmon比特进行重置与泄漏消除；该结构在提供Purcell保护的同时将比特与较热的外部电路隔离。

Result: |g⟩-|e⟩跃迁的中位重置时间常数约48 ns，有效浴温30 mK；400 ns重置脉冲下，初始化为|g⟩和|e⟩的残余激发态布居分别为0.29%与0.67%；|f⟩态残余布居降至0.51%。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了一种简单、可复现且易于集成的耗散工程方法，可推广应用于超导量子处理器。

Abstract: Rapid and high-fidelity qubit reset is essential for quantum state initialization and quantum error correction. Conventional reset protocols for superconducting qubits often rely on dissipative elements connected to external circuitry, exposing qubits to thermal noise from higher-temperature environments. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a scalable approach to qubit reset and leakage removal over multiple transmon qubits using dedicated dissipation units comprising resonators coupled to on-chip resistive loads. This architecture provides Purcell protection while isolating the qubits from warmer external circuitry. We achieve a median reset time constant of approximately 48 ns for the $|g\rangle-|e\rangle$ transition, with an effective bath temperature of 30 mK. A 400-ns reset pulse yields residual excited-state populations of $0.29\%$ and $0.67\%$ for qubits initialized in $|g\rangle$ and $|e\rangle$, respectively. We further demonstrate efficient leakage removal from the $|f\rangle$ state, reducing its residual population to $0.51\%$. These results establish a simple, reproducible, and easy-to-integrate approach to dissipation engineering in superconducting quantum processors.

</details>


### [200] [Simulating closed- and open-system dynamics of Tavis--Cummings lattices on fault-tolerant quantum computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14398)
*Soumya Sarkar,Rakshit Gharat,Nam Nguyen,Gavin Brennen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文分析Tavis–Cummings晶格在容错量子计算下的逻辑资源，提出基于ladder-operator块编码的光子跃迁与光-物质交换电路，并以闭/开系统模拟估计可观测量成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tavis–Cummings晶格含多发射体的腔体耦合，二维实时关联经典计算代价高，尤其当发射体弛豫或退相位速率不等破坏置换对称性时；需评估容错量子模拟的逻辑资源以作参考。

Method: 基于ladder-operator块编码构造光子跃迁与局部光-物质交换电路并组合编码晶格哈密顿量；跃迁电路就地更新源/目标光子占据数以消除双寄存器方案的辅助光子寄存器；闭系统用广义量子信号处理实现实时演化并界定延迟腔谱函数估计成本；开系统（光子损耗、发射体弛豫、退相位）用采样一阶膨胀界定估计g^(2)(0)等瞬态光子统计所需运行数与时间步。

Result: 在16至256腔、三组均匀系数算例中，相同共享制备、精度、控制与工作空间约定下，受控行走较配对双寄存器实现少用4.75%–8.11%的Toffoli门；并给出含态制备与测量成本的显式估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的块编码电路与资源估计为评估容错光-物质晶格模拟成本提供了参考框架。

Abstract: Tavis--Cummings lattices couple cavities containing several emitters and host polaritonic many-body dynamics whose real-time correlations are costly to compute classically in two dimensions, especially when unequal emitter relaxation or dephasing rates break permutation symmetry. We analyze logical resources for fault-tolerant closed- and open-system simulation of an atom-resolved lattice with $L$ cavities, $N$ emitters per cavity, and photon cutoff $n\_{\max}$, including target observable estimation. Building on ladder-operator block encoding, we construct circuits for photon hopping and local light--matter exchange and combine them to encode the lattice Hamiltonian. By updating source and destination photon occupations in place, the hopping circuit eliminates the auxiliary $L\lceil\log_2(n\_{\max}+1)\rceil$-qubit photon register used by two-register implementations. Across three uniform-coefficient examples with $16$ to $256$ cavities, the controlled walk uses $4.75$--$8.11%$ fewer Toffoli gates than the paired two-register implementation under matched shared preparation, precision, control, and workspace conventions. For closed systems, we implement real-time evolution with generalized quantum signal processing and bound the cost of estimating retarded cavity spectral functions. For open systems with photon loss, emitter relaxation, and dephasing, we use a sampled first-order dilation and bound the runs and timesteps needed to estimate transient photon statistics such as $g^{(2)}(0)$. With state-preparation and measurement costs explicit, these estimates provide a reference for assessing fault-tolerant light--matter lattice simulation costs.

</details>


### [201] [Multidimensional Quantum Block Turbo Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14406)
*Khaled Jebari,Luiz Anet Neto,Ramesh Pyndiah,Jean-Louis de Bougrenet de la Tocnaye*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将经典turbo迭代译码思想推广到量子编码，提出多维量子分组turbo码（基于多层乘积码的turbo译码技术），给出算法描述并在去极化信道上对3维情形进行性能仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作通过量子串行turbo码与量子分组turbo码把turbo迭代译码理论引入量子编码框架，但尚缺乏针对多层乘积码结构的多维推广理论，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出多维量子分组turbo码理论：以多层乘积码为基础构造码字，设计相应的turbo迭代译码算法，并在去极化信道（depolarizing channel）上对3维情形开展数值仿真。

Result: 给出了多维量子分组turbo码的译码算法，并通过3维情形的仿真获得其在去极化信道下的性能表现。

Conclusion: 多维量子分组turbo码是多层乘积码上的可行turbo译码方案，扩展了量子turbo码的理论体系，3维案例为其实用性能提供了初步验证。

Abstract: The theory of turbo iterative decoding was brought into the quantum coding framework through the development of quantum serial turbo codes and quantum block turbo codes. In this paper, we present a theory for multidimensional quantum block turbo codes, a turbo decoding technique for multi layer product codes. We describe its algorithm and simulate the performance for the 3D case on a depolarizing channel.

</details>


### [202] [The energetic cost of loading classical data in quantum computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14422)
*Joao F. Doriguello,Alessandro Luongo,Harshit Verma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将QRAM数据加载视为硬件过程，在超导处理器上量化单次QRAM查询的动态能量代价，并基于噪声-资源框架分析保真度与功率随门时长、驱动强度及制冷衰减分布的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习、化学与密码分析的算法通常假定经典数据的相干访问物理代价可忽略，这一假设掩盖了数据加载的真实硬件能耗，因此需要将数据加载能耗显式量化。

Method: 采用度量-噪声-资源（metric-noise-resource）框架，将查询保真度与功率同门持续时间、相对双比特驱动强度和多级制冷机衰减分布联系起来，并计入热光子与门误差；分析线性深度多路复用器与抗噪声bucket-brigade架构，将后者适配到原生超导门集，并用完全分解的门时序改进保真度分析。

Result: 对12比特地址寄存器的代表性bucket-brigade QRAM查询，查询保真度达0.969，动态能量为3.51×10⁻⁴ J，平均动态功率4.38 W，峰值64.4 W；在固定目标保真度下，优化工作点与衰减配置可使平均功率变化两倍以上。

Conclusion: QRAM查询的能耗是显著且可优化的硬件资源，数据加载代价不应被忽略；工作点选择与制冷衰减分布对功率有实质影响。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms for machine learning, chemistry, and cryptanalysis often assume coherent access to classical data at negligible physical cost. We instead treat data loading as a hardware process and quantify the dynamic energetic cost of a quantum random access memory (QRAM) query on a superconducting processor. Using the metric-noise-resource framework, we relate query fidelity and power to gate duration, relative two-qubit drive strength, and the distribution of attenuation across a multi-stage refrigerator, while accounting for thermal photons and gate errors. We analyze the linear-depth multiplexer and the noise-resilient bucket-brigade architectures, adapting the latter to a native superconducting gate set and refining its fidelity analysis using the fully decomposed gate schedule. For a representative bucket-brigade QRAM query with a $12$-qubit address register we obtain a query fidelity of $0.969$. The query consumes $3.51\times10^{-4}\,\mathrm{J}$ of dynamic energy, corresponding to an average dynamic power of $4.38\,\mathrm{W}$ and a peak of $64.4\,\mathrm{W}$. Our optimization shows that, at fixed target fidelity, the operating point and attenuation profile can change the average power by a factor of two or more.

</details>


### [203] [Quantum Thermal Transistor via Nonlinear Environmental Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14458)
*Laiba Hayat,M. Tahir Naseem*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究谐振子与发射极、集电极及基底热库耦合的热输运，证明单量子交换下不存在热放大；引入弱非线性展开与数值稳态解表明发射极两量子交换是热放大的起源，且当发射极与集电极均以两量子交换主导时，调节基底温度可在奇偶宇称扇区间重新分配布居，从而大幅放大热流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热放大可实现对热流的灵敏调控，是量子器件热管理与热信号处理的基础；但传统线性（单量子交换）系统-热库耦合下热放大缺失，需要揭示其产生机制并给出可控实现方案。

Method: 以耦合三热库（发射极、集电极、基底）的谐振子为模型；采用受控弱非线性展开并辅以数值稳态解，分析发射极/集电极两量子交换对热流的影响及基底温度调控机制；提出用纵向耦合辅助二能级系统并结合热库谱滤波的实现方案，并给出消除该辅助系统以获得所需谐振子动力学的条件。

Result: 单量子交换限制下确实无热放大；两量子交换在发射极出现时产生放大并预测其阈值条件；当发射极与集电极均由两量子交换主导时，基底温度变化主要重分配奇偶扇区布居，对发射极、集电极热流的改变远大于基底电流，实现大热放大。

Conclusion: 非线性系统-热库耦合可作为控制热流与实现热放大的资源；辅助二能级系统加谱滤波方案在给定消除条件下可实际构造所需非线性动力学。

Abstract: Thermal amplification enables sensitive control of heat currents, providing a basis for heat management and thermal signal processing in quantum devices. For a harmonic oscillator coupled to emitter, collector, and base thermal reservoirs, we recover the absence of thermal amplification when system-reservoir interactions permit only single-quantum exchange. A controlled weak-nonlinearity expansion, supported by numerical stationary solutions, shows that two-quantum exchange at the emitter enables amplification and predicts its onset. When two-quantum exchange dominates at the emitter and collector, varying the base temperature redistributes populations between even and odd oscillator sectors. This changes the emitter and collector heat currents far more than the base current, enabling large thermal amplification. We propose a realization using a longitudinally coupled auxiliary two-level system and bath spectral filtering, specifying conditions for its elimination to yield the required oscillator dynamics. These results establish nonlinear system-reservoir coupling as a resource for controlling heat flow and enabling thermal amplification.

</details>


### [204] [Finite-Key Readiness and Authentication-Key Replenishment in QKD-Assisted UAV Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14461)
*Dang H. Le,Ban Q. Tran,Susan Mengel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量化了无人机作为可信中继为两个地面网关提供量子密钥时的"等钥/拒钥"代价，发现新鲜密钥将双任务95分位完成时间从10.37 s推高到28.65 s，并经蒙特卡洛与半解析模型给出脉冲预算的可行窗口与损耗上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要新鲜量子密钥的服务必须先等待密钥释放且可能被拒绝，之后才能开始受保护计算，但这一等待/拒绝代价此前缺乏定量刻画；同时哈希认证、悬停能耗与截止时间之间的耦合未被现有标量速率模型捕捉。

Method: 构建单次有限密钥诱骗态BB84、参数预先固定的密钥获取场景：一台可信定点UAV服务两个地面网关，建立计数级蒙特卡洛模型并用半解析模型复现，将密钥接受与受保护上传、计算、下载（含失败尝试与悬停能耗）串联；对比标量速率模型、代理基准的近零哈希成本与全额计费的应力参考情形。

Result: 26 dB有效光损耗下，新鲜密钥使双任务95分位完成时间由10.37 s升至28.65 s，悬停能耗成正比增长；标量速率模型遗漏了块长的截止限制。脉冲预算窗口下界由认证密钥补充决定、上界由截止时间决定，认证扣减移动下界而哈希成本不移动。35 s截止时可行窗口的最大损耗为27 dB(1%失准)、22 dB(2%)，3%下无可行设置。选定配置在留出试验上以96.9–97.3%的同时置信下界满足截止；全额计费应力参考将损耗上限降至26 dB与20 dB。错误率或损耗小幅上升即令截止概率远低于目标并耗尽认证密钥，而协议安全参数不变。

Conclusion: 密钥获取的等待与拒绝是必须显式建模的可行性约束；脉冲预算窗口受认证密钥补充与截止时间双重夹逼，且对失准、损耗与哈希计费方式高度敏感，安全参数并不能反映这一运行层面的脆弱性。

Abstract: A service that needs fresh quantum keys must wait for key release, and may be denied it, before protected computation starts. We quantify this cost for a trusted stationary UAV serving two ground gateways, each obtaining its keys from one finite-key decoy-state BB84 attempt with parameters fixed in advance. A count-level Monte Carlo model, reproduced by a semi-analytic model, links key acceptance to protected upload, computation, and download, including failed attempts and hover energy. At 26~dB effective optical loss, with authentication hashing at its proxy-benchmarked, near-zero cost, fresh keys raise the 95th-percentile completion time of both tasks from 10.37 to 28.65~s; hover energy grows in proportion. The scalar rate models evaluated here miss the deadline limit on the block size. The pulse budget lies in a window bounded below by authentication-key replenishment and above by the deadline; for a fixed source, channel, and acceptance model, the authentication debit shifts the floor, whereas hashing cost does not. At a 35-s deadline the largest tested loss with a non-empty window is 27~dB at 1\% misalignment and 22~dB at 2\%; at 3\% no tested setting has one. Conditional on the assumed acquisition probability, the selected configurations meet this deadline with simultaneous lower confidence bounds of 96.9--97.3\% on held-out trials, at 26~dB and at 18--20~dB with 2\% misalignment. A high-cost stress reference that charges hashing in full narrows the window and lowers these loss limits to 26 and 20~dB at the reference processing capacity. A small rise in error rate or loss pushes the deadline probability well below its target and drains authentication keys, while the protocol's security parameter is unchanged.

</details>


### [205] [Nonperturbative string dynamics in a (2+1)D lattice gauge theory on a superconducting quantum processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14484)
*Sanziana Stelea,Anthony Gandon,Francesco Tacchino,Jad C. Halimeh,Ivano Tavernelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用63个超导量子比特（含12个辅助比特）实现(2+1)维Z2格点规范场论量子模拟，直接观测强禁闭与非微扰去禁闭区间的电通量弦动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电通量弦是规范理论中禁闭机制的核心，其演化涉及电荷运动、对产生与横向形变等竞争过程。2维空间中超越微扰极限的动力学难以用经典方法（如张量网络）求解，因为纠缠增长限制了可模拟的演化时间。

Method: 采用63个超导量子比特（12个辅助比特）构建(2+1)D Z2格点规范理论，包含动力学物质场与显式磁面（plaquette）相互作用，覆盖强禁闭与非微扰去禁闭区。演化初始局域化的弦，端点电荷可静止或动力学；保留物质与规范自由度分离，使局域高斯定律约束可直接用于错误检测，结合辅助比特校验与噪声重标定，实现最多15个二阶Trotter步的电路演化，并进行空间分辨测量。

Result: 去禁闭区观察到电荷与磁通的快速扩散；在对产生共振附近，弦内部物质占据增强；强电场下扩散受抑制；弦路径占据测量揭示了通过面操作连接的构型之间的横向重排。

Conclusion: 量子模拟是探索2维非微扰弦动力学的可行途径，且规范冗余可作为一种实验资源用于错误检测。

Abstract: Electric flux strings underpin confinement in gauge theories. In two spatial dimensions, their evolution involves competing processes of charge motion, pair creation, and transverse deformation.
