Accessing these dynamics beyond perturbative limits challenges classical approaches, as entanglement growth restricts the times accessible to tensor-network simulations. Here we use quantum simulation to investigate a (2+1)D Z2 lattice gauge theory with dynamical matter and an explicit magnetic plaquette interaction, spanning strongly confined and nonperturbative deconfined regimes. Using 63 superconducting qubits, including 12 ancillas, we evolve initially localized strings with static or dynamical endpoint charges. Spatially resolved measurements reveal rapid charge and flux spreading in the deconfined regime, enhanced matter occupation in the string interior near the pair-creation resonance, and suppressed spreading at strong electric fields. String-path occupations further probe transverse rearrangements between configurations connected by plaquette operations. Retaining separate matter and gauge degrees of freedom makes local Gauss-law constraints directly accessible for error detection. Combining these constraints with ancilla checks and noise rescaling, we measure dynamics in circuits containing up to 15 second-order Trotter steps. Our results establish quantum simulation as a practical approach to exploring nonperturbative string dynamics in two spatial dimensions, with gauge redundancy serving as an experimental resource.

</details>


### [206] [Alchemically Transferable Parameters for Variational Quantum Eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11445)
*Jonas Motyl,Jakob S. Kottmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"炼金术式"几何投影方法，将已有氢体系机器学习电路参数求解器直接迁移到有机分子，无需针对具体分子重新训练，仅需每种键型拟合一个投影半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子本征求解器（VQE）的经典优化回路是主要瓶颈（贫瘠高原、局部极小、能量与梯度评估代价高）。已有工作用机器学习直接预测电路参数替代优化回路，并在氢体系内实现了跨体系尺寸与结构迁移，但扩展到有机分子通常被认为需要用例专属训练集重新训练。

Method: 建立"炼金术"（alchemical）迁移途径：用几何投影沿目标分子键方向放置代理氢原子，将目标分子的局域成键环境映射到小氢片段上，从而原样复用已有氢体系求解器。唯一新增量为每种键型的单个投影半径，在小参考分子上拟合一次后保持不变。方法在可分离对（separable-pair）电路设计上演示，并扩展到跨越量子子空间有效基的多电路方法。

Result: 表明无需针对有机分子重新训练即可复用氢体系求解器；每个键型仅需一个投影半径参数，且一次拟合后可跨分子复用，方法对可分离对电路与多电路子空间基方法均适用。

Conclusion: 机器学习电路参数预测可通过几何"炼金术"投影实现从氢片段到有机分子的零重训练迁移，大幅降低数据成本，拓宽了VQE免优化回路方法的应用范围。

Abstract: Machine-learned circuit parameters could remove one of the main bottlenecks of variational quantum eigensolvers: the classical optimization loop, with its typical barren plateaus, local minima, and costly energy and gradient evaluations, is replaced by a direct prediction. Prior work showed that machine-learning models paired with a structured circuit design can transfer across system size and structure within hydrogenic systems. The next step: Extending them to organic molecules would seem to require re-training on use-case-specific training sets. In this work, we show that this is not necessary by establishing an alchemical approach. A geometric projection places surrogate hydrogen atoms along the bond directions of the target molecule and so maps its local bonding environment onto small hydrogenic fragments. Existing hydrogenic solvers can then be reused as they are. The only new quantity is a single projection radius per bond type, which is fitted once on a small reference molecule and reused unchanged thereafter. We demonstrate the approach on the separable-pair circuit designs that have been used in prior work and extend the approach to multi-circuit approaches that span effective bases for quantum subspaces.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [A NURBS-enhanced high-order finite-volume method for compressible laminar boundary layers on curved domains with hybrid meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13463)
*Yisu Li,Xucheng Meng,Guanghui Hu*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种面向混合网格上定常可压缩Navier–Stokes方程的NURBS增强k-exact有限体积方法，结合顺序重构壁面边界处理、NURBS高阶曲面表示与dGRP黏性通量，实现曲线域上可压缩层流边界层的高保真模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可压缩层流边界层在曲线域上的高保真模拟需要与高阶重构相容的物理壁面边界处理和高精度几何表示，因为黏性剪应力与导热热流集中于薄近壁区。传统高阶方法在曲壁附近常损失精度或需复杂的壁面处理。

Method: 1）在混合网格上构造NURBS增强的k-exact有限体积格式求解定常可压缩N-S方程；2）设计壁面边界处理，采用顺序重构（sequential reconstruction），无需重构变量与施加边界条件所用变量一致；3）利用NURBS边界表示获得曲边界的高阶精确描述；4）采用扩散型广义黎曼问题（dGRP）黏性通量以增强数值鲁棒性。

Result: 多组数值算例验证了该方法的高阶精度与有效性，表明其在曲线壁面附近能保持高精度并稳健地模拟可压缩层流边界层。

Conclusion: NURBS增强k-exact有限体积方法配合顺序重构壁面处理与dGRP黏性通量，可有效克服曲壁处高阶精度损失与壁面处理复杂的问题，为曲线域上可压缩层流边界层的高保真模拟提供了可行方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulation of compressible laminar boundary layers on curved domains requires physical wall boundary treatment and geometric representation compatible with high-order reconstruction, since viscous shear stresses and conductive heat fluxes are concentrated in a thin near-wall region. Conventional high-order methods often suffer from loss of accuracy near curved walls or require complicated wall treatments. To address these issues, in this paper, we develop a NURBS-enhanced $k$-exact finite-volume method for the steady compressible Navier--Stokes equations on hybrid meshes. To incorporate physical wall conditions into the high-order discretization, we develop a wall boundary treatment that employs sequential reconstruction without requiring the reconstruction variables to coincide with those used to prescribe the boundary conditions. The NURBS-based boundary representation is used to obtain a high-order accurate description of curved boundaries, and the diffusive generalized Riemann problem (dGRP) viscous flux is employed to enhance numerical robustness. A variety of numerical examples are provided to demonstrate the high-order accuracy and effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [208] [Fully GPU-accelerated semistochastic Epstein-Nesbet perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13522)
*Lizhong Fu,Honghui Shang,Jinlong Yang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出全GPU加速的分布式半随机二阶Epstein-Nesbet微扰理论（EN-PT2）实现，通过单次核遍历融合激发生成、筛选与PT2权重计算，并用固定规模随机相位CountSketch阵列替代大规模外部行列式交换，显著降低显存开销与通信成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EN-PT2是改进selected configuration interaction（SCI）能量的重要工具，但其庞大的外部行列式空间在GPU集群上带来沉重的内存与通信负担，现有CPU实现（如Arrow）难以满足大参考波函数的微扰修正需求。

Method: 开发分布式半随机EN-PT2的完全GPU加速实现：将激发生成、筛选和PT2权重计算融合于单一kernel遍历；采用固定尺寸的随机相位CountSketch阵列限制内存占用，并以有界集合求和（bounded collective summation）取代大规模外部行列式交换。

Result: 在相同请求统计标准误下，单块A100完成EN-PT2计算比64核CPU上的Arrow快8.21–37.53倍；固定负载强扩展测试在跨两节点的16块GPU上达到93.5%并行效率。应用于(60e, 187o)活性空间的铁氧配合物、每态含2亿行列式的铁卟啉参考态以及神经网络量子态。

Conclusion: 该方法在GPU时代为大参考波函数的微扰修正提供了高效可行的方案，兼具高加速比、良好并行扩展性与对超大规模行列式空间的可扩展性。

Abstract: Second-order Epstein-Nesbet perturbation theory (EN-PT2) is an important tool for improving selected configuration interaction (SCI) energies, but the large external determinant space creates substantial memory and communication costs on GPU clusters. We develop a fully GPU-accelerated implementation of distributed semistochastic EN-PT2, fusing excitation generation, screening, and PT2 weighting in a single kernel traversal. Fixed-size random-phase CountSketch arrays limit memory use and replace large external determinant exchange with bounded collective summation. The implementation on one A100 completes EN-PT2 calculations 8.21-37.53 times faster than Arrow on 64 CPU cores at the same requested statistical standard error, and fixed-workload strong-scaling tests achieve 93.5% parallel efficiency on 16 GPUs across two nodes. Applications to an iron-oxo complex in a (60e, 187o) active space, iron-porphyrin references containing 200 million determinants per state, and neural-network quantum states establish the method's efficiency for perturbative corrections to large reference wave functions in the GPU era.

</details>


### [209] [Neural network scaling and generalization for 3D strain fields in heterogeneous materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14158)
*Lucas Fourel,Paul Devianne,Marine Debouvry,Tristan Destruhaut,Russell Gunn,Ashwini Gupta,Lori Graham-Brady,Jean-François Molinari*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种U-Net代理模型，可在高达256³体素分辨率下直接预测混凝土细观结构中完整的36分量应变局部化张量场，并通过大规模超参数优化给出面向高分辨率三维张量场预测的网络缩放规律与精度-速度权衡，最终在Nvidia H100上相较FFT求解器实现约8.7倍加速（MAE 1.118%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算均匀化与耦合多尺度模拟需反复在高分辨率三维代表体积元（RVE）上求解力学场，成本过高；现有机器学习代理多针对固定宏观载荷、仅预测单一载荷下的场，且缺乏针对高分辨率三维张量场预测的网络架构缩放（width/depth、通道策略）指导。

Method: 以FFT基线弹性求解器生成训练用应变局部化张量标签；训练U-Net回归完整的36分量应变局部化张量场；开展大规模超参数优化，推导网络宽度/深度随问题复杂度缩放的规则，并进行精度-速度权衡分析；在Nvidia H100 GPU上评测推理加速；并引入在内部特征图上计算的最大均值差异（MMD）指标用于分布外（OOD）微结构检测，避免昂贵参考解计算。

Result: 验证集与独立测试集MAE均为1.118%；相较FFT求解器推理加速约8.7倍；仅网络宽度与深度需随问题复杂度缩放，而"下采样后通道翻倍"等常见U-Net设计在本问题上次优；泛化实验显示模型可跨骨料体积分数迁移，并表明训练集形态多样性至关重要；基于特征图MMD的OOD检测无需参考解即可工作。

Conclusion: 该工作为高分辨率三维RVE力学场预测给出了可操作的U-Net架构缩放与设计准则，证明张量场代理可在保持精度的同时显著加速多尺度计算；强调形态多样性训练对泛化的关键作用，并提供一种低成本、无需参考解的可靠性保障手段（特征图MMD OOD检测）。

Abstract: Computational homogenization and coupled multiscale simulations of heterogeneous materials require repeated solution of mechanical fields on Representative Volume Elements, which becomes prohibitively expensive for high-resolution three-dimensional microstructures. Machine-learning surrogates can accelerate these computations, yet most existing approaches predict fields for fixed macroscopic loadings and provide limited guidance on how to scale network architectures for high-resolution 3D tensor-field prediction. This work presents a U-Net surrogate trained to predict the full 36-component strain localization tensor field in voxelized concrete mesostructures at resolutions up to $256^3$. Training localization tensors are generated with an FFT-based linear elastic solver, and an extensive hyperparameter optimization is performed to derive scaling rules and an accuracy-speed trade-off analysis. The results show that only network width and depth need to be scaled with problem complexity, while several conventional U-Net design choices, such as doubling channels after downsampling, are suboptimal for this problem. The final neural network achieves a Mean Absolute Error of 1.118% on both the validation and the held-out test sets, and an inference speedup of approximately 8.7x over the FFT-based solver on an Nvidia H100 GPU. Generalization experiments reveal transfer across aggregate volume fractions and highlight the importance of training on diverse morphologies. To safeguard reliability, a Maximum Mean Discrepancy metric computed on internal feature maps is proposed for detecting out-of-distribution microstructures without costly reference computations.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [Shaping black hole resonances II: Spectroscopic landscapes and interference in black hole ringdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13075)
*Alejandro Svyatkovskyy Kholyavka,Sayak Datta,Jose Antonio León Vega,Xisco Jiménez Forteza*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文解析地刻画了振荡高斯扰动对Schwarzschild时空准正规模（QNM）的激发，发现激发振幅仅窄带时在载频等于模式实部处峰值，宽带时峰值移至ν=0，表明QNM激发不能仅由频率匹配理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为高效激发QNM需要扰动载频与模式频率匹配；作者希望系统理解脉冲宽度σ与载频ν如何影响激发系数，并检验频率匹配是否充分。

Method: 将激发系数C_n分解为内禀激发因子与在复QNM频率处取值的源重叠积分；对振荡高斯源，重叠为源正、负频分支的相干叠加，其振幅精确因子化为高斯谱包络与干涉因子。再解析分离相位为模式项、宽度项与ν相关项，并用时域数值模拟校验。

Result: 激发振幅仅在窄带源时于ν=ω_n^Re处峰值，足够宽带的脉冲峰值位于ν=0；干涉仅在ν≠0时调制振幅，其显著程度由ω_n^Re/ω_n^Im控制；相位中ν相关项在固定宽度下于ν=0取唯一极大。数值验证显示|C_0|的中位相对误差约4%，|C_1|在σ≤5时约16%、全参数空间约36%；相位中位差n=0约0.14 rad，n=1且σ≤5约0.34 rad。

Conclusion: QNM激发无法仅通过频率匹配来理解，相干干涉与源带宽起关键作用；该解析框架可为更真实的天体物理场景提供定性洞见。

Abstract: We map analytically the excitation of Schwarzschild quasinormal modes (QNMs) $C_n$ by oscillatory Gaussian perturbations across the pulse width $σ$ and carrier frequency $ν$. The excitation coefficient of each mode is determined by the product of an intrinsic excitation factor and a source-dependent overlap evaluated at the complex QNM frequency. For oscillatory Gaussian perturbations, this overlap is the coherent sum of the positive- and negative-frequency branches of the source, with its amplitude factorizing exactly into a Gaussian spectral envelope and an interference factor. We observe that the excitation amplitude peaks at $ν=ω_n^{\rm Re}$ only for narrowband sources. For sufficiently broadband pulses, the maximum lies at $ν=0$, so efficient excitation does not necessarily require matching the carrier and the mode frequency. We observe that the interference between branches, modulates the amplitude only for nonzero $ν$, a feature generic to real oscillatory sources. The prominence of the interference-induced modulation is controlled by the ratio $ω_n^{\rm Re}/ω_n^{\rm Im}$. The phase separates into a mode-only, a width-only and a $ν$-dependent term, the last generated by the same interference, with $ν=0$ being its unique maximum at fixed width. The analytical predictions are validated against time-domain numerical simulations, showing a relative median error of $\sim 4\%$ for $|C_0|$. For $|C_1|$, the median error is $\sim 16\%$ when $σ\leq 5$, increasing to $\sim 36\%$ across the full parameter space. The median phase difference for $n=0$ is $\sim0.14 \,\rm rad $ and for $n=1$, $σ\leq5$ it is $0.34\,\rm rad$. Our results demonstrate that QNM excitation cannot be understood through frequency matching alone and may provide qualitative insights into more realistic astrophysical scenarios.

</details>


### [211] [Depletion of Dark Matter Spikes by Massive Black Hole Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13170)
*Charlie Sharpe,Thomas F. M. Spieksma,Yonadav Barry Ginat,Ricarda S. Beckmann,Alexander Heger,Bence Kocsis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 大质量黑洞并合会严重破坏暗物质尖峰：质量比q=0.03的单次并合即可使100（5）史瓦西半径处密度下降约3（5）个量级；结合Horizon-AGN模拟的并合历史，可将可探测暗物质尖峰的黑洞质量范围限制在10^6–5×10^7 M☉（z≤3）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质尖峰可通过使IMRI/EMRI引力波退相位来间接探测暗物质，但该通道要求尖峰在宇宙学时间尺度上存活；而主黑洞的并合历史对尖峰的破坏程度尚不清楚，因此需评估并合对尖峰的削减作用及其对探测可行性的影响。

Method: 数值研究大质量黑洞双星旋进对中心约100史瓦西半径内暗物质尖峰的改造与破坏，考察不同质量比、不同极角及不同主黑洞质量；并将结果与Horizon-AGN宇宙学模拟中的黑洞并合历史及吸积驱动的绝热增长相结合。

Result: 质量比q=0.03的单次并合在100（5）史瓦西半径处使密度下降约3（5）个量级，且与主黑洞质量无关、对相对双星平面的各极角均成立，更大质量比破坏更强；即使q=0.005也会使中心密度下降2–3个量级，使退相位难以探测。Horizon-AGN显示并合随黑洞质量增加而更常见：z=0时q>0.03的并合发生于50%的3×10^7 M☉黑洞与95%的10^8 M☉黑洞，z=3时相应比例在2×10^7与5×10^7 M☉即达到。吸积驱动的绝热增长无法在并合间实现显著尖峰再生长。

Conclusion: 结合此前关于EMRI诱导尖峰耗竭的研究，本工作将能够承载可探测暗物质尖峰的大质量黑洞质量范围限制为10^6–5×10^7 M☉（z≤3）。

Abstract: Dark matter over-densities around massive black holes, known as dark matter spikes, can dephase gravitational waves emitted by intermediate- and extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (IMRIs/EMRIs), offering a promising way to indirectly detect dark matter. This detection channel, however, requires the spike to survive over cosmological time-scales. We investigate the impact that a host black hole's merger history has on its dark-matter spike. We focus on how the inspiral of a massive-black-hole (MBH) binary modifies and disrupts these spikes within the central $\sim 100$ Schwarzschild radii of the black hole. We find that a single merger with mass ratio $q = 0.03$ is capable of reducing a spike's density by around 3 (5) orders of magnitude at 100 (5) Schwarzschild radii across all polar angles relative to the binary's plane, independent of the primary MBH's mass, with larger mass ratios inducing even stronger depletion. In fact, a single past merger with mass ratio as small as $q = 0.005$ reduces central densities by 2--3 orders of magnitude, making gravitational-wave dephasing difficult to detect. We relate these results to the MBH merger history by analysing cosmological simulation data from Horizon-AGN. We find that mergers become increasingly common as the MBH mass increases, with mergers of mass ratio q > 0.03 having occurred in 50% of MBHs of mass $3\times10^7M_\odot$ and 95% of mass $10^8M_\odot$ at z=0. These fractions are reached at even lighter masses ($2\times10^7M_\odot$ and $5\times10^7M_\odot$, respectively) at z=3. We find that accretion-induced adiabatic MBH growth is unable to cause appreciable spike re-growth between such mergers in {\tt Horizon-AGN}. Thus, in combination with previous studies of EMRI-induced depletion, this work constrains the mass range over which massive black holes can host detectable DM spikes to $10^6M_\odot$--$5 \times 10^7M_\odot$ for $z \leq 3$.

</details>


### [212] [Eccentricity greatly enhances the detectability of line-of-sight acceleration in gravitational wave signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13197)
*János Takátsy,Anna Puecher,Gonzalo Morras,Lorenz Zwick*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 偏心引力波信号显著增强对视线加速度（LSA）等环境效应的敏感性；若从首个可探测偏心谐波进入探测带起开始分析（而非惯用的 n=2 谐波），LSA 约束可提升一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学形成的致密双星具有偏心率分布尾部，且可能处于环境扰动中。作者希望利用偏心率这一特征来提升对视线加速度等环境效应的探测能力。

Method: 结合 Fisher 分析预测与基于最先进偏心波形模型的完整贝叶斯参数估计；关键做法是改从首个可探测偏心谐波进入探测器频段（而非 n=2 谐波进入时刻）开始分析信号，从而覆盖更长的早期旋近阶段，使 LSA 引起的引力波退相位显著增大。

Result: 20 Hz 处偏心率从 e20=0 增至 e20≈0.1 时，在信噪比充足情形下 LSA 约束可提升一个数量级以上；即使在低信噪比情形下也预测约 5 倍的增强。

Conclusion: 偏心信号对 LSA 的敏感性增强在 Fisher 预报与完整贝叶斯推断中均成立；充分释放该效应需持续改进高效偏心波形模型与似然评估，以支持持续数千秒、含大量谐波信号的高效分析。

Abstract: Compact object binaries formed dynamically are characterized by a tail of eccentric sources and by the potential presence of environmental perturbations. Here we show that the presence of eccentricity in a gravitational-wave (GW) signal strongly enhances the sensitivity to environmental effects, focusing in particular on line-of-sight acceleration (LSA). We demonstrate that this enhancement is recovered in Fisher-analysis forecasts and, crucially, persists in full Bayesian parameter estimation using state-of-the-art eccentric waveform models. The key insight is that the signal should be analyzed from the moment the first detectable eccentric harmonic enters the detector band, rather than from the customary entry of the $n=2$ harmonic: this allows to probe the early inspiral and thus a longer signal, over which the GW dephasing introduced by LSA becomes substantially larger. We show that with this approach constraints on LSA can increase by more than an order of magnitude between eccentricities at 20~Hz $e_{20}=0$ and $e_{20}\sim0.1$, for signals with sufficient signal-to-noise-ratio (SNR), while we predict a factor $5$ amplification even for low-SNR ones. Fully exploiting this effect necessitates the continued improvement of efficient eccentric waveform models and likelihood evaluations, enabling the efficient analysis of signals lasting thousands of seconds and containing a large number of harmonics.

</details>


### [213] [Polarized Equatorial Emission around Kerr Black Holes with Synchronized Scalar Hair. II. Indirect images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13253)
*Valentin O. Deliyski,Tsanimir Angelov,Galin N. Gyulchev,Stoytcho S. Yazadjiev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了带同步标量毛的自洽Kerr黑洞周围薄吸积盘间接像的偏振强度特性，发现高阶（n≥2）中心环状像不被指数级削弱，可显著贡献偏振辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量毛会显著改变时空透镜性质，但以往通常认为观测特征主要由n=1像主导，需检验强标量化黑洞中高阶间接像（尤其偏振信号）是否被忽略。

Method: 针对几何薄、光学薄吸积盘，在完全自洽的带同步标量毛Kerr黑洞背景下计算光子测地线与间接像的偏振强度分布，取代表性高标量荷模型并分析低观测倾角下的高阶中心环状像。

Result: 在高标量荷模型中，低倾角下出现高阶中心环状像；这些像未被指数级去放大（demagnified），其相对直接像的偏振强度显著。

Conclusion: 强标量化Kerr黑洞的观测特征不一定仅由n=1像主导；n≥2像可显著贡献偏振辐射，其重要性依赖盘的径向发射率分布与观测倾角。

Abstract: We investigate the intensity polarization properties of the indirect images of a geometrically and optically thin accretion disk around fully self-consistent models of Kerr black holes with synchronized scalar hair. The presence of a massive, self-gravitating scalar field can dramatically alter the lensing properties of the spacetime, resulting in morphologically complex indirect images. We show that for a representative high scalar charge model, this results in the presence of high order central ring-like images at low observer inclinations. These images are not exponentially demagnified, and their polarized intensity, relative to the direct images, is significant. We therefore conclude that the observational signature of strongly scalarized Kerr black holes may not be dominated solely by the $n=1$ image, as images with $n\geq2$ may also contribute significantly to the observed polarized emission, depending on the radial emissivity profile of the disk and the observer inclination.

</details>


### [214] [Challenging the hoop conjecture with spinning pancakes and baguettes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13266)
*Robin Croft,Gerardo García-Moreno,Paolo Pani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文构造旋转尘埃的轴对称初始数据族，检验适用于该情形的环猜想表述，发现当准局域质量包含转动能时，环猜想在14%以内成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞唯一性定理只刻画稳态终态，奇点定理只能给出测地线不完备性，二者均未提供视界形成的一般判据；环猜想（hoop conjecture）将封闭质量与各方向致密度联系起来，是有望填补这一空白的一般判据，但此前缺乏旋转、非球对称构型下的系统检验。

Method: 构造描述旋转尘埃的轴对称初始数据族，包含高度扁长（prolate）与扁圆（oblate）构型，角速度剖面取常数与微分重标度（differential rescaled）两类；选取接近表观视界形成阈值的构型；在此基础上检验一种适配该设定（轴对称、旋转）的环猜想表述，并比较不同质量定义（含转动能的准局域质量、表观视界的不可约质量、物质面内封闭能、ADM质量）下的偏离程度。

Result: 对旋转构型，若准局域质量包含转动能，则环猜想成立，系数与 unity 的偏差在14%以内，与非旋转情形相当；若改用表观视界不可约质量，偏离随自旋增大，与Kerr黑洞的行为一致。对于无视界构型，当质量限于物质面内封闭能量时猜想同样成立；而采用含外部转动场能的ADM质量则出现最高约25%的表观违背。

Conclusion: 环猜想在旋转尘埃构型中以适当的质量定义（含转动能的准局域质量或物质面内封闭能）成立，其偏差量级与非旋转情形一致，说明环猜想可作为视界形成的有效判据；文章还讨论了无视界致密构型的可能演化、与弱宇宙监督假设的关系，及其对无视界致密天体的启示。

Abstract: Understanding when gravitational collapse produces a black hole remains a central challenge in general relativity. Black-hole uniqueness results characterize the stationary endpoint under suitable assumptions, while singularity theorems establish geodesic incompleteness in the presence of trapped surfaces and appropriate energy and global conditions. Neither, however, provides a general criterion for horizon formation. The hoop conjecture offers such a criterion by relating the enclosed mass to the compactness of a matter distribution in every direction. We construct axisymmetric families of initial data describing rotating dust and test a formulation of the conjecture adapted to this setting. We explore highly prolate and oblate configurations with both constant and differential rescaled angular-velocity profiles, including configurations near the threshold for apparent-horizon formation. We find that the conjecture holds for rotating configurations, with a coefficient within 14% of unity as in the nonrotating case, provided the quasilocal mass includes the rotational energy. If the irreducible mass of the apparent horizon is used instead, the deviations grow with spin, as they do for Kerr black holes. For horizonless configurations, the conjecture likewise holds when the mass is restricted to the energy enclosed by the matter surface, whereas using the ADM mass, which also includes rotational field energy outside the matter, leads to apparent violations of up to $\approx$25%. We also discuss the possible evolution of compact configurations without apparent horizons, their relation to weak cosmic censorship, and their implications for horizonless compact objects.

</details>


### [215] [Viscosity heats, diffusion drains: Open-system thermodynamics in unimodular cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13269)
*Miguel Cruz,Samuel Lepe*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文比较广义相对论中的体黏滞与幺模引力中的能量扩散两种非绝热物质描述，指出二者在平衡、熵与温度方程上可通过 Π↔Q̇/3H 对应，但吸收扩散能量扇区的温度 T_D 决定了共动熵产生率 Q̇(1/T_D−1/T_m)，从而区分黏滞与扩散的物理与可观测后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于幺模宇宙学是否为绝热的争论，源于将熵只归属于物质还是归属于总有效流体；同时需澄清体黏滞与能量扩散之间的对应关系在何种条件下成立、在何处失效。

Method: 对比体黏滞GR与能量扩散幺模引力的平衡、熵与温度方程，引入吸收扩散能量扇区的温度 T_D 计算共动熵产生；用宇宙学运动学量（减速参数 q 与急动参数 j）分别固定 Π 与扩散；并以扰动稳定性确定有效声速偏移 c_s^2−ω 作为 |Π|/ρ 的扩散类比；最后用表观视界温度检验广义第二定律。

Result: T_D→∞ 与 T_D→T_m 分别复现更早的熵产生极限；有限 T_D 时扩散等价于与外部热库的热交换，可冷却开放系统，破坏黏滞-扩散对应；ΛCDM 是幺模宇宙学的绝热极限，但在黏滞描述下仍为耗散性；若暗物质为守恒静质量气体，一致扩散只移除热能、禁止幽灵行为，并以有限热库限制宇宙学项增长；得到守恒粒子数的对数型暗流体需 z≈12 的相对论性暗物质；在Hayward-Kodama表观视界温度下，幺模引力的广义第二定律由局域定律推出，而体黏滞情形需另行施加。

Conclusion: 黏滞与扩散的对应仅在特定熵归属与温度假设下成立；T_D 与稳定性条件决定可观测与热力学差异，幺模引力的热力学性质优于黏滞模型。

Abstract: In GR with bulk viscosity and in unimodular gravity with energy diffusion, the matter sector is non-adiabatic, and their balance, entropy, and temperature equations match if $Π\leftrightarrow \dot{Q}/3H$. Disagreement over whether unimodular cosmology is adiabatic comes from assigning entropy to matter only or to the total effective fluid. The key is the temperature $T_D$ of the sector absorbing the diffused energy: comoving entropy production is $\dot{Q}(1/T_D-1/T_m)$, yielding earlier limits for $T_D \rightarrow \infty$ and $T_D \rightarrow T_m$. For finite $T_D$, diffusion acts like heat exchange with an external reservoir: unlike viscosity, which only heats a closed fluid, diffusion into a colder bath can cool an open one, breaking the viscosity-diffusion correspondence for observables and stability. Cosmographically, $Π$ is fixed by the deceleration $q$, while diffusion is fixed by the jerk $j$; thus $Λ$CDM is the adiabatic limit of unimodular cosmology but remains dissipative when modeled via viscosity. The diffusive analogue of $|Π|/ρ$ is the shift $c_s^2-ω$ in effective sound speed, set by perturbative stability rather than acceleration. If dark matter is a gas with conserved rest mass, consistent diffusion removes only thermal energy, forbids phantom behavior, and bounds the growth of the cosmological term by the finite thermal reservoir; producing a logotropic dark fluid with conserved particle number would require relativistic dark matter at $z\simeq12$. With the Hayward-Kodama apparent-horizon temperature, the generalized second law in unimodular gravity follows from the local one, whereas for bulk viscosity it must be imposed separately.

</details>


### [216] [The out-state of scalar-induced gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13462)
*Yizhou Lu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将标量诱导引力波（SIGW）中的自由辐射识别为张量扰动的"出态"，并证明其在不同内在规范下具有规范不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIGW由二阶张量扰动描述，其中既含自由辐射又含依赖规范的标量源响应，二者混杂使自由辐射振幅的物理意义与规范依赖性长期不清，需要严格界定"出态"并给出其规范不变性判据。

Method: 对常状态方程0<w<1的理想流体，用核函数的光锥留数（light-cone residue）构造每对标量模对应的出态；借助经典LSZ型约化公式由初始数据与光锥投影源求振幅；并证明一个经典等价定理：若张量扰动的重定义其晚期偏移仅有幂律包络且无张量频率成分，则振幅不变。

Result: 内在规范间（切片法向观察者与空间坐标由物质和几何确定、无外部时钟或独立振荡动力学）的变换即属此类重定义（除零测度的共振面外），故出态在所有内在规范（含宇宙学微扰论标准规范）中相同；晚期Newton核即为出态核，从而为其用于自由辐射能谱提供依据。

Conclusion: SIGW自由辐射振幅是规范不变量，晚期Newton核可合法用于能谱计算；在物质主导期，出态进一步将自由辐射与静态潮汐响应分离。

Abstract: Scalar-induced gravitational waves (SIGWs) are described by second-order tensor perturbations that contain free radiation together with a gauge-dependent response maintained by the scalar source. For a perfect fluid with constant equation of state $0<w<1$, we identify the free radiation with the out-state of the tensor perturbation, assembled from the light-cone residue of kernel functions for each pair of scalar modes. Its amplitudes follow from a classical LSZ-type reduction formula, in terms of the initial data and the light-cone-projected source. We prove a classical equivalence theorem: a redefinition of the tensor perturbation whose late-time shift has power-law envelopes and no tensor-frequency component leaves these amplitudes unchanged. Transformations between intrinsic gauges, whose slice-normal observers and spatial coordinates are fixed by the matter and geometry without an external clock or independent oscillatory dynamics, are redefinitions of this kind, except on resonance surfaces of zero measure. The out-state is therefore the same in all intrinsic gauges, including the standard gauges of cosmological perturbation theory. The late-time Newtonian kernel is then the out-state kernel, which justifies its use for the energy spectrum of the free radiation. In matter domination, the out-state instead separates the free radiation from the static tidal response.

</details>


### [217] [Quasinormal overtones and grey-body factors of Frolov black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13569)
*Ying Huang,Huajie Gong,Li-Gang Zhu,Jian-Pin Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Frolov黑洞（介于RN与Hayward极限之间）的标量准正规模（尤其高阶模overtone）与灰体因子。结果表明：在允许参数范围内所有QNM频率虚部为负，黑洞对标量扰动稳定；增大电荷q或正则化参数α0会触发高阶模"爆发"，且Frolov阈值参数比RN/Hayward更小；角动量不能消除爆发只推迟其发生；overtone修正对QNM–灰体对应公式至关重要，且q对势垒与透射的抑制强于α0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Frolov黑洞作为RN与Hayward之间的插值解，其标量扰动稳定性、高阶overtone行为及灰体因子尚缺乏系统研究；特别是overtone爆发（outburst）现象在插值参数空间中的依赖、以及QNM与灰体因子对应关系在高阶修正下的精度，是本文关注的动机。

Method: 在允许的电荷q与正则化参数α0范围内数值计算标量QNM频谱并考察各阶overtone；分析有效势随q、α0、角动量l的变化；利用l=1的QNM–灰体因子对应关系（含overtone修正）计算透射率，并与eikonal截断比较。

Result: 所有QNM频率虚部为负，标量扰动稳定；增大q或α0可触发overtone爆发，Frolov阈值低于RN与Hayward情形；l=0时爆发从n=1起始，l=1时推迟至n≥2；l=1时电荷对势垒峰值的抬升远强于α0，与Hayward/RN的显著偏离集中在视界附近；含overtone修正的灰体公式显著优于eikonal截断；固定频率下q与α0均抑制透射，q为主导因素。

Conclusion: Frolov黑洞在允许参数空间内对标量扰动稳定；overtone爆发对q、α0与l敏感，且Frolov比两个极限情形更早出现；高阶模修正对QNM–灰体对应不可或缺；电荷在抬高势垒与抑制透射方面起主导作用，与势垒分析一致。

Abstract: We investigate scalar quasinormal modes (QNMs), with particular attention to overtones, and grey-body factors of Frolov black holes, which interpolate between the Reissner--Nordström (RN) and Hayward limits. Over the allowed ranges of charge $q$ and regularization parameter $α_0$, all QNM frequencies have negative imaginary parts, confirming stability against scalar perturbations. Increasing either $q$ or $α_0$ can trigger overtone outbursts, with the Frolov threshold shifted to smaller parameter values than in the RN or Hayward cases. Angular momentum does not eliminate the outburst but postpones its onset from $n=1$ at $l=0$ to $n\geq 2$ at $l=1$. Comparisons of the effective potential show that, for $l=1$, charge raises the barrier peak much more strongly than $α_0$, while the clearest deviations from the Hayward and RN references are localized near the horizon. Using the QNM--grey-body correspondence at $l=1$, we find that overtone corrections are essential, and the amended formula substantially outperforms the eikonal truncation. At fixed frequency, both $q$ and $α_0$ suppress transmission, with charge being the dominant factor, consistent with the stronger barrier enhancement under the charge scan.

</details>


### [218] [Inverse Scattering & Isospectrality in (Analog) Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13871)
*Christian F. Schmidt,Stefan Floerchinger*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 将宇宙学粒子产生映射为一维量子散射问题（标度因子→零能共振，宇宙奇点→束缚态），证明给定粒子数谱与相位后，正则渐近静态宇宙的膨胀历史可被唯一重建，并在解析与数值上验证至0.02%精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上粒子产生是"由历史推谱"的正向问题；作者提出反问题：能否由激发谱反演产生它的宇宙学历史，且反演是否唯一。这为早期宇宙情景分类与替代暴胀模型构建提供了新视角。

Method: 建立粒子产生与一维量子散射的对应：膨胀历史充当散射势景，产生谱对应反射/透射波。借助散射理论分析共振与束缚态结构，发现标度因子反演（膨胀↔收缩）的等谱性（仅差π相位）；再结合功率谱的振幅（或粒子数）与相位进行解析（精确可解势景）与数值重建，并考察动量分辨率与测量不确定度下的稳健性。

Result: 存在等谱性：膨胀与收缩历史给出相同粒子数、相位差π；仅用粒子数不足以唯一确定历史。给定粒子数与相位，对正则、渐近静态宇宙重建唯一；数值上对常见logistic膨胀历史重建精度优于0.02%；并量化了对动量空间分辨率与测量误差的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 观测功率谱与产生它的宇宙学历史之间存在直接、可反演的联系；散射理论框架可用于早期宇宙情景的分类及暴胀替代方案的构造，并适配冷原子类比量子模拟的实验条件。

Abstract: Cosmological particle production, the excitation of field quanta by a dynamical spacetime, underlies the primordial perturbations observed in the cosmic microwave background and has been realized in analog quantum simulators with ultra-cold atoms. We ask the inverse question: given the excitation spectrum, can the cosmological history that produced it be reconstructed, and is that reconstruction unique? We approach this problem through a mapping of particle production to one-dimensional quantum scattering, in which the expansion history plays the role of a scattering landscape and the produced spectrum the role of reflected and transmitted waves. This mapping reveals a rich interplay between scattering theory and cosmology. The scale factor enters as a zero-energy resonance, an exceptional case of scattering theory, while cosmic singularities correspond to bound states, so that regular cosmologies are governed entirely by the scattering continuum. We find an interesting isospectrality: histories related by an inversion of the scale factor (expansion <-> contraction) yield identical particle numbers, differing only by a pi-shift in phase. Given both the particle number (or alternatively the amplitude) and phase of the cosmological power spectrum, the reconstruction is unique for regular, asymptotically static cosmologies. We demonstrate the reconstruction both analytically, for exactly solvable scattering landscapes, and numerically, recovering common logistic expansion histories to better than 0.02%. We further quantify its robustness to the momentum-space resolution and measurement uncertainties characteristic of analog quantum simulation. This work establishes a direct link between the observable power spectrum and the cosmological history that produced it, offering a new perspective on the classification of early-universe scenarios and the construction of alternatives to inflation.

</details>


### [219] [Non-singular cosmology from effective LQG-inspired black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14001)
*V. F. C. Belo,F. G. S. Menezes,S. M. Rupf,I. P. R. Baranov,H. A. Borges*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 将圈量子引力启发的黑洞几何通过Darmois-Israel连接条件匹配到膨胀的FLRW宇宙，得到以量子反弹取代奇点的修正Friedmann方程，并引入标量场实现从普朗克反弹到标准慢滚暴胀的衔接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在普朗克区间内，经典广义相对论的奇点问题需要被量子引力效应修正；本文旨在构造一个非奇异原初宇宙模型，并为普朗克尺度与亚普朗克尺度之间建立可行的能量衔接机制。

Method: （1）采用圈量子引力启发的有效黑洞几何；（2）通过Darmois-Israel薄壳连接条件将其与膨胀的FLRW时空匹配；（3）将动态薄壳的表面能动量张量映射为均匀标量场；（4）将框架推广至双标量场系统以衔接能隙。

Result: 所得修正Friedmann方程在普朗克区间以量子反弹替代奇点；标量场映射显示暂现的幽灵样（phantom-like）行为；对满足ω>-1的任意壳层状态方程，其有效能量密度随宇宙膨胀而稀释；双标量场系统可桥接普朗克反弹与亚普朗克尺度之间的能隙。

Conclusion: 该模型给出了一个非奇异的反弹宇宙图像：前暴胀阶段短暂，随后由第二个标量场接管并驱动标准慢滚暴胀，从而将量子反弹阶段与常规暴胀阶段自然衔接。

Abstract: We propose a non-singular primordial cosmology derived from matching an effective Loop Quantum Gravity-inspired black hole geometry to an expanding Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker universe via Darmois-Israel junction conditions. The resulting modified Friedmann equation replaces the singularity with a quantum bounce in the Planckian regime. Mapping the surface energy-momentum tensor of the dynamic thin shell onto a homogeneous scalar field reveals a transient phantom-like behavior. For any equation of state of the shell satisfying $ω> -1$, its effective energy density is diluted with cosmic expansion. Extending the framework to a two-scalar-field system provides a mechanism to bridge the energy gap between the Planckian bounce and the sub-Planckian scale. The pre-inflationary phase is brief, enabling the second scalar field to take over to drive the standard slow-roll inflation.

</details>


### [220] [Gravitational Origin of Black Hole Thermodynamic Geometry from Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14031)
*Ling-Zheng Xia,Lixin Xu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从四维欧氏 Einstein-Maxwell-AdS 引力的体作用量与边界项出发推导热力学 Hessian 度规，证明度规的变号与奇点源自系综（Legendre 变换）而非物理不稳定，并通过边界条件追踪区分真正的稳定边界与变换奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞热力学中 Hessian 度规在不同统计系综间会改变符号与奇点位置，负本征值既可能意味着物理不稳定，也可能仅是一次 Legendre 变换的产物，二者难以区分，需要几何化、系统化的判据。

Method: 在体区利用径向辛通量将质量与电荷同其渐近源配对，由视界正则性筛选平衡态；在无穷远由边界项确定系综极化（哪些蓄水池参数固定、哪些共轭量涨落）。固定温度与电势得到 Ruppeiner 度规；加入 Maxwell 边界项则固定电荷并反转相应度规符号。另考察全息抵消项与混合电边界条件的作用。

Result: 边界条件的选择直接决定系综：固定 (T, Φ) 给出 Ruppeiner 度规，加 Maxwell 边界项后固定 Q 且度规相应符号反转；局域全息抵消项不改变度规；混合电边界条件在两系综间插值并移动稳定极限。

Conclusion: 热力学几何的符号与奇点可由边界条件完整解释，从而把真正的稳定边界与 Legendre 变换的奇点分离开来，为系综依赖的黑洞稳定性分析提供了从作用量出发的判据。

Abstract: In black hole thermodynamics, Hessian metrics change both their signature and their singularities from one statistical ensemble to another. A negative eigenvalue may therefore signal either a physical instability or only a Legendre transformation. We derive this geometry from the bulk action and boundary terms of four-dimensional Euclidean Einstein-Maxwell-anti-de Sitter gravity. In the bulk, the radial symplectic flux pairs the mass and charge with their asymptotic sources, and horizon regularity selects the equilibrium states. At infinity, the boundary terms determine the ensemble polarization by specifying which reservoir parameters are held constant and which conjugate quantities fluctuate. Fixing the temperature and electric potential gives the Ruppeiner metric, whereas adding the Maxwell boundary term fixes the charge and reverses the corresponding metric sign. Tracking the boundary conditions thus separates true stability boundaries from singularities of the Legendre transform. Local holographic counterterms leave the metric unchanged, while mixed electric boundary conditions interpolate between the two ensembles and shift their stability limits.

</details>


### [221] [Renormalized dynamical tidal response of neutron stars in binary dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14077)
*M. V. S. Saketh,Raj Patil,Godwin Martin,Nils Andersson,Suprovo Ghosh,Jan Steinhoff*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 基于星体微扰理论与世界线有效场论（通过潮汐散射相位匹配），计算了中子星的相对论动力学潮汐响应，获取直至四阶 post-Minkowskian 的响应函数修正，并将其匹配到四极谐振子有效理论以建模 f 模与低频 g 模，结果与完整中子星响应高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潮汐响应描述多基于牛顿或低阶后牛顿近似，缺少相对论框架下中子星动力学潮汐响应的系统计算，尤其是将微扰理论与有效场论一致匹配、并涵盖高阶 post-Minkowskian 引力修正的模型。

Method: 采用中子星星体微扰理论计算相对论动力学潮汐响应；定义潮汐散射相位为引力波 Compton/Raman 散射相位与黑洞散射相位实部之差，据此与世界线有效场论匹配；利用自能修正将响应函数计算到四阶 post-Minkowskian 阶；对遵循 Brussels-Skyrme 族 BSk22 状态方程的中子星，将响应函数一致匹配到相对论四极谐振子有效理论，逐模确定频率、潮汐耦合及 post-adiabatic 动力学潮汐 Love 数。

Result: 匹配后的谐振子模型与完整中子星响应高度一致；在外潮汐场计算至三阶 post-Newtonian 阶时，分析了圆轨道双星系统中中子星诱导四极矩的潮汐耦合以及绝热束缚能。

Conclusion: 散射相位匹配为微扰理论与有效场论之间提供了自洽桥梁，所构建的相对论多模谐振子模型可高精度复现中子星动力学潮汐响应，并可用于双星系统潮汐耦合与束缚能的高阶 PN 研究。

Abstract: We present the relativistic dynamical tidal response of a neutron star computed using stellar perturbation theory and matched to a worldline effective field theory via the tidal scattering phase. This phase is defined as the difference between the Compton/Raman scattering phase of gravitational-waves from a neutron star and the real part of the black hole scattering phase. The gravitational corrections to the response function are obtained using self-energy corrections up to fourth post-Minkowskian order. The response function for neutron stars obeying the BSk22 equation-of-state from Brussels-Skyrme family, is then consistently matched to an effective theory of relativistic quadrupolar harmonic oscillators to model the fundamental-mode ($f$-mode) and low-frequency gravity-modes ($g$-modes). This matching includes the frequency and tidal coupling of each mode and the post-adiabatic dynamical tidal Love number, and once matched this model is in excellent agreement with the full neutron star response. With the external tidal field up to third post-Newtonian order, we also analyse the tidal coupling of the induced quadrupole on the neutron star in a circular binary system, and the adiabatic binding energy.

</details>


### [222] [Response of Circular Binaries to Monochromatic Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14151)
*Corey McInerney,Phil J. Sutton*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了开普勒双星系统中试验粒子与持续入射的线化引力波之间的轨道能量交换，解析推导了能量、半长轴与平均运动的变化率，并在数值上揭示了引力波频率与初始轨道频率之间的共振效应，区分了有界短周期振荡与共振天平动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨引力波与束缚轨道系统之间的能量交换机制，特别关注共振条件是否可能产生可观测的轨道效应，以评估引力波共振在真实天体系统中的观测意义。

Method: 在牛顿开普勒轨道基础上叠加线化引力波微扰，解析推导试验粒子能量、半长轴和平均运动的变化率；通过数值积分分析共振行为，区分有界短周期振荡与共振天平动，并考察引力波入射角相对轨道平面的影响，采用高应变示例计算偏心率与轨道倾角的演化。

Result: 数值结果表明，当引力波频率与粒子初始轨道频率匹配时出现共振效应，表现为共振天平动而非单纯有界振荡；引力波入射角会调制交换速率；在高应变示例中，偏心率变化有界，而初始为平面圆轨道时轨道倾角呈小幅线性增长。

Conclusion: 引力波共振需极长的相干时间才能显现，因而其观测意义仅限于轨道周期极短的天体系统；对一般天体系统而言，该共振效应难以被观测到。

Abstract: The exchange in specific orbital energy between a test particle in a Keplerian binary orbit and a continuously incident linearized gravitational wave (GW) is considered. Analytical expressions for the rate of change of energy, semi-major axis and mean motion of the test particle are derived. Numerical solutions illustrate a resonance effect betwen the GWs frequency and the particle's initial orbital frequency. We distinguish between bounded short-period oscillations and resonant libration. The effect of the GWs angle of incidence with the particle's orbital plane is also considered. Using an illustrative high GW strain we show that changes to the eccentricity are bounded while inclination exhibits small linear growth for an initially flat, circular orbit. Long coherence requirements linit the observational significance of GW resonance to astrophysical systems with very short orbital periods.

</details>


### [223] [Search Method for Continuous Gravitational Waves from Rotating Neutron Stars in Highly Eccentric Wide Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14197)
*M. Ball,S. Bussières,L. Mirasola,J. R. Merou,A. K. Sharma,N. Singh,D. Keitel,A. M. Sintes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对Gaia DR3给出的21个宽轨道高偏心率双星系统中潜在中子星候选体，开展定向连续引力波（CW）搜索的可行性研究：构建频率度规以在考虑轨道参数不确定度的情况下放置搜索模板，并基于FastTracks框架结合降采样与插值高效生成双星多普勒修正，通过注入实验验证了该搜索策略的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gaia第三次数据发布给出了21个宽轨道、高偏心率双星系统中潜在的中子星候选体及其轨道参数估计，为定向连续引力波搜索提供了全新的、具有明确先验信息的候选目标；但此类系统的高维参数空间与轨道参数不确定度带来的计算代价问题尚缺乏系统性评估，需要开展可行性研究。

Method: 1）计算频率度规（frequency metric），在参数空间中依据轨道参数不确定度确定模板放置方式，保证搜索覆盖效率；2）在FastTracks基础设施上扩展，联合使用降采样（downsampling）与插值（interpolation）以降低生成双星多普勒修正的计算成本，从而应对高维搜索空间；3）开展注入实验（injection campaign）以评估该搜索流程的探测性能。

Result: 注入实验表明该流程具备良好性能，采用该方法的搜索在计算与灵敏度上均是可行的（viable）。

Conclusion: 该工作表明可对Gaia候选中子星双星系统开展定向连续引力波搜索，此类搜索将成为Gaia观测与连续引力波分析的首次结合，为后续实际搜索奠定方法学基础。

Abstract: Recent results from the Gaia third data release have highlighted 21 potential neutron stars candidates in wide, highly-eccentric binary systems and provide estimates of their orbital parameters. We present a feasibility study for a directed search for continuous gravitational waves (CWs) tailored for these and similar systems. To this extent, we have computed a frequency metric to place templates in the parameter space accounting for uncertainties on the orbital parameters. We have built upon the FastTracks infrastructure to efficiently tackle the high-dimensional search space, leveraging a combination of downsampling and interpolation to reduce the computational cost of generating the binary Doppler correction. We performed an injection campaign to demonstrate the performance of this procedure and showed that a search using this approach is viable. Such a search would represent the first combination of Gaia observations and CW analysis.

</details>


### [224] [Stellar Scalar Monopoles from Gauss--Bonnet Curvature beyond Four Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14212)
*Sudan Hansraj*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在标量–高斯–玻内特（scalar–Gauss–Bonnet）耦合的一阶展开下，任意静态球对称星体（无论各向同性或各向异性）在四维以上都携带一个由能量密度加径向压强加权的内部积分给出的标量单极荷；在径向零能量条件下其符号与耦合常数一致，与同阶的Schwarzschild–Tangherlini黑洞相反；该荷由空间的高斯–玻内特曲率的lapse加权积分决定，在三维空间中恒为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标量–高斯–玻内特引力中标量场的单极（monopole）自由度是否在物质源（星体）内部被激发，并厘清其与真空黑洞解（Schwarzschild–Tangherlini）的标量荷之关系与符号差异，从而明确标量荷是否为纯粹的内部效应及其几何来源。

Method: 在标量–高斯–玻内特耦合下对静态球对称、各向同性/各向异性的星体度规与标量场作耦合常数的微扰展开，保留一阶项；标量单极荷表示为能量密度与径向压强加权（lapse 加权）的星体内部体积分，并借助径向零能量条件与空间高斯–玻内特曲率（lapse 加权形式）分析其符号与维度依赖性。

Result: 一阶标量单极荷由内部积分 ∫（能量密度 + 径向压强）× 权重给出，对任意状态方程均存在；在径向零能量条件下其符号与耦合常数同号，而同一阶下Schwarzschild–Tangherlini黑洞的标量荷符号相反；该荷由 lapse 加权的空间高斯–玻内特曲率固定，且在空间维度为三（即时空维数四）时该曲率恒为零，故单极荷消失。

Conclusion: 标量–高斯–玻内特引力中的标量单极是星体内部物质分布的直接产物而非真空效应；其符号由耦合常数与零能量条件决定，与黑洞情形相反，并且本质上依赖于空间维度高于三的结构曲率，因此该效应对维度与物态方程具有普适却可预测的特征。

Abstract: Above four dimensions every static, spherically symmetric star, isotropic or anisotropic, carries a scalar monopole at first order in a scalar--Gauss--Bonnet coupling, given by an interior integral weighted by the energy density plus radial pressure. Under the radial null energy condition its sign is that of the coupling for any equation of state, opposite to that of a Schwarzschild--Tangherlini black hole at the same order. The charge is fixed by the lapse-weighted Gauss--Bonnet curvature of space, which vanishes identically when space is three-dimensional.

</details>


### [225] [Simulation-Based Inference on EMRI Frequency Tracks: Harmonic Identification and Parameter Constraints from Search Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14255)
*Federico Fantoccoli,Lorenzo Speri,Bert Depoorter,Jonathan R. Gair*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 基于频率轨迹的SBI可快速识别EMRI谐波并将内禀参数先验体积压缩至最多10^17量级，为后续MCMC分析提供紧致先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI波形长且含多谐波，导致搜索与参数估计计算代价高昂；时频搜索得到的频率轨迹可能是更廉价的替代输入。

Method: 在大规模方位角与径向基频轨迹库上训练SBI网络；谐波识别通过在不同谐波标签假设下组合候选轨迹对，用后验中位数与网络接受率分类；参数识别采用序贯（截断先验）SBI，并用时频检测统计量交叉检验后验样本进行精化。

Result: 正确谐波对在各掩蔽水平下以>0.99概率进入前二；限于物理合理候选时98%直接识别正确。序贯SBI在数小时GPU时间内将先验体积压缩至多10^13倍，检测统计量精化再压缩约四个数量级，重度掩蔽亦有效。

Conclusion: 频率轨迹驱动的SBI提供了免波形、快速的谐波识别与先验压缩手段，显著减轻后续MCMC分析负担。

Abstract: Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals (EMRIs) are key targets for the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna, but their long, multi-harmonic waveforms make search and parameter estimation computationally expensive. A promising alternative is to work directly with frequency tracks recovered by a time-frequency search pipeline. We investigate whether such tracks can be used to (i) identify which harmonic(s) a recovered track corresponds to, and (ii) constrain the intrinsic EMRI parameters, including when tracks are partially masked near plunge. We first train Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) networks on large banks of azimuthal and radial fundamental-frequency tracks. For harmonic identification, we combine pairs of candidate tracks under different harmonic-label assumptions and use the resulting posterior medians and network acceptance rates to classify the correct labelling. For parameter identification, we apply a sequential (truncated-prior) SBI scheme and refine the results by cross-checking posterior samples against a time-frequency detection statistic. The correct harmonic pair is among the top-2 ranked candidates with probability $>0.99$ across masking levels, and is identified outright in 98% of cases when restricted to physically plausible candidates. Sequential SBI shrinks the intrinsic-parameter prior volume by up to $10^{13}$ within a few hours of GPU time, and the detection-statistic refinement reduces it by up to four further orders of magnitude, even for heavily masked tracks. Frequency-track-based SBI thus provides a fast, waveform-free way to identify EMRI harmonics and obtain tight prior volumes for targeted follow-up, substantially reducing the burden on subsequent MCMC-based analyses.

</details>


### [226] [Can torsion mimic a cosmological constant? A one-parameter logarithmic $f(T)$ extension of $Λ$CDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14305)
*Adriel O. Aquino,J. E. G. Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出一种为f(T)引力赋予ΛCDM几何起源的新函数形式，通过微扰展开与多数据集MCMC拟合，表明该模型与ΛCDM统计上一致，偏差参数γ被限制在接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为ΛCDM行为寻找几何起源，并在修正远平行引力f(T)框架下刻画对标准宇宙学模型的微小偏离。

Method: 构造新的f(T)函数形式使ΛCDM作为适当极限出现；在平坦FLRW背景中进行围绕ΛCDM的微扰展开，求解宇宙动力学；使用CC、DES-SN5YR/Pantheon+SH0ES/Union3.0、DESI DR2 BAO及Planck 2018 CMB距离先验等多数据集进行MCMC参数约束。

Result: 模型能准确再现观测到的加速膨胀，与当前宇宙学观测在统计上一致；描述偏离ΛCDM的额外参数γ被限制在接近零的取值。

Conclusion: 所提f(T)模型可自然嵌入ΛCDM极限，仅允许极小的动力学修正，与观测数据高度兼容。

Abstract: We study a modified teleparallel gravity model of the $f(T)$ type. We propose a new functional form for in order to obtain a geometric origin to the $ΛCDM$ behavior in an appropriate limit. A perturbative expansion around the $ΛCDM$ regime is carried out, and the model's cosmic dynamics are calculated inside the flat FLRW background. By employing a Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) analysis using multiple observational datasets, such as Cosmic Chronometers, Type Ia Supernova compilations (DES-SN5YR, Pantheon+SH0ES, and Union 3.0), DESI DR2 BAO measurements, and Planck 2018 CMB distance priors, to constrain the model parameters. The findings show that the suggested $f(T)$ model accurately replicates the observed accelerated expansion and is statistically consistent with current cosmological observations. The model stays close to the $ΛCDM$ scenario while permitting tiny dynamical adjustments, as evidenced by the extra parameter $γ$, which quantifies deviations from the conventional cosmological model, being limited to values near zero.

</details>


### [227] [Images of Regular Black Holes with Hedgehog Scalar Hair Embedded in Radiatively Inefficient Accretion Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14306)
*Yuxuan Shi,Hao Wei*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用M87*的质量与距离，对具有hedgehog标量毛的几何正则黑洞周围的辐射低效吸积流(RIAF)进行同步辐射成像，系统研究观测倾角、盘厚度与频率的影响，并指出区分此类正则核心与Schwarzschild黑洞所需的角分辨率超出当前基线能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞的几何临界尺度如何在吸积流图像中显现，受光子传播、等离子体辐射过程与仪器分辨率共同支配。作者希望量化正则黑洞核心（标量毛参数L/M）对临界曲线与辐射环的可观测印记，判断现有观测能否区分其与Schwarzschild黑洞。

Method: 采用M87*的质量和距离，构建hedgehog标量毛正则黑洞背景下的RIAF模型，计算总强度同步辐射图像；系统扫描观测倾角、吸积盘厚度与观测频率（84、230、345 GHz）；将L/M从0增至0.9，测量临界曲线直径、半通量半径及所需电子密度标度的变化。

Result: 在基准230 GHz设置下，L/M由0升至0.9使临界曲线直径收缩4.78%、半通量半径减小4.07%、所需电子密度标度降低9.15%。84 GHz时因同步自吸收增强，主亮度峰明显外移于临界曲线之外；230与345 GHz下更锐利的辐射环则紧贴临界曲线。

Conclusion: 正则核心会系统性地收缩辐射环并改变所需等离子体密度，但这些相对Schwarzschild黑洞的偏离需要优于当前基线所能达到的角分辨率才能分辨，故现有EHT类观测尚难检验该模型。

Abstract: How a black hole's geometric critical scale manifests in an accretion-flow image is governed by photon propagation, plasma radiative processes, and instrumental resolution. In this work, we investigate radiatively inefficient accretion flows (RIAF) surrounding geometrically regular black holes with hedgehog scalar hair. Adopting the mass and distance of $\mathrm{M87}^*$, we generate total-intensity synchrotron images and systematically explore the viewing inclination, disk thickness, and observing frequency. For the fiducial setup at $230\,\mathrm{GHz}$, raising $L/M$ from $0$ to $0.9$ contracts the critical curve diameter by $4.78\%$, shrinks the half-flux radius by $4.07\%$, and lowers the required electron-density scale by $9.15\%$. At $84\,\mathrm{GHz}$, the primary brightness peaks shift farther outside the critical curve due to enhanced synchrotron self-absorption, whereas the sharper emission rings at $230$ and $345\,\mathrm{GHz}$ closely trace the critical curve. Ultimately, we find that although the regular core systematically contracts the emission ring and alters the required plasma density, resolving these deviations from a Schwarzschild black hole demands angular resolution finer than that achievable with current baselines.

</details>


### [228] [Dynamical obstructions to primary Proca hair and its logarithmic Maxwell continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14344)
*Anirudh Pradhan,K. Ghaderi,Ulug'bek Eshkarayev,Sardor Sabirov,Javlon Rayimbaev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究正则化矢量Gauss-Bonnet引力中黑洞初级"毛"的动力学可容许性：原始常模家族无法同时满足正偶极动能与实的非退化径向张量特征；构建的正则背景虽满足径向与横向条件，但受约束的偶角系统存在负平方角速度的纵向模，形成局部角向障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验矢量Gauss-Bonnet理论中黑洞初级毛是否能作为动力学上可容许的构型，即同时满足动能正定性与各扰动扇区的稳定性（无非鬼/快子/梯度不稳定）。

Method: 对退化的原始常模家族施加正则哈密顿约化以处理退化边界；在非退化径向分支上为同一矢量引入Maxwell项，要求模函数变化，取正的对数延拓；用数值方法构造具有有限质量和正则未来视界的渐近平直外部解；推导受约束偶角系统的纵向模平方角速度及其逆对数系数（含矢量约束与四次相互作用），并用独立规范约化与完整背景解进行交叉验证。

Result: 原始非常数常模家族无法同时满足正偶极动能与实非退化径向张量特征；构造的背景在采样点上满足径向张量与横向矢量条件，空间无穷远质量有限且具有数值正则未来视界；但受约束偶角系统出现负平方角速度的纵向模，解析得到的逆对数系数与独立规范约化及完整背景数值一致。

Conclusion: 径向可容许性不足以保证整体动力学稳定性：局部角向障碍持续存在，说明该正则化矢量Gauss-Bonnet框架下的黑洞初级毛在偶角扇区不稳定，需进一步修改理论或背景才能得到自洽解。

Abstract: We examine the dynamical admissibility of primary black hole hair in a regularized vector theory of Gauss--Bonnet gravity. The original nontrivial constant norm family cannot satisfy both positive dipole kinetic energy and real nondegenerate radial tensor characteristics. A regular Hamiltonian reduction treats the degenerate boundaries. For asymptotically flat exteriors on the nondegenerate radial branch, adding a Maxwell term for the same vector requires a varying norm. Its positive logarithmic continuation has finite mass at spatial infinity and numerically constructed regular future horizons. The constructed backgrounds satisfy the radial tensor and transverse vector conditions at the sampled points. However, the constrained even angular system has a longitudinal mode with negative squared angular speed. We derive its inverse logarithm coefficient, including both the vector constraint and quartic interaction, and verify it using independent gauge reductions and the full backgrounds. This local angular obstruction persists despite the compatible radial conditions.

</details>


### [229] [Black Holes beyond General Relativity: The Effective Metric Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14353)
*Manuel Del Piano,Stefan Hohenegger,Francesco Sannino*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文综述有效度规描述（EMD）：以坐标不变的物理量（固有径向距离、自由下落观者固有时间）在视界附近作级数展开，建立模型无关的静态球对称黑洞形变框架，并用于阴影与准正规模的可观测量计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞是强场引力的理想窗口，GR之外（量子引力等）的偏离可能体现在黑洞几何中；但形变黑洞几何数量庞大，逐模型与观测比对不切实际，亟需统一的模型无关参数化框架。

Method: 以物理、坐标不变的可观测量（如距视界的固有径向距离、自由下落观者的固有时间）为展开变量，在视界附近做级数展开构造EMD；给出满足几何对称性的自洽条件通解，推导保证霍金温度与曲率不变量有限的普适正则性条件，证明不同物理量描述的等价性，并推广至带电、极端及低维黑洞；用Padé重求和将描述外推至远离视界区域，从而在检验场近似下计算黑洞阴影与eikonal极限的准正规模频率；与多种超越GR几何及其他模型无关参数化（通过显式变换映射）作对比检验。

Result: 建立了完整的EMD体系：自洽条件通解、普适正则性条件、不同物理量描述的等价性、对带电/极端/低维黑洞的扩展；结合Padé重求和实现阴影与eikonal准正规模的模型无关计算；在大量超越GR几何上验证成功，并给出与其他模型无关参数化的显式变换映射。

Conclusion: EMD作为超越GR形变的有效描述，在基础黑洞物理、量子引力启发形变与天体物理观测之间提供了连贯的桥梁，避免了逐模型比对的不便。

Abstract: Black holes provide a privileged window on gravity in the strong-field regime, and deviations from the classical solutions of General Relativity (GR) may encode information about quantum gravity and other extensions of the theory. The abundance of proposed deformed black hole geometries, however, makes model-by-model comparison with observations impractical. This review presents a unified account of the Effective Metric Description (EMD), a model-independent framework in which deformations of static, spherically symmetric black hole spacetimes are parametrized by physical, coordinate-invariant quantities, such as the proper radial distance from the event horizon or the proper time of a free-falling observer. After reviewing the classical black hole solutions and the open issues that motivate going beyond GR, we develop the EMD as a series expansion near the horizon for a given physical quantity. We provide the general solution of the resulting self-consistency conditions compatible with the symmetries of the geometry, derive universal regularity conditions ensuring a finite Hawking temperature and curvature invariants, establish the equivalence of descriptions based on different physical quantities, and extend the framework to charged, extremal and lower-dimensional black holes. Padé resummation extends the description away from the horizon, enabling model-independent computations of the black hole shadow and, in the eikonal limit, of quasi-normal mode frequencies in the test-field approximation. The framework is tested on a broad set of geometries used to model physics beyond GR, and compared with other model-independent parametrizations through explicit transformation maps. As an effective description of deformations beyond GR, the EMD provides a coherent bridge between fundamental black hole physics, quantum-gravity-inspired deformations, and astrophysical observables.

</details>


### [230] [Gravitational radiation from a particle in circular orbit around a wormhole: the nonrotating case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14455)
*Shauvik Biswas,Sayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将Poisson(1993)关于黑洞周围圆轨道粒子引力辐射的计算推广到虫洞情形，在慢速近似下用Teukolsky形式得到无穷远通量的解析结果，发现22模中存在黑洞极限下消失的0.5PN新项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将圆轨道粒子引力辐射的经典计算从黑洞推广到Schwarzschild型虫洞，以探索涉及虫洞等奇异致密天体（ECOs）的post-Newtonian波形特征。

Method: 考虑测试粒子绕Schwarzschild型虫洞运动，采用Teukolsky形式，在小速度近似下解析计算无穷远处的引力波通量，并聚焦22模进行PN展开；同时用数值方法验证波形。

Result: 在22模中发现了超出四极阶的新0.5PN阶项，该项在黑洞极限下消失；所有更高阶项均被修正，且在黑洞极限下给出正确对应。数值研究表明，当粒子远离ISCO时两种波形吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该工作是对涉及虫洞（ECOs）的post-Newtonian波形研究的首批尝试之一。

Abstract: In this paper, we have generalized Poisson's(1993) calculation for the case of gravitational radiation from a particle in a circular orbit around a black hole to the case of the wormhole. Considering the motion of a test particle around a Schwarzschild like wormhole and using Teukolsky formalism, we have obtained an analytical result of flux at infinity under the assumption of small velocities. Focusing on the 22-mode, we have found a novel 0.5PN order term beyond the quadrupole order, which vanishes in the black hole limit. On the other hand, all higher order terms are getting modified in such a way that they give correct correspondence in the black hole limit. We have further corroborated our results with the numerical investigations, which show that the two waveforms match well when the particle lies far from ISCO. Thus, our work provides one of the first attempts toward the study of the post-Newtonian waveforms involving wormholes (ECOs).

</details>


### [231] [Singustars: Between Black Holes and Compact Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14482)
*Pablo Laguna*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出并研究"奇异星"(singustars)：TOV方程的奇异解族，由负质量裸奇点外包裹自引力流体厚壳构成。Γ=2情形的稳定区为M≲0.63、C≲0.25，仍比任何稳定正则polytrope更致密，但无光环；非线性演化表明终态依赖扰动，可出现视界生成元的过去端点落在奇点上（先于首个俘获面），或奇点保持裸暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典TOV方程通常只讨论中心正则的星体。作者希望系统考察含负点质量奇点的奇异解（包围负质量裸奇性的自引力流体壳），明确其参数空间、稳定性与动力学终态，并检验其在致密度上能否超越Buchdahl界与正则polytrope的限制。

Method: 对Γ律polytrope状态方程求解TOV方程得到精确平衡解（双零压面所界定的厚壳+Schwarzschild外部），以(M, C=M/R)两参数刻画；通过径向微扰模式分析稳定性；数值扫描参数空间；并在入射零坐标(ingoing null coordinates)下做非线性演化，配合出射零测地线反向追踪定位视界生成元与俘获面。

Result: Singustars构成双参数族，正则polytrope对应中心负质量趋于零的极限；其致密度可超过Buchdahl界。Γ=2时仅M≲0.63、C≲0.25稳定，该稳定区虽含比任何稳定正则polytrope更致密的位形，但均无光环。数值分辨的参数空间中不稳定解仅有一个不稳定径向模，许多解能量上非束缚。非线性演化显示终态依扰动而异：坍缩模型中反向追踪出射零射线可识别过去端点位于奇点上的视界生成元，且早于首个俘获面出现；外向扰动使壳层无束缚并最终仅留下裸奇点；束缚壳则向同守恒质量的稳定位形迁移并围绕其振荡。

Conclusion: 含负质量裸奇点的奇异TOV解是有物理意义的双参数解族，可超越Buchdahl致密度极限并具有可解析的线性稳定性结构；其非线性命运非唯一，取决于扰动性质，既可能通过视界生成元与奇点相连而形成黑洞，也可能维持裸奇性或弛豫到稳定振荡位形。

Abstract: The Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff equations admit singular solutions in which a negative point mass is surrounded by a self-gravitating fluid. For a $Γ$-law polytropic equation of state, these solutions, which we call singustars, are thick shells bounded by two zero-pressure surfaces around a naked, repulsive, negative-mass singularity, with a Schwarzschild exterior. They form a two-parameter family, labeled by the mass $M$ and the compactness $C\equiv M/R$. Singustars join regular polytropes as the central negative mass vanishes and reach compactness beyond the Buchdahl bound. From exact equilibria and their radial modes, we find that singustars with $Γ= 2$ are stable only for $M\lesssim0.63$ and $C\lesssim0.25$, a region that nonetheless contains configurations more compact than any stable regular polytrope, although none with a light ring. In the numerically resolved parameter space, unstable singustars have a single unstable radial mode; many are energetically unbound. Nonlinear evolutions in ingoing null coordinates show that the end state depends on the perturbation. For the collapsing models studied, backward tracing of outgoing null rays identifies an event-horizon generator with a past endpoint on the singularity, before the first trapped surface appears. Outward perturbations disperse unbound shells and leave the singularity naked, while a bound shell migrates toward, and oscillates about, a stable configuration with the same conserved masses.

</details>
